Motivation: 当前主体保持视频生成的核心瓶颈在于"点参考范式"——仅用单张静态图像表征身份，无法解耦姿态、配饰、光照等混杂因素，导致生成人物在运动、视角变化、遮挡、表情变化下不可识别。

Method: 提出 Argus 框架，核心为 SMII（堆叠多视角身份马赛克注入）：(1) MLLM 身份导演从图像/视频中筛选信息量大的身份证据；(2) 将证据排列为 3×3 堆叠马赛克，与扩散时间同步；(3) 作为负时间只读记忆注入 Wan 原生 token 空间。配套策略包括：无交叉对反事实训练、时序身份退火（TIA）、自适应自相似度引导（ASLG），无需配对主题-视频监督。另构建 HardID-Celeb 基准及 YawScore/OccScore 指标。

Result: 在 OpenS2V-Eval Human-Domain 上：Total Score 64.38，FaceSim 71.86，NexusScore 51.62，NaturalScore 79.14，均为 SOTA。在 HardID-Celeb 上：FaceSim 76.80，YawScore 和 OccScore 分别超越最强基线 12.60 和 15.10 分。

Conclusion: 动态身份记忆与大规模反事实自监督对主体保持视频生成极为有效；SMII 将身份表征从单点静态推向紧凑动态分布是突破点参考瓶颈的关键。

Abstract: Subject-preserving video generation is not solved by frontal-face similarity alone: a generated person must remain recognizable across motion, large viewpoint changes, expression shifts, occlusion, scale variation, and conflicts among text, first-frame, and identity references. We argue that the central bottleneck is the point-reference paradigm, which collapses identity into a single static observation entangled with pose, accessories, lighting, background, and camera statistics. We introduce Argus, a Wan-based framework centered on Stacked Multi-View Identity Mosaic Injection (SMII). SMII converts MLLM-selected image/video identity evidence into a 3*3 stacked mosaic, synchronizes the mosaic with the current diffusion time, and injects it as negative-time read-only memory in Wan's native token space. This turns identity from an external clean adapter or a single reference image into a compact dynamic distribution. Around SMII, an MLLM Identity Director selects informative identity moments and resolves condition conflicts, while no-cross-pair counterfactual training, Temporal Identity Annealing, and Adaptive Self-Likeness Guidance improve robustness without paired subject-video supervision. We further release HardID-Celeb, a public-figure identity-stress benchmark, and introduce YawScore and OccScore to probe large-yaw and first-frame-occlusion robustness. Argus achieves state-of-the-art results on OpenS2V-Eval Human-Domain, reaching 64.38 Total Score, 71.86 FaceSim, 51.62 NexusScore, and 79.14 NaturalScore. On HardID-Celeb, Argus obtains 76.80 FaceSim and improves YawScore and OccScore by 12.60 and 15.10 points over the strongest baselines, demonstrating that dynamic identity memory and large-scale counterfactual self-supervision are highly effective for subject-preserving video generation.

</details>


### [37] [Parameter-Efficient Adapter Tuning for Tabular-Image Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11682)
*Jiaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TI-Adapter 是一种针对表格-图像多模态学习的适配器微调框架，通过冻结预训练编码器并在表格和图像分支中分别引入适配器，在显著减少可训练参数的同时，达到甚至超越全量微调的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格-图像多模态学习中，预训练编码器虽能提供强表征，但全量微调计算成本高昂，而完全冻结编码器又限制了任务特异性适应能力，因此需要一种高效的多模态适配方案来平衡性能与效率。

Method: 提出 TI-Adapter 框架：冻结预训练表格编码器，在其输出的表格嵌入后接一个适配器；图像分支则采用嵌入级和瓶颈级适配器进行适应，而非全量微调。两种模态均通过轻量级适配器实现任务特异性调整。

Result: 在20个表格-图像数据集上的实验表明，TI-Adapter 取得了与全量微调相当甚至更优的预测性能，且可训练参数量大幅减少。消融研究进一步验证了适配器放置位置对于平衡性能和实际效率的关键作用。

Conclusion: TI-Adapter 为表格-图像多模态学习提供了一种参数高效且性能优异的微调范式，通过合理设计适配器的插入位置，可在不牺牲预测精度的前提下大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Tabular-image multimodal learning aims to improve predictive modeling by jointly using structured tabular attributes and visual data. Although pretrained encoders provide strong modality-specific representations, full fine-tuning can be computationally expensive, while keeping encoders frozen may limit task-specific adaptation. We propose the Tabular-Image Adapter (TI-Adapter), a modality-specific adapter-based fine-tuning framework for efficient multimodal adaptation. TI-Adapter freezes the pretrained tabular encoder and learns an adapter after the extracted tabular embedding, while adapting the image branch with embedding-level and bottleneck-level adapters instead of full fine-tuning. Experiments on 20 tabular-image datasets show that TI-Adapter achieves competitive or better predictive performance than full fine-tuning while using substantially fewer trainable parameters. Ablation studies further demonstrate the importance of adapter placement for balancing performance and practical efficiency.

</details>


### [38] [Reason, Then Re-reason: Cross-view Revisiting Improves Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11683)
*Chaofan Ma,Zhenjie Mao,Yuhuan Yang,Fanqin Zeng,Yue Shi,Yingjie Zhou,Xiaofeng Cao,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReRe框架，通过"先推理、再重新推理"的两阶段推理策略，结合从预测3D几何合成的新视角视频，显著提升MLLM在自中心视频空间推理上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅进行单轮推理，模型被迫依赖语义先验而非可验证证据来解决几何歧义。作者认为空间推理应具有"可重访性"——在有限证据下形成的结论，应在获得互补视角时能够被修正。

Method: 提出训练无关的推理时框架ReRe：(1) Reason阶段，MLLM从原始视频形成空间假设；(2) Re-reason阶段，通过Geometry-to-Video管线从预测的3D几何渲染合成的新视角视频（高视点、斜视角、场景全覆盖），MLLM观察后验证或修正假设。整个过程无需修改模型架构。

Result: 在VSI-Bench和STI-Bench上的广泛评估表明，ReRe显著提升开源MLLM的性能，使其媲美闭源的最先进模型。

Conclusion: 将空间推理建模为可重访过程是有效的，ReRe通过引入互补几何证据实现了这一范式，为自中心视频空间理解提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning from egocentric videos is inherently challenging because the observable evidence is constrained by the camera trajectory. Existing methods rely on single-turn inference, forcing models to resolve geometric ambiguity through semantic priors rather than verifiable evidence. We argue that spatial reasoning should be revisitable: conclusions formed under limited evidence should remain open to revision when complementary viewpoints become available. Building on this insight, we propose Reason, then Re-reason (ReRe), a training-free, inference-time framework with two phases: in the Reason Phase, an MLLM forms a spatial hypothesis from the original video; in the Re-reason Phase, it verifies or revises the hypothesis by observing a synthesized novel-view video. To enable effective cross-view revisiting, we design a Geometry-to-Video pipeline that renders strategically complementary novel views from predicted 3D geometry. These views feature an elevated, oblique perspective with scene-spanning coverage, while preserving the MLLM's native video interface without architectural modifications. Extensive evaluations on VSI-Bench and STI-Bench demonstrate that ReRe substantially boosts open-source MLLMs to rival proprietary state-of-the-art performance. Project page: https://zhenjiemao.github.io/ReRe/

</details>


### [39] [DroneShield-AI: A Multi-Modal Sensor Fusion Framework for Real-Time Autonomous Drone Threat Detection, Behavioral Intent Classification, and Swarm Intelligence in Contested Airspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11687)
*Marius Bayizere*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DroneShield-AI是一个统一的开放框架，集成RF信号分类、声学检测、YOLOv8视觉检测、证据加权传感器融合、行为意图分类引擎(BICE)和GNN群体智能模块六层处理，实现96.1%检测精度、3.2%误报率和142ms延迟，BICE首次提出六类威胁分类体系并提供30秒预警窗口，GNN-SIM是首个基于图注意力网络的对抗性多无人机编队分析开源框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机威胁已成为21世纪关键安全挑战，现有系统缺乏统一的多模态融合框架、缺乏系统化的无人机行为威胁分类体系，且对多无人机群体协同攻击的分析手段不足。

Method: 提出DroneShield-AI框架，包含六层处理流水线：(1)RF信号分类；(2)声学电机特征检测；(3)基于YOLOv8的视觉检测；(4)证据加权多传感器融合；(5)行为意图分类引擎(BICE)——首次定义六类无人机飞行模式威胁分类体系，实现30秒提前预警；(6)基于图注意力网络(GAT)的群体智能模块(GNN-SIM)——首个面向对抗性多无人机编队分析的开源框架。系统总成本约$500-$780，运行于消费级CPU硬件。

Result: 在三个公开真实数据集上评估，融合流水线达到96.1%检测准确率、3.2%误报率、AUC-ROC为0.981，端到端延迟142ms。

Conclusion: DroneShield-AI以低成本消费级硬件实现了高性能的多模态无人机检测与威胁预警，BICE和GNN-SIM分别填补了无人机行为威胁分类和多机群体分析领域的空白，框架完全开源。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) threats have emerged as a defining security challenge of the 21st century. This paper presents DroneShield-AI, a unified open framework integrating six processing layers: RF signal classification, acoustic motor-signature detection, YOLOv8-based visual detection, evidence-weighted sensor fusion, a Behavioral Intent Classification Engine (BICE), and a Graph Neural Network Swarm Intelligence Module (GNN-SIM). BICE introduces the first systematic six-class threat taxonomy for drone flight patterns, enabling predictive operator alerts with a 30-second advance-warning horizon. GNN-SIM is the first open framework for adversarial multi-drone formation analysis using Graph Attention Networks. Evaluated on three publicly available real-world datasets, the fused pipeline achieves 96.1% detection accuracy, 3.2% false alarm rate, AUC-ROC: 0.981, and 142ms end-to-end latency on commodity CPU-class hardware at approximately $500-$780 USD total system cost. All code, model weights, and simulation datasets are publicly released at submission.

</details>


### [40] [RankVR: Low-Rank Structure Perception and Value Recalibration for Robust Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11689)
*Jiale Huang,Zixu Li,Zhiheng Fu,Zhiwei Chen,Qinlei Huang,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对组合图像检索中的噪声三元组对应问题，提出RankVR框架，通过全局结构一致性感知和自适应语义价值校准，实现鲁棒的CIR模型训练，在FashionIQ和CIRR上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIR去噪方法仅关注二元不匹配或逐点标量估计，忽略了样本群体间的全局结构相关性和训练过程中的动态价值变化，导致两个关键挑战未被解决：语义相关性的全局结构不一致性和难样本判别不确定性。

Method: (1) 提出GSCP模块，利用相关矩阵的有效秩解耦干净样本与结构噪声，通过秩差度量识别破坏宏观语义对称性的样本；(2) 提出ASVC模块，融合训练潜力和可靠性动态量化每个三元组的语义价值，以区分高价值难干净样本并抑制逻辑冲突噪声。

Result: 在FashionIQ和CIRR基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，RankVR显著优于现有SOTA方法，验证了其在噪声环境下的优越鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RankVR通过全局结构一致性与动态价值感知两大机制，有效解决了NTC问题中的结构噪声和难样本不确定性，为鲁棒组合图像检索提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) constitutes a pivotal paradigm requiring models to perform joint reasoning on reference images and modification texts. However, the prevalence of Noisy Triplet Correspondence (NTC) in large-scale datasets severely constrains model performance. Existing denoising methods either target binary mismatches or rely on scalar-based point-wise estimation, neglecting rich global structural correlations among sample populations and dynamic value variations during training, thereby yielding suboptimal results. This paper identifies two critical unresolved challenges: Global Structural Inconsistency of Semantic Correlations and Hard Sample Discrimination Uncertainty. To address these, we propose RankVR, a framework designed to construct a robust CIR model via global structure consistency and dynamic value perception. Specifically, we introduce the Global Structure Consistency Perception (GSCP) module, which utilizes the Effective Rank of the Correlation Matrix to decouple clean samples from structural noise. By measuring rank difference, GSCP identifies samples disrupting macroscopic semantic symmetry. Furthermore, we develop the Adaptive Semantic Value Calibration (ASVC) module to distinguish high-value hard clean samples. By integrating training potential and reliability, it dynamically quantifies the semantic value of each triplet, ensuring effective utilization of hard samples while suppressing noise characterized by logical conflicts. Extensive experiments on the FashionIQ and CIRR benchmark datasets demonstrate that RankVR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, validating its superior robustness in noisy environments.

</details>


### [41] [MedCTA: A Benchmark for Clinical Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11702)
*Tajamul Ashraf,Hyewon Jeong,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedCTA是一个面向临床多模态工具智能体的评测基准，包含107个真实临床任务、5种已部署工具及临床医生验证的可执行轨迹，揭示了当前前沿模型在多步临床工具使用中的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI评测大多局限于孤立感知或单轮问答，无法评估智能体在工具检索、证据获取与整合中的规划、工具调用及执行可靠性。临床决策需要多步骤工具协同，缺乏过程感知的评测导致无法诊断这些环节的失效模式。

Method: 构建MedCTA基准，包含107个真实世界临床任务，覆盖放射影像、病理切片和报告等多模态输入，配备5种已部署工具及临床医生验证的可执行轨迹。采用过程感知评估体系，量化工具选择正确性、参数有效性、执行稳定性、轨迹保真度和结果质量五个维度，并对18个开源和闭源多模态模型进行系统评测。

Result: 即使是最前沿的多模态系统，在自主多步临床工具使用中仍表现出显著脆弱性：自主执行中普遍出现协议违规、过早停止和错误工具调用；即便使用金标准工具路由，性能虽有大幅提升但仍不完整。强骨干感知能力并未转化为可靠的临床智能体行为。

Conclusion: MedCTA为审计、诊断和推进可信医疗AI智能体提供了严格的测试平台，揭示了当前多模态模型从感知能力到智能体可靠行为之间存在关键缺口，需针对性改进规划、工具调用和执行鲁棒性。

Abstract: To make clinically grounded decisions, medical AI agents are expected to go beyond simple recognition and be capable of tool retrieval, evidence acquisition, and integration. Existing benchmarks largely evaluate isolated perception or single-turn question answering, and therefore provide limited visibility into failures of planning, tool recruitment, and rollout reliability. We introduce MedCTA, a benchmark for evaluating medical tool agents on clinician-validated, step-implicit tasks grounded in realistic multimodal clinical inputs, including radiology images, pathology slides, and reports. MedCTA comprises 107 real-world clinical tasks with clinician-verified executable trajectories over 5 deployed tools, and supports process-aware evaluation of tool selection, argument validity, execution stability, trajectory fidelity, and outcome quality. We benchmark 18 open- and closed-source multimodal models and find that even frontier systems remain brittle in multi-step clinical tool use: autonomous rollouts are dominated by protocol failures, premature stopping, and incorrect tool recruitment, while gold-standard tool routing yields large but still incomplete gains. These results show that strong backbone perception does not translate into reliable agentic behavior in clinical settings. MedCTA provides a rigorous testbed for auditing, diagnosing, and advancing trustworthy medical AI agents. The dataset and evaluation suite are available at https://ivul-kaust.github.io/MedCTA/

</details>


### [42] [ERN-Net : Evolving Reason Node-Net for Document Binarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11710)
*Hsin-Jui Pan,Sheng-Wei Chan,Jen-Shiung Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ERN-Net 是一种用于文档图像二值化的高效模型，通过演化推理节点和多尺度推理来增强退化敏感区域，并在低数据、低内存场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档图像二值化中，微弱笔划、断裂字符和噪声背景等退化敏感区域难以处理，且实际应用中对内存和数据的限制要求模型轻量高效。

Method: 提出 ERN-Net，核心包含演化推理节点（Evolving Reason Nodes）和多尺度推理机制，用于增强退化敏感区域的特征表达；同时对比了 ResNet-101、ConvNeXt-Tiny、ConvNeXt-Base 三种骨干网络，最终选用 ConvNeXt-Tiny 以实现精度与内存的最佳权衡；并采用基于 DIBCO 的预训练策略来提升性能。

Result: ConvNeXt-Tiny 在精度与内存之间取得了最佳的实际权衡；DIBCO 预训练仅需约 1.5 小时额外训练时间，即可在不增加内存消耗的前提下提升二值化性能；在 DIBCO 风格基准测试中，ERN-Net 在低数据和低内存设置下均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: ERN-Net 是一种适用于资源受限场景的文档图像二值化解决方案，通过演化推理节点、多尺度推理以及高效的骨干网络选择与预训练策略，在低数据、低内存条件下实现了优异的二值化性能。

Abstract: This paper presents ERN-Net, an Evolving Reason Node-Net for efficient document image binarization. ERN-Net enhances degradation-sensitive regions, such as faint strokes, broken characters, and noisy backgrounds, through evolving reason nodes and multi-scale reasoning. We further compare ResNet-101, ConvNeXt-Tiny, and ConvNeXt-Base, and find that ConvNeXt-Tiny provides the best practical trade-off between accuracy and memory usage. In addition, DIBCO-based pretraining improves binarization performance without increasing model memory consumption, requiring only about 1.5 additional training hours. Experiments on DIBCO-style benchmarks show that ERN-Net is effective under low-data and low-memory settings.

</details>


### [43] [Ouroboros-Spatial: Closing the Data-Model Loop for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11719)
*Enhan Zhao,Wei Wu,Yuanrui Zhang,Xueliang Zhao,Di He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ouroboros-Spatial，一种自演进的训练框架，通过模型交替扮演“提议者”和“求解器”的角色，实现训练分布与模型能力的协同演化，在显著降低数据需求的同时大幅提升多模态大模型的空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理训练方法依赖静态数据集，对所有样本一视同仁，忽略了模型能力的动态变化，导致数据效率低下——大量训练消耗在过易或过难的样本上。

Method: 设计闭环自演进框架：冻结的提议者从3D场景元数据和原始视频帧生成空间问答对及可执行代码（用于获取可靠真值）；可学习的求解器在筛选后的样本上微调，其逐样本预测置信度作为难度信号反馈给提议者，指导下一轮生成更匹配当前能力的题目。

Result: 在六个空间推理基准上，Ouroboros-Spatial以比近期大规模数据集少一个数量级的训练样本，显著提升Qwen3-VL-4B和Qwen3-VL-8B；在VSI-Bench上分别获得9.9和6.8个百分点的绝对提升，使两个模型均超越多种强开源和闭源基线。

Conclusion: 自演进训练范式有效解决了空间推理中静态数据策展的固有低效问题，通过训练分布与模型能力的闭环协同演化，在极低数据成本下实现了可观的性能增益。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a persistent challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing approaches largely rely on large-scale, statically curated datasets, where all training samples are treated uniformly regardless of the model's evolving capabilities. This static paradigm is inherently data-inefficient: training capacity is often spent on samples that are either trivial or overly difficult for the model at its current stage. To address this limitation, we propose Ouroboros-Spatial, a self-evolving training framework in which the model plays dual roles as a proposer and a solver. In each iteration, a frozen proposer generates spatial question-answer (QA) pairs from 3D scene metadata and raw video frames, together with executable code for deriving reliable ground truth. A learnable solver is then fine-tuned on the accepted samples, and its per-sample prediction confidence is used as a difficulty signal. This signal is fed back to the proposer in the next iteration, guiding it to generate questions better matched to the solver's current capabilities. Through this closed-loop design, the training distribution co-evolves with model ability, reducing redundant trivial examples while filtering out ambiguous or uninformative samples with limited learning value. Across six spatial reasoning benchmarks, Ouroboros-Spatial substantially improves Qwen3-VL-4B and Qwen3-VL-8B while using an order of magnitude fewer training examples than recent large-scale curated datasets. On VSI-Bench, it yields absolute gains of 9.9 and 6.8 points for the 4B and 8B models, respectively, enabling both to outperform a wide range of strong open-source and proprietary baselines.

</details>


### [44] [Multi-View In-Cabin Monitoring System for Public Transport Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11739)
*Evgeny Gorelik,Kenny Dean Karrow,Fikret Sivrikaya,Sahin Albayrak,Christian Baumann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个面向公共交通的多视角座舱内监控数据集，包含4个RGB-D相机和旋转LiDAR的同步数据，提供3D人体姿态与定向3D边界框标注，并配套校准、伪标签流水线及nuScenes格式转换，支持多视角3D检测模型的基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有座舱内监控数据集缺乏面向公共交通场景的多视角、多模态（RGB-D+LiDAR）同步数据，难以支持自动驾驶巴士等应用中乘客3D感知模型的训练与评估。

Method: 在一辆数字化、部分自动化的德国城市巴士内部署4个朝内的RGB-D相机和一个旋转LiDAR，采集9136组同步样本；开发了一套标定与伪标签流水线，自动生成乘员的3D人体姿态估计和定向3D边界框；提供nuScenes格式转换，并在此数据集上对Lift-Splat-Shoot和BEVFusion等代表性多视角3D检测模型进行基准测试。

Result: 构建了一个包含9136组同步标注样本的多视角座舱内监控数据集；通过伪标签流水线生成了3D人体姿态与定向3D边界框；在数据集上对主流多视角3D检测模型进行了基准评估，验证了其在座舱内感知任务上的可行性，并支持小规模训练。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了公共交通座舱内多视角3D感知任务的空白，配套工具链降低了使用门槛，为未来座舱内乘员检测、姿态估计等研究提供了标准化的基准平台。

Abstract: We introduce a multi-view in-cabin monitoring dataset for public transportation with synchronized RGB and depth images from four inward-facing cameras and a rotating LiDAR covering the vehicle interior of a digitalized and partly automated German city bus. The dataset contains 9.136 synchronized samples with annotations and is accompanied by a calibration and pseudo-labeling pipeline that generates 3D human pose estimates and oriented 3D bounding boxes for occupants. We further provide a nuScenes-format conversion and benchmark representative multi-view 3D detection models (e.g., Lift-Splat-Shoot and BEVFusion), supporting comparative evaluation and small-scale training of multi-view in-cabin perception models. The dataset and tools are available at https://github.com/EvgenyGorelik/multiview_incabin_dataset.

</details>


### [45] [UniReason-Med: A Shared Grounded Reasoning Interface for 2D-to-3D Transfer in Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11740)
*Mengzhuo Chen,Yan Shu,Chi Liu,Hongming Piao,Xidong Wang,Derek Li,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniReason-Med，一个统一处理2D和3D医学影像的单检查点框架，通过共享的grounded reasoning接口实现跨模态推理迁移。同时构建了220K的UniMed-CoT数据集，实验证明2D+3D联合训练显著提升3D医学VQA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D医学图像标注数据丰富，但3D医学VQA数据稀缺且标注成本高。作者探索是否可以通过共享的grounded reasoning接口，将2D图像上的推理监督迁移到3D体积医学理解任务中。

Method: 提出UniReason-Med框架：推理时接受2D图像或切片序列化的3D体积，通过共享的框语法(box syntax)、区域token注入(region-token injection)和通用grounded reasoning策略生成交错文本推理与定位视觉证据。训练数据UniMed-CoT包含220K指令调优样本（170K 2D + 50K 3D），采用监督微调(SFT)后接结果级强化学习(RL)，且RL阶段不使用IoU/Dice定位奖励。

Result: 数据混合与组件消融实验表明：(1) 2D+3D联合grounded监督相比仅3D训练显著提升3D推理性能；(2) grounding机制和region-token注入对2D和3D任务均持续有益。

Conclusion: 共享的grounded reasoning接口可将2D图像的推理结构有效迁移至切片序列化的3D医学理解中，为解决3D医学VQA数据稀缺问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study whether grounded reasoning supervision from abundant 2D medical images can improve 3D medical VQA when both input types are aligned through a common reasoning interface. We introduce UniReason-Med, a single-checkpoint framework that processes either a 2D image or a slice-serialized 3D volume at inference time, generating interleaved textual reasoning and localized visual evidence through shared box syntax, region-token injection, and a common grounded reasoning policy. To train this interface, we construct UniMed-CoT, a 220K instruction-tuning dataset with interleaved textual reasoning and grounded visual evidence, including 170K 2D and 50K 3D samples. Through supervised fine-tuning followed by outcome-level reinforcement learning, UniReason-Med learns to generate grounded reasoning traces without IoU/Dice-based localization rewards during RL. Data-mixture and component ablations show that joint 2D+3D grounded supervision substantially improves 3D reasoning over 3D-only training, while grounding and region-token injection consistently benefit both 2D and 3D tasks. These results suggest that a shared grounded reasoning interface can transfer reasoning structure from 2D images to slice-serialized volumetric medical understanding. The code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/IQuestLab/unireason-med.

</details>


### [46] [From Prompts to Tokens: Internalizing Causal Supervision in Vision-Language Model for Multi-Image Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11745)
*Haoping Yu,Yuanxi Li,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BridgeVLM 提出将因果图结构内化到 VLM 推理流中，通过因果令牌和 RAMP 层实现因果消息传递，配合多粒度监督 M3S，显著提升多图干预、反事实推理及因果结构学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLM 在视觉因果推理（尤其是多图干预和反事实查询）上表现脆弱，现有方法多通过文本提示注入因果知识，因果机制外挂于模型执行过程，推理时缺乏可靠控制。需要将因果推理内化到模型架构中。

Method: BridgeVLM 包含三个核心组件：(1) 从多图输入中归纳因果图，并转换为结构化的因果令牌（Causal Tokens）；(2) 在 LLM 解码器中注入 RAMP（Relational-Aware Message Passing）层，对因果令牌执行消息传递；(3) 提出统一训练接口 M3S，从局部和全局两个粒度提供细粒度因果监督。

Result: 在 CausalVLBench 干预任务上达到 54.4% 准确率（对比提示级监督的 33.2%）；Causal3D 从 43.6% 提升至 49.0%；因果结构学习 F1 分数从 33.4% 跃升至 75.1%。

Conclusion: 将因果图内化到 VLM 推理流程中，配合结构化因果令牌和专用消息传递机制，能够显著增强模型的视觉因果推理能力，尤其在干预和因果结构学习方面带来大幅提升。

Abstract: Visual causal reasoning is essential for understanding and intervening in the physical world, requiring identification of causal variables from visual inputs and reasoning over intervention effects. Despite recent progress, large vision--language models (VLMs) remain brittle at such tasks, especially for interventional and counterfactual queries over multi-image inputs. Most existing explorations inject causal knowledge via textual prompts, leaving causal mechanisms external to model execution and limiting reliable control during inference. To address this problem, we propose BridgeVLM, which internalizes visual causal reasoning by inducing a causal graph from multi-image inputs and converting it into structured Causal Tokens executed by RAMP layers injected into the LLM decoder for causal message passing. We further introduce a unified training interface M3S for fine-grained causal supervision from different granularities (local/global level). BridgeVLM achieves 54.4% accuracy on intervention tasks on CausalVLBench (vs. 33.2% with prompt-level supervision), improves results on Causal3D from 43.6% to 49.0%, and substantially improves causal structure learning on CausalVLBench ($F_1$: 33.4% $\rightarrow$ 75.1%).

</details>


### [47] [AnchorEdit: Maintaining Temporal Consistency in Multi-turn Image Editing via Causal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11751)
*Hang Xu,Xiaoxiao Ma,Guohui Zhang,Yu Hu,Siming Fu,Jie Huang,Lin Song,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorEdit 是首个专为高分辨率、长序列多轮图像编辑设计的自回归扩散框架，通过三阶段训练课程和记忆机制解决身份漂移与误差累积问题，在10轮以上编辑中保持领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮图像编辑模型存在身份漂移和误差累积问题。基于视频先验的方法使用双向注意力机制，与交互式编辑的因果、序列化本质根本性不匹配。

Method: 提出AnchorEdit，包含：(1) 三阶段训练课程——身份保持单轮预训练、因果自回归微调（含新型自 rollout 策略缓解曝光偏差）、一致性蒸馏实现4步高效生成；(2) 推理时引入记忆机制锚定初始主体身份，确保长编辑轨迹上的稳定外推。

Result: 在新建的高分辨率多轮编辑基准上，AnchorEdit 实现了最优结果，在10轮以上交互中仍能保持卓越的主体保真度和指令遵循能力。

Conclusion: 通过将视频先验与因果推断桥接，AnchorEdit 有效解决了多轮编辑中的身份一致性问题，为迭代式设计场景提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Multi-turn image editing is essential for iterative design, yet current models often struggle with identity drift and error accumulation over successive steps. While existing research leverages video priors for consistency, their reliance on bidirectional attention is fundamentally misaligned with the causal, sequential nature of interactive editing. In this paper, we propose AnchorEdit, the first autoregressive (AR) diffusion-based framework designed specifically for high-resolution, long-term multi-turn editing. AnchorEdit bridges the gap between video priors and causal inference through a three-stage training curriculum: identity-preserving sing-turn pretraining, causal AR forcing fine-tuning with a novel self-rollout strategy to mitigate exposure bias, and consistency distillation for efficient 4-step generation. During inference, we introduce a memory mechanism to anchor the initial subject identity and ensure stable extrapolation across extended editing trajectories. To evaluate performance, we provide a new high-resolution multi-turn editing benchmark designed to stress-test long-horizon stability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AnchorEdit achieves state-of-the-art results, maintaining exceptional subject fidelity and instruction following even over 10+ interaction rounds.

</details>


### [48] [Battery detection of XRay images using transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11779)
*Nermeen Abou Baker,David Rohrschneider,Uwe Handmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究利用迁移学习，基于YOLOv5m实现锂电池（棱柱形、软包、圆柱形）的检测与分类，电池检测精度达94%，推理时间22ms，较原始预训练权重提升5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电子废弃物回收、电池分拣等应用场景对电池自动检测与分类的需求急剧增长，现有预训练模型在电池检测这一特定领域精度有限，亟需探索更高效、更精准的迁移学习方案。

Method: 采用两阶段迁移学习策略：(1) 首先使用预训练YOLOv5m在大规模电子设备数据集上训练，使模型获得电子设备通用特征；(2) 将第一阶段训练得到的权重作为初始化，迁移至电池检测与分类任务，针对棱柱形、软包和圆柱形三类锂电池进行定位与识别。

Result: 电池检测精度达到94%，较YOLOv5m原始预训练权重提升了5个百分点，推理时间仅为22毫秒，满足实时检测需求。

Conclusion: 迁移学习可显著提升电池检测与分类的性能，两阶段迁移策略有效弥合了通用预训练模型与特定领域任务之间的差距，为电池自动分拣系统提供了高效可行的技术方案。

Abstract: The need for detecting and sorting batteries is drastically increasing for many applications. This study proves the potential of transfer learning in predicting whether the image contains a battery or not, the location and identifying three types of batteries, namely: prismatic, pouch, and cylindrical Lithium-Ion Batteries (LIB). Particularly, it focuses on the transfer learning method in two applications: Training a large-scale dataset to detect electronic devices using a pre-trained YOLOv5m, then using these latter trained weights to detect and classify the batteries. The precision of battery detection achieves 94%, which outperforms the pretrained YOLOv5m weights with 5%, in 22 ms inference time.

</details>


### [49] [Seeing What Matters: Perceptual Wrapper with Common Randomness for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11782)
*He-Bi Yang,Jing-Zhong Chen,Yen-Kuan Ho,Sang NguyenQuang,Fan-Yi Hsu,Yun-Yu Lee,Jui-Chiu Chiang,Wen-Hsiao Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种2D感知包装器，利用轻量合成网络以内容与视角依赖的方式增强3DGS渲染输出，通过Wasserstein Distortion监督匹配局部特征统计而非逐像素重建，有效缓解模糊问题，在内存受限和率失真优化场景下显著提升感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting虽能实时渲染，但在合成高频纹理方面存在明显不足，这一问题在内存受限和率失真优化（RDO）管线中尤为严重，导致渲染结果模糊。

Method: 提出即插即用的2D感知包装器：以伪随机高斯噪声为条件的轻量合成网络，对现有3DGS渲染输出进行内容与视角依赖的增强；采用Wasserstein Distortion作为监督信号，使网络学习匹配局部特征统计量，而非强制逐像素重建保真度。

Result: 在标准3DGS、内存受限3DGS和RDO 3DGS方法上广泛验证，主观与客观实验一致表明，该方法显著优于现有基线，在大幅缩减文件或模型尺寸的同时获得更优的感知质量。

Conclusion: 该2D感知包装器作为通用插件，有效弥补了3DGS在高频纹理合成上的短板，为内存和带宽受限场景下的高质量渲染提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves impressive real-time rendering, it frequently struggles to synthesize high-frequency textures, a limitation heavily exacerbated in memory-constrained and rate-distortion-optimized (RDO) pipelines. To address this, we propose a versatile 2D perceptual wrapper that enhances the rendered outputs of existing 3DGS representations in a content- and view-dependent manner. Our method leverages a lightweight synthesis network conditioned on pseudo-random Gaussian noise to synthesize perceptually plausible textures. Supervised by Wasserstein Distortion, the network learns to match local feature statistics rather than strictly enforcing pixel-wise reconstruction fidelity, effectively mitigating the blurriness inherent in standard frameworks. We demonstrate the broad applicability of our plug-and-play approach across vanilla, memory-constrained, and RDO 3DGS methods. Comprehensive subjective and objective experiments confirm that our method significantly improves over existing baselines, yielding superior perceptual quality at sharply reduced file or model sizes.

</details>


### [50] [A Comprehensive Ecosystem for Open-Domain Customized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11783)
*Jingxu Zhang,Yuqian Hong,Daneul Kim,Kai Qiu,Qi Dai,Jianmin Bao,Yifan Yang,Xiaoyan Sun,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个百万量级的身份保持视频生成数据集PexelsCustom-1M（涵盖8000+类别），以及参数高效的CustoMDiT框架（仅增加8%可学习参数），并构建了1000+类别的OpenCustom评测基准，全面超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放域定制化视频生成受限于缺乏大规模、标注良好的身份特定属性数据集；现有基准如DreamBooth仅覆盖100个类别，远不足以评估真实世界应用场景。

Method: （1）构建PexelsCustom-1M数据集：从Pexels收集并整理100万组<身份, 文本, 视频>三元组，横跨8000+类别；（2）提出CustoMDiT框架：在预训练多模态Diffusion Transformer基础上，以参数高效方式（仅增加8%可学习参数）适配为定制视频生成器；（3）构建OpenCustom基准：通过跨数据集知识融合技术，融合ImageNet和MS-COCO构建1000+类别的评测集。

Result: CustoMDiT在多个评测中超越了先前的最先进方法；PexelsCustom-1M数据集和OpenCustom基准的有效性通过大量实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 大规模高质量数据集与参数高效模型架构的结合是推进身份保持视频生成的关键路径。该工作将开源包括数据集、流水线、基准和代码在内的完整生态系统，以促进社区后续研究。

Abstract: Recent progress in video generation has shown impressive visual synthesis capabilities. However, open-domain customized video generation remains limited by the lack of large-scale, annotated datasets capturing diverse identity-specific attributes. To address this, we introduce PexelsCustom-1M, the first publicly available million-scale dataset for identity-preserving video generation, containing one million curated <identity, text, video> triplets across 8,000+ categories. Leveraging this, we propose CustoMDiT, a parameter-efficient framework that adapts a pretrained multimodal Diffusion Transformer into a customized video generator with only 8% additional learnable parameters. Our method surpasses prior state-of-the-art. However, benchmarks such as DreamBooth cover only 100 classes, which is insufficient for real-world applications. To overcome this, we construct OpenCustom, a new benchmark with 1,000+ categories, created via cross-dataset knowledge fusion from ImageNet and MS-COCO. Extensive experiments confirm the advantages of both our dataset and model. We will open-source the entire ecosystem--including dataset, pipeline, benchmark, and implementations--to support further research.

</details>


### [51] [MultiToP: Learning to Patch Visual Tokens to Mitigate Hallucinations in Video Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11792)
*Yuansheng Gao,Wenbin Xing,Jiahao Yuan,Kaiwen Zhou,Han Bao,Zonghui Wang,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MultiToP 提出一种多模态上下文感知的视觉 token 修补框架，通过在语言生成前动态替换不可靠的视觉 token 来缓解视频大模型的幻觉问题，在保持通用理解能力的同时显著降低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大模型在理解任务中虽取得显著进展，但仍容易产生幻觉（生成的回复缺乏输入视频的忠实支撑）。现有方法通常需要修改原模型或引入大量计算开销，亟需一种轻量、即插即用的幻觉缓解方案。

Method: 提出 MultiToP 框架，核心包括：(1) 轻量级 Visual Token Patcher，预测 token 级别的替换分布，选择性将不可靠视觉 token 替换为动态全局 patch token；(2) 信息引导的排序校准（Information-Guided Rank Calibration），利用 backbone 中基于答案条件的帧级信息线索指导 token 替换；(3) 结合真值答案监督与稀疏正则化进行训练，实现局部视觉证据精炼，无需修改原始模型。

Result: 在 Vript-HAL 幻觉评估基准上，Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct 的 F1 分数提升 50.60%；在 ActivityNet-QA 上，Video-LLaVA-7B 获得 18.58% 的相对准确率增益。同时推理开销几乎可忽略不计。

Conclusion: MultiToP 通过轻量 token 级视觉证据精炼，在不修改原模型的前提下有效缓解视频大模型幻觉，并保持通用视频理解能力，展现出良好的实用性。

Abstract: Video Large Multimodal Models have achieved remarkable progress in video understanding, yet they remain prone to hallucinations, where generated responses are not faithfully supported by the input video. In this paper, we propose MultiToP, a multimodal-context-aware visual token patching framework that mitigates hallucinations by refining unreliable visual tokens before language generation. MultiToP introduces a lightweight Visual Token Patcher to predict token-level replacement distributions and selectively substitute unreliable visual tokens with a dynamic global patch token. To train the patcher effectively, we further propose information-guided rank calibration, which uses answer-conditioned frame-level information cues derived from the backbone to guide token replacement. Combined with ground-truth answer supervision and sparsity regularization, MultiToP enables localized visual evidence refinement without modifying the original model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MultiToP effectively reduces hallucinations on Vript-HAL with negligible inference overhead, improving the F1 scores of Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct by 50.60% over the vanilla model. Meanwhile, MultiToP preserves general video understanding ability, yielding an 18.58% relative accuracy gain on ActivityNet-QA for Video-LLaVA-7B.

</details>


### [52] [TextHOI-3D: Text-to-3D Hand-Object Interaction via Discrete Multi-View Generation and Joint Mesh Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11805)
*Zixiong Hao,Zhencun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextHOI-3D 是一个分阶段框架，通过生成式多视图视觉 token 作为文本语义与几何重建之间的显式接口，实现了文本条件下的手-物体三维网格生成，在多视图设置下显著降低了物体倒角距离和穿透体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件的三维生成在图像和孤立物体上进展迅速，但手-物体网格生成仍面临严峻挑战：需同时保持语言语义、跨视图一致性、物体几何、手部关节形状以及物理合理的接触。现有方法缺乏将语义生成与几何恢复有效解耦的中间表示。

Method: 提出分阶段框架：(1) 学习固定相机下手-物体观测的紧凑 VQ token 空间；(2) 利用 CLIP 条件的视觉自回归模型从文本预测多视图视觉 token；(3) 通过先验初始化、多视图联合优化和反穿透细化恢复统一的手-物体网格。核心设计在于用离散多视图表示连接语义生成与几何恢复两个阶段。

Result: 在 HO3D 衍生评测中，多视图设置相比单视图：物体倒角距离从 17.26 mm 降至 4.92 mm，穿透体积从 5.3721 cm³ 降至 0.2193 cm³，同时手部误差和表面 F-score 均有改善。

Conclusion: 多视图视觉 token 作为文本驱动三维手-物体网格生成的有效中间表示得到了实证支持，分离语义生成与几何恢复的设计范式在手-物体交互场景中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Text-conditioned 3D generation has progressed rapidly for images and isolated objects, but producing a hand-object mesh remains challenging: the output must preserve language semantics, cross-view consistency, object geometry, articulated hand shape, and physically plausible contact. We present TextHOI-3D, a staged framework that uses generated multi-view observations as an explicit interface between text-conditioned visual generation and geometry-aware hand-object recovery. TextHOI-3D learns a compact VQ token space for fixed-camera hand-object observations, predicts multi-view visual tokens from text with a CLIP-conditioned visual autoregressive model, and recovers a unified hand-object mesh through prior initialization, multi-view joint optimization, and anti-penetration refinement. The design separates semantic generation from geometric recovery while keeping both stages connected by a discrete multi-view representation. On HO3D-derived evaluations, the multi-view setting reduces object CD from 17.26 mm to 4.92 mm and penetration volume from 5.3721 cm^3 to 0.2193 cm^3 compared with a single-view counterpart, while improving hand errors and surface F-scores. These results support multi-view visual tokens as an effective intermediate representation for text-driven 3D hand-object mesh creation.

</details>


### [53] [LASA: A Weak Supervision Method for Open-Vocabulary Scene Sketch Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11837)
*Liwen Yi,Xianlin Zhang,Yue Zhang,Yue Ming,Xueming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LASA框架，通过聚合Vision Transformer不同层的注意力图来获取互补的空间结构先验（浅层捕获全局布局，深层关注局部细节），从而在弱监督下实现开放词汇场景草图语义分割，在三个数据集上显著超越先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇场景草图语义分割面临的核心挑战是：草图缺乏纹理和颜色线索，语义理解严重依赖笔画布局和空间配置。单层视觉-语言特征因仅利用单层注意力而 inherently unstable。作者观察到ViT不同层的注意力图编码了互补的空间信息——浅层捕获全局结构布局，深层聚焦局部笔画交叉和物体部件，这暗示跨层聚合能提供比任何单层更鲁棒的结构先验。

Method: 提出LASA（Layer-wise Accumulated Structural Attention）框架：(1) 聚合ViT多层注意力图，构建跨层结构先验；(2) 利用该结构先验在弱监督下引导层次化语义对齐；(3) 在推理阶段进一步利用聚合注意力精炼预测结果。

Result: 在FS-COCO、SFSD和FrISS三个数据集上，LASA相比先前弱监督基线方法分别提升mIoU +3.43、+8.01和+15.74，在分割精度和空间一致性上均取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 跨层聚合ViT注意力图能够有效提取草图的鲁棒结构先验，显著提升开放词汇场景草图语义分割的性能，同时保持对灵活类别词汇的适应性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary scene sketch semantic segmentation aims to assign dense semantic labels to sparse line drawings based on flexible category vocabularies specified at inference time, without relying on pixel-level annotations during training. Unlike natural images, sketches lack texture and color cues, making semantic understanding heavily dependent on stroke layout and spatial configuration, a challenge that renders single-layer vision-language features inherently unstable. Our key observation is that attention maps from different Vision Transformer layers encode complementary spatial cues: shallow layers capture global structural layouts, while deeper layers focus on local stroke intersections and object parts. This suggests that cross-layer aggregation provides a more robust structural prior than any individual layer alone. Leveraging this insight, we propose a structure-aware framework built upon \textbf{L}ayer-wise \textbf{A}ccumulated \textbf{S}tructural \textbf{A}ttention (\textbf{LASA}), which aggregates multi-layer attention to guide hierarchical semantic alignment under weak supervision and refine predictions during inference. Experiments on FS-COCO, SFSD, and FrISS show that LASA improves mIoU by $+3.43$, $+8.01$, and $+15.74$ over the prior weakly supervised baselines, demonstrating consistent gains in both segmentation accuracy and spatial coherence. Our source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [54] [Plan-and-Verify Video Reward Reasoning with Spatio-Temporal Scene Graph Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11838)
*Hyomin Kim,Junghye Kim,Joanie Hayoun Chung,Yoonjin Oh,Kyungjae Lee,Sungbin Lim,Sungwoong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SG-PVR 是一种视频奖励模型，通过基于时空场景图的"计划-验证"推理机制，将提示分解为原子声明并逐条对照结构化视觉证据进行验证，解决了现有奖励模型在细粒度语义对齐上的不足，并在测试时重排序中提升了文本到视频生成的组合对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的文本到视频奖励模型存在两个结构性缺陷：(1) 未系统性地验证提示中描述的每一个条件；(2) 支持判断的视觉证据在其自由形式的推理中保持隐式状态。这导致模型在细粒度语义对齐（尤其是时序语义）上表现不佳。

Method: 提出 SG-PVR，核心包括：(1) 验证计划模块，将提示分解为原子声明，确保每个需求都被检查；(2) 时空场景图提取模块，从视频中编码实体、属性和时序化关系，作为贯穿推理过程的持久结构化视觉参考；(3) 逐条验证机制，将每个声明同时与视频和场景图进行比对，将判断锚定在显式视觉证据上。

Result: SG-PVR 在语义对齐任务上取得了显著性能，尤其在细粒度时序语义方面表现突出。作为测试时重排序器，进一步增强了文本到视频生成中的组合对齐能力。

Conclusion: 通过将结构化场景图与系统化计划-验证推理相结合，SG-PVR 有效弥补了现有奖励模型在细粒度语义验证上的不足，为文本到视频生成的语义对齐提供了更可靠的奖励信号。

Abstract: Reward models for text-to-video (T2V) generation guide post-training but often fail at fine-grained semantic alignment. We trace this to two structural weaknesses in existing reasoning-based reward models: they do not systematically verify every condition described in the prompt, and the visual evidence supporting each judgment remains implicit in their free-form reasoning. We propose SG-PVR, a video reward model that addresses these limitations through plan-and-verify reasoning grounded in spatio-temporal scene graphs. The verification plan decomposes the prompt into atomic claims, ensuring every requirement is checked. The spatio-temporal scene graph, encoding entities, attributes, and temporally-grounded relations, is extracted from the video and maintained as a persistent structured visual reference throughout reasoning. Each claim is verified against both the video and the scene graph, anchoring judgments in explicit visual evidence. SG-PVR achieves strong performance on semantic alignment, including fine-grained temporal semantics. As a test-time reranker, it further enhances compositional alignment in T2V generation.

</details>


### [55] [Scene-Adaptive Nonlinear Tone Curves for Pseudo Ground-Truth Generation in Low-Light 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11841)
*Mingzhe Lyu,Jinqiang Cui,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对低光照新视角合成，本文提出一种场景自适应的非线性色调曲线框架，用于替代3DGS中传统的线性伪GT生成方法，通过百分位归一化、自适应偏移量和两条互补曲线（Adaptive SoftExp和Adaptive Poly3），在多个基准上实现PSNR最高+4.34 dB的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照下的多视图图像包含噪声、弱结构细节和压缩的动态范围，现有3DGS方法通过生成伪GT图像作为监督目标来应对，但当前伪GT方法仅对所有像素施加统一线性增益，导致亮区过曝、暗区增强不足。作者观察到非线性色调映射在2D低光增强中长期使用，却未被探索用于3D重建中的伪GT生成，因此提出非线性替代方案。

Method: 提出场景自适应的非线性色调曲线框架，包含三个组件：(1) 基于百分位的归一化，实现场景无关的曲线应用；(2) 场景自适应偏移量，用于自动黑电平调整；(3) 两条互补曲线：Adaptive SoftExp（ASE，有界指数曲线）和Adaptive Poly3（AP3，数据驱动的三次多项式）。该模块仅改变伪GT计算，保持3DGS主干不变。

Result: 在LOM、RealX3D等三个基准共21个场景上实验，ASE和AP3均一致优于线性基线，PSNR提升最高+4.34 dB（LOM）和+3.25 dB（RealX3D）。两条曲线虽数学形式不同但性能相近，表明改进具有曲线无关性。

Conclusion: 非线性色调曲线可有效替代线性伪GT生成，且改进效果对所选用曲线的具体数学形式不敏感。该方法即插即用，不改动3DGS主干网络，具有较好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Low-light novel view synthesis is challenging because dark multi-view images contain noise, weak structural detail, and compressed dynamic range. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods address these challenges by generating pseudo ground-truth (pseudo-GT) images as supervision targets when paired normal-light references are unavailable. Existing pseudo-GT methods apply a uniform linear gain to all pixels, which clips bright regions while providing insufficient enhancement in dark regions, limiting reconstruction quality. We observe that nonlinear tone mappings, long established in 2D low-light enhancement, have not been explored for pseudo-GT generation in 3D reconstruction. Accordingly, we propose a scene-adaptive nonlinear tone-curve framework that replaces linear pseudo-GT with nonlinear alternatives. The framework introduces percentile-based normalisation for scene-agnostic curve application, a scene-adaptive offset for automatic black-level adjustment, and two complementary curves: Adaptive SoftExp (ASE), a bounded exponential curve, and Adaptive Poly3 (AP3), a data-driven cubic polynomial. The module changes only the pseudo-GT computation and leaves the 3DGS backbone unchanged. Experiments on three benchmarks covering 21 scenes show that both curves consistently outperform the linear baseline with PSNR improvements up to +4.34 dB on LOM and +3.25 dB on RealX3D. Both curves achieve similar performance despite their different mathematical forms, suggesting the improvement is curve-agnostic. Code is available at https://github.com/lvmingzhe/adaptiveToneCurve

</details>


### [56] [SheafStain: Sheaf-Theoretic Schrödinger Bridge for Spatially and Biologically Coherent Virtual Staining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11846)
*Hyeongyeol Lim,Hongjun Yoon,Eunjin Jang,Daeky Jeong,Won June Cho,Hwamin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SheafStain提出将视觉基础模型(VFM)特征重新解释为层(sheaf)截面，通过薛定谔桥框架整合类别token和patch token，解决全切片图像虚拟染色中的空间不连续性和上下文污染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前虚拟染色方法在全切片图像(WSI)上分patch推理时会产生空间不连续性伪影，导致与真实图像的严重不匹配。病理视觉基础模型的自注意力机制会引入"上下文污染"——相同物理区域因上下文不同而产生不一致的嵌入。作者将这一问题形式化为层论(sheaf theory)问题：这些嵌入构成一个违反粘合公理(g̳luing axiom)的预层(presheaf)。

Method: 提出SheafStain方法：(1)将VFM特征重新解释为层截面(sheaf-like sections)；(2)在薛定谔桥(Schrödinger Bridge)框架中整合class token和patch token作为层截面——class token锚定生物学一致性，patch token形成逐位置空间映射；(3)使用在H&E和IHC上联合预训练的主干网络，产生非退化的交叉染色茎(cross-stain stalks)，使单一VFM特征空间同时监督输入条件和输出染色对齐；(4)在$1024\times1024$拼接输出上进行评估，而非孤立$256\times256$ patch。

Result: 在HER2、ER、PR和Ki-67四种生物标志物上，SheafStain相较于六种先前方法展示了有前景的结果，同时减轻了patch边界拼接伪影。

Conclusion: 通过层论框架重新组织VFM特征可有效解决虚拟染色中的空间一致性问题，为WSI级别的端到端虚拟染色提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current virtual staining approaches offer the potential for time- and cost-efficient biomarker quantification in cancer diagnostics and prognostics. However, patch-wise inference for gigapixel whole slide images (WSIs) fails to maintain spatial continuity, yielding artifacts that cause catastrophic mismatches with ground-truth images. Although pathology Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) offer rich representations, their self-attention causes varying global contexts to produce inconsistent embeddings for the same physical region. We formalize and validate this ``context contamination'' as a sheaf-theoretic problem where these embeddings form a presheaf that violates the gluing axiom. To address this, we propose SheafStain, a new approach that reinterprets VFM features as sheaf-like sections for spatially and biologically coherent virtual staining. Specifically, SheafStain integrates class and patch tokens into a Schrödinger Bridge framework as sheaf-like sections. While the class token anchors biological consistency, patch tokens form a per-position spatial map. A backbone co-pretrained on Hematoxylin \& Eosin (H\&E) and Immunohistochemistry (IHC) yields non-degenerate cross-stain stalks, so a single VFM feature space supervises both input conditioning and output stain alignment. Departing from prior work that evaluates on isolated $256 \times 256$ patches and either random-crops or resizes the $1024 \times 1024$ ground truth, we translate at $256 \times 256$ and evaluate on the stitched $1024 \times 1024$ outputs across HER2, ER, PR, and Ki-67. SheafStain demonstrates promising results against six prior methods while mitigating patch-boundary stitching artifacts. Code will soon be released.

</details>


### [57] [Task-Aware Structured Memory for Dynamic Multi-modal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11853)
*Zhirui Chen,Ziwei Chen,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TASM是一种无需训练的框架，通过任务感知引导压缩、语义保留的token合并以及分层记忆结构（Core Memory + Latent Bank），在重度压缩下仍能维持多模态大模型的上下文学习性能，兼顾效率与自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型的上下文学习受限于有限的上下文窗口与长序列KV缓存开销，而现有记忆压缩方法依赖刚性token删除或样本相关的重要性估计，存在引入偏差、破坏语义结构（尤其是视觉表征）、产生无法适应新查询的静态记忆等问题。

Method: TASM提出三大核心机制：(1) 任务向量引导压缩——用任务级方向替代样本特定信号，捕捉跨示例共享的相关性；(2) 基于二分图匹配的语义感知token合并，实现无损聚合而非破坏性剪枝；(3) 构建分层记忆架构，包括紧凑的Core Memory和Latent Bank，支持查询自适应的动态检索。

Result: 实验评估表明，TASM在重度压缩条件下仍能保持高性能，有效实现了效率与自适应性的平衡。

Conclusion: TASM作为一种无训练的、任务感知的结构化记忆框架，成功解决了现有多模态ICL中记忆压缩的偏差、结构破坏和静态性三大痛点，为长序列多模态推理提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) depend on in-context learning (ICL) for rapid task adaptation, but their scalability is severely limited by finite context windows and the growing cost of key-value (KV) caches in long multi-modal sequences. Existing memory compression approaches typically rely on rigid token removal or sample-dependent importance estimation, which introduces bias, disrupts semantic structure, particularly for visual representations, and yields static memories that cannot adapt to new queries. We introduce TASM (Task-Aware Structured Memory), a training-free framework that addresses these limitations through task-aware, structure-preserving, and dynamically accessible memory construction. TASM employs task-vector guided compression to replace sample-specific signals with a task-level direction that captures shared relevance across demonstrations. To preserve the underlying manifold, it applies semantics-aware token merging via bipartite graph matching, aggregating tokens without destructive pruning. Finally, TASM structures memory into a hierarchy comprising a compact Core Memory and a Latent Bank, facilitating query-adaptive dynamic retrieval. Evaluations confirm TASM maintains high performance under heavy compression, effectively balancing efficiency with adaptability.

</details>


### [58] [SG2Loc: Sequential Visual Localization on 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11880)
*Nicole Damblon,Olga Vysotska,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D场景图的轻量级序列视觉定位方法，用紧凑的场景图（节点为带粗网格的物体，边编码空间关系）替代传统稠密地图。在粒子滤波框架下，通过语义特征匹配实现高效定位，显著降低存储开销的同时保持良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉定位方法需存储大规模图像库或点云，存储开销巨大。序列化定位对自主代理至关重要，但现有方案在资源受限场景下难以兼顾紧凑性与精度。因此需要一种轻量级、存储高效的序列视觉定位方案。

Method: 提出基于3D场景图的序列视觉定位方法：（1）环境建模：构建场景图，节点为带有粗网格的物体，边编码物体间空间关系；（2）语义特征提取：对每帧图像提取逐块（per-patch）语义特征，预测物体身份；（3）粒子滤波定位：每个粒子表示一个相机位姿，将场景图中的粗物体网格投影至图像，根据可见性为图像块分配物体身份；通过匹配输入图像逐块特征与场景图物体特征的相似度计算粒子权重；（4）序列融合：逐帧序贯融入后续图像，迭代优化位姿估计。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上验证，该方法在显著降低存储需求的同时，保持了与传统方法相当的定位性能。

Conclusion: 利用紧凑场景图和高效语义匹配，3D场景图方法能以极低存储开销实现鲁棒的序列视觉定位，适用于资源受限的机器人与AR场景。代码即将开源。

Abstract: Visual localization in complex indoor environments remains a critical challenge for robotics and AR applications. Sequential localization, where pose estimates are refined over time, is important for autonomous agents. However, traditional methods often require storing extensive image databases or point clouds, leading to significant overhead. This paper introduces a novel, lightweight approach to sequential visual localization using 3D scene graphs. Our method represents the environment with a compact scene graph, where nodes represent objects (with coarse meshes) and edges encode spatial relationships. For each image in the localization phase, we extract per-patch semantic features, predicting object identities. Localization is performed within a particle filter framework. Each particle, representing a camera pose, projects the coarse object meshes from the scene graph into the image, assigning object identities to patches based on visibility. The similarity of the per-patch features, in the input image, and object features from the scene graph determines the weight of a particle. Subsequent images are incorporated sequentially, refining the pose estimate. By leveraging a compact scene graph and efficient semantic matching, our method significantly reduces storage while maintaining performance on real-world datasets. The code will be available at https://github.com/DmblnNicole/sg2loc.

</details>


### [59] [Image Quality Assessment of Identity Cards Using Measures from Open Face Image Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11884)
*Gregor Grote,Juan E. Tapia,Christian Rathgeb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将Open Face Image Quality (OFIQ)标准中的采集相关质量度量应用于身份证图像，通过预处理流水线和与PAD算法性能的相关性分析，验证OFIQ度量对提升伪造攻击检测效果的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程身份验证系统中，身份证图像质量评估缺乏标准化方法，现有面像质量度量未系统应用于证件图像，且图像质量对后续PAD性能的影响尚不明确。因此需要探索OFIQ度量在身份证图像上的适用性。

Method: 构建预处理流水线（角点检测、透视归一化、前景掩码），将OFIQ的采集相关质量度量应用于身份证图像；在四个数据集（两个真实证件、两个打印伪造证件）上，计算OFIQ度量与三种PAD算法性能之间的相关性。

Result: 部分OFIQ质量度量与PAD算法性能存在显著相关性，基于这些度量进行质量评估可有效提升PAD性能。

Conclusion: OFIQ标准的采集质量度量可有效迁移至身份证图像场景，通过质量驱动的预处理和筛选，能显著改善远程身份验证中伪造攻击检测的可靠性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of assessing image quality in ID cards in remote verification systems by applying capture-related quality measures from the Open Face Image Quality (OFIQ) standard to ID card images. Our preprocessing pipeline includes corner detection, perspective normalization, and comprehensive foreground masking to ensure accurate and unbiased quality measure computation. We evaluate the effectiveness of these measures by analyzing their correlation with the performance of three presentation attack detection (PAD) algorithms across four diverse ID card datasets, where two datasets contain bona fide, i.e. pristine, images and two contain printed mock ID cards. Our results suggest that quality assessment based on some OFIQ measures can significantly improve PAD performance.

</details>


### [60] [Task-Aligned Stability Analysis of Vision-Language Models for Autonomous Driving Hazard Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11889)
*Everett Richards*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型在自动驾驶场景理解中，受腐蚀扰动后嵌入漂移与任务对齐的 hazard score 漂移之间的关系，发现两者耦合程度因腐蚀类型而异，且不同腐蚀会导致不对称的失效模式（漏检 vs 虚警），建议基准测试应同时纳入任务对齐的稳定性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLM 鲁棒性分析多依赖任务无关的嵌入稳定性指标，缺乏对嵌入漂移是否真正预测任务层面决策变化的系统性研究。作者希望揭示嵌入漂移与 hazard score 漂移之间的实际关系，从而推动更务实的鲁棒性评估标准。

Method: 在 BDD100K 道路场景数据集上施加多种受控腐蚀（corruption），使用 CLIP 模型提取图像-文本相似度并计算 hazard score；定义嵌入漂移（embedding drift）和 margin drift（hazard score 的变化量），系统比较两者在不同腐蚀族下的耦合关系及失效方向（false negative vs false alarm）。

Result: 嵌入漂移与决策漂移的关系高度依赖腐蚀类型：部分腐蚀族两者强耦合，另一些在嵌入变化较小时即引发严重决策不稳定；多数腐蚀通过误判抑制 hazard 检测（假阴性），而遮挡（occlusion）则引发虚警（假阳性）。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性基准测试不应仅依赖嵌入级扰动统计，还应纳入任务对齐的稳定性度量，并充分考虑不对称失效模式，以更准确地反映实际场景中的安全风险。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used for scene understanding in autonomous driving, but robustness analysis often relies on task-agnostic embedding stability alone. We study whether corruption-induced embedding drift predicts changes in a task-aligned hazard score derived from CLIP image-text similarities. Using controlled corruptions on BDD100K road scenes, we compare embedding drift against margin drift, defined as the change in hazard score under perturbation. The relationship is highly corruption-dependent: some families exhibit strong coupling between representation drift and decision drift, while others induce hazardous decision instability despite relatively modest embedding change. Furthermore, corruption families differ in failure direction: most suppress hazard detections via false negatives, while occlusion instead triggers false alarms, suggesting that benchmark design should account for asymmetric failure modes, not just overall instability rates. These results suggest that robustness benchmarks should include task-aligned stability measures in addition to embedding-level perturbation statistics.

</details>


### [61] [Wild3R: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unconstrained Sparse Photo Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11894)
*Yuto Furutani,Takashi Otonari,Kaede Shiohara,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wild3R 提出了一种前馈式3D高斯泼溅方法，针对无约束的真实世界照片集合，通过引入包含200个场景、170种光照条件和瞬态物体的WildCity数据集（共337,500张图像），学习跨视点的外观一致性并去除瞬态内容，在无需逐场景优化的前提下，性能超越现有前馈方法，并与逐场景优化方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3DGS方法在处理包含多样光照条件和瞬态物体的真实世界照片集合时表现不佳，核心瓶颈在于缺乏同时提供多视角、多光照和瞬态变化的训练数据来学习鲁棒的场景表示。

Method: （1）提出WildCity数据集：包含200个场景、170种光照条件及瞬态物体，共计337,500张图像；（2）基于该数据集训练前馈式3DGS模型，以参考视图为条件学习跨视点的外观一致性，同时能够去除瞬态内容。

Result: 在多项实验中，Wild3R超越了现有的前馈式方法，并取得了与先前基于逐场景优化的方法相比具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 通过构建大规模、多光照、含瞬态物体的训练数据集，前馈式3DGS可以有效克服真实世界无约束照片集合带来的挑战，实现高效且鲁棒的新视角合成，摆脱对逐场景优化的依赖。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) removes the need for time-consuming per-scene optimization required by traditional 3DGS. However, existing feed-forward approaches struggle with real-world photo collections that include diverse lighting conditions and transient objects. In this paper, we present Wild3R, a feed-forward approach for unconstrained sparse photo collections. The main bottleneck is the lack of training data that provides multiple viewpoints, a variety of illuminations, and transient variations necessary for learning robust scene representations. To address this, we introduce the WildCity dataset, which comprises 200 scenes, 170 lighting conditions, and transient objects, resulting in 337,500 images in total. By leveraging the dataset, our model learns appearance consistency across viewpoints conditioned on reference views, while removing transient content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing feed-forward approaches and achieves results competitive with prior per-scene optimization-based methods.

</details>


### [62] [From Content to Knowledge: Lightning Fast Long-Video Understanding with Neural Knowledge Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11913)
*Yuchen Guan,Xiao Li,Zongyu Guo,Xiaoyi Zhang,Xiulian Peng,Chun Yuan,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将长视频视为神经知识表征（NKR），通过智能体知识蒸馏（AKD）将视频语义内容压缩到一小部分网络权重中。推理时，轻量级NKR挂载到冻结的VLM上，实现免重载的查询理解，解耦视频长度与推理成本。在LVBench上，该方法以降低两个数量级的端到端延迟达到SOTA级别的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解面临的核心矛盾：视频长度与推理成本强耦合。传统方法要么将长视频作为token流输入（计算量随长度线性/二次增长），要么预先构建结构化数据库（丧失端到端学习的灵活性）。作者希望找到一种既能保持语义完整性、又能将推理开销与视频长度解耦的表示范式，同时支持高效的多轮交互式理解。

Method: 核心方法分两阶段：（1）**编码阶段（AKD）**：引入一个智能体（Agent），自动为长视频生成密集描述和问答对，通过知识蒸馏将视频的语义信息优化到一组称为NKR的小型网络权重中。NKR不是token序列，而是直接附加在VLM骨干上的可学习参数。（2）**推理阶段**：将冻结的VLM与训练好的NKR权重组合，直接接收用户查询并生成回答，无需重新加载或重新编码原始视频。NKR作为可移植、可复用的知识资产，支持多轮交互。

Result: 在长视频理解基准LVBench上，所提方法取得了与现有最先进方法相当的性能，同时端到端推理延迟降低了超过两个数量级（100倍以上），显著提升了多轮视频理解场景下的摊销效率。

Conclusion: NKR范式成功将长视频的语义内容压缩为便携的网络权重，实现了视频长度与推理成本的有效解耦。该方法为交互式长视频理解开辟了新可能，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低延迟，展示了神经知识表征作为视频理解新抽象的优势。

Abstract: We propose a new paradigm for long video understanding by treating a long video as a Neural Knowledge Representation (NKR). NKR represents video contents neither as a stream of tokens nor pre-organized databases, but as an individual small portion of network weights attached to the VLM backbone. The NKR weights are optimized to encapsulate the video's semantic content via a novel Agentic Knowledge Distillation (AKD) process, where an agent automatically synthesizes dense descriptions and question-answer pairs to distill the video's knowledge into the NKR. While AKD serves as a comprehensive, one-time encoding phase, the resulting NKR transforms the video into a portable, reusable asset. At inference, the lightweight NKR is mounted onto a frozen Vision-Language Model (VLM), enabling direct, query-based understanding without reloading or re-encoding the original video. This approach decouples video length from inference cost, offering high amortized efficiency for multi-turn video understanding. Experiments on the LVBench benchmark show our method achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art approaches while reducing end-to-end latency by over two orders of magnitude, opening new possibilities for interactive long-video understanding.

</details>


### [63] [Corpus Augmentation for Sign Language Translation via LLM-Guided Video Stitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11925)
*Zsolt Robotka,Ádám Rák,Jalal Al-Afandi,András Horváth,György Cserey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需额外人工标注的语料增强方法，利用现有gloss标注训练语料和LLM生成合成手语视频-文本对，在GFSLT-VLP基线上实现+2.92 BLEU-4的提升，远超先前工作中0.98 BLEU-4的最大增益。同时发现合成数据虽有损视觉-语言预训练目标却提升下游性能，且视觉上不平滑的剪辑边界反而充当了隐式正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量平行手语视频-文本对稀缺，限制了SLT模型在长尾词汇和未见结构上的泛化能力。现有方法要么依赖昂贵的人工标注，要么需要外部手语视频语料或生成模型。作者希望仅利用已有的gloss标注训练语料和LLM，实现零额外标注成本的数据增强。

Method: 提出三阶段语料增强流程：(1) 通过CTC强制对齐从训练视频中提取per-gloss片段；(2) 使用语料锚定的LLM生成新颖的gloss-句子对；(3) 通过随机句子采样和片段分配组装合成视频序列。生成的合成RGB视频-文本对与下游架构无关，可直接用于RGB-based SLT模型或转换为pose/特征表示。

Result: 在GFSLT-VLP框架下，该方法实现+2.92 BLEU-4提升，显著优于Sincan等人复现的五种最近gloss-free方法中观察到的最大增益0.98 BLEU-4。同时发现两个反直觉现象：(1)合成数据虽损害视觉-语言预训练目标却提升下游性能；(2)基于L2准则优化片段过渡的视觉平滑性反而适得其反，突变边界可能作为隐式正则化。

Conclusion: 所提出的语料增强方法有效且低成本，仅依赖现有gloss语料和LLM即可显著提升SLT性能。合成数据的反直觉效应——损害预训练目标却提升下游性能、不平滑边界作为隐式正则化——为理解合成数据在SLT中的作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) converts sign language video into spoken language text and holds significant promise for improving accessibility and enabling communication between signing and non-signing communities. While large weakly-aligned datasets have enabled pre-training at scale and gloss-free methods have reduced reliance on expert annotation, high-quality parallel sign video-text pairs for fine-tuning remain scarce, limiting generalisation on long-tail vocabulary and unseen constructions. We propose a corpus augmentation approach that requires no additional human annotation, external sign-language video corpora, or generative video models, relying only on the existing gloss-annotated training corpus and an LLM for sentence generation: per-gloss clips are extracted from training videos via CTC forced-alignment, novel gloss-sentence pairs are generated by a corpus-anchored LLM, and synthetic sequences are assembled through random sentence sampling and clip assignment. The resulting synthetic RGB video-text pairs are architecture-agnostic at the downstream training stage and can be consumed directly by RGB-based SLT models, or converted into pose or feature representations by pipelines that derive such inputs from video. Sincan et al. re-evaluated five recent gloss-free methods under strictly identical conditions; the largest verified gain over the GFSLT-VLP baseline was only 0.98 BLEU-4. Our augmentation, applied within the same framework, achieves +2.92 BLEU-4 without any change to architecture or training protocol. We further identify that synthetic data harms vision-language pretraining despite improving its objectives, and that optimising clip transitions for visual smoothness is counter-productive under L2-based criteria; we propose that abrupt boundaries may act as a form of implicit regularisation. Code is available at https://github.com/robizso/slt-datagen.

</details>


### [64] [Feature extraction for plant growth estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11966)
*Simbarashe Aldrin Ngorima,Albert Helberg,Marelie H. Davel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种植物生长阶段实时估计的特征提取方法：基于Gabor滤波器+形态学操作的手工特征，以及基于预训练CNN迁移学习的深度特征。在canola和radish数据集上测试，CNN特征（VGG-19 + RBF-SVM）以98.4%准确率和每张0.08秒的处理速度全面优于手工特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业需要实时估计植物生长阶段以减少资源浪费，但不同生长阶段的植株形态相似，导致自主估计困难。因此需要开发高效、准确的特征提取方法来实现实时生长阶段分类。

Method: 提出两种特征提取方法：(1) Gabor滤波器组结合形态学操作的手工特征提取；(2) 使用预训练CNN（如VGG-19）进行迁移学习的深度特征提取。两者分别结合支持向量机（含RBF核）和提升树分类器进行对比评估。数据集为公开的bccr-segset（canola和radish两个物种）。

Result: CNN特征在速度和准确率上均优于手工Gabor特征。最佳系统（VGG-19特征 + RBF-SVM）在两个物种上均达到98.4%的准确率，单张图像处理时间仅0.08秒，满足实时应用需求。

Conclusion: 基于预训练CNN迁移学习的特征提取方法在植物生长阶段估计任务中比传统手工特征更优，兼顾高精度与实时性，适合精准农业中的实际部署。

Abstract: Precision agriculture requires the estimation of plant growth stages in real-time. When the plant growth stage is known, the wastage of resources in cultivation, such as nutrients and water, is reduced as only the required resources need to be supplied. Plants at different growth stages, however, have similar morphological features, which can make autonomous growth stage estimation difficult. This paper presents two feature extraction methods for growth stage estimation: one that uses a bank of Gabor filters and morphological operations, and the other that uses pre-trained convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and transfer learning. We test these methods on a publicly available plant growth stage dataset (``bccr-segset``) for two species, canola and radish, grown and captured under indoor conditions. The two proposed feature extraction methods are compared, using support vector machines and boosted trees as classifiers. We find that both methods are suitable for real-time applications, and that CNN features outperform the hand-crafted features, both with regard to speed and accuracy. The best system (VGG-19 features, classified with a radial basis function support vector machine) obtained an accuracy of 98.4% for both species, processing an image in 0.08 seconds.

</details>


### [65] [SpecLoR: Spectral Lookahead Rectification for Motion-Coherent Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11969)
*Xu Zhang,Yu Lu,Ruijie Quan,Zhaozheng Chen,Bohan Wang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpecLoR 是一种即插即用的推理时修正方法，通过在频域中利用自然视频的统计先验对 Flow Matching 生成过程中的漂移潜变量进行频谱前瞻整流，显著减少视频生成的时空伪影并增强运动连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching 在文本到视频生成中因速度近似和数值离散化误差累积导致采样轨迹漂移，引发严重的时空不一致性。直接修正这些漂移的含噪潜变量面临两大困难：(1) 时间步依赖的噪声遮蔽了可靠的结构线索；(2) 空间干预可能破坏局部几何结构且计算开销巨大。

Method: SpecLoR 包含三个步骤：(1) 在采样早期阶段进行前瞻预测，估计干净潜变量 z_{t,0} 并计算其 3D 时空频谱；(2) 保持相位不变，仅对幅度谱进行修正以匹配自然视频的通用统计先验；(3) 将修正后的状态重新加噪，继续 ODE 采样积分。

Result: 在 Wan2.2 上的实验表明，SpecLoR 显著减少了物理伪影，多项基准上提升了运动连贯性，且计算开销极小（仅增加 4 次额外 NFE）。

Conclusion: SpecLoR 通过在频域进行幅度谱修正，有效绕过了时空纠缠和噪声遮蔽问题，为 Flow Matching 视频生成提供了一种高效、轻量的推理时修正方案。

Abstract: Flow Matching has enabled robust text-to-video generation via latent ODE sampling. However, velocity approximation and numerical discretization errors inevitably accumulate, causing sampling trajectories to drift. Consequently, generated videos often suffer from severe spatiotemporal inconsistencies. Nevertheless, directly correcting these drifted, noisy latents is challenging: (i) timestep-dependent noise obscures reliable structural cues; (ii) spatial interventions risk disrupting intricate local geometry while incurring heavy computational costs. To address this, we propose Spectral Lookahead Rectification (SpecLoR), a plug-and-play inference method that bypasses noise via lookahead prediction, and circumvents spatiotemporal entanglement by shifting corrections to the frequency domain, where universal statistical priors of natural videos are readily available. First, during early sampling stages, SpecLoR looks ahead to estimate the clean latent $z_{t,0}$ and computes its 3D spatiotemporal spectrum. Next, SpecLoR rectifies the amplitude spectrum to match the prior, leaving the phase intact. Finally, the corrected state is re-noised to resume ODE integration. Experiments on Wan2.2 demonstrate that SpecLoR significantly reduces physical artifacts and enhances motion coherence across multiple benchmarks with minimal computational overhead (4 additional NFEs).

</details>


### [66] [ParseFixer: An Agentic Framework for Document Parsing via Selective Multimodal Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11977)
*LeKai Yu,Hao Liu,Kun Wang,Zhiran Li,Ruping Cao,Fan Liu,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ParseFixer 是一个用于文档解析的智能体框架，结合全页骨干解析（FBP）和智能体选择性校正（ASC），在 DataMFM 挑战赛文档解析赛道中取得第三名（总分 61.78）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档解析任务要求从页面图像中恢复结构化 Markdown，同时兼顾文本内容的准确性和文档结构的忠实还原。现有方法难以同时满足这两方面需求，因此需要一个既能稳定解析骨干内容、又能有选择性地修复高价值错误的框架。

Method: 提出 ParseFixer 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Full-Page Backbone Parsing (FBP)：使用 MinerU2.5 Pro 生成稳定的初始 Markdown 输出；(2) Agentic Selective Correction (ASC)：通过“验证-回滚”校正流程，检测并修复高价值的解析失败点，仅在骨干解析基础上进行选择性多模态校正，避免重写可靠的解析结果。

Result: 在 DataMFM Challenge Track 1 的测试集上，ParseFixer 取得总分 61.78，排名第三。

Conclusion: ParseFixer 通过骨干解析与选择性智能体校正相结合，在无需重写可靠解析结果的前提下，有效提升关键文档元素的恢复质量，为准确文档解析提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: In this report, we present our third-place solution for the DataMFM Challenge Track 1: Document Parsing. This track requires models to recover structured Markdown documents from document page images while preserving textual content and document structure. To address the complementary requirements of accurate content recovery and faithful structure reconstruction, we propose ParseFixer, an agentic framework for backbone parsing and selective correction. ParseFixer consists of two key modules: Full-Page Backbone Parsing (FBP) and Agentic Selective Correction (ASC). FBP produces stable initial Markdown outputs with MinerU2.5 Pro, while ASC detects high-value parsing failures and repairs them through a verify-and-rollback correction process. By placing selective multimodal correction after open-source backbone parsing, ParseFixer improves the recovery of key document elements without rewriting reliable backbone predictions. On the test set, our final system achieves an overall score of 61.78 and ranks third in Track 1, demonstrating its effectiveness for accurate document parsing. Our code will be released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/CVPRW26-ParseFixer.

</details>


### [67] [From Nominal Intensity to Equivalent Rainfall: A Path-Based Credibility Evaluation Framework for Simulated Rainfall in Autonomous-Driving Perception Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11989)
*Tian Xia,Xin Zhao,Shaolingfeng Ye,Junyi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于路径的模拟降雨可信度评估方法，通过雨滴谱分布、感知一致性校正等指标，从多条候选路径中筛选出最能代表真实降雨感知效应的路径，为自动驾驶雨天测试的条件描述与结果外推提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶封闭场地降雨测试仅以名义降雨强度或单点测量描述条件，难以将模拟雨场与真实降雨对齐，也无法将测试结果映射到真实场景，缺乏面向SOTIF的降雨条件可信度评估手段。

Method: 以真实降雨的雨滴尺寸-速度联合分布为参考，构建路径等效降雨强度、不确定性带和路径平均雨滴分布真实感（RRD）三项指标；进一步利用激光雷达目标点云数量和平均反射率进行感知一致性校正，量化每条模拟降雨路径对真实降雨感知效应的代理能力。实验基于约1万份真实雨滴谱样本、728个RainSense感知样本及2.4m×7.2m区域内45个空间采样点进行验证。

Result: 同一名义降雨强度下空间非均匀性显著，验证了逐路径评估的必要性。Path IV（11.54±0.31 mm/h, RRD=0.43）和Path VI（8.28±0.34 mm/h, RRD=0.46）在降雨强度稳定性、雨滴谱真实感和感知一致性方面表现最为均衡，被识别为优选路径。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效支持降雨条件下自动驾驶感知测试的路径选择、条件描述和结果可信解释，为SOTIF导向的风险评估提供了可操作的评估框架。

Abstract: Credible simulated-rainfall conditions are essential for identifying perception-system boundaries and supporting SOTIF-oriented risk assessment in automated driving. However, closed-field tests are often described only by nominal rainfall intensity or single-point measurements, making it difficult to align simulated rain fields with real rainfall and map test results to real-world scenarios. This paper proposes a path-based credibility evaluation method for simulated rainfall in autonomous-driving perception tests. Using the drop size and velocity joint distribution of real rainfall as the reference, each candidate path is represented by path-equivalent rainfall intensity, an uncertainty band, and a path-averaged Realism of Raindrop Distribution (RRD) score. Lidar target point-cloud count and mean reflectivity are further used for perception-consistency correction, quantifying the proxy capability of each simulated-rainfall path for real-rainfall perception effects. Experiments are conducted using about 10,000 real-rainfall raindrop-spectrum samples, 728 RainSense perception samples, and 45 spatial sampling points in a 2.4 m x 7.2 m simulated-rainfall area. Results show that spatial non-uniformity remains under the same nominal condition, confirming the need for path-based evaluation. The method identifies Path IV and Path VI as preferable candidates, with results of 11.54 +/- 0.31 mm/h, RRD = 0.43, and 8.28 +/- 0.34 mm/h, RRD = 0.46, respectively. These paths show more balanced performance in rainfall-intensity stability, raindrop-spectrum realism, and perception consistency. The proposed method supports path selection, condition description, and credible interpretation of autonomous-driving perception tests under rainfall.

</details>


### [68] [FitVTON: Fit-aware Virtual Try-On via Body-Garment Size Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12012)
*Yiqun Ning,Ao Shen,Chenhang He,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FitVTON 是一个尺寸感知的虚拟试穿模型，通过结构化文本提示编码服装-身体尺寸关系，并从参数化服装模型的模拟试穿三元组中学习，显著提升了不同体型上的试穿合身度保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的虚拟试穿方法大多将任务视为2D修复问题，虽能生成视觉逼真的图像，但忽视了服装在不同体型上的物理合身性（fit），导致生成的穿着效果未能真实反映服装尺寸与身体形态的匹配关系。

Method: FitVTON 通过结构化文本提示编码服装-身体的尺寸信息，从参数化服装模型生成的模拟试穿三元组中学习；引入两个辅助头分别预测服装掩码和暴露身体掩码以改善服装轮廓的合身效果；并增加纹理校正阶段来提升模拟数据的真实感外观。

Result: 在 curated 的真实数据集 FittingEffect3K 上，结合 VLM 评分协议，主观和定量实验均表明 FitVTON 在尺寸准确性和形状保持上显著优于现有方法，同时保持有竞争力的图像质量。

Conclusion: FitVTON 有效解决了虚拟试穿中服装合身度的物理真实性问题，在保证图像质量的同时实现了对不同体型的真实合身效果。

Abstract: While diffusion-based virtual try-on has achieved impressive visual realism, most methods treat the task as 2D inpainting, prioritizing texture preservation over physical plausibility. Consequently, they often produce plausible-looking images that fail to reflect authentic garment fit across diverse body shapes. We present FitVTON, a Fit-aware virtual try-on model on different bodies in the wild. FitVTON encodes garment-body size through structured text prompts, and learn from simulated try-on triplets from parameterized garment model. To improve the fitting effects over garment silhouettes, we introduce two auxiliary head to predict the masks for both the garment and the exposed body. We further introduce a texture rectification stage to improve realistic appearance from simulated data. To evaluate the fitting fidelity, we curate a real-world dataset, FittingEffect3K, combining VLM-based scoring protocol. Both subjective and quantitive experiments show that FitVTON demonstrate authentic fitting fidelity, with significant sizing accuracy and shape preservation over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining competitive image quality. Project Page: https://zenoning.github.io/FitVTON/.

</details>


### [69] [ViT-FREE: Efficient Face Recognition via Early Exiting and Synthetic Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12023)
*Tahar Chettaoui,Guray Ozgur,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对ViT人脸识别推理效率问题，本文提出ViT-FREE训练免费的多出口框架，利用中间层特征直接进行人脸验证，在第10层退出可实现20%加速而精度仅降1.5。还提出ViT-FREE_FT轻量微调策略，仅调整投影层以提升浅层出口性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在人脸识别中表现优异，但高计算开销阻碍其在资源受限设备上的部署。现有加速方法通常需要重训练或修改模型结构，缺乏一种简单、无需训练的推理效率优化方案。

Method: 1. ViT-FREE：利用ViT各Transformer层输出特征维度一致的特点，在预训练ViT的多个中间层设置出口，直接从中间表示进行人脸验证，无需修改或重训练骨干网络。2. 通过分析patch embedding和attention map在深度上的演进，验证中间层特征已具备判别性和稳定性。3. ViT-FREE_FT：提出轻量级出口微调策略，仅使用小型合成数据集微调各出口的投影层，保持Transformer骨干冻结。

Result: 1. 在第10层（共12层）退出可实现20%加速，IJB-C等基准上验证性能仅下降1.5。2. Patch embeddings与attention maps在连续层间高度相似，并逐渐向最终表示收敛，表明特征逐步细化。3. ViT-FREE_FT显著提升了浅层出口性能，且不影响深层出口表现。

Conclusion: ViT-FREE作为一种训练免费的多出口推理策略，能有效平衡人脸识别的效率与精度；配合轻量微调策略ViT-FREE_FT可进一步提升浅层出口性能，为ViT在资源受限场景下的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have gained significant attention in computer vision and shown strong potential for face recognition (FR). However, their high computational cost makes deployment on resource-constrained devices challenging, motivating the need for methods that balance efficiency and accuracy. In this work, we investigate early exiting in pretrained ViTs as a simple yet effective training-free strategy for efficient FR inference. Leveraging the uniform feature dimensionality across transformer encoder blocks, we introduce ViT-FREE, a multi-exit framework that enables face verification directly from intermediate representations without modifying or retraining the backbone model, and thus, reducing inference cost. Empirically, we show that patch embeddings and attention maps evolve progressively across depth, exhibiting high similarity between consecutive ViT blocks and increasing alignment with the final representation. This indicates gradual feature refinement and attention convergence, suggesting that intermediate layers already provide stable and discriminative representations suitable for early exiting. Through extensive experiments on multiple FR benchmarks, we systematically analyze the accuracy-efficiency trade-off across exit depths. Our results demonstrate that later exits achieve a highly favorable balance, with exiting at layer 10 yielding up to a 20% speedup while incurring only a 1.5 drop in verification performance on benchmarks such as IJB-C. Also, we propose ViT-FREE_FT, a lightweight exit-specific fine-tuning strategy that adapts only the projection layers using a small synthetic dataset while keeping the transformer backbone frozen. This approach improves the performance of shallow exits while preserving the efficiency benefits and leaving deeper exits largely unaffected.

</details>


### [70] [SpikeTAD: Spiking Neural Networks for End-to-End Temporal Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12033)
*Min Yang,Mi Zhou,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于脉冲神经网络(SNN)的端到端时序动作检测(TAD)架构SpikeTAD，在保持极低功耗的同时，在THUMOS14和ActivityNet-1.3上分别达到67.2%和37.42%的mAP，验证了低功耗TAD模型的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解模型因体积大、功耗高而难以部署到移动设备上。SNN虽具有生物合理性和低功耗优势（尤其在神经形态芯片上），但转换时间步过长、性能退化严重，限制了其应用。本文旨在探索SNN在视频理解中时序动作检测任务上的应用潜力。

Method: 提出首个基于SNN的端到端时序动作检测架构SpikeTAD，直接利用脉冲神经网络处理视频时序信息，实现从输入到检测输出的全流程SNN推理。

Result: SpikeTAD在THUMOS14数据集上平均mAP达67.2%，在ActivityNet-1.3上达37.42%，同时保持极低功耗。

Conclusion: SpikeTAD证明了构建低功耗时序动作检测模型的可行性，为SNN在视频理解任务中的实际部署开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Video understanding is a crucial part of computer vision, with numerous application scenarios. With the increasing popularity of mobile devices, an increasing number of efforts are trying to deploy video understanding models on them. However, existing video understanding models are difficult to deploy due to their large size and prohibitive power consumption. Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have shown bioplausibility and low power advantages over Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs), especially on neuromorphic chips which are regarded as essential components of future mobile devices. However, excessively long conversion time-steps and severe performance degradation problems limit their application. To solve the problems above, we explore the application of SNNs on temporal action detection (TAD), which is an important task in video understanding, and propose the first SNN-based end-to-end TAD architecture coined as SpikeTAD. While maintaining extremely low power consumption, SpikeTAD achieves an average mAP of 67.2% in THUMOS14 and 37.42% in ActivityNet-1.3, demonstrating the feasibility of a low-power TAD model. Our code is available at https://github.com/MCG-NJU/SpikeTAD.

</details>


### [71] [Vision Transformers for Face Recognition Need More Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12036)
*Tahar Chettaoui,Guray Ozgur,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对ViT在人脸识别中采用CPE范式时注意力图存在伪影的问题，引入register tokens机制，提出ViT-8R模型，在提升可解释性的同时达到了ViT类人脸识别模型的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPE范式虽然比CLS-token范式的人脸识别性能更好，但定性分析发现其注意力图存在伪影（artifacts），限制了模型的可解释性。需要一种方法在不牺牲性能的前提下提升注意力图的结构化和可解释性。

Method: 在CPE范式基础上，将可学习的register tokens拼接到初始patch embeddings中，随ViT编码器各层联合处理。该方法源自Darcet等人（2023）的工作，本文将其首次应用于人脸识别领域。最终模型ViT-8R基于ViT-B架构，使用CPE方式并加入8个register tokens。

Result: （1）注意力图伪影现象在各类ViT骨干网络中普遍存在（涵盖小模型和大模型）；（2）引入4或8个register tokens可显著改善可解释性，其中8个register tokens带来最高的验证精度和最平滑的注意力结构；（3）ViT-8R在IJB-B和IJB-C大规模基准上达到ViT类人脸识别模型的SOTA性能，同时注意力图更清晰，能深入揭示模型的注意力行为。

Conclusion: Register tokens机制有效消除了CPE范式下的注意力伪影，兼顾了高性能与高可解释性。ViT-8R在多个大规模基准上验证了其优越性，为可解释的人脸识别ViT提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision Transformers (ViTs) for face recognition (FR) have moved beyond the standard CLS-token paradigm. In this paradigm, a special classification token (CLS) is prepended to the patch embeddings and used as a representation of the input for downstream tasks. An alternative approach, Concatenated Patch Embeddings (CPE), instead leverages all patch tokens by concatenating them into a single vector, which is then projected into a compact face representation. CPE has been shown to improve recognition performance in comparison to CLS-based ones, but our qualitative analysis of attention maps showed the presence of artifacts that limit their interpretability. To address this issue, we incorporate register tokens, learnable tokens concatenated to the initial patch embeddings, and processed jointly through the ViT encoder blocks. This mechanism has been shown to produce more structured and interpretable attention maps compared to baseline ViT. We empirically demonstrate that these artifacts consistently appear across various ViT backbones, including small and large models, and that introducing register tokens effectively mitigates them. Adding four or eight registers significantly enhances interpretability, with eight registers providing the highest verification accuracies and smoothest attention structures. Our resulting model, ViT-8R, corresponds to a CPE-based ViT-B architecture augmented with eight register tokens achieves state-of-the-art performance among ViT-based FR models on large-scale IJB-B and IJB-C benchmarks. Also, ViT-8R produces substantially clearer attention maps compared with the baseline model, which offer deeper insight into the model's attention behavior (https://github.com/TaharChettaoui/ViT-FR-Registers)

</details>


### [72] [MFEN:Multi-Frequency Expert Network for Visible-Infrared Person Re-ID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12051)
*Xulin Li,Yan Lu,Bin Liu,Qinhong Yang,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多频率专家网络(MFEN)，通过混合专家设计实现多频率调制与自适应频带组合，辅以随机频率增强(RFA)和频率辅助优化(FAO)，解决可见光-红外人员重识别中因光照条件差异导致的模态差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-红外人员重识别(VI-ReID)的核心挑战在于模态间差异，作者认为该差异主要源于光照条件的不同（光波长和光源类型）。现有频率域方法要么不区分频带，要么仅关注单一频带，无法应对多样化的光照条件，因此需要更全面的频率域学习方法。

Method: 提出MFEN（多频率专家网络），采用混合专家(mixture-of-experts)设计，实现多频率调制并自适应组合不同频带。同时引入RFA（随机频率增强）和FAO（频率辅助优化）两个辅助模块，三者互补，联合捕获关键的频率域细节信息以学习鲁棒表征。

Result: 在三个VI-ReID数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MFEN结合RFA与FAO，通过全面的频率域学习，有效减小可见光与红外模态间的差异，提取身份相关的鲁棒特征，在VI-ReID任务上取得优异表现。

Abstract: Visible-infrared person re-identification (VI-ReID) is challenging due to the large modality discrepancy between visible and infrared images. We contend that this discrepancy is largely related to differing lighting conditions, including differences in light wavelength and light source type. Recently, frequency-based VI-ReID approaches have achieved notable success because frequency information can better extract identity-relevant contours and details while excluding irrelevant lighting and color.
