To fill this gap, we aim to achieve explainable hateful video detection, enabling models to provide contextual rationales that integrate relevant evidence and logical reasoning alongside decisions. This approach can comprehensively enhance the understanding of video content and the explainability of the decision-making process. We first introduce two datasets, Ex-HateMM and Ex-ImpliHateVid, for explainable hateful video detection. Each dataset provides fine-grained annotations of multimodal harmful elements, along with contextual rationales. We then propose an Information Augmentation and Reasoning Enhancement (IARE) framework designed for explainable detection. The framework employs an information augmentation phase that leverages the multimodal chain-of-thought to integrate harmful elements, thereby enriching rationale evidence. Additionally, IARE incorporates a reasoning enhancement phase, in which Direct Preference Optimization guides the model toward correct reasoning paths and away from incorrect ones, thereby improving the logical coherence of its justifications. We conduct extensive experiments on the two datasets, comparing multiple baselines with our proposed IARE framework. The results demonstrate that IARE achieves state-of-the-art performance while also generating accurate rationales.

</details>


### [175] [Agreement in Representation Space for Open-Ended Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12003)
*Paula Ontalvilla,Gorka Azkune,Aitor Ormazabal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于嵌入一致性(EBA)的方法，将自洽性从精确匹配扩展到开放式生成任务。通过在嵌入空间中聚类采样生成结果来估计语义一致性，实验表明该方法在代码生成、文本摘要等任务中优于随机选择和基于LLM评估的方法，且生成结果在表示空间中的几何位置与生成质量高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自洽性方法依赖精确匹配，仅适用于分类输出任务，无法应用于代码合成、文本摘要等开放式生成任务。作者希望将自洽性推广到更广泛的生成场景中。

Method: 提出Embedding-Based Agreement (EBA)，一种无需训练的、通过在嵌入空间中对采样生成结果进行聚类来估计语义一致性的方法。具体做法是将多次采样结果映射到嵌入空间，利用聚类分析评估生成结果之间的语义一致性程度，以此作为自洽性的代理信号。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和摘要任务上，EBA一致优于随机选择，且比基于LLM评估或不确定性估计的选择方法展现出更稳定的规模化行为。一致性信号在不同模型家族和嵌入空间（包括原生隐藏表示）中保持稳定。此外，生成结果在表示空间中的几何位置与质量强相关：靠近中心区域的生成更可靠，边缘区域则显著更差。

Conclusion: 自洽性应被理解为采样生成结果在几何空间中的组织属性，而非简单的符号重叠。EBA为开放式生成任务提供了一种鲁棒且可扩展的自洽性信号。

Abstract: Self-consistency improves LLM reasoning by sampling multiple outputs and selecting the most consistent answer, but existing formulations largely rely on exact matching and therefore remain limited to tasks with categorical outputs. In this work, we study self-consistency in open-ended generation tasks such as code synthesis and text summarization. We hypothesize that consistency can be understood as a geometric property of the generation space, where semantically compatible generations concentrate in similar regions of representation space. To study this hypothesis, we introduce Embedding-Based Agreement (EBA), a simple training-free operationalization that estimates agreement by clustering sampled generations in embedding space. Through experiments on mathematical reasoning, code generation, and summarization, we show that agreement in representation space provides a robust and scalable signal of self-consistency for open-ended tasks. In particular, EBA consistently outperforms random selection and exhibits more stable scaling behavior than recent selection approaches based on LLM evaluation or uncertainty estimation. We further show that these agreement signals remain stable across model families and embedding spaces, even with native hidden representations. Finally, our analysis shows that the geometric location occupied by sampled generations is strongly correlated with generation quality: generations concentrated near central regions of representation space tend to correspond to more reliable outputs, whereas peripheral generations are substantially less accurate. Overall, our findings support viewing self-consistency as a property of the geometric organization of sampled generations rather than exact symbolic overlap.

</details>


### [176] [StanceNakba Shared Task: Actor and Topic-Aware Stance Detection in Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12068)
*Kholoud K. Aldous,Md Rafiul Biswas,Mabrouka Bessghaier,Shimaa Ibrahim,Kais Attia,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为StanceNakba 2026的立场检测共享任务，包含两个子任务：英文帖子中亲巴勒斯坦/亲以色列/中立的立场分类，以及阿拉伯语帖子中针对关系正常化和难民存在两个话题的立场分类。基于2606条标注数据，13支团队参与，最佳系统Macro F1分别达0.9620和0.8724，主要采用微调多语言Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对社交媒体上巴以冲突相关话语的极化现象，构建一个系统性的立场检测评测基准，推动阿拉伯语和英语冲突语境下多级别、跨话题立场检测研究，识别领域内现有方法和模型的优劣。

Method: 构建包含2606条社交媒体帖子的人工标注数据集（英语和阿拉伯语），设计两个子任务：子任务A为演员级三分类（亲巴勒斯坦/亲以色列/中立），子任务B为跨话题三分类（支持/反对/中立），针对"与以色列关系正常化"和"约旦境内难民存在"两个话题。参与团队主要采用微调MARBERT、AraBERT、DeBERTa-v3等Transformer变体，辅以交叉验证、集成方法和话题条件架构。

Result: 共7支团队参与子任务A，6支团队参与子任务B。子任务A最佳Macro F1达0.9620，子任务B最佳Macro F1达0.8724。Transformer类方法在冲突域立场检测中表现优异，但跨话题泛化和中立类预测仍存在明显挑战。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的模型对冲突领域立场检测非常有效，但跨话题泛化（子任务B表现明显低于A）和中立类分类仍是待解决的核心难点，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We present StanceNakba 2026, a shared task on stance detection in polarized social media discourse related to the Palestinian-Israeli conflict, organized as part of Nakba-NLP 2026 at LREC-COLING 2026. The task introduces two subtasks: Subtask A (Actor-Level Stance Detection), which classifies English social media posts as Pro-Palestine, Pro-Israel, or Neutral; and Subtask B (Cross-Topic Stance Detection), which identifies Favor, Against, or Neither stances in Arabic posts toward two conflict-related topics, normalization with Israel and refugee presence in Jordan. The task is grounded in an annotated dataset of 2,606 social media posts. A total of 7 teams participated in Subtask A and 6 teams in Subtask B. Participating systems primarily fine-tuned Arabic and multilingual transformer-based models, including MARBERT, AraBERT, and DeBERTa-v3 variants, with several teams employing cross-validation, ensemble methods, and topic-conditioned architectures. The best-performing systems achieved a Macro F1 of 0.9620 on Subtask A and 0.8724 on Subtask B, demonstrating that transformer-based approaches are highly effective for conflict-domain stance detection while highlighting persistent challenges in cross-topic generalization and neutral class prediction.

</details>


### [177] [FORT-Searcher: Synthesizing Shortcut-Resistant Search Tasks for Training Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12087)
*Jia Deng,Yimeng Chen,Xiaoqing Xiang,Ziyang Zeng,Shuo Tang,Wayne Xin Zhao,Feng Chang,Chuan Hao,Yuan Wei,Ran Tao,Bryan Dai,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对深度搜索智能体训练中"表面难度"与"实际搜索难度"之间的鸿沟，本文提出了一种捷径感知的难度框架，识别出四类捷径风险，并设计了FORT——一种抗捷径训练数据合成框架，最终仅通过监督微调训练的FORT-Searcher在同规模开源搜索智能体中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度搜索训练数据的合成方法通常通过丰富图结构来增加表面难度，但结构复杂性并不保证实际搜索难度——智能体可能通过更廉价的"捷径"（如直接识别答案实体）绕过预期的多步搜索过程。这种"难度坍缩"问题缺乏系统性的诊断和解决方案。

Method: 首先，作者提出了一个**捷径感知的难度框架**，形式化定义了搜索难度与捷径风险之间的差距，并识别出四类可操作的捷径风险：证据共覆盖（evidence co-coverage）、单线索选择性（single-clue selectivity）、暴露常量（exposed constants）和先验知识绑定（prior-knowledge binding）。为诊断这些风险的实际影响，设计了包括求解成本、答案命中时间和先验捷径率在内的轨迹签名指标。基于该框架，提出了**FORT（Framework of Shortcut-Resistant Training-Data Synthesis）**，通过实体选择、证据图构建、问题表述和对抗性精炼四个阶段的捷径风险控制，合成抗捷径的训练数据。

Result: 实验表明，FORT合成的数据相较于现有开源深度搜索数据集，能诱导出更长的答案前搜索轨迹和更少的捷径模式。仅使用监督微调（SFT）训练的FORT-Searcher，在具有挑战性的深度搜索benchmark上，取得了同等规模开源搜索智能体中的最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地识别和控制捷径风险来合成训练数据，可以显著提升深度搜索智能体的真实搜索能力，而非仅仅增加表面难度。FORT框架及其产出的FORT-Searcher验证了这一方法的有效性。

Abstract: Training deep search agents requires verifiable questions whose answers remain unavailable until sufficient evidence has been acquired through search. Existing synthesis methods often increase apparent difficulty by enriching graph structures, but structural complexity alone does not guarantee realized search difficulty: the intended search process can collapse through a cheaper identifying route. We formalize this gap with a shortcut-aware difficulty framework and identify four actionable shortcut risks: evidence co-coverage, single-clue selectivity, exposed constants, and prior-knowledge binding. To diagnose their realized effects, we use trajectory signatures including solving cost, answer hit time, and prior-shortcut rate. Guided by this framework, we introduce FORT, a Framework of Shortcut-Resistant Training-Data Synthesis. FORT constructs shortcut-resistant training data by controlling shortcut risks across entity selection, evidence graph construction, question formulation, and adversarial refinement. Experiments show that FORT induces longer pre-answer search and fewer shortcut patterns than existing open-source deep search datasets. Using the resulting trajectories, we train FORT-Searcher with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) only, and it achieves the best overall performance among comparable-size open-source search agents on challenging deep search benchmarks. Relevant resources will be made available at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/FORT-Searcher.

</details>


### [178] [Debiasing Without Protected Attributes: Latent Concept Erasure from Textual Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12088)
*Shun Shao,Zheng Zhao,Anna Korhonen,Yftah Ziser,Shay B. Cohen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出H-SAL方法，利用自我描述文本作为隐式去偏信号，在无法直接获取受保护属性（如性别、种族）的现实约束下进行概念和属性消除，并在Stack Exchange多领域有用性预测基准上验证其有效性，发现隐式自我描述去偏常能达到甚至超越显式标签去偏的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实NLP应用中，受保护属性（性别、种族等）常因隐私、法律限制或元数据缺失而不可直接获取，但模型仍可能从文本间接推断出这些属性，导致偏见问题。现有公平性研究多假设可直�接入敏感属性，亟需探索无需直接访问敏感属性的去偏方法。

Method: 提出H-SAL（一种后验概念与属性消除方法），利用自我描述文本（self-description text）作为隐式去偏信号进行后处理去偏；同时构建了一个基于Stack Exchange的多领域有用性预测公平性基准，涵盖显式和隐式信号，支持有无敏感标签两种设置下的去偏对比。

Result: 在编码器和解码器语言模型上，采用隐式自我描述的去偏方法通常能够匹敌甚至超越基于显式标签的去偏效果。

Conclusion: 研究拓宽了表示层公平性研究的边界，为在现实数据约束下开展去偏研究提供了新的基准，表明在不直接访问敏感属性时，利用自我描述文本进行隐式去偏是一条可行且有效的路径。

Abstract: Most fairness research in NLP assumes direct access to protected attributes such as gender, race, or nationality. In practice, however, such information is often unavailable due to privacy constraints, missing metadata, or legal restrictions, even though models may infer it from indirect textual cues. This raises a key question: can debiasing succeed without direct access to sensitive attributes? We propose H-SAL, which performs post-hoc concept and attribute erasure using self-description text as an implicit debiasing signal. To support this setting, we introduce a multi-domain Stack Exchange-based fairness benchmark for helpfulness prediction that includes both explicit and implicit signals, enabling comparison between standard debiasing with protected labels and debiasing without access to sensitive information. Across encoder and decoder-only language models, we find that implicit self-description often matches or outperforms explicit-label-based debiasing. Our results broaden representation-level fairness research and provide a new benchmark for studying debiasing under realistic data constraints.

</details>


### [179] [Augmenting Molecular Language Models with Local $n$-gram Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12113)
*Xinni Zhang,Zijing Liu,He Cao,Yu Li,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MolGram 通过条件 n-gram 记忆模块，将局部化学子结构模式显式注入 Transformer 隐状态，解决了 SMILES 字符级分词导致的"局部性鸿沟"问题，在分子生成、正向反应预测和逆合成三项任务上以更少参数超越基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准字符级分词会割裂 SMILES 中具有化学意义的基序（如官能团、环结构），导致 Transformer 模型将大量容量消耗在学习局部语法上，难以捕获长程依赖——即"局部性鸿沟"。现有解决方案（如化学感知分词）会破坏标准分词器的兼容性，因此需要一种不改变分词器又能弥补局部模式学习缺陷的方法。

Method: 提出 MolGram，核心是一个条件 n-gram 记忆模块。该模块通过可扩展的哈希查找将局部字符串模式映射为可学习的嵌入向量，并将这些区域上下文动态注入 Transformer 的隐状态中。该模块作为即插即用组件，无需修改标准 tokenizer。训练时，模型同时学习 n-gram 记忆库与主模型参数，推理时根据输入序列中的局部 n-gram 模式检索对应记忆嵌入来增强表示。

Result: 在无条件分子生成、正向反应预测和单步逆合成三项任务上，MolGram 均取得一致性能提升。特别地，MolGram 在参数量仅为基线 1/3 的情况下即可超越后者，表明显式的局部模式记忆是一种高效的归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 显式建模局部模式记忆是弥合分子语言模型局部性鸿沟的高效策略。MolGram 以极小的参数代价实现了显著的性能增益，证明了将区域化学语法作为归纳偏置直接注入模型的有效性，为分子语言建模提供了一种兼容性强、效率高的新范式。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models for SMILES strings suffer from a locality gap: standard character-level tokenization fragments chemically meaningful motifs, forcing models to repeatedly learn local syntax at the expense of long-range dependencies. To address this without disrupting standard tokenizers, we propose MolGram, which integrates a conditional $n$-gram memory module into molecular language models. MolGram maps local string patterns to learned embeddings via scalable hash lookups and dynamically injects this regional context into hidden states. Evaluations across three tasks, including unconditional molecule generation, forward reaction prediction, and single-step retrosynthesis, show that MolGram consistently improves performance. Crucially, our analyses demonstrate that MolGram outperforms baselines with 3$\times$ more parameters, establishing explicit local pattern memory as a highly efficient inductive bias.

</details>


### [180] [Detecting Sensitive Personal Information in Japanese Pre-Training Corpora for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12114)
*Rei Minamoto,Yusuke Oda,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了基于日本《个人信息保护法》中"需特别关照的个人信息"（SCPI）的数据集，并训练了机器学习分类器用于日文文本中的敏感信息检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型预训练语料中可能包含敏感个人信息，需要检测和过滤以确保隐私合规。然而，与英语等其他语言相比，日语的敏感个人信息检测研究非常有限，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 基于日本《个人信息保护法》（APPI）对"需特别关照的个人信息"（SCPI）的定义，利用大语言模型进行标注，构建SCPI数据集；随后训练机器学习模型，实现日文文本中SCPI的快速检测。

Result: 所训练的SCPI分类器能够有效识别与SCPI相关的信息。

Conclusion: 本研究首次探索了日文文本语料中的SCPI检测问题，并揭示了实现精确检测所面临的挑战。

Abstract: Sensitive personal information can appear in large-scale pre-training corpora for large language models (LLMs). Detecting and filtering such information is therefore essential to ensure compliance with privacy regulations and prevent unintended information leakage. However, in contrast to English and other languages, research into sensitive personal information has been limited in the Japanese language. In this study, we focus on sensitive personal data defined as special care-required personal information (SCPI) under Japan's Act on the Protection of Personal Information (APPI). We construct an SCPI dataset using LLM-based annotation and train machine learning models to rapidly detect SCPI in text. As a result, our SCPI classifier can effectively identify information related to SCPI. This study is the first to explore SCPI detection in Japanese text corpora, highlighting the challenges of accurate detection.

</details>


### [181] [Soft-Prompt Tuning for Fair and Efficient LLM Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12117)
*Selen Erkan,Bastian Boll,Kristian Kersting,Björn Deiseroth,Letitia Parcalabescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出软提示微调（soft-prompt tuning）作为一种高效、公平且架构无关的LLM评估方法——仅需优化10个软提示向量（约7B模型参数的0.0006%），在80步内即可消除格式遵循差距，使基准分数真实反映模型知识，而无需完整后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试分数常因模型必须遵循特定输出格式而失真，这对缺乏后训练格式能力的基座模型尤为不利——它们可能知道答案却无法按规定格式输出，导致知识被低估。需要一种公平、低成本且不依赖完整后训练的评估方案。

Method: 软提示微调：仅优化10个可学习的软提示向量（嵌入层前缀），冻结模型其余全部参数。在少量样本（约640个，80步）上进行短时微调，使模型适配特定基准的格式要求。同时提出将格式遵循与知识准确性解耦的评估指标。

Result: 在7个模型和7个数据集上验证：(a) 80步内格式遵循饱和，效率极高；(b) 显著优于零样本和少样本提示，暴露了标准提示遗漏的基座模型知识；(c) 即使后训练模型也能受益于软提示以最大化格式合规；(d) 软提示基座模型分数比零/少样本基线更可靠地预测后训练模型排名，为下游模型质量提供低成本代理指标。

Conclusion: 软提示微调是一种公平、高效、低成本的LLM知识评估方案。三大贡献：(1)解耦格式遵循与知识准确性的指标；(2)更公平的LLM知识基准测试协议；(3)在LLM开发早期识别最优预训练策略的高性价比方法。

Abstract: Benchmark scores often misrepresent a large language model's (LLM's) knowledge, because they rely, e.g., on the model's ability to follow specific formatting requirements. This especially penalizes base models that may know the correct answers but lack the ability -- typically introduced in post-training -- to structure them as instructed. To overcome this, we propose soft-prompt tuning, an efficient, fair, and architecture-agnostic model evaluation. By optimizing only 10 soft-prompt vectors (roughly 0.0006% parameters for a 7B model) over a short tuning period, we adapt models to specific benchmark formats, closing gaps in format-following and ensuring that underlying knowledge is accurately reflected in benchmark scores. This allows one to fairly compare different base models -- trained with various pre-training recipes -- on benchmarks without the need for full post-training. We evaluated soft-prompt tuning across 7 models and 7 datasets. The results show that (a) soft-prompt tuning saturates format-following within 80 steps (~640 samples) making it highly efficient, (b) soft-prompt tuning significantly outperforms zero- and few-shot prompting, surfacing base model knowledge that standard prompting misses, that (c) even post-trained models can benefit from soft-prompts to maximize format compliance, and that (d) soft-prompted base model performance predicts post-trained model rankings more reliably than zero- and few-shot baselines, offering a low-cost proxy for downstream model quality. Our contributions include (1) metrics which disentangle format-following and knowledge accuracy, (2) a fairer benchmarking protocol of LLM knowledge, and (3) a cost- and memory-effective recipe to identify optimal pre-training strategies early in LLM development.

</details>


### [182] [A Controlled Study of Decoding-Time Truthfulness Methods on Instruction-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12160)
*Ao Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CHAIR 是一个通过分析大语言模型每层每个 token 的内部 logits 来检测幻觉的监督框架，提取最大值、最小值、均值、标准差和斜率等紧凑特征，在 TruthfulQA 和 MMLU 上表现出色，尤其在零样本场景下泛化能力强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的幻觉（hallucination）问题严重影响其事实性和可靠性。现有检测方法往往依赖外部知识或黑箱探测，作者希望利用模型内部 logits 的层次化模式，构建一个轻量、可泛化且无需外部监督信号的幻觉检测方法。

Method: 提出 CHAIR 框架，从每一层每个 token 的 logits 中提取最大值、最小值、均值、标准差和斜率五类紧凑特征，利用这些层次化特征训练一个监督分类器来判别幻觉。该方法不依赖于特定任务的微调，仅使用内部表示即可完成检测。

Result: 在 TruthfulQA 和 MMLU 数据集上的实验表明，CHAIR 显著提升了幻觉检测准确率，尤其是在零样本（zero-shot）场景下表现出强大的鲁棒性和泛化能力，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CHAIR 不仅提供了一个实用的幻觉检测方案，还揭示了利用 LLM 内部表示设计更先进解码策略的潜力，为通过更丰富的内部表征来提升模型事实性和连贯性奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce CHAIR (Classifier of Hallucination As ImproveR), a supervised framework for detecting hallucinations by analyzing internal logits from each layer of every token. Our method extracts a compact set of features such as maximum, minimum, mean, standard deviation, and slope-from the token logits across all layers, enabling effective hallucination detection without overfitting. Experiments on TruthfulQA and MMLU datasets demonstrate that CHAIR significantly improves detection accuracy, particularly in zero-shot scenarios, showcasing its robustness and generalizability. Beyond hallucination detection, CHAIR highlights the potential of using internal representations for designing advanced decoding strategies. By leveraging patterns in logits, we suggest that more sophisticated models and adaptive decoding methods could further reduce hallucinations and enhance text completion quality. CHAIR not only offers a practical solution for detecting hallucinations but also lays the groundwork for exploring richer representations in LLMs to improve their factuality and coherence.

</details>


### [183] [A Resource for Enthymeme Detection in Controversial Political Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12186)
*Martial Pastor,Nelleke Oostdijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含1,482条政治争议性推文的语料库，由五位标注者对推文中的省略三段论及其论证结构进行标注，旨在研究标注变异。作者采用基于Walton论证方案的结构化标注指南，保留任务的主观解释空间，并证明利用标注者分歧训练的模型优于基于硬多数投票标签训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 省略三段论（enthymemes）的标注具有高度主观性，但现有资源倾向于消除标注分歧，掩盖了分歧的来源及其对模型性能的潜在益处。本文旨在系统研究标注变异，将其视为有价值的信息而非噪声。

Method: 1) 构建包含1,482条政治争议推文的语料库；2) 基于Walton论证方案设计结构化标注指南，既提供约束又保留解释空间；3) 由五位标注者独立标注省略三段论的存在性及论证结构；4) 对标注任务进行复杂度分析，识别高认知负荷点；5) 进行初步实验，对比基于标注者分歧训练的模型与基于硬多数投票标签训练的模型性能。

Result: 基于标注者分歧信息训练的模型优于基于硬多数投票标签训练的模型，表明标注变异中包含有助于模型学习的有价值信号。

Conclusion: 省略三段论定义和指南中的结构性开放为研究主观推理过程的变异提供了可能，对未来的语料资源和关注人类推理的下游NLP应用具有重要意义。标注分歧不应被简单消除，而应被积极利用。

Abstract: Enthymemes, arguments with unstated premises or conclusions, are pervasive in persuasive discourse, yet their annotation remains notoriously subjective. We present a resource of 1,482 tweets from politically controversial discourse, annotated by five annotators for the presence of enthymemes and their argument structure, designed to study label variation. We first revisit the definition of enthymemes and propose annotation guidelines anchored in Walton's argumentation schemes, offering a structured and constrained approach that nonetheless preserves room for the interpretive nature of the task. This contrasts with past resources, which tend to eliminate disagreement, obscuring its sources and preventing investigation of its potential benefits for model performance. We further propose a complexity analysis of the task, identifying where annotation imposes high cognitive load and may give rise to inconsistent annotation. Our preliminary experiments show that models trained on annotator disagreement outperform models trained on hard majority-vote labels. We close by reflecting on how structural openness in enthymeme definitions and guidelines enables the study of variation in subjective inferential processes for future resources and downstream NLP applications concerned with human inference.

</details>


### [184] [Agentic Environment Engineering for Large Language Models: A Survey of Environment Modeling, Synthesis, Evaluation, and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12191)
*Jiachun Li,Zhuoran Jin,Tianyi Men,Yupu Hao,Kejian Zhu,Lingshuai Wang,Dongqi Huang,Longxiang Wang,Shengjia Hua,Lu Wang,Jinshan Gao,Hongbang Yuan,Ruilin Xu,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从环境工程生命周期的视角，系统性地综述了面向LLM智能体的环境研究，涵盖环境的建模、自动合成（符号/神经范式）、评估及应用，并重点分析了智能体-环境协同演化的四条路径和三种环境演化范式，最后展望了EaaS、多智能体环境等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对环境（LLM智能体的交互系统）的系统性分类与深度分析，尽管环境对模型能力持续演化至关重要。本文旨在填补这一空白，从环境工程全生命周期角度进行系统性梳理。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，从环境工程生命周期（建模、合成、评估、应用）四个阶段展开。具体包括：1）按八种属性与八个领域对代表性环境进行归类分析；2）将自动环境合成为符号合成与神经合成两类范式；3）从智能体-环境协同演化视角，划分出记忆中心型、编排中心型、轨迹中心型、探索中心型四条智能体演化路径，以及神经驱动、难度驱动、规模驱动三种环境演化范式。

Result: 建立了系统化的环境分类框架，明确了不同领域环境的核心能力与发展路径；揭示了符号合成与神经合成两种环境自动生成范式的特点与评估方法；刻画了动态环境中智能体演化的四条互补路径和环境自身演化的三种范式，为理解智能体-环境协同进化提供了结构化视角。

Conclusion: 环境工程是LLM智能体能力持续进化的关键驱动力。未来应重点关注Environment-as-a-Service（EaaS）、多智能体环境以及神经-符号融合环境等方向，以进一步推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Environments serve as interactive systems for large language model (LLM) based agents across diverse scenarios and play a crucial role in driving the continual evolution of model capabilities. Despite this importance, existing work lacks a systematic categorization and deep analysis. This paper systematically studies current researches on agentic environments from the perspective of the environment engineering lifecycle, covering their modeling, synthesis, evaluation and application. Specifically, the paper first introduces representative environments from the perspectives of eight attributes and eight domains, providing detailed analyses of their development paths and highlighting their core capabilities. Second, for automated environment synthesis, two paradigms are introduced, such as symbolic synthesis and neural synthesis. This paper also shows different environment evaluation methods in each paradigm. Thirdly, the corresponding environment applications from the perspective of agent-environment co-evolution are discussed. In specific, the paper characterizes the primary pathways for agent evolution in dynamic environments from four complementary perspectives: memory-centric experience evolution, orchestration-centric workflow evolution, trajectory-centric offline evolution, and exploration-centric online evolution. And three paradigms of environment evolution are identified, namely neural-driven, difficulty-driven, and scaling-driven approaches. At last, several promising future directions are discussed, including Environment-as-a-Service, Multi-agent Environments, and Neural-Symbolic Environments.

</details>


### [185] [Adaptive Multi-Resolution Procedural Knowledge Compression for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12203)
*Changyue Wang,Weihang Su,Qingyao Ai,Yichen Tang,Runzhong Qiao,Xuancheng Li,Min Zhang,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SKIM框架，一种针对LLM中可复用程序性技能的自适应多分辨率软令牌压缩方法，在将技能压缩至原文30%-60%长度的同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本压缩方法主要面向文档中的事实性知识，无法有效压缩程序性技能（需保留工作流逻辑依赖、工具协议等）；且常用技能被反复调用，完整文本每次放入上下文会显著增加prefill成本和延迟。

Method: 提出SKIM（SKIll coMpression）框架：一种自适应多分辨率软令牌压缩方法，根据技能复杂度生成不同数量的软令牌，实现轻量级离线压缩，同时保留工作流中的逻辑依赖关系和工具协议。

Result: 实验表明SKIM可将技能压缩至原始令牌长度的30%-60%，且任务性能保持优于现有压缩方法。

Conclusion: SKIM有效解决了LLM应用中程序性技能的压缩问题，兼顾推理效率与技能使用效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used to tackle complex tasks with autonomous workflows. Recently, reusable natural language skills have emerged as a popular paradigm to inject procedural knowledge into LLM applications. Since popular skills are often invoked repeatedly, placing their full text in every context significantly increases prefill cost and latency. While text compression techniques have the potential to solve this problem, most existing methods are designed to compress factual knowledge in documents instead of procedural knowledge, making them insufficient for skill compression. In this paper, we argue that an effective skill compression method should: 1) preserve logical dependencies among workflows and tool protocols, 2) enable lightweight, offline compression for frequently updated community skills, and 3) be adaptable to varying complexities across skills. To address this, we present SKIM (SKIll coMpression), an adaptive multi-resolution soft token compression framework for procedural skills. Depending on the complexity of each skill, SKIM creates different numbers of soft tokens that not only improve the efficiency of LLM inference, but also preserve the effectiveness of skill usage. Experiments indicate that SKIM compresses skills to 30 to 60 percent of their original token length while preserving task performance better than existing compression methods.We have released our code at https://github.com/bebr2/SKIM .

</details>


### [186] [Can News Predict the Market? Limits of Zero-Shot Financial NLP and the Role of Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12210)
*Ali M Karaoglu,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了零样本NLP框架下利用金融新闻预测短期股票走势的可行性，发现零样本方法始终无法超越简单基线，尤其在预测下跌时表现薄弱，但其可解释性信号能有效区分可信与不可信的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型取得显著进展，金融新闻能否可靠预测短期股票走势仍未解决。现有研究多依赖领域微调，作者希望探究零样本方法是否能在无需领域特定训练的情况下从金融新闻中提取可操作信号，同时解决高 stakes 场景下的透明度需求。

Method: 设计了一个结构化流水线：(1) 零样本自然语言推理（NLI）用于从新闻文本中提取情感/信号；(2) 时间聚合模块，显式建模新闻的时效性（recency）和事件依赖的影响窗口，整合多篇文章信息；(3) 多层可解释性框架，连接预测结果到token级、文章级和聚合级证据，并生成有依据的自然语言解释。

Result: 零样本方法在多个模型和预测时间窗口上始终无法超越简单基线；在预测负向价格变动时表现尤其薄弱。但可解释性信号能够可靠地区分高可信度与低可信度的预测，在预测准确率有限的情况下仍具实用价值。

Conclusion: 揭示了零样本金融NLP的局限性，表明将新闻情感映射到短期价格动态存在更深层的结构性问题。倡导从单纯追求预测准确率转向构建以透明性和不确定性感知为核心的决策支持系统。

Abstract: Can financial news reliably predict short-term stock movements? Despite advances in large language models, this question remains unresolved. We revisit this problem using a zero-shot natural language processing framework, investigating whether models can extract actionable signals from financial news without domain-specific training. We design a structured pipeline that combines zero-shot natural language inference with temporal aggregation, explicitly modelling recency and event-dependent impact horizons when integrating information across articles. To address the need for transparency in high-stakes settings, we introduce a multi-layered explainability framework that links predictions to token-level, article-level, and aggregate evidence, and produces grounded natural language rationales. Across multiple models and prediction horizons, we find that zero-shot approaches consistently fail to outperform simple baselines, with particularly weak performance on negative movements, suggesting deeper structural limitations in mapping news sentiment to short-term price dynamics. However, explainability signals reliably distinguish between trustworthy and unreliable predictions, offering practical value even when accuracy is limited. These findings highlight the limits of zero-shot financial NLP and motivate a shift toward decision-support systems that prioritise transparency and uncertainty awareness. Code: https://github.com/alimert05/zero-shot-stock-xai

</details>


### [187] [On The Effectiveness-Fluency Trade-Off In LLM Conditioning: A Systematic Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12234)
*Iuri Macocco,Pau Rodríguez,Arno Blaas,Luca Zappella,Marco Baroni,Xavier Suau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 系统研究了LLM输出控制（概念注入与移除）中多种条件化方法的权衡，发现高效的激活引导方法常以牺牲流畅性为代价，且激活引导在指令微调模型上效果远不如基座模型；提示和SFT适合注入但不擅移除；文本指标可廉价替代LLM-as-judge评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM输出控制方法评估过于狭窄，仅关注概念注入/移除的有效性，忽视了生成质量；且各方法在不同场景下的权衡缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 系统对比了多种条件化方法（激活引导、提示、监督微调等）在概念注入和移除两种场景下的表现，同时评估有效性和流畅性，并考察了模型训练范式（基座模型 vs 指令微调模型）的交互影响，以及文本指标与LLM评判分数的相关性。

Result: 激活引导方法虽高效但严重损害流畅性；激活引导对指令微调模型效果远差于基座模型；提示和SFT适合概念注入但对概念移除效果不佳；廉价文本指标（如困惑度等）与昂贵的LLM-as-judge评分高度相关。

Conclusion: LLM输出控制方法的评估必须同时考虑条件化有效性和生成质量；方法选择应与任务场景（注入/移除）和模型类型匹配；简单文本指标可作为可靠且低成本的评估替代方案。

Abstract: Controlling the output of Large Language Models (LLMs) is a central challenge for their reliable deployment, yet a clear understanding of the involved trade-offs remains elusive. Current approaches to conditioning are often evaluated with a narrow focus on their effectiveness at injecting or removing a target concept, neglecting generation quality. We systematically investigate a range of conditioning methods in both injection and removal scenarios. We find that efficient steering methods frequently achieve conditioning at a steep cost to fluency. Furthermore, we identify a critical yet previously overlooked interaction with the training paradigm: activation steering methods are far less effective on instruction-tuned models than on their base counterparts. Simple prompting and full-fledged supervised fine-tuning, on the other hand, are viable options for concept injection, but are not as good at concept removal. Finally, cheaply computed textual metrics highly correlate to costly LLM-as-judge scores, and provide insights on the behavior of conditioning methods.

</details>


### [188] [VIA-SD: Verification via Intra-Model Routing for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12243)
*Yuchen Xian,Yang He,Yunqiu Xu,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VIA-SD 提出一种多层推测解码框架，通过模型内路由构建"瘦验证器"，将草稿 token 按置信度分层处理：高置信直接接受、中置信由瘦验证器处理、低置信由完整模型验证，从而减少昂贵的大模型调用，实现 10-20% 的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码采用二元决策（接受或完全重算），但作者发现许多被拒绝的 token 实际上无需完整大模型即可正确验证。这揭示了在"直接接受"与"完整验证"之间存在一个被忽视的中间地带，可以用更轻量的验证资源来处理。

Method: 提出 VIA-SD 多层推测解码框架：(1) 通过模型内路由（intra-model routing）从完整验证器中派生出"瘦验证器"（slim-verifier），无需额外训练；(2) 将草稿 token 按置信度分为三层：高置信直接接受、中置信由瘦验证器重新生成验证、低置信由完整模型验证；(3) 该框架兼容现有 SD 方法，无需修改其训练流程。

Result: 在四个代表性任务和多个模型系列上，VIA-SD 将拒绝率降低 0.10-0.22，相比强 SD 基线实现 10-20% 的加速，相比无草稿解码实现 2.5-3 倍加速。

Conclusion: 多层推测解码是一种可扩展且高效的大模型推理通用范式，VIA-SD 通过引入路由瘦验证器，在不牺牲质量的前提下显著降低了验证成本。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) addresses the high inference costs of LLMs by having lightweight drafters generate candidates for large verifiers to validate in parallel. Existing draft-verify methods use binary decisions: accept or fully recompute. Yet we find that many rejected tokens can be verified correctly by a slim submodel derived from the full verifier via intra-model routing, instead of the full verifier. This motivates our slim-verifier to handle tokens requiring moderate verification resources, reducing expensive large-model calls. We propose Verification via Intra-Model Routing for Speculative Decoding (VIA-SD), a multi-tier framework using a routed slim-verifier. Draft tokens are processed hierarchically: direct acceptance for high-confidence cases, slim-verifier regeneration for medium-confidence cases, and full-model verification for uncertain cases. Across four representative tasks and multiple model families, VIA-SD reduces rejection rates by 0.10-0.22 and delivers 10-20% speedups over strong SD baselines, while achieving 2.5-3x acceleration over non-drafting decoding. Moreover, VIA-SD is compatible with existing SD frameworks without modifying their training procedures. Our results suggest multi-tier SD as a general paradigm for scalable and efficient LLM inference. Project page: https://zju-xyc.github.io/VIA-SD-Project-Page/

</details>


### [189] [Reassessing High-Performing LLMs on Polish Medical Exams: True Competence or Bias-Driven Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12250)
*Antoni Lasik,Jakub Pokrywka,Łukasz Grzybowski,Jeremi Ignacy Kaczmarek,Gabriela Korzańska,Janusz Świeczkowski-Feiz,Oskar Pastuszek,Paulina Hoffman,Jakub Tomasz Dąbrowski,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出医学LLM评估中常用的多选题基准存在猜测偏差和答题偏倚，会高估真实临床能力。作者基于波兰医学考试构建了包含15,000+题目、两个新领域和四种结构修改的更难基准，评估21个LLM后发现：在更难设定下，最佳模型分数骤降28-31个百分点，标准MCQA分数无法可靠反映真实医学能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学领域大语言模型（LLMs）的评估主要依赖多选题问答（MCQA），但这种方式存在严重缺陷：模型可通过猜测策略和利用答案偏差（如选项长度、位置偏好等）获得虚高分数，从而高估其真实临床推理能力。因此需要构建一个更严格、更能反映真实医学能力的评估基准。

Method: 作者基于波兰医学考试构建了扩展基准，具体包括：(1) 新增超过15,000道题目；(2) 扩展两个新的医学领域；(3) 引入四种结构性修改以减少MCQA特有的伪影——这些修改旨在消除猜测空间和答题偏差，更严格地测试推理能力。随后在该基准上评估了21个LLMs的表现。

Result: 评估设计对结果影响显著。在更严格的设定下，表现最好的模型（Qwen3.5-122B）在英文考试和波兰语考试上的分数分别下降了28.4和31个百分点。尽管数据污染证据较低，标准MCQA分数仍无法可靠反映模型的真实医学能力。

Conclusion: 当前主流MCQA评估范式系统性地高估了LLMs的临床能力。需要更严格、结构更合理的基准来推动医学AI的可靠评估。作者已将该基准公开发布以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) in medicine are mainly evaluated using multiple-choice question answering (MCQA), which can overestimate real clinical ability due to guessing strategies and answer biases. To address these limitations, we introduce an expanded and more challenging benchmark based on Polish medical exams, adding over 15,000 questions, two new domains, and four structural modifications that reduce MCQA-specific artifacts and better test reasoning. We evaluate 21 LLMs and show that evaluation design strongly affects results. Under our harder setup, the best model (Qwen3.5-122B) drops by 28.4 and 31 pp on English and Polish exams, respectively. Despite low evidence of data contamination, standard MCQA scores do not reliably reflect true medical competence. To facilitate further research, we make our benchmark publicly available.

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Fully Random Masking: Attention-Guided Denoising and Optimization for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12273)
*Jia Deng,Junyi Li,Wayne Xin Zhao,Jinpeng Wang,Hongyu Lu,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AGDO框架，通过分析扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）中的注意力机制，发现注意力权重更高的token在生成中更稳定且对推理至关重要。基于此，AGDO利用注意力结构指导去噪顺序，并在监督微调和强化学习中重点优化注意力关键token，在数学和编程推理任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有dLLMs的后训练方法大多依赖随机掩码策略，忽略了token之间的内在依赖关系，限制了模型推理能力的提升。

Method: 首先对dLLMs中的注意力进行实证分析，揭示注意力权重与生成稳定性及推理重要性的关联；然后提出AGDO框架，包含：(1) 基于注意力结构确定去噪顺序，(2) 在监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）阶段强调注意力关键token的优化。

Result: 在数学和编程基准测试上，AGDO一致地提升了推理性能，超越了当前最先进的dLLMs后训练方法。

Conclusion: 注意力引导的去噪与优化策略能有效捕获token间依赖关系，显著增强扩散大语言模型的推理能力，为dLLMs的后训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer an efficient alternative to autoregressive models through parallel decoding, yet existing post-training methods largely rely on random masking strategies that overlook intrinsic token dependencies. In this work, we present an empirical analysis of attention in dLLMs and show that tokens attending more strongly to unmasked context exhibit greater generation stability and play a critical role in reasoning. Motivated by these findings, we propose AGDO, an attention-guided denoising and optimization framework that aligns both training and optimization with attention-derived dependencies. AGDO determines the denoising order based on attention structure and emphasizes attention-critical tokens during supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical and coding benchmarks demonstrate that AGDO consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming state-of-the-art post-training methods for dLLMs.

</details>


### [191] [Measuring Epistemic Resilience of LLMs Under Misleading Medical Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12291)
*Hongjian Zhou,Xinyu Zou,Jinge Wu,Sean Wu,Junchi Yu,Bradley Max Segal,Tobias Erich Niebuhr,Sara Amro,Michael Petrus,Sheikh Momin,Alexandra M. Cardoso Pinto,Rachel Niesen,Laura Sophie Wegner,Dhruv Darji,Jung Moses Koo,Joshua Fieggen,Kapil Narain,Mingde Zeng,Lei Clifton,Linda Shapiro,Fenglin Liu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 揭示了LLM在医学问答中的一个结构性盲点：当注入误导性上下文时，即使模型原本能正确回答的问题也会被攻破。论文提出"认知韧性"（epistemic resilience）概念，并构建MedMisBench基准（10,932题/48,889个误导对），发现平均准确率从71.1%骤降至38.0%，其中权威框架型虚假陈述攻击成功率高达69.5%。14人跨国临床专家组认定38.2%的案例存在严重潜在危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在医学执照考试中已达专家级分数，这引发了"高分即安全医学判断"的危险假设，同时患者日益依赖LLM获取健康建议。然而现有医学基准仅测量模型"知道什么"，却未评估其在误导性上下文下是否能保持正确判断。作者旨在揭示这一结构性评估盲点，证明高分不等于稳健的医学推理能力。

Method: (1) 提出"认知韧性"概念——在对抗性上下文中维持正确判断的能力；(2) 构建MedMisBench基准，包含10,932道医学题目和48,889个误导上下文-选项对，涵盖医学推理、智能体能力和患者旅程三个维度；(3) 设计误导注入策略，包括权威框架虚假陈述、例外毒化声明等不同类型的对抗性上下文；(4) 在11个模型配置上评估，并由来自7个国家的14名临床专家组成评审团进行危害性判定。

Result: (1) 11个模型配置的平均准确率从原始问题的71.1%降至误导上下文下的38.0%，攻击成功率达51.5%；(2) 最具破坏性的是正式的、类规则的虚假陈述：权威框架型虚假陈述攻击成功率69.5%，例外毒化声明攻击成功率64.1%；(3) 14人跨国临床专家组在38.2%的审查案例中识别出严重潜在危害。

Conclusion: 现有医学LLM基准存在结构性盲点——仅评估知识掌握程度，不评估误导性上下文下的判断保持能力。MedMisBench揭示了这一脆弱性，表明高分模型在对抗性信息面前认知韧性不足，这对临床部署和患者安全构成重大风险。亟需将认知韧性纳入医学AI评估体系。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now reach expert-level scores on medical licensing exams, encouraging the assumption that high scores imply safe medical judgment while patients increasingly use them for health advice. We show this assumption is fragile: when misleading context is injected into questions that LLMs originally answer correctly, they abandon the correct answer. We call the ability to maintain correct judgment under adversarial context epistemic resilience, and introduce MedMisBench to measure it. MedMisBench contains 10,932 medical question items and 48,889 misleading context-option pairs spanning medical reasoning, agentic capability, and patient-journey evaluation. Across 11 model configurations, mean accuracy falls from 71.1% on original questions to 38.0% under focused misleading context, with 51.5% attack success. The most damaging injections are formal, rule-like fabrications: authority-framed falsehoods reach 69.5% attack success and exception-poisoning claims reach 64.1%. A 14-member clinical panel from 7 countries identified serious potential harm in 38.2% of reviewed cases. MedMisBench exposes a structural blind spot in LLM evaluation in medical settings: existing benchmarks measure what models know, but not whether they preserve correct medical judgment under misleading context.

</details>


### [192] [Measuring Semantic Progress in Multi-turn Dialogue via Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12332)
*Paul He,Shiva Kasiviswanathan,Dominik Janzing*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的多轮对话语义进展评估指标，将语义进展形式化为以问题为条件的不确定性减少，并通过嵌入空间中的高斯公式进行近似计算。该指标具备单调性、可加性分解和冗余递减等优良理论性质，在MT-Bench、Chatbot Arena和UltraFeedback等基准上取得了与人类判断竞争性一致的结果，且仅需轻量级嵌入模型即可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话评估的难点在于对话质量体现在多轮交互的累积过程中，而非单轮回复。现有LLM-as-a-judge方法依赖自回归推理，计算成本高且不可复现。作者希望捕捉"语义进展"——即对话中新信息、问题相关信息和非冗余信息的逐步积累——这一关键维度，并以可复现、计算高效的方式进行量化。

Method: 将语义进展形式化为问题条件下的不确定性减少量，提出一种信息论指标，在嵌入空间中用可处理的高斯公式进行近似，具有闭式解更新。主要估计器采用高斯假设下的对数行列式结构，并通过最大熵论证说明当仅保留二阶嵌入信息时该结构会自然出现。该指标满足单调性、跨轮次信息增益的可加性分解、以及冗余证据的边际递减等理论性质。

Result: 在MT-Bench、Chatbot Arena和UltraFeedback上的实验表明，尽管仅针对语义进展这一维度，该指标与人类判断的一致性具有竞争力；在MT-Bench和UltraFeedback上，对齐效果优于多个基于LLM的评判方法。此外，该方法在使用轻量级嵌入模型、仅CPU执行的情况下仍保持有效。

Conclusion: 语义进展可以被有效量化而无须依赖大模型能力，所提指标具备理论优雅性和实践高效性，为多轮对话评估提供了一种可复现、低成本且与人类判断对齐良好的替代方案。

Abstract: Evaluating multi-turn dialogue is challenging because quality emerges across turns rather than within individual responses. We focus on a key dimension of information-seeking dialogue: semantic progress, defined as the accumulation of new, question-relevant, and non-redundant information over the course of a conversation. We formalize semantic progress as question-conditioned uncertainty reduction and introduce an information-theoretic metric that approximates it in embedding space. Our main estimator uses a tractable Gaussian formulation with closed-form updates, while a complementary maximum-entropy argument shows why log-determinant structure arises more broadly when only second-order embedding information is retained. This formulation yields desirable theoretical properties, including monotonicity, additive decomposition of total information gain across turns, and diminishing returns for redundant evidence. Unlike LLM-as-a-judge approaches, our metric requires no autoregressive inference at evaluation time and is fully reproducible for a fixed embedding model. Experiments on MT-Bench, Chatbot Arena, and UltraFeedback show that the proposed metric achieves competitive agreement with human judgments despite targeting only semantic progress, with improved alignment on MT-Bench and UltraFeedback compared to several LLM-based judges. Notably, the method remains effective with lightweight embedding models under CPU-only execution, indicating that semantic progress can be captured without reliance on large model capacity.

</details>


### [193] [ALIGNBEAM : Inference-Time Alignment Transfer via Cross-Vocabulary Logit Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12342)
*Chirag Chawla,Pratinav Seth,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ALIGNBEAM 是一种无需训练的推理时安全对齐方法，通过将锚点模型的 logits 逐 token 翻译到目标模型的词表中，并用小型 LLM 裁判在 K 个候选续写中选择最安全的一个，从而在跨词表模型族之间转移安全对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域微调会严重削弱大语言模型的安全性，微调后的专家模型容易响应领域语言包装的有害提示。现有推理时防御方法（如对比解码）要求安全锚点模型与目标模型共享词表，无法应用于安全性退化最严重的跨模型族场景。

Method: 提出 ALIGNBEAM：首先将锚点模型的 logits 在每一步解码时逐 token 翻译到目标模型的词表中（通过语义对齐实现跨词表映射）；然后在 beam search 框架下生成 K 个候选续写序列；最后使用一个小型 LLM 作为安全性裁判，从 K 个候选中选择最安全的输出。整个过程无需修改任何模型权重，安全性与有用性的权衡可在部署时动态调节。

Result: 在跨词表和同词表评估对上，ALIGNBEAM 在对抗性基准测试中显著提升了拒绝率（refusal rate），同时保持了任务准确率，推理开销控制在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 安全对齐可以在推理时跨模型族进行转移，无需修改任一模型的权重，为领域微调模型的安全部署提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Domain fine-tuning degrades the safety of large language models: fine-tuned specialists readily comply with harmful prompts framed in domain language. Existing inference-time defenses that mix logits from a safe anchor model require both models to share a vocabulary, which rules them out for the cross-family specialists where safety is most degraded. We present ALIGNBEAM, a training-free method that lifts this restriction by translating anchor logits into the target model's vocabulary token-by-token at each decoding step; a small LLM judge then selects the safest among K candidate continuations. No weights are changed, and the safety-utility trade-off can be tuned at deployment without retraining. Across both cross-vocabulary and same-vocabulary evaluation pairs, ALIGNBEAM substantially raises refusal on adversarial benchmarks while keeping task accuracy and inference overhead within practical bounds. The results show that safety alignment can be transferred between model families at inference time, without touching either model's weights.

</details>


### [194] [Verifiable Environments Are LEGO Bricks: Recursive Composition for Reasoning Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12373)
*Hao Xiang,Qiaoyu Tang,Le Yu,Yaojie Lu,Xianpei Han,Ben He,Le Sun,Bowen Yu,Peng Wang,Hongyu Lin,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RACES 是一个递归自动化合成框架，将可验证环境视为可组合的构建块，通过定义组合算子（SEQUENTIAL、PARALLEL、SORT、SELECT）实现环境的递归融合与规模化扩展，从而以更少的基础环境数量达到与大规模独立环境相当的LLM推理增强效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证环境（如数学题、代码执行等）依赖人工或独立构造，其数量增长受限于线性扩展瓶颈，难以支撑RL训练所需的大规模、多样化的推理场景，阻碍了LLM推理泛化能力的进一步提升。

Method: RACES框架将每个可验证环境统一为"领域（输入类型）→共域（输出类型）"的映射，当两个环境满足类型匹配条件时自动融合。定义四种组合算子：SEQUENTIAL（顺序组合）、PARALLEL（并行组合）、SORT（排序组合）和SELECT（选择组合），以300个独立环境为基础实现递归组装，生成多样化的复合推理环境。

Result: 在六个未见于训练环境构造的benchmark上，RACES将DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B平均提升3.1分（48.2→51.3），将Qwen3-14B从58.8提升至61.1。此外，仅使用50个基础环境即可达到与300个独立环境训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: RACES通过递归环境组合有效突破了线性扩展的限制，以更少的基础环境实现了高效且可扩展的推理泛化训练，验证了"环境即可组合构件"这一核心洞见的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) with verifiable environments has emerged as a powerful approach for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). While prior research demonstrates that scaling environment quantity improves RL performance, existing manual or individual construction methods suffer from linear scaling limits, thereby hindering scalable reasoning generalization. This paper introduces RACES (\textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{A}utomated \textbf{C}omposition for \textbf{E}nvironment \textbf{S}caling), a framework that conceptualizes verifiable environments as composable building blocks that can be recursively assembled. The key insight is that when the codomain (output type) of one environment matches the domain (input type) of another, they can be automatically fused into a new verifiable environment, enabling recursive composition. RACES is implemented with 300 individual environments and defines a set of composition operators (\textsc{SEQUENTIAL}, \textsc{PARALLEL}, \textsc{SORT}, and \textsc{SELECT}) that induce diverse reasoning patterns. Extensive experiments show that RL training on these composite environments consistently enhances reasoning generalization. Specifically, RACES improves DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B by an average of 3.1 points (from 48.2 to 51.3) and boosts Qwen3-14B performance from 58.8 to 61.1 on six benchmarks, which are unseen during the construction of training environments. Moreover, RACES achieves performance comparable to training on 300 individual environments using only 50 base environments, demonstrating significant efficiency in environment utilization.

</details>


### [195] [Which Models Are Our Models Built On? Auditing Invisible Dependencies in Modern LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12385)
*Sanjay Adhikesaven,Haoxiang Sun,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ModSleuth 是一个智能体系统，能够从公开 artifacts 中递归重建 LLM 依赖图，解决依赖定义、artifact 引用一致性等核心挑战。在四个 LLM 发布版本中恢复了 1,060 条来源验证的依赖关系，揭示了多跳许可证义务、训练-评估耦合、发布与训练时 artifact 差异等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 训练流程日益依赖其他模型来生成数据、过滤语料、评判输出和指导开发决策，这些依赖是递归的且分散在异构的公开 artifacts 中，其复杂性和递归深度远超人工追踪能力。缺乏统一的依赖结构视图使得许可证合规、可复现性等问题难以发现。

Method: 提出 ModSleuth 智能体系统：(1) 形式化定义直接与间接依赖，通过操作中心关系（operation-centered relationships）表示异构流水线角色；(2) 解析跨名称、版本和仓库的 artifact 身份；(3) 从公开 artifacts 中递归重建依赖图，并提供来源依据的证据。

Result: 在四个公开 artifact 丰富的 LLM 发布版本上应用 ModSleuth，恢复了 1,060 条来源验证的依赖关系，构建了大规模依赖图。揭示了多跳许可证义务、训练-评估耦合、发布 artifacts 与训练时 artifacts 之间的差异，以及文档不一致等问题。

Conclusion: 依赖追踪的主要挑战已不再是信息抽取，而是定义什么构成依赖以及调和跨不一致文档的 artifact 引用。ModSleuth 和其生成的依赖图已开源，以支持对现代 LLM 日益复杂生态系统的透明分析。

Abstract: Modern LLM training pipelines increasingly rely on other models to generate data, filter corpora, judge outputs, and guide development decisions. These dependencies are recursive: a model may depend on an upstream artifact whose own dependencies are documented only in separate releases and artifacts. As a result, the full dependency structure is fragmented across heterogeneous public artifacts, with complexity and recursive depth far outpacing humans' ability to trace. We introduce ModSleuth, an agentic system that recursively reconstructs LLM dependency graphs from public artifacts with source-grounded evidence. We find that the primary challenge is no longer information extraction, but defining what constitutes a dependency and reconciling artifact references across inconsistent documentation. We address these challenges through a formalization that distinguishes direct and indirect dependencies, represents heterogeneous pipeline roles through operation-centered relationships, and resolves artifact identities across names, versions, and repositories. Applying ModSleuth to four public-artifact-rich LLM releases, we recover 1,060 source-verified dependencies and construct large-scale dependency graphs of modern LLM development. These graphs reveal multi-hop license obligations, train-evaluation coupling, discrepancies between released and training-time artifacts, and documentation inconsistencies that would otherwise be difficult to uncover. We release ModSleuth and the resulting dependency graphs to support transparent analysis of the increasingly complex ecosystems underlying modern LLMs.

</details>


### [196] [System Report for CCL25-Eval Task 5: New Dataset and LoRA-Fine-Tuned Qwen2.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12392)
*Haotao Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向古典诗词鉴赏的领域专用大语言模型PoetryQwen，将诗词鉴赏任务分解为术语解释、语义解释和情感推理三个子任务，构建了包含49K条高质量指令-响应对的CCPoetry-49K数据集，并基于Qwen2.5-14B进行LoRA微调。在CCL25-Eval Task 5基准上，PoetryQwen取得0.757分，较基线模型提升9.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前古典诗词的精准翻译与情感语义理解研究不足，主要原因在于多数研究将诗词鉴赏视为通用域问题，忽视了其领域特性，且缺乏高质量、领域专用的数据集。

Method: （1）任务分解：将诗词鉴赏拆分为术语解释、语义解释、情感推理三个子任务；（2）数据集构建：基于多个开源数据集进行数据清洗与对齐，构建CCPoetry-49K（49,404条指令-响应对）；（3）模型微调：采用Low-Rank Adaptation（LoRA）对Qwen2.5-14B进行领域专项微调，得到PoetryQwen。

Result: 在CCL25-Eval Task 5基准测试中，PoetryQwen得分0.757，相比Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct基线（0.690）提升9.7%。

Conclusion: 通过构建领域专用数据集并结合LoRA微调，PoetryQwen在古典诗词的精准翻译与情感理解方面性能显著提升。所提出的数据集与方法论可为LLM的领域专项优化提供参考。

Abstract: Recently, large language models (LLMs) have achieved promising progress in the fields of classical Chinese translation and the generation of classical poetry. However, domain-specific research on precise translation and affective-semantic understanding of classical poetry remains limited. The main challenge is that most studies treat the poetic appreciation task as a general-domain problem, neglecting the distinctive features of poetic appreciation, while high-quality and domain-specific datasets are extremely limited. To address this limitation, we decompose the task into three subtasks: term interpretation, semantic interpretation, and emotional inference. Based on multiple open-source datasets, we perform data cleansing and alignment to construct the Classical Chinese Poetry Instruction Pair Dataset (CCPoetry-49K), which comprises 49,404 high-quality instruction-response pairs explicitly optimized for this domain. We then propose a domain-specialized LLM, called PoetryQwen, by applying Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to fine-tune the Qwen2.5-14B model. Experimental results on the CCL25-Eval Task 5 benchmark demonstrate that PoetryQwen achieves a score of 0.757, representing a 9.7% improvement over the Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct baseline (0.690). These findings clearly indicate that PoetryQwen significantly enhances performance in precise translation and emotional understanding of classical poetry. We present new dataset and methodological considerations intended to support the domain-specific optimization of LLMs.

</details>


### [197] [Doc-to-Atom: Learning to Compile and Compose Memory Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12400)
*Xingjian Diao,Wenbo Li,Yashas Malur Saidutta,Avinash Amballa,Lazar Valkov,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Doc2Atom 将文档分解为语义类型化的知识原子（knowledge atoms），每个原子编译为独立的 micro-LoRA 适配器，推理时由查询路由器选择性组装，以查询特定的适配器替代单一整体适配器，从而缓解无关查询干扰、提升组合召回能力，并在六个 QA 基准上以更低内存成本超越 Doc-to-LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中注意力机制的二次复杂度代价高昂，现有上下文蒸馏方法（如 Doc-to-LoRA）为每个文档生成单一整体 LoRA 适配器，存在三方面问题：(1) 无关查询干扰——与查询无关的上下文信息混入适配器；(2) 组合召回受限——难以跨文档片段组合信息；(3) 长文档扩展性差。需要一种更细粒度、可组合的参数化记忆框架。

Method: 提出 Doc2Atom（Doc-to-Atom），一个组合式参数化记忆框架。核心思路：(1) 文档分解：将每篇文档分解为若干语义类型化的知识原子；(2) 原子编译：每个知识原子被编译为一个独立的 micro-LoRA 适配器和一个溯源检索键（provenance retrieval key）；(3) 推理路由：推理时，轻量级查询路由器根据查询选择并组装相关原子，形成查询特定的适配器；(4) 注入推理：组装后的适配器注入冻结的基座模型进行推理；(5) 训练：整个系统通过多目标蒸馏框架端到端训练。

Result: 在六个不同的 QA 基准测试上，Doc2Atom 在性能上超越 Doc-to-LoRA 基线，同时降低了文档内部化（document internalization）的内存开销。

Conclusion: Doc2Atom 通过将文档细粒度分解为知识原子并实现查询条件化的适配器组装，有效解决了单一体适配器带来的无关干扰和组合召回问题，为长文档推理提供了一种更高效、更可扩展的参数化记忆方案。

Abstract: Long input sequences are central to document understanding and multi-step reasoning in Large Language Models, yet the quadratic cost of attention makes inference both memory-intensive and slow. Context distillation mitigates this by compressing contextual information into model parameters, and recent work such as Doc-to-LoRA amortizes context distillation into a single forward pass that generates one LoRA adapter per document. However, producing a single monolithic adapter for all queries leads to irrelevant-query interference, limited compositional recall, and poor scalability to long-document reasoning. To address these challenges, we propose Doc-to-Atom (Doc2Atom), a compositional parametric memory framework that decomposes each document into semantically typed knowledge atoms. Each atom is compiled into an independent micro-LoRA adapter and a provenance retrieval key. At inference time, a lightweight query router selects and assembles only the relevant atoms into a query-specific adapter, which is then injected into a frozen base model. The entire system is trained end-to-end through a multi-objective distillation framework. Experiments on six diverse QA benchmarks demonstrate that Doc2Atom outperforms Doc-to-LoRA baselines while reducing the memory cost of document internalization.

</details>


### [198] [Context-Driven Incremental Compression for Multi-Turn Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12411)
*Yeongseo Jung,Jaehyeok Kim,Eunseo Jung,Jiachuan Wang,Yongqi Zhang,Ka Chun Cheung,Simon See,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对长对话中上下文窗口膨胀导致的冗余注意力与编码开销问题，提出上下文驱动的增量压缩方法C-DIC。该方法将会话视为交织的上下文线索，通过轻量级的检索-修订-写回循环实现跨轮次信息共享与记忆更新，并引入截断BPTT以学习跨轮依赖。实验表明C-DIC在数百轮对话中保持稳定的推理延迟和困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代对话Agent在每轮都需处理不断增长的对话历史，造成冗余的注意力和编码成本随对话长度线性增长。现有方案中，简单截断或摘要会降低保真度，而现有上下文压缩器缺乏跨轮次记忆共享或修订机制，导致信息丢失并在长对话中引发复合误差。因此亟需一种兼顾效率与鲁棒性的上下文压缩方法。

Method: 提出C-DIC（Context-Driven Incremental Compression），核心包括：(1)将会话建模为交织的上下文线索(threads)，每条线索维护可修订的压缩状态，统一存储在紧凑的对话记忆中；(2)每轮对话执行轻量级的检索-修订-写回(retrieve-revise-write-back)循环，实现跨轮信息共享与过时记忆更新；(3)将截断BPTT(TBPTT)适配到多轮对话场景，无需全历史反向传播即可学习跨轮依赖。

Result: 在长对话基准测试上，C-DIC在性能与效率方面均表现优越。尤其突出的是，C-DIC在数百轮对话中推理延迟和困惑度保持稳定，展现了良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: C-DIC通过增量式、可修订的线索压缩与跨轮记忆共享，有效解决了长对话建模中的效率-保真度权衡问题，为高质量对话建模提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Modern conversational agents condition on an ever-growing dialogue history at each turn, incurring redundant attention and encoding costs that grow with conversation length. Naive truncation or summarization degrades fidelity, while existing context compressors lack cross-turn memory sharing or revision, causing information loss and compounding errors in long dialogues. We revisit the context compression under conversational dynamics and empirically present its fragility. To improve both efficiency and robustness, we introduce Context-Driven Incremental Compression (C-DIC), which treats a conversation as interleaved contextual threads and stores revisable per-thread compression states in a single, compact dialogue memory. At each turn, a lightweight retrieve, revise, and write-back loop shares information across turns and updates stale memories, stabilizing long-horizon behavior. In addition, we adapt truncated backpropagation-through-time (TBPTT) to our multi-turn setting, learning cross-turn dependencies without full-history backpropagation. Extensive experiments on long-form dialogue benchmarks demonstrate superior performance and efficiency of C-DIC; notably, C-DIC shows stable inference latency and perplexity over hundreds of dialogue turns, supporting a scalable path to high-quality dialogue modeling.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Annealed Entropic Allocation for Ranking and Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11347)
*Xin Fei,Juergen Branke*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出退火熵分配框架，用加权log-sum-exp替代不可微的maximin大偏差率目标，通过软最小权重聚合成对得分，缓解多挑战者接近活跃时的硬切换问题，并结合鞍点近似修正有限预算下的判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序与选择中的序贯预算分配问题中，经典的maximin大偏差率目标函数不可微，且当多个挑战者接近活跃时会出现硬切换（hard switching）现象，导致分配策略不稳定。需要一种既能保持一阶大偏差性质、又能平滑化目标函数的替代方案。

Method: 1) 将不可微的maximin大偏差率目标替换为加权log-sum-exp代理函数，通过退火（annealing）平滑参数逐步趋于零；2) 引入鞍点近似（saddlepoint approximation）作为次指数修正项，源自精细的成对尾部渐近分析；3) 证明代理函数一致收敛到硬最小值，软最小权重集中在活跃挑战者上，且在固定权重下诱导的目标分配映射在单纯形内部是连续的。

Result: 高斯和指数分布实例的数值实验表明，该方法具有竞争力，尤其在多个挑战者接近平局时表现优异。

Conclusion: 退火熵分配框架在保持一阶大偏差目标一致性的前提下，通过软最小平滑和鞍点修正，有效解决了经典maximin方法的不可微性和硬切换问题，在近似平局场景下具有明显优势。

Abstract: We propose Annealed Entropic Allocation, an annealed weighted soft-min framework for sequential budget allocation in ranking and selection. The central idea is to replace the non-smooth maximin large-deviation rate objective with a weighted log-sum-exp surrogate that aggregates challenger-specific pairwise scores through soft-min weights, mitigating hard switching when several challengers are nearly active. To improve finite-budget discrimination, we incorporate the saddlepoint approximation -- a sub-exponential correction derived from refined pairwise tail asymptotics. Because these corrections are sub-exponential and the smoothing parameter is annealed to zero, the surrogate preserves the same first-order large-deviation target as the classical maximin formulation. We show that the surrogate converges uniformly to the hard minimum, that the soft-min weights concentrate on the active challengers, and that, under fixed weights, the induced target allocation map is continuous on the simplex interior. Numerical experiments on Gaussian and exponential instances demonstrate competitive performance, especially when multiple challengers are nearly tied.

</details>


### [200] [Enhancing Spectral Embedding through Robust and Flexible Knowledge Transfer in Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11570)
*Feiqing Huang,Zongqi Xia,Rong Ma,Tianxi Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于谱方法的无监督表示学习框架，利用从更广泛人群中提取的知识矩阵，为罕见病电子健康记录中的临床概念和患者生成低维嵌入，通过允许部分对齐的灵活共享结构克服高维小样本挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病电子健康记录数据维度高但样本量小，现有方法依赖严格的一对一信号对齐假设，无法适应罕见病场景中共享信号弱且仅部分对齐的现实情况。

Method: 提出两步谱嵌入流程：(1) 识别并移除知识矩阵中的无关成分；(2) 应用基于投影的方法分别恢复共享成分和异质成分。核心创新在于放宽了潜在数据矩阵与知识矩阵间的一对一信号对齐假设。

Result: 模拟实验和真实多发性硬化症队列分析表明，该方法在共享信号弱且部分对齐的困难场景下显著优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的灵活结构化共享框架能够有效应对罕见病数据的高维小样本挑战，为临床概念和患者的低维表示学习提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a spectral-based, unsupervised representation learning framework to derive low-dimensional embeddings for clinical concepts and patients in rare disease cohorts from electronic health records, where data are high-dimensional but sample sizes are limited. To overcome this challenge, we incorporate a knowledge matrix extracted from a broader population that shares a partially overlapping subspace with the rare-disease cohort. Our method departs from existing approaches by relaxing restrictive one-to-one signal-alignment assumptions between the latent data matrix and knowledge matrix, allowing more flexible and realistic forms of structured sharing. We introduce a novel two-step spectral embedding procedure: first, we identify and remove irrelevant components from the knowledge matrix; then, we apply a projection-based method to separately recover shared and heterogeneous components. Simulations and an analysis of a real-world multiple sclerosis cohort show that the proposed method outperforms competing approaches, particularly in challenging scenarios where shared signals are weak and only partially aligned, as is common in rare-disease data.

</details>


### [201] [Renewable Lasso without Batch-Number Constraints: A Gradient-Enhanced Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11738)
*Junzhuo Gao,Ling Peng,Xu Guo,Heng Lian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对高维广义线性模型的在线估计问题，本文提出了一种梯度增强的替代损失方法，在非分布式和分布式流数据场景下均放宽了以往方法对批次数量的严格限制，并给出了高维设定下的非渐近误差界，数值实验表明方法优于现有可更新估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向流数据的高维广义线性模型的可更新估计方法存在批次数量的严格约束，且分布式场景下直接套用Jordan等人(2019)的替代似然方法计算成本高。本文旨在突破这些限制，提出更灵活、更高效的在线估计算法。

Method: 在非分布式场景下，提出梯度增强的替代损失（gradient-enhanced surrogate loss），仅利用历史汇总统计量近似累积损失；在分布式主从架构下，各客户端仅需交换梯度向量而非完整替代损失，降低通信与计算开销。两种设定下均给出高维非渐近误差界。

Result: 理论分析表明，本文方法在高维标度下无需以往研究中对批次数量的严格约束即可获得非渐近误差界。在线性模型和逻辑回归模型的模拟实验及真实数据应用中，所提方法相较现有可更新估计器展现了更高的估计精度。

Conclusion: 提出的梯度增强替代损失方法成功去除了批次数量的约束，在非分布式和分布式流数据场景下均能实现高效的高维在线估计，理论和实证结果均验证了其优越性。

Abstract: We study online estimation for high-dimensional generalized linear models with streaming data. First, for the non-distributed setting, we propose a gradient-enhanced surrogate loss that approximates the cumulative loss using only historical summaries, which modifies and improves upon the existing renewable estimation approach for the same model in the high-dimensional setting, and removes the batch-number constraint in previous studies. We then extend the method to distributed streaming data under the master-client architecture, where batches are partitioned across sites and only summaries (gradient vectors) are exchanged. Instead of directing applying the popular method of Jordan et al. (2019) to the surrogate quadratic loss, our adjusted approach does not require the clients to compute the full surrogate loss. We derive non-asymptotic error bounds under the high-dimensional scaling, without the stringent constraint on the number of batches in the previous studies. Simulation results under linear and logistic models, together with a real-data application, show improved accuracy over existing renewable estimators.

</details>


### [202] [Conformal Bayes under Label Shift: Post-Hoc Calibration vs. In-Training Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11865)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究标签偏移（label shift）下的共形贝叶斯（Conformal Bayes），提出两种互补的重要性加权校准策略——事后校准（post-hoc calibration）和训练中适应（in-training adaptation），二者均通过重要性加权恢复目标域覆盖率，但作用机制独立。实验表明，在无偏训练中两者表现相当，而在先导优化场景下，训练中适应可起到去偏作用，在保持覆盖率的同时缩小预测区间宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形贝叶斯能在源域生成有效且高效的预测集，但在标签偏移（label shift）下，源域和目标域的分布失配会导致覆盖率下降。现有方法缺乏对标签偏移下共形贝叶斯校正机制的统一理解，亟需系统化的解决方案来恢复目标域的名义覆盖率。

Method: 从统一视角出发，识别出两种互补方法：（1）事后校准：保持参数后验不变，通过重要性加权分位数校正共形阈值，并使后验预测分布向目标域倾斜；（2）训练中适应：直接使参数后验向目标域倾斜，生成修正的预测分布，以该分布的最高预测密度区域作为预测集。两种方法均基于重要性加权的共形校准。

Result: 在无偏训练体制下，两种策略均同等实现有效覆盖率。在先导优化（lead-optimization）体制下，训练中适应起到去偏算子的作用，在覆盖率不变的情况下缩小了预测区间宽度。但训练中适应的效率依赖于模型，且不保证有限样本下的条件最优性。

Conclusion: 共形贝叶斯在标签偏移下可通过两种独立机制的重要性加权校准恢复目标域覆盖率。训练中适应在特定场景下具有额外的去偏和效率优势，但其效率依赖于模型选择，不具有限样本条件最优性的保证。

Abstract: Conformal Bayes combines Bayesian posterior predictives with conformal calibration to produce prediction sets that are both statistically valid and geometrically efficient. We study conformal Bayes under label shift from a unified perspective, identifying two complementary approaches that restore nominal target-domain coverage through importance-weighted conformal calibration but operate through independent mechanisms. \emph{Post-hoc calibration} tilts the posterior predictive toward the target domain and corrects the conformal threshold via an importance-weighted quantile, leaving the parameter posterior unchanged. \emph{In-training adaptation} tilts the parameter posterior itself to the target domain, producing a corrected predictive whose highest predictive density region serves as the highest predictive density (HPD) based prediction set under the fitted target predictive; efficiency is model-dependent and does not imply finite-sample conditional optimality. Two controlled experiments show that in an unbiased training regime both strategies achieve valid coverage equally, while in a lead-optimization regime in-training adaptation acts as a debiasing operator, reducing interval width at unchanged coverage.

</details>


### [203] [From Persistence to Survival: Hypothesis Testing, Effect Sizes and Vectorisation for Topological Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11911)
*Juliette Murris,Bernadette Stolz,Karsten Borgwardt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: STRAND 提出将持久性图视为生存数据，用持久性生存函数 $S(t) = \mathbb{P}(p > t)$ 作为核心表示，统一实现非参数双样本检验、可解释效应量和1-Wasserstein稳定的特征向量化，在一个框架内同时支持统计推断和机器学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性图（PD）是拓扑数据分析的常用表示，但其天然不具备向量空间结构，导致统计比较工具与下游预测工具长期割裂发展，缺乏统一的、可解释的表示框架。

Method: 将每个拓扑特征的持久性值 $p = d - b$ 视为完全观测的生存时间（time-to-event），定义持久性生存函数 $S(t) = \mathbb{P}(p > t)$。基于此表示，推导出：(i) 非参数双样本检验（校准I类错误，少量图即可获得高功效）；(ii) 可解释的效应量；(iii) 具有1-Wasserstein稳定性的特征向量。

Result: 在受控拓扑的合成流形上验证了检验的校准性和功效；在14个图基准和3D点云基准上展示了竞争性的向量化性能；成功应用于fMRI/神经科学的脑功能连接分析。

Conclusion: STRAND 是首个从单一、一致且可解释的表示出发，同时实现持久性图的假设检验和向量化的方法。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams are common representations in topological data analysis, but they do not naturally live in a vector space, and the statistical tools developed for comparing them have largely evolved separately from those used for downstream prediction. We introduce STRAND (Survival Topological Representation ANalysis of Diagrams), which treats (collections of) PDs as survival data: each topological feature with persistence value $p = d - b$ is a fully observed time-to-event, and the persistence survival function $S(t) = \mathbb{P}(p > t)$ is the central object for comparing diagrams. From this single representation we derive (i) a non-parametric two-sample test with calibrated Type I error and high power from a small number of diagrams; (ii) interpretable effect sizes; and (iii) a 1-Wasserstein-stable feature vector for downstream machine learning. We validate calibration and power on synthetic manifolds with controlled topology, demonstrate competitive vectorisation across 14 graph and 3D point cloud benchmarks, and apply the method to study functional brain connectivity in fMRI/neuroscience data. To our knowledge, STRAND is the first method to provide hypothesis testing and vectorisation for persistence diagrams from a single coherent and interpretable representation.

</details>


### [204] [Phase Transitions in Attention: A Bayesian Theory of Copy Head Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12058)
*Itay Lavie,Kirsten Fischer,Andrey Lekov,Frederic Van Maele,Zohar Ringel,Moritz Helias*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关于注意力机制中特征学习的贝叶斯理论，聚焦于分析单层softmax注意力网络在复制任务上如何学习induction head的copy子电路。作者推导了注意力矩阵的闭式后验，并将其约化为低维序参量空间，揭示了训练数据量上的相变现象：softmax注意力呈现一阶相变，而线性注意力则经历二阶相变后平滑过渡到结构化注意力模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力模式在训练过程中会突然涌现，这一现象在大型语言模型训练中已被经验观察到，但缺乏第一性原理的理论解释。作者旨在从理论上理解induction head中copy子电路是如何突然被学习的。

Method: 作者采用贝叶斯方法对单层softmax注意力网络在复制任务上进行理论分析。具体而言：1）推导注意力矩阵的闭式后验分布；2）将高维后验约化到低维序参量空间；3）通过序参量分析揭示相变行为；4）使用贝叶斯采样和Adam标准训练两种方式进行数值验证；5）对比softmax注意力和线性注意力的相变差异。

Result: 1）注意力矩阵后验可被约化为低维序参量描述；2）softmax注意力呈现一阶相变（突变式），即copy子电路在达到临界数据量后突然涌现；3）线性注意力则先经历二阶相变，随后平滑连续地演化为结构化注意力模式（crossover），而非突变；4）贝叶斯采样和Adam训练均验证了上述相变现象。

Conclusion: 该工作从第一性原理出发，为copy子电路的突然涌现提供了理论解释，与大型语言模型训练中观察到的注意力模式突变现象一致。softmax注意力和线性注意力在相变行为上存在本质差异：前者为一阶相变（突变），后者为二阶相变接续平滑过渡（crossover）。

Abstract: Attention is the key mechanism underlying in-context learning in transformers, and attention patterns have been observed empirically to emerge abruptly during training. We present a Bayesian theory of feature learning in attention; we then focus on how the copy subcircuit in the first layer of an induction head is learned by analyzing a single-layer softmax attention network trained on a copy task. We derive a closed-form posterior over the attention matrix and reduce it to a low-dimensional order parameter space. This reduction reveals a phase transition in the amount of training data, which we verify using both Bayesian sampling and standard training with Adam. We contrast our results with linear attention and find that softmax attention exhibits a \emph{first-order phase transition} while in linear attention an initial \emph{second-order phase transition} is followed by a smooth, continuous evolution toward the structured attention pattern (\emph{crossover}). Our work provides a first-principles theoretical account of the abrupt emergence of the copy subcircuit, reminiscent of the one observed in training large language models.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Restless bandits with imperfect binary feedback: PCL-indexability analysis and computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11192)
*José Niño-Mora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究带二进制隐状态和不完美二进制反馈的 restless bandit 问题，提出基于部分守恒律（PCL）的分析框架来建立可索引性并计算 Whittle 指数。通过确定性骨架、更新分解和词组合学分析随机动态，在多数阈值机制下得到解析表达式，其余机制下给出高效数值方案。实验表明 MP 指数策略显著优于基准策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受频谱感知中带传感误差的机会频谱接入场景驱动，需要解决带有不完美反馈的 restless bandit 最优调度问题。现有工作在可索引性验证上依赖严格的参数限制，缺乏通用的分析与计算框架。

Method: 基于部分守恒律（PCL）框架和实状态折扣 restless bandit 的验证定理，构建信念状态模型。通过确定性骨架系统、更新分解和词组合学方法分析随机动态，在若干阈值机制下导出折扣奖励和资源度量的解析表达式。对剩余机制，开发高效数值方案计算边际生产力和 MP 指数。

Result: 在多个阈值机制下完整验证了 PCL-可索引性条件并得到 Whittle 指数的解析表达式。在未完全解析验证的机制下，广泛数值实验表明可索引性条件在宽参数范围内成立，且无需先验工作的严格参数限制。MP 指数策略通常显著优于标准基准策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的 PCL 框架为 restless bandit 的可索引性分析和 Whittle 指数计算提供了有力工具，MP 指数策略在频谱接入等实际应用中展现出优越性能，且适用范围远广于先验工作所限定的参数区间。

Abstract: We study restless bandits with binary latent states and imperfect binary feedback, motivated by opportunistic spectrum access with sensing errors. For the associated belief-state model, we develop a partial conservation laws (PCL)-based analytical and computational framework for establishing indexability and evaluating the Whittle index, building on a verification theorem for real-state discounted restless bandits. The framework analyzes the stochastic dynamics via an associated deterministic skeleton, renewal decompositions, and combinatorics on words. It yields tractable expressions for discounted reward and resource metrics in several threshold regimes, enabling full verification of the PCL-indexability conditions there. For the remaining regime, where a complete analytic verification is not achieved in this paper, we derive efficient numerical schemes for computing the relevant marginal metrics and the marginal productivity (MP) index, which equals the Whittle index when those conditions hold. Extensive computational experiments provide strong evidence that these conditions also hold in that regime across broad parameter ranges and without the stringent parameter restrictions imposed in prior work. The experiments further show that theMP index policy typically outperforms standard benchmark policies, often by a substantial margin.

</details>


### [206] [To Intervene or Not: Guiding Inference-time Alignment with Probabilistic Model Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11201)
*Jin Gan,Xin Li,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BlendIn 提出一种推理时对齐框架，通过将二元的引导决策转变为混合分布，根据可靠性按比例加权各模型的贡献，从而稳定且高效地实现模型对齐，在困难模型对上性能提升最高达50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法从某些已对齐模型中提取引导，但未评估引导的可靠性。系统评估发现，引导有效性在不同模型间差异巨大；无效引导会导致进一步困惑和过度干预，反而降低性能。因此需要一种能评估引导质量、减少无效干预的方法。

Method: 提出 BlendIn 框架，核心是将推理时对齐从二元决策（采纳/拒绝引导）转变为创建混合分布，融合两个模型的知识。具体通过质量感知对齐（quality-aware alignment）和基于可靠性按比例加权各模型贡献来实现，保留有益引导的同时降低不可靠建议的权重。

Result: BlendIn 提供了对错误引导的诊断信号和缓解策略，在具有挑战性的模型对上实现了稳定且最高达50%的性能提升。

Conclusion: BlendIn 通过混合分布和可靠性加权机制，有效解决了推理时对齐中引导质量参差不齐导致的过度干预问题，显著提升了对齐的稳定性和效率。

Abstract: The wide deployment of LLMs has made model alignment necessary to make newly trained models safely and effectively respond to user instructions. Among different methods, inference-time alignment is often cheaper as it intervenes (i.e., offers guidances) only during output generation. Existing proposals apply guidances extracted from certain aligned models without properly assessing their reliability. Nonetheless, our systematic evaluation reveals that guidance effectiveness varies drastically across models; since ineffective guidances lead to further confusion and thus further interventions, the resulting excessive interventions typically indicate poor performance. To make interventions more effective and thus more efficient, we introduce BlendIn, an inference-time alignment framework that shifts from binary decisions to creating hybrid distributions integrating both models' knowledge. BlendIn stabilizes inference-time alignment by performing quality-aware alignment and proportionally weighting each model's contribution based on reliability. Compared with existing works, it preserves beneficial guidance while downweighting unreliable suggestions. BlendIn provides both diagnostic signals and mitigation strategies for misaligned guidance, achieving consistent and up to 50% performance improvement on challenging model pairs. Our code is available at: https://github.com/DecayingSeart/BlendIn.

</details>


### [207] [Dual-Stance Evaluation of Sycophancy: The Structure of Agreement and the Limits of Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11205)
*Matthew James Buchan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 激活引导（activation steering）虽能改变LLM行为，但研究发现：减少谄媚（sycophancy）的引导方向同时也会抑制模型对事实正确陈述的同意。两种行为在几何上可分，但引导方向却无法区分它们，揭示了"可读性"与"可写性"之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导评估通常只测试单一立场，忽略了减少谄媚的引导方向是否也会损害模型对事实正确陈述的认同。作者旨在揭示这种潜在的副作用，并探究其背后的表征几何原因。

Method: 提出"双立场评估"（dual-stance evaluation），对每个主题同时测试两种立场；在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上应用质心差分引导（centroid-difference steering），对比谄媚性同意与事实性同意的激活表征，分析引导方向在这两个子空间上的投影关系。

Result: 发现两者在残差流中位于几何上不同的子空间，但引导方向等量投影到两个子空间上，无法差异化地仅针对谄媚行为。结果：引导方向同时减少了谄媚性同意和事实性同意。两组激活的所有其他静态属性均匹配，暗示行为分离源于生成动态或更细粒度的结构。

Conclusion: 揭示了表征工程中的一个普遍性问题：从激活中"可读"的表征未必能通过激活"写入"，即表征的可读性与可写性之间存在根本性差距。这对激活引导方法的实际应用构成了重要限制。

Abstract: Activation steering can shift LLM behaviour, but standard evaluations do not typically test whether a sycophancy-reduction direction also suppresses agreement with factually correct statements. We introduce dual-stance evaluation, which tests both stances of each topic, and apply it to centroid-difference steering on Llama-3-8B-Instruct. We find a dissociation: the model represents sycophantic and factual agreement in geometrically distinct subspaces, yet the steering direction projects equally onto both and cannot differentially target either. The direction accordingly reduces agreement with factually correct statements (e.g. that the Earth is round) as well as sycophantic ones. All other static properties of the two activation groups are matched, suggesting the behavioural dissociation arises from generation dynamics or from finer-grained structure that residual-stream analysis cannot resolve. The pattern illustrates a general gap: representations that are readable from activations may not be writable through them.

</details>


### [208] [Few-Shot Resampling for Scalable Statistically-Sound Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11235)
*Leonardo Pellegrina,Fabio Vandin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FewRS是一种新颖的重采样方法，通过推导测试统计量上确界偏差的新界限，仅需极少量重采样数据集即可评估数据挖掘结果的统计显著性，大幅降低计算成本同时保持统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重采样方法需生成并分析数千个重采样数据集，对大规模数据或计算密集型分析不实用。需要一种高效、可扩展且保证错误发现概率的统计显著性评估方法。

Method: 提出FewRS方法，核心是推导测试统计量上确界偏差的新界限（supremum deviation bound），据此只需生成和分析极少量的重采样数据集即可对统计显著性做出严格保证。

Result: 在模式挖掘和网络分析等任务上，相比现有方法运行时间减少达两个数量级，同时保持高统计功效，可应用于大规模真实数据集。

Conclusion: FewRS是一种广泛适用、高度可扩展的重采样显著性检验方法，以极低计算开销实现了对数据挖掘结果统计有效性的严格保证。

Abstract: A key step in knowledge discovery is the evaluation of data mining results. In several applications, including pattern mining, graph analysis, and others, this step includes the evaluation of the statistical significance of the results, to avoid spurious discoveries due only to noise or random fluctuations in the data. While specialized procedures have been developed for some specific applications, resampling-based approaches are widely used, in particular for complex analyses where analytical results cannot be derived. However, current resampling-based approaches require the generation and analysis of thousands of resampled datasets, and are therefore impractical for large datasets or computationally intensive analyses.
  In this paper, we introduce FewRS, a simple and effective resampling-based approach to assess the statistical significance of data mining results with rigorous guarantees on the probability of false discoveries. Our approach can be used in every situation where resampling-based approaches are applied. FewRS builds on our derivation of a novel bound to the supremum deviation of test statistics representing the quality of data mining results. We prove that FewRS needs to generate and analyze an extremely small number of resampled datasets, leading to a highly scalable approach with wide applicability. We test our approach on common tasks such as pattern mining and network analysis. In all cases, our approach results in a reduction of up to two orders of magnitude in running time compared to the state of the art, while preserving high statistical power, enabling the statistical validation of data mining results on large-scale real-world datasets.

</details>


### [209] [ProHiFlo: Hierarchical Flow Matching with Functional Guidance for De Novo Protein Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11243)
*Chuanzhen Wang,Meade Cleti,Pete Jano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProHiFlo 是一个层次化流匹配框架，通过粗到细生成、功能引导和自适应 SE(3)-等变架构，在蛋白质从头生成任务上以更少采样步骤实现了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散或流匹配方法通常在单一分辨率下操作，缺乏整合功能约束的机制，且计算开销大。需要一种既能高效多尺度建模、又能灵活融入功能约束的蛋白质生成框架。

Method: 提出 ProHiFlo，包含三大创新：(1) 粗到细层次化生成策略，先建模骨架几何再细化到全原子坐标；(2) 功能引导机制，利用预训练预测器在无需重新训练的条件下引导生成朝向目标属性；(3) 自适应 SE(3)-等变架构，实现高效多尺度处理。

Result: 在无条件生成、motif 骨架搭建和功能设计任务上达到最先进水平，且采样步骤减少 4 倍。在酶活性位点骨架搭建任务中，ProHiFlo 达到 58.9% 的成功率，显著优于 RFDiffusion 的 41.2%。

Conclusion: ProHiFlo 通过层次化流匹配、功能引导和等变架构的协同设计，在蛋白质生成质量和效率上均超越现有方法，尤其在功能约束条件下的设计任务中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: De novo protein generation has transformative potential in therapeutic design, enzyme engineering, and synthetic biology. While diffusion-based and flow matching approaches have achieved progress, they typically operate at single resolution and lack mechanisms for incorporating functional constraints. We introduce ProHiFlo, a hierarchical flow matching framework with three innovations: (1) coarse-to-fine generation that models backbone geometry before refining to all-atom coordinates, reducing computational cost while maintaining accuracy; (2) functional guidance leveraging pretrained predictors to steer generation toward desired properties without retraining; (3) adaptive SE(3)-equivariant architecture for efficient multi-scale processing. Experiments on unconditional generation, motif scaffolding, and functional design demonstrate state-ofthe-art performance while requiring 4 fewer sampling steps. On enzyme active site scaffolding, ProHiFlo achieves 58.9% success rate compared to 41.2% for RFDiffusion.

</details>


### [210] [Physics-informed generative AI for semiconductor manufacturing: Enforcing hard physical constraints in generative models by construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11247)
*Yaser Mike Banad,Sarah Sharif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了半导体制造领域对生成式AI提出的独特挑战：物理约束是硬性要求而非可选的感知合理性。作者提出，面向物理约束领域的生成模型必须从架构层面"由构造保证物理一致性"（physics-informed by construction），而非依赖事后过滤，并系统梳理了物理信息扩散模型、PDE约束变分模型、神经算子先验等新兴工具包，提出了物理保真度基准、可微分模拟器基础设施和多模态基础模型的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造是一个物理约束极其严格的领域——光刻掩模、版图、缺陷数据和工艺配方必须同时满足光刻、输运、反应和器件物理等多重硬约束。物理上无效的样本不仅是低质量的，而是完全不可用的。当前生成式AI多服务于感知合理性（如图像、文本），而半导体制造暴露了一个更广泛的科学计算挑战：如何让生成模型在约束物理领域从架构层面就内嵌物理规律，而非事后纠错。

Method: 本文采用Perspective（前瞻性综述）文体，系统梳理了面向约束物理领域的生成式AI架构工具包，包括：物理信息扩散模型、PDE约束变分模型、神经算子先验、遵从守恒律的生成网络等。进一步，将这些方法与可微分光刻、TCAD、工艺仿真和自主实验建立联系，识别出生成模型与物理仿真器之间的四类集成模式。

Result: 作者提出了一个以三点为核心的研究议程：(1) 物理保真度基准测试；(2) 可微分模拟器基础设施；(3) 面向物理设计与制造的多模态基础模型。核心分析性论断是：在物理有效性作为成功的刚性标准时，架构层面强制执行物理约束的方法应优于事后过滤方法，而晶圆厂（fab）是这一区分最为尖锐的场景。

Conclusion: 本文的中心论点具有分析性而非修辞性：在物理有效性是绑定性成功准则的领域，"由构造保证物理一致性"的架构应在性能上超越"事后过滤"的方法，半导体制造为这一论断提供了最清晰的验证场域。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to propose designs, data, and control actions for physical systems, yet many such systems are governed by hard physical constraints rather than by perceptual plausibility. Semiconductor manufacturing provides a demanding test case: generated masks, layouts, synthetic defect data, and process recipes must obey lithography, transport, reaction, and device-physics constraints, because physically invalid samples are not merely low quality but unusable.
