This Perspective argues that semiconductor manufacturing exposes a broader computational-science challenge, namely that generative AI for constrained physical domains must be physics-informed by construction, not corrected only through post-hoc filtering. We survey the emerging architectural toolkit, including physics-informed diffusion, PDE-constrained variational models, neural-operator priors, and conservation-law-respecting generative networks, and show how it connects to differentiable lithography, TCAD, process simulation, and autonomous experimentation. We identify four integration patterns between generative models and physics-based simulators, and we propose a research agenda centered on physics-fidelity benchmarks, differentiable simulator infrastructure, and multimodal foundation models for physical design and manufacturing. The central claim is analytical rather than rhetorical: where physical validity is the binding criterion of success, architectures that enforce it by construction should be expected to outperform those that filter for it after the fact, and the fab is the setting where this distinction is sharpest.

</details>


### [211] [Signed Compression Progress on a Sealed Audit is Goodhart-Resistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11417)
*Ayush Mittal,Dhruv Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：以固定密封审计损失的有符号下降作为内在奖励时，累积奖励恰好等于端点审计改进，从而提供了一种"可信"的压缩进度信号。该理论在有限审计面板下同样成立，并给出了精确的误报预算。实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，"压缩进度"被直觉性地视为内在动机的有效来源——智能体因其世界模型预测能力提升而获得奖励，且该奖励被认为是"可信的"（只为真正的学习付费）。然而，这一主张从未被精确定义和严格证明。本文旨在填补这一空白，形式化地定义什么条件下内在奖励是可信的，并找出其失效模式。

Method: 作者将内在奖励定义为固定密封审计损失的有符号下降 r_t = ℓ(θ_{t-1}) - ℓ(θ_t)。核心洞察是累积奖励可通过 telescoping 精确等于端点审计改进。对于有限审计面板，引入均匀审计偏差 Δ_n(F, δ) 控制误报，给出 horizon-free 的有限样本界。理论分析识别了四种失效模式（奖励裁剪、流泄漏、可复用面板上的高容量模型、神经网络类导致 Δ_n 空洞），并使用 Lean 4 形式化了核心结构。实验在 ARC-TGI 网格变换生成器上进行，采用自适应 holdout 攻击验证理论。

Result: 理论上证明：累积奖励不会超过真实审计改进加上 2Δ_n 的误报预算，且该界与时间范围无关（horizon-free）。实验方面，有限审计偏差按 n^{-0.527} 缩放；有符号进度能够抵抗 clip-farming、流泄漏和 noisy-TV 好奇心等常见问题；朴素的复用审计可被黑盒标量反馈利用，而标准发布防御可将攻击控制在 2Δ_n 阈值以下。

Conclusion: 密封审计上的有符号压缩进度是一种可靠的会计信号，能够真实反映智能体的学习改进。该框架为内在动机研究提供了严格的理论基础，同时明确指出了不可信设计的具体模式，为安全、可信的强化学习内在奖励设计提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Compression progress is a long-standing proposal for intrinsic motivation: reward an agent when its world model becomes better at predicting or compressing experience. The folk claim is that this reward is "credible" because it is paid only for learning. We make this precise and prove it. If intrinsic reward is the signed decrease of a fixed sealed-audit loss, r_t = E(theta_{t-1}) - E(theta_t), then cumulative reward telescopes exactly to endpoint audit improvement, so no policy can push reward up indefinitely while true audit performance stagnates or degrades. For finite audit panels the same result holds with a sharp false-positive budget: cumulative empirical reward is at most true audit improvement plus 2 Delta_n(F, delta), the uniform audit deviation of the model class. This is horizon-free: adaptivity over time costs nothing once the sealed panel uniformly controls the class.
  The theorem also identifies the failure modes: the guarantee disappears if progress is clipped, scored on the agent's own stream, exposed to a high-capacity model on a reusable panel, or applied to a neural class that makes Delta_n vacuous. We give a Lean 4 mechanization of the structural core (telescoping, the finite-audit bound, finite Gibbs, and the entropy floor) and an experiment suite on ARC-TGI grid-transformation generators with adaptive holdout attacks. Experiments confirm the theory: finite-audit deviation scales as n^{-0.527}; signed progress resists clip-farming, stream leakage, and noisy-TV curiosity; naive reusable audits are exploitable by black-box scalar feedback, while standard release defenses keep the attack below the 2 Delta_n threshold. Signed compression progress on a sealed audit is an accounting signal of genuine improvement.

</details>


### [212] [Mechanical Field Networks: Structured Neural Dynamics for Multivariate Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11251)
*Xingji Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MF-Net 是一种循环动力学模型，将所有变量表示为共享场状态，通过可学习的"关系-运动"机械转换实现联合演化。模型在预测的同时可直接读出交互结构，在 Lorenz-96 等测试中展现出强预测性能和高精度的耦合结构恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多变量动力学系统建模方法要么需要预先指定交互结构，要么将结构隐含在学习的动力学中而无法显式读出。作者希望构建一个既能灵活学习状态转移、又能显式揭示变量间交互结构的统一框架。

Method: 提出 MF-Net：将每个变量赋予场分量，所有场分量构成共享场状态；通过可学习的"关系律"（relation law）来参数化状态转移，具体包括学习关系矩阵以塑造状态依赖的流（flows）、场响应（field responses）和运动趋势（motion tendencies），三者共同推进场状态前向演化。该机械式（mechanical）设计的核心是"从关系到运动"的组织方式，使得同一组内部量同时服务于预测和结构读出。

Result: 在已知定律的交互系统、混沌基准、真实神经记录和生态时间序列上，MF-Net 在短中期预测上具有竞争力，同时可读出可解释的交互结构。在 40 维 Lorenz-96 测试台上，8 步 R² 达 0.798±0.018；五个种子下，学习的关系矩阵以局部/非局部强度比 19.80±1.00 和 Precision@K=1.000±0.000 恢复局部耦合支撑集。

Conclusion: MF-Net 提供了一种结构可读的动力学建模框架：关系通过前向演化训练得到，在真实数据中可在适当的观测限制下被解释为功能性预测耦合，实现了预测性能与结构可解释性的统一。

Abstract: Many multivariate dynamical systems are observed only through trajectories, leaving the mechanisms governing their joint dynamics hidden. Existing approaches can impose interpretable dynamics or learn flexible state transitions, yet the resulting interaction structure is typically either specified in advance or left implicit within the learned dynamics. We introduce MF-Net, a recurrent dynamical model that represents all variables in a shared field state and updates this state through a learned relation law. Each variable carries a field component, and these components evolve jointly through a learnable mechanical transition. Here, mechanical refers to the relation-to-motion organization of the transition, where learned relations shape state-dependent flows, field responses, and motion tendencies that move the field state forward. The resulting structure is part of the rollout itself: learned relations influence how the field moves, and the same internal quantities support both forecasting and structural readout. Across known-law interaction systems, chaotic benchmarks, real neural recordings, and ecological time series, MF-Net achieves competitive short- and medium-horizon forecasting while retaining inspectable structural readout. On the 40-dimensional Lorenz--96 testbed, MF-Net achieves an eight-step $R^2$ of $0.798\pm0.018$; across five seeds, its learned relation matrix recovers the local coupling support with a local/nonlocal strength ratio of $19.80\pm1.00$ and Precision@$K$ of $1.000\pm0.000$. MF-Net provides a structure-readable dynamical modeling framework in which learned relations are trained through forward evolution and, on real data, interpreted as functional predictive couplings under appropriate observational limits.

</details>


### [213] [CRUMB: Efficient Prior Fitted Network Inference via Distributionally Matched Context Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11473)
*Jamie Heredge,Mattia J. Villani,Pranav Deshpande,Akshay Seshadri,Niraj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRUMB 是一种三阶段推理包装器，通过聚类测试查询、基于最小化最大均值差异（MMD）贪心选择分布匹配的训练子集，然后在缩减后的上下文上执行精确PFN推理，从而解决PFN自注意力机制对大训练集二次方扩展的推理瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN架构中自注意力机制的平方复杂度使得在大规模训练集上推理成本过高，需要一种无需重新训练、架构无关的上下文选择策略来降低计算开销，同时保持预测性能。

Method: CRUMB分为三个阶段：(1) 对测试查询进行聚类；(2) 对每个聚类，通过贪心最小化最大均值差异（MMD）来选择一个与测试分布匹配的小规模训练子集；(3) 在每个缩减后的上下文中运行精确的PFN推理。该方法与PFN架构无关，且无需重新训练。

Result: 在51个数据集的TabArena基准上，跨三种PFN架构（TabPFNv2、TabICLv1、TabICLv2）进行评估，CRUMB优于同类最先进的上下文选择策略。此外，CRUMB对协变量漂移具有鲁棒性，因为MMD最小化步骤自然地使训练上下文分布与当前测试批次分布对齐。

Conclusion: CRUMB为PFN在大规模表格数据上的高效推理提供了一种有效的解决方案，其分布匹配的上下文选择策略不仅降低了计算成本，还增强了对分布漂移的鲁棒性，具有良好的架构通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Prior-fitted networks (PFNs) are a promising class of tabular foundation models that perform in-context learning, whereby the entire labelled training set is supplied as context, and predictions for test queries are produced in a single forward pass. However, the quadratically scaling self-attention mechanism in many PFN architectures makes inference prohibitive for very large training datasets. We propose CRUMB (Clustered Retrieval Using Minimised-MMD Batching), a three-stage inference wrapper that (i) clusters the test queries, (ii) selects a small, distributionally matched training subset for each cluster by greedily minimising the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), and (iii) runs exact PFN inference on each reduced-context batch. CRUMB is architecture-agnostic and requires no retraining. On the 51-dataset TabArena benchmark, evaluated across three PFN architectures (TabPFNv2, TabICLv1, TabICLv2), we show that CRUMB outperforms similar state-of-the-art context selection strategies. We also show that CRUMB is resilient to covariate drift, as the MMD-minimisation step naturally helps align the training context distribution to match the current test batch distributions.

</details>


### [214] [Bernstein-Schur Kernels: Random Features by Sketched Modulation and Radial Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11255)
*Taha Bouhsine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对 Bernstein–Schur 核的随机特征构造方法，该方法同时随机化有限调制因子和完全单调径向因子：前者通过 sketching 处理，后者通过一维 Bernstein–Widder 尺度采样并结合高斯随机傅里叶特征。文章给出了无偏性、方差解析、算子范数界及谱稳定性等理论保证，并以有偏 yat 核为典型实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernstein–Schur 核介于平移不变核与点积核之间，兼具有限特征核与完全单调平移不变核的乘积结构，因此既不能直接应用 Bochner 采样，也无法直接使用多项式 sketching。现有随机特征方法无法整体处理此类非平稳核，需要一种能同时处理两个因子的统一随机特征构造方案。

Method: 作者提出对 Bernstein–Schur 核进行双重随机化：(1) 对有限调制因子使用 sketching（以 m 控制 sketch 规模）；(2) 对完全单调径向因子，通过 Bernstein–Widder 定理将其表示为单变量尺度上的混合，从中抽取 D 个尺度，再对每个尺度施加高斯随机傅里叶特征（d 维频率）。最终特征维度为 Dm，远小于精确调制特征的 O(d²) 规模。在 m→∞ 的极限下，调制变为精确，此时可分析无偏性、方差及算子范数界；而有限 m 时，通过条件化 sketch，估计量继承内在维度算子范数保证并附加一个可通过 m 独立调节的 sketch 项。

Result: 主要理论结果包括：(1) m→∞ 时推荐 flat 估计量的无偏性及精确方差公式；(2) 期望矩阵-Bernstein 算子范数界，由核与调制 Gram 矩阵的顶端特征值及内在维度控制，优于基于 N·max 逐元素路径的粗糙界，并配有匹配的高概率尾部；(3) 确定性的相对谱核岭回归稳定性结果；(4) 双重随机化估计量通过条件化 sketch，继承内在维度算子范数保证，外加一个可由 m 独立调节的 sketch 加性项。在径向特征预算固定时，每个尺度使用一个频率在方差上是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Bernstein–Schur 核族提供了首个统一的随机特征构造与理论分析框架，打破了平移不变核与点积核之间的方法壁垒。以 yat 核为典型应用，通过有限差分可覆盖逆多二次核（IMQ），且 IMQ 谱采样器自然地作为径向混合出现。理论结果表明，该方法的逼近质量由核的内在维度而非原始数据维度控制，具有优良的统计与计算效率。

Abstract: Bernstein--Schur kernels are products of a finite-feature kernel (one with an explicit finite-dimensional feature map) and a completely monotone shift-invariant kernel: nonstationary kernels that fall between the shift-invariant and dot-product templates random features usually exploit, so in general neither Bochner sampling nor polynomial sketching applies to the full kernel directly. We give one random-feature construction for the whole class that \emph{randomizes both factors: it sketches the finite modulation and randomizes the completely monotone radial factor, sampling the latter's one-dimensional Bernstein--Widder scale and then applying Gaussian random Fourier features (whose frequency is still $d$-dimensional). The feature dimension is then $Dm$, set by the sketch size $m$ and the radial-draw count $D$, free of the $O(d^2)$ size of the exact modulation feature. Keeping the modulation \emph{exact} is the analyzable limit ($m\to\infty$): there we prove unbiasedness, an exact variance for the recommended flat estimator, an expected matrix-Bernstein operator-norm bound (with a matching high-probability tail) controlled by the top eigenvalues of the kernel and modulation Gram matrices together with an intrinsic dimension rather than the crude $N\max_{ij}$ entrywise route, and a deterministic relative-spectral kernel-ridge stability result. By conditioning on the sketch, the doubly-randomized estimator inherits the same intrinsic-dimension operator-norm guarantee plus a single additive sketch term, tunable by $m$ independently of $D$. The motivating instance is the biased $yat$-kernel $k_{yat,b}(w,x)=(w^\top x+b)^2/(\|w-x\|^2+\varepsilon)$, $b\ge0$, whose family span contains the inverse-multiquadric kernel by finite differences in $b$; for it the radial mixture is the IMQ spectral sampler, and one frequency per scale is variance-optimal at a fixed radial-feature budget.

</details>


### [215] [Range-Aware Bayesian Optimization for Discovering Diverse Designs within Target Property Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11574)
*Shengli Jiang,Jason Wu,Charles M. Schroeder,Michael A. Webb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向范围感知的贝叶斯优化框架，其采集函数直接对候选点满足目标范围的后验概率进行评分，能有效恢复多个满足规格的多样化设计，在基准测试和聚合物合成、寡聚物光学吸收等实际案例中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多材料与产品设计中，理想候选方案落在可接受范围内即可，而非追求单一最优值。同时，恢复多个满足规格的不同解具有实际价值（如成本、可加工性、鲁棒性等难以直接编码为目标函数的因素）。现有BO方法多聚焦于单点最优，难以高效处理范围规格和多解发现需求。

Method: 提出范围感知贝叶斯优化（Range-Aware BO）框架：采集函数直接对候选点满足目标区间的后验概率进行建模和评分，而非传统的期望改进等单点最优导向的指标。该框架自然扩展到在共享候选空间上并行追踪多个不同规格目标。

Result: 在基准测试任务中，范围感知采集函数始终能恢复比标准BO基线和近期目标搜寻方法数量更多、多样性更丰富的有效设计。在两个实际案例中验证了其实用性：(1)聚合物合成的反应条件优化；(2)基于量子化学计算支持的序列定义寡聚物发现，用于特定光学吸收带。

Conclusion: 范围感知BO为规格驱动设计提供了实用且样本高效的基础方法，尤其适用于设计灵活性和解多样性至关重要的场景。

Abstract: In many materials and product design problems, desirable candidates exhibit properties that fall within an acceptable range rather than achieve a single optimum. Recovering multiple, distinct solutions that satisfy such specifications is also practically valuable, as some candidates may be preferred for reasons of cost, processability, or robustness that are difficult to encode directly in an objective function. Here, we develop a range-aware Bayesian optimization (BO) framework in which the acquisition function directly scores the posterior probability that a candidate satisfies a target range. The framework naturally extends to parallel pursuit of multiple distinct specifications over a shared candidate space. Across benchmark tasks, range-aware acquisition consistently recovers larger and more diverse sets of valid designs than standard BO baselines and recent goal-seeking methods. Its utility is further demonstrated in two practically motivated design case studies involving optimizing reaction conditions for polymer synthesis and sequence-defined oligomer discovery for prescribed optical absorption bands, supported by quantum chemical calculations. These results suggest that range-aware BO can provide a practical and sample-efficient foundation for specification-driven design, particularly when design flexibility and solution diversity are important considerations.

</details>


### [216] [Loss Landscape Diagnosis for Gradient-Based Gray-Scott System Inversion: Disentangling the Roles of PINN Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11258)
*Yan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过直接对展开的Gray-Scott模拟进行反向传播来恢复参数，发现损失景观中存在平坦高原和陡峭悬崖的病理结构，这源于分岔边界。以该最小设置作为PINN的消融实验，作者解耦了各组件角色：残差损失本身即可避免此病理，而神经网络仅用于补全观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应扩散系统的梯度反演通常依赖代理模型或PINN，而通过PDE结构本身直接反向传播这一最直接路径却鲜有人探索。作者旨在通过这一直接路径诊断其失败原因，并借此解耦PINN各组件的作用。

Method: 以Gray-Scott反应扩散系统为对象，直接对展开的稳态模拟进行反向传播以恢复参数，不使用代理模型或神经网络增强。通过绘制损失景观直接定位优化失败原因，并将此最小设置作为PINN的消融实验，分析残差损失和神经网络各自的作用。

Result: 直接反向传播优化无法收敛；损失景观呈现平坦高原（无梯度信号）与陡峭悬崖的几何结构，悬崖恰好对齐分岔边界，且此结构在不同损失函数和梯度路径下均出现。残差损失在神经网络固定时对PDE参数呈二次形式，产生平滑景观，隐含编码了所有初始条件下的完整PDE动力学，从而单独避免了该病理。神经网络无法修复不适定的参数子空间，仅用于补全观测数据。

Conclusion: PINN中残差损失与神经网络之间存在明确分工：残差损失提供平滑优化景观，神经网络仅补全数据。这一发现为PINN类方法的设计提供了具体启示，并提出了关于"增加维度何时真正有效"的更广泛启发式。

Abstract: Gradient-based inversion of reaction-diffusion systems is typically approached via surrogate models or physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), while the most direct route, backpropagation through the PDE's structure itself, has largely been avoided. We pursue this direct route as a diagnostic probe, backpropagating a steady-state loss through unrolled Gray-Scott simulation to recover its parameters, with no surrogate or neural-network augmentation. Optimization fails to converge, and plotting the landscape directly locates the failure in its geometry -- flat plateaus with no gradient signal, bounded by sharp cliffs that align with bifurcation boundaries -- a structure that recurs across loss functions and is inherited however the gradients are routed to parameters. Reading this minimal setup as an ablation of PINN, we disentangle each component's role: with the neural network fixed, the residual loss is quadratic in the PDE parameters and yields a smooth landscape, so it alone already avoids the pathology, by implicitly encoding the full PDE dynamics across all initial conditions. The neural network, for its part, cannot repair an ill-posed parameter subspace, and so serves only to complete the observed data -- a division of labor not previously made explicit. These findings carry concrete design implications for PINN-type methods and a broader heuristic on when added dimensions actually help.

</details>


### [217] [Tree-Structured Orthonormal Decomposition of the Aitchison Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11646)
*Daisuke Yamada,Qijun Zhang,Travis Pence,Barbara B. Bendlin,Federico Rey,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyILR 提出一种与任意树拓扑对齐的 Aitchison 切空间规范正交分解方法，为成分数据提供完整坐标系统，在微生物组和单细胞基准上展现出稳定、可解释的多尺度特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成分数据（compositional data）广泛存在于生态学、地球化学、基因组学等领域，特征常具有已知的层次结构（如分类树、系统发育树、本体）。然而现有方法要么忽略该结构、丢弃 Aitchison 几何、仅支持二叉树，或产生不完整坐标系统。

Method: 提出 PolyILR 方法：(1) 在每个内部节点定义加权局部几何以捕获完整分支结构；(2) 将局部几何提升为全局规范正交基，使每个坐标对应特定的树位置。

Result: 在微生物组和单细胞基准测试中，PolyILR 产生稳定、可解释的特征，并支持多尺度树分辨率的推断。同时建立了与 softmax 分类器的新理论联系，提示在概率建模中的潜在应用。

Conclusion: PolyILR 是第一个能与任意树拓扑对齐的完整 Aitchison 切空间规范正交分解，为层次化成分数据分析提供了统一且原则性的框架，兼具几何保真性、可解释性和计算实用性。

Abstract: Compositional data -- vectors encoding relative proportions -- arise across scientific domains, including ecology, geochemistry, and genomics. The features in these data often come with known hierarchical structure (e.g., taxonomies, phylogenies, ontologies), yet existing methods either ignore this structure, discard the intrinsic Aitchison geometry, are designed for binary trees, or yield incomplete coordinate systems. We describe PolyILR, a canonical orthonormal decomposition of the Aitchison tangent space aligned with any tree topology. Our construction defines a weighted local geometry at each internal node capturing full branching structure, then lifts these to a global orthonormal basis where every coordinate corresponds to a specific tree location. On microbiome and single-cell benchmarks, PolyILR yields stable, interpretable features and enables inference at multiscale tree resolution. We also establish a novel theoretical connection to softmax classifiers, suggesting possible applications to probabilistic modeling.

</details>


### [218] [PermDoRA -- Understanding Adapter Interference in Language Models: Limits of Parameter-Space Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11262)
*Gowtham Sivaramakrishnan,Sarvesha Kumar Kombaiah Seetha,Kishan Gupta Balaji,Santhosh Baradwaj Vaduvur Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了“适配器组合中的干扰源于线性参数更新的重叠”这一假说，通过在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Mistral-7B上使用DoRA-RBAC框架，对比欧氏合并与黎曼启发的几何感知合并策略，发现几何感知合并相比标准平均并无一致优势，且角度对齐与正交性对组合性能预测能力弱，表明适配器干扰并非主要由参数空间几何决定，而是与共享非线性表征中的交互一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的访问控制需要模块化机制以实现领域特定行为，同时避免跨域干扰。现有假说认为适配器组合时的干扰源于线性参数更新的重叠，因此强制正交性或方向独立性应能提升多域性能。本文旨在严格检验这一假说是否成立。

Method: 采用DoRA-RBAC（基于权重分解低秩适应的层次化适配器组合框架），在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Mistral-7B上，跨GPQA、PubMedQA、SimpleQA、WMDP四个QA基准，对比传统欧氏合并与基于归一化方向平均逼近Fréchet均值的黎曼启发几何感知合并策略。同时进行诊断分析，评估角度对齐与正交性对组合性能的预测能力。

Result: 单域性能与LoRA相当；在多域设置下，几何感知合并相比标准平均无一致优势。诊断分析表明，适配器更新的角度对齐和正交性是组合性能的弱预测因子。

Conclusion: 适配器干扰并非主要由参数空间几何主导，而更可能与共享非线性表征中的交互一致。这对基于参数空间正交性假设的模块化LLM访问控制设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Access control in large language models (LLMs) requires modular mechanisms to enable domain-specific behavior without retraining or cross-domain interference. A common hypothesis is that interference during adapter composition arises from overlap in linear parameter updates, suggesting that enforcing orthogonality or directional independence should improve multi-domain performance. We test this hypothesis using DoRA-RBAC, a hierarchical adapter composition framework based on weight-decomposed low-rank adaptation. We compare conventional Euclidean merging with a geometry-aware Riemannian-inspired merging strategy that approximates the Frechet mean via normalized directional averaging across multiple QA benchmarks (GPQA, PubMedQA, SimpleQA, WMDP) on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B. Our results show that while single-domain performance matches LoRA, geometry-aware merging provides no consistent advantage over standard averaging in multi-domain settings.Diagnostic analysis further reveals that angular alignment and orthogonality of adapter updates are weak predictors of composition performance. These findings suggest that adapter interference is not governed primarily by parameter-space geometry, but is instead consistent with interactions in shared nonlinear representations.

</details>


### [219] [Capacity-Constrained Online Convex Optimization with Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11711)
*Alexander Ryabchenko,Idan Attias,Daniel M. Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限追踪容量约束下的延迟在线凸优化（OCO）问题。与以往假设所有待处理轮次均可追踪不同，作者引入硬容量约束（最多追踪C个待处理轮次），并提出了半先知模型（semi-clairvoyant）来描述延迟信息。通过将问题归约为"延迟加权OCO"并结合随机化调度器，作者提出Delayed-Weighted FTRL及其bandit变体，给出了凸损失和强凸损失下的一阶反馈与bandit反馈的遗憾界。结果表明，一阶反馈下容量C = Ω(log T)即可恢复标准延迟OCO速率；bandit反馈下遗憾界按(1 + σ_max/C)的幂次退化，在C < σ_max时仍保持次线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统中追踪资源有限，反馈从未追踪轮次永久丢失。现有延迟在线学习工作要么假设无容量约束（可追踪所有待处理轮次），要么采用过于理想化的全知延迟假设。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究硬容量约束下的延迟OCO，并建模更贴近经典延迟在线学习的延迟信息获取方式。

Method: 1）引入半先知模型（semi-clairvoyant）：学习者在线观察延迟到期事件，而非在预测时已知延迟，与经典无约束延迟设定一致。2）问题归约：通过随机化调度器（以一定概率追踪各轮次）并对观测值进行重要性加权，将容量约束问题归约为"延迟加权OCO"问题。3）基学习器：提出并分析Delayed-Weighted FTRL及其bandit版本，建立刻画时变权重与延迟反馈交互作用的遗憾界。4）组合分析：将调度器与基学习器组合，导出凸损失和强凸损失下的一阶/bandit反馈遗憾界。

Result: 1）一阶反馈下，容量C = Ω(log T)即可恢复标准延迟OCO速率（仅差对数因子）。2）bandit反馈下，遗憾界为Õ((1 + σ_max/C)^{ρ} · √T)形式，其中ρ ∈ {1,2}取决于损失函数性质。当C ≥ σ_max时恢复到无容量约束的速率；当C < σ_max时遗憾界以(1 + σ_max/C)的幂次因子退化，但仍保持次线性。3）提供了凸损失和强凸损失下的具体遗憾上界。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出容量约束下延迟OCO的遗憾保证。半先知模型在现实性和可处理性间取得良好平衡。核心技术贡献在于通过随机化调度加重要性加权的归约框架，以及Delayed-Weighted FTRL对时变权重与延迟反馈交互的精细分析。未来可探索更一般的反馈结构和自适应调度策略。

Abstract: Online learning with delayed feedback typically assumes that the learner can track all pending rounds until their feedback arrives. In practice, tracking resources are finite, and feedback from untracked rounds is permanently lost. In this paper, we study delayed online convex optimization (OCO) under a hard capacity constraint, where at most $C$ pending rounds can be tracked at any time. To model delay information, we introduce a semi-clairvoyant model that refines the clairvoyant assumption from prior work: rather than requiring delays to be known at prediction time, the learner observes delay expirations online, consistent with the classical unconstrained delayed setting. Our approach proceeds via a reduction to a novel ``delayed and weighted'' OCO problem, using a scheduler that randomizes tracking decisions and importance-weights the resulting observations. For this base problem, we propose and analyze Delayed-Weighted FTRL and its bandit analogue, establishing regret bounds that explicitly characterize the interaction between time-varying weights and delayed feedback. Combining these base learners with our schedulers yields the first regret guarantees for capacity-constrained OCO under convex and strongly convex losses, for both first-order and bandit feedback. For first-order feedback, capacity $C = Ω(\log T)$ suffices to recover standard delayed OCO rates up to logarithmic factors. For bandit feedback, the regret rates are modulated by powers of $(1 + σ_{\text{max}}/C)$, where $σ_{\text{max}}$ is the maximum number of pending observations at any time. This allows the regret bound to degrade gracefully when $C < σ_{\text{max}}$, while remaining sublinear.

</details>


### [220] [Seeing Before Colliding: Anticipatory Safe RL with Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11266)
*Samuel Tetteh,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有的受限强化学习（constrained-RL）算法的成本信号是反应性的——仅在碰撞开始后才发出非零成本，这在高速场景下为时已晚。本文提出VLM-Safe-RL框架，将冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）作为预期性成本项集成到CMDP的拉格朗日乘子更新中，使智能体能够提前预判风险。实验表明，该方法在Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2任务上是唯一同时保持实质回报且将成本控制在预算内的配置，并能泛化到MetaDrive Medium等环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有constrained-RL算法中的成本信号几乎总是反应式的：仿真器仅在碰撞开始后才发出非零成本，PPO-Lagrangian的拉格朗日乘子也仅在回合预算超支后才增长。在赛车速度下，碰撞是瞬时且不可逆的，任何等待成本累积的安全机制在结构上都太迟了。因此需要一个能提前预测风险的预期性（anticipatory）安全机制。

Method: 提出VLM-Safe-RL框架，包含四个组件：(1) Decoupled Dual-Path CLIP——将奖励路径和成本路径解耦，各自独立处理以尊重CMDP的因子分解结构；(2) VLM-Lagrange——一种增强的拉格朗日乘子更新机制，将逐步VLM成本作为预期性项纳入；(3) Confidence Gating——基于CLIP边距的逻辑噪声模型推导出贝叶斯最优权重，对VLM预测的置信度进行门控；(4) VLMPPOLag——整合前三者的完整算法。核心思想是将冻结的VLM前向传播作为预期性成本信号，在拉格朗日乘子更新中提前反映潜在风险。

Result: 在Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2任务上（5个种子，100万步，预算d_lim=25），VLMPPOLag+Conf是唯一同时满足两个条件的配置：保持实质回报（J_r≈40）且在多数种子上将成本控制在预算内；五个约束感知基线（PPOLag、CPO、CPPOPID、CPO-CLG、PPOLag-RND）各自至少在一个要求上失败。在MetaDrive Medium上，灾难率从41%降至26%（95% bootstrap CI [-26,-5] pp）。在Bullet Safety-Gym上表现方向一致，但在MetaDrive Easy/Hard及Qwen2-VL骨干上未成功，Hard上的失败源于拉格朗日正则化病理而非VLM信号本身。

Conclusion: 首次将冻结VLM信号用作CMDP拉格朗日更新中的预期性成本项。该方法通过前瞻性的视觉-语言风险预判弥补了传统反应式成本信号的固有时滞缺陷，在高速安全关键任务中展现了有效性，但拉格朗日正则化机制仍需改进以处理更高难度场景。

Abstract: The cost signal that constrained-RL algorithms optimize against is almost always reactive: the simulator emits a non-zero cost only after a collision has begun, and the Lagrange multiplier of PPO-Lagrangian grows only after the episode budget has been exceeded. At race speeds, where collisions are instantaneous and irreversible, any safety mechanism that waits for cost to accumulate is structurally too late. We present VLM-Safe-RL, a framework that integrates a frozen vision-language model into the CMDP Lagrangian update as an anticipatory cost term. The framework comprises four contributions: (i) Decoupled Dual-Path CLIP, independent reward/cost paths that respect the CMDP's factorization; (ii) VLM-Lagrange, an augmented multiplier update that incorporates a per-step VLM cost as an anticipatory term; (iii) Confidence Gating, a Bayes-optimal weight derived from a logistic noise model on the CLIP margin; and (iv) VLMPPOLag, the composed algorithm. On Safety-Gymnasium FormulaOne L2, our principal evaluation ($n{=}5$ seeds, $10^{6}$ steps, budget $d_{\text{lim}}{=}25$) VLMPPOLag$+$Conf is the only configuration in our default budget comparison that simultaneously retains substantive return ($J_r{\approx}40$) and holds cost within budget on a majority of seeds; the five constraint-aware baselines (PPOLag, CPO, CPPOPID, CPO-CLG, PPOLag-RND) each fail at least one requirement. The mechanism generalizes to held-out MetaDrive Medium (catastrophe rate $41\%{\to}26\%$, 95\% bootstrap CI $[-26,-5]$\,pp) and shows directionally consistent transfer to Bullet Safety-Gym; we report honestly where it does not (MetaDrive Easy/Hard, Qwen2-VL backbone) and trace the Hard failure to a Lagrangian-regulation pathology rather than the VLM signal itself. To our knowledge, this is the first work to use frozen VLM signals as an anticipatory cost term inside the CMDP Lagrangian update.

</details>


### [221] [Online Shift Detection and Conformal Adaptation for Deployed Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11949)
*Jun Wen Leong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在线监控系统，用于检测安全分类器的分布偏移，并通过校准的序贯统计量触发检测，随后利用共形 abstention 层调整决策阈值以恢复目标错误率。在因子实验中，系统实现了86.6%的有效检测率，并揭示了重要性权重在高维嵌入空间中的崩溃现象及降维修复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后的安全分类器在真实世界中会遭遇分布偏移（如对抗攻击、越狱提示等），导致错误率上升。现有方法缺乏对分布偏移的在线检测与自适应纠错能力，因此需要一种能实时监控偏移并在检测后自动恢复目标错误率的系统。

Method: 系统包含两大组件：(1) 基于校准的序贯统计量（calibrated sequential statistics）进行在线分布偏移检测；(2) 检测触发后，使用加权共形预测（weighted conformal prediction）构建abstention层，自适应调整决策阈值以恢复目标错误率ε=0.1。实验采用预注册的因子设计（4分类器×5偏移条件×20种子×2窗口大小，共800个单元格），覆盖三种偏移机制：合成偏移、真实时间越狱攻击、GCG对抗攻击。还引入PCA降维（至32维）来分析高维嵌入空间中重要性权重崩溃问题。

Result: 系统在800个单元格中实现86.6%有效检测（693/800），平均检测延迟39.5步。三种偏移机制下检测率分别为：合成偏移86.6%，真实越狱85%（17/20），GCG攻击同样有效。加权共形预测在DeBERTa上恢复最多39个百分点覆盖率（ESS=46/300），但其他分类器因logistic密度比估计在高维嵌入空间中完美分离源/目标域，导致重要性权重全部被裁剪至下限而崩溃（ESS≈300）。PCA降至32维可打破崩溃，Llama Guard恢复33pp，ShieldGemma恢复21pp。方差分解显示分类器（η²=0.243）、偏移类型（η²=0.237）及其交互（η²=0.185）对检测延迟方差均有显著贡献（p<0.001），表明需要针对每个分类器分别构建监控方案。

Conclusion: 该系统能有效在线检测安全分类器的分布偏移并通过共形abstention恢复目标错误率，但加权共形预测在高维嵌入空间中存在重要性权重崩溃的固有问题，PCA降维可部分缓解。检测延迟受分类器类型和偏移类型的显著交互影响，因此实际部署需为每个分类器定制监控参数。

Abstract: We present an online monitoring system for distributional shift in deployed safety classifiers, using calibrated sequential statistics to detect when a classifier has moved out of distribution. Upon detection, a conformal abstention layer adapts decision thresholds to recover a target error rate epsilon=0.1. In a pre-registered factorial evaluation (4 classifiers x 5 shift conditions x 20 seeds x 2 window sizes, 800 cells), the system achieves 86.6% valid detection (693/800, 95% CI [84.1%, 88.8%]) with mean latency of 39.5 steps. Detection holds across three ground-truth regimes: synthetic onset (86.6%), real temporal jailbreaks (85%, 17/20), and GCG adversarial attacks. Weighted conformal prediction recovers up to 39 pp of lost coverage for DeBERTa (ESS=46/300) but collapses for all other classifiers (ESS~300): logistic density ratio estimation achieves perfect source/target separability in high-dimensional embedding spaces, clipping all importance weights to the floor. DeBERTa shows a gradient from effective correction (paraphrase, ESS=46) to near-total collapse (adversarial suffix, ESS=206). PCA to 32 dimensions breaks the collapse, recovering 33 pp for Llama Guard and 21 pp for ShieldGemma. Variance decomposition reveals classifier (eta^2=0.243), shift type (eta^2=0.237), and their interaction (eta^2=0.185) all contribute substantially to detection latency variance (all p<0.001), indicating per-classifier monitoring profiles are necessary.

</details>


### [222] [A prior-free blind detection of information leakage from model predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11267)
*Laurence A. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了仅从模型预测和结果判断数据泄露的决策理论框架，将泄露分为误校准、广泛校准和确定性三类，并证明：（1）重新校准的泄露在预测分布上无法与诚实模型区分；（2）近确定性泄露会留下独特的单位纯度头部特征，可被无先验检测。在UK Biobank上的验证显示检测下限Δc*≈0.007，低于此限的残差泄露既不可检测也不影响结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据泄露是机器学习科学中最主要的可重复性失败原因，但现有检测工具依赖训练代码、外部数据或领域专业知识，无法在审计者最常持有的工件——模型输出——上直接操作。因此需要发展仅从预测和结果就能判断泄露的理论和方法。

Method: 作者提出决策理论框架，将泄露诊断定义为预测风险/结果分布的泛函，通过阈值加权参数化，与proper scoring rules和决策曲线分析相关联。框架内证明了两条核心定理：一是"尖锐不可能性"——重新校准后与诚实模型校准度和区分度匹配的泄露，在预测的任意函数下都无法区分；二是"泄露无法隐藏的内容"——近确定性泄露会产生持续的单元纯度头部，任何非确定性结果的合法预测器都无法制造，从而构成无先验检验。由此将泄露组织为误校准、广泛校准和确定性三类，每类配备对应的检测器和失败模式。

Result: 在UK Biobank上使用时窗合并症泄露（已知分级严重程度）进行验证，测量到该终点的检测下限Δc*≈0.007，低于此限的残差泄露从输出不可检测且太小不足以改变结论。数值下限是队列和终点特定的，但结构性教训是普遍的：当残差泄露与一个诚实但更强的预测器不可区分时，仅输出检测就会失败。检测算法在普通硬件上对预测向量的判定可在1秒内完成。

Conclusion: 仅从模型输出检测数据泄露存在根本性的信息论限制——重新校准后的泄露在理论上无法与诚实模型区分。但确定性泄露（如近标签泄露）会在输出中留下不可隐藏的结构签名，可被可靠检测。这为审计实践提供了原则性指导：输出检测的有效性边界，以及何时必须借助外部信息（如可达区分度上限）才能发现泄露。

Abstract: Data leakage -- contamination of a model with information unavailable at baseline -- is the dominant reproducibility failure in machine-learning-based science, yet detection tools require training code, external data, or domain expertise. None operates on the artifact an auditor most often holds: the model's output. We ask what can be decided about leakage from predictions and outcomes alone. We give a decision-theoretic framework in which leakage diagnostics are functionals of the predicted-risk/outcome law, parameterized by a threshold-weighting linked to proper scoring rules and decision-curve analysis. We prove a sharp impossibility: a recalibrated leak matching an honest model's calibration and discrimination is indistinguishable from honest performance by \emph{any} function of the predictions, so the broad class is detectable only against an externally supplied ceiling on achievable discrimination. We then prove what leakage cannot hide: a near-deterministic subgroup -- the signature of a near-label leak -- produces a sustained unit-purity head that no legitimate predictor of a non-deterministic outcome can manufacture, yielding a prior-free test. These results organize leakage into a trichotomy -- miscalibrated, broad-calibrated, and deterministic -- each with a matched detector and failure mode. We validate on UK Biobank using time-windowed comorbidity leakage with known, graded severity, measuring a detection floor of $Δ\cstar \approx 0.007$ on this endpoint, below which residual leakage is undetectable from output and too small to alter conclusions. The numerical floor is cohort- and endpoint-specific; the structural lesson is general: output-only detection fails where residual leakage is indistinguishable from an honestly stronger predictor. The test returns a verdict on a prediction vector in under a second on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [223] [Efficient Multinomial Logistic Bandit via Frequent Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11968)
*Linzhe He,Yu-Jie Zhang,Sifan Yang,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多项Logistic Bandit问题提出EOPD-MLogB算法，通过引入Frequent Directions矩阵草图技术，将OFUL-MLogB的每轮复杂度从$\mathcal{O}(K^3d^3)$时间/$\mathcal{O}(K^2d^2)$空间降至$\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K)^2)$时间/$\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K))$空间，同时保持接近原算法的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OFUL-MLogB虽在多项Logistic Bandit中取得$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(Kd\sqrt{T})$遗憾界，但其参数估计和乐观奖励构造每轮需$\mathcal{O}(K^3d^3)$时间和$\mathcal{O}(K^2d^2)$空间，高维场景下计算代价过高，亟需降维加速方案。

Method: 提出EOPD-MLogB算法，核心是用Frequent Directions矩阵草图维护累积Hessian的低秩SVD近似。由此，参数估计中的约束在线牛顿更新化为一维求根任务，奖励bonus中$Kd \times K$谱范数计算化为$K \times K$特征值计算，大幅降低复杂度。遗憾分析中引入草图误差因子$\Delta_T$（由Hessian的$m$-截断谱尾部控制）。

Result: 算法每轮时间复杂度主导项为$\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K)^2)$，空间复杂度$\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K))$，其中$m \ll d$为草图尺寸。遗憾界为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\Delta_T(Kd\ln\Delta_T+m)\sqrt{T})$；当Hessian近似低秩时，$\Delta_T$接近1，遗憾接近OFUL-MLogB。实验验证了计算效率和竞争性能。

Conclusion: EOPD-MLogB通过矩阵草图技术有效解决了高维多项Logistic Bandit的计算瓶颈，在保持遗憾界可控的前提下显著降低时空复杂度，为高维在线决策提供了实用方案。

Abstract: This paper studies efficient online algorithms for multinomial logistic bandits (MLogB), where the feedback distribution over $K+1$ outcomes follows a multinomial logistic model of $d$-dimensional action vectors. A representative UCB-type algorithm, OFUL-MLogB, achieves a regret bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(Kd\sqrt{T})$, but still requires $\mathcal{O}(K^3d^3)$ time and $\mathcal{O}(K^2d^2)$ space per round due to parameter estimation and optimistic reward construction, which is prohibitive in high-dimensional settings. To address this limitation, we propose EOFD-MLogB, which integrates frequent directions matrix sketching into OFUL-MLogB. By maintaining a low-rank SVD sketch of the accumulated Hessian, constrained online Newton updates in parameter estimation and $Kd \times K$ spectral-norm computations in the reward bonus are reduced to one-dimensional root-finding tasks and $K \times K$ eigenvalue computations, respectively. This yields dominant per-round time complexity $\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K)^2)$ and space complexity $\mathcal{O}(Kd(m+K))$, where $m \ll d$ is the sketch size. We further prove a regret bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(Δ_T(Kd\lnΔ_T+m)\sqrt{T})$, where the sketching error factor $Δ_T$ is controlled by the $m$-truncated spectral tail of the Hessian. Thus, when the Hessian is approximately low-rank, the regret is close to that of OFUL-MLogB. Experiments validate the computational efficiency and competitive performance.

</details>


### [224] [LakeFM: Toward a Foundation Model for Aquatic Ecosystems Using Irregular Multivariate Multi-depth Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11268)
*Abhilash Neog,Sepideh Fatemi,Medha Sawhney,Kazi Sajeed Mehrab,Aanish Pradhan,Bennett J. McAfee,Emma Marchisin,Arka Daw,Robert Ladwig,Cayelan C. Carey,Paul Hanson,Anuj Karpatne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LakeFM是一个针对水生系统的基础模型，通过大规模生态数据预训练，能处理不同湖泊的异构变量、深度和观测模式，在预测性能上优于现有模型，且预测结果符合真实湖泊动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法应用于生态时间序列数据时，假设时间和深度上的规则采样，难以泛化到具有异构变量、不同深度和不规则观测模式的不同湖泊。因此需要一种能够跨湖泊泛化的基础模型。

Method: 提出LakeFM，一个针对水生系统的基础模型。该模型在包含模拟湖泊和观测湖泊的大规模生态数据集上进行预训练，学习跨湖泊的通用表征。

Result: LakeFM能够学习涵盖更广泛湖泊级别特征的有意义表征，在预测性能上与现有时间序列基础模型和非基础模型相比具有竞争力或更优，同时产生物理上合理、符合真实湖泊动力学的预测结果。

Conclusion: LakeFM有效解决了跨湖泊泛化的难题，证明基础模型范式在水生生态系统预测中具有巨大潜力，为湖泊水质和生态健康监测提供了新方法。

Abstract: Understanding and forecasting lake dynamics is critical for monitoring water quality and ecosystem health across lakes and reservoirs. While machine learning methods have been recently applied to ecological time-series data, existing works assume regular sampling in time and depth, and struggle to generalize across lakes with heterogeneous variables, depths, and observation patterns. To address these limitations, we introduce \textsc{LakeFM}, a foundation model for aquatic systems, pre-trained on large-scale ecological datasets comprising both simulated and observed lakes. Through extensive empirical evaluation, we show that \textsc{LakeFM} learns meaningful representations spanning broader lake-level characteristics, and achieves competitive or often superior-forecasting performance compared to existing time-series foundation and non-foundation models, while producing physically plausible predictions consistent with real-world lake dynamics.

</details>


### [225] [What Uncertainties Do We Need for Dynamical Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11988)
*Yusuf Sale,Christopher Bülte,Felix Czaja,Joshua Stiller,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了动力系统中不确定性建模的问题，区分了偶然不确定性和认知不确定性在动力系统语境下的来源与性质，并分析了不同任务中不确定性表示与量化的目标差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习领域对偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的研究主要集中在监督学习和生成建模等场景，而动力系统中的不确定性建模尚未得到充分关注。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统性地探讨动力系统需要何种不确定性建模。

Method: 本文采用概念性分析框架：首先梳理动力系统中不确定性的各类来源，然后对每种不确定性进行性质界定（偶然性或认知性），最后结合不同任务（如预测、控制、推理等）讨论不确定性的表示与量化目标如何变化。

Result: 阐明了动力系统中不确定性的多种来源及其偶然/认知属性，揭示了不同任务对不确定性建模的不同需求，为动力系统的不确定性量化提供了概念基础和分析视角。

Conclusion: 动力系统中的不确定性建模需要根据具体任务目标进行定制化设计，区分偶然与认知不确定性对于选择合适的建模方法至关重要。该方向仍有广阔的研究空间。

Abstract: The distinction between aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty has received considerable attention in machine learning research, mainly in the context of supervised learning but also in other settings such as generative modeling. In this paper, we offer a machine learning perspective on uncertainty modeling for dynamical systems, which has been studied much less so far. In particular, we ask: what uncertainties do we need for dynamical systems? We discuss sources of uncertainty, clarify their nature (aleatoric or epistemic), and consider how the objectives of representing and quantifying uncertainty vary across different tasks.

</details>


### [226] [Quantifying Subliminal Behavioral Transfer Ratios in Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11270)
*Uwe Konig,Hamza Kazmi,Ruizhe Li,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了"阈下学习"现象——即教师模型中不良行为在蒸馏过程中向学生模型迁移的程度。通过对Llama-2-7B-Chat和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct两个模型在不同引导强度下进行蒸馏实验，发现行为迁移确实存在，但两种模型呈现不同的迁移模式：Llama-2存在明显阈值效应，而Qwen2.5则表现为连续且更高水平的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前仅有定性证据支持阈下学习效应的存在，但缺乏对其程度的系统量化。理解不良行为在知识蒸馏中的迁移规律，对安全部署蒸馏模型至关重要。

Method: 对两个教师模型（Llama-2-7B-Chat、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）施加不同强度的行为引导（steering），随后仅使用良性数据蒸馏学生模型。以GPT-4.1作为评估器，在100条JailbreakBench提示上评估学生模型的有害行为迁移比例（τ）。

Result: 行为迁移现象确实稳健存在，但两种模型呈现不同模式：Llama-2在α=-0.15之后出现急剧阈值（τ={0.25,0.32}）；Qwen2.5则表现为连续且更高水平的迁移（τ最高达0.61）。

Conclusion: 阈下学习效应可被量化，且不同模型架构/训练方式导致行为迁移呈现截然不同的缩放规律。这对安全蒸馏具有重要警示意义——即使仅使用良性数据，教师模型中的不良行为仍可能显著迁移至学生模型。

Abstract: Distillation of a language model intended to transfer benign behavior to a student model may also transfer undesirable characteristics, if they are present in the teacher model, a phenomenon known as subliminal learning. While qualitative evidence supports the existence of this effect, its magnitude has not been systematically characterized. This study quantifies subliminal behavioral transfer ratios by steering two teacher models (Llama-2-7B-Chat and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) at varying steering strengths and distilling student models using only benign data. Evaluation on 100 JailbreakBench prompts with GPT-4.1, serving as the evaluator, indicates that transfer is robust but exhibits distinct scaling behaviors. Llama-2 demonstrates a sharp threshold ($τ= {0.25,0.32} \ \text{beyond} \ α= -0.15$), whereas Qwen2.5 displays continuous and higher levels of transfer ($τ$ up to $0.61$).

</details>


### [227] [Federated continual learning: A comprehensive survey on lifelong and privacy-preserving learning over distributed and non-stationary data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11272)
*Masoume Gholizade,Fabrizio Ruffini,Pietro Ducange,Francesco Marcelloni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦持续学习（FCL）进行了全面综述，阐述了其在非平稳数据流下结合联邦学习与持续学习的必要性，提出了多维分类法，并总结了应用领域、评估指标、实验视角及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习系统大多假设数据平稳，但在医疗、工业物联网、网络安全等实际场景中，数据流是非平稳的，导致经典FL方法性能退化、不稳定和灾难性遗忘。持续学习虽能处理演变数据，但多在集中式环境研究，忽视了隐私、通信受限和客户端异构等联邦系统约束。FCL旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法：（1）给出FCL问题的形式化定义并阐明其特征；（2）分析经典FL在非平稳条件下的局限性，阐释CL原理如何支持长期自适应；（3）提出FCL方法的多维分类法以组织文献；（4）梳理代表性应用领域、数据模态、常用评估指标及实验视角。

Result: 综述揭示了FCL领域的研究现状：提出了统一的问题定义和多维分类框架，覆盖了现有FCL方法；识别了关键应用场景（医疗、IIoT、网络安全、智慧城市）和常用评估指标；指出了标准化基准缺失、极端异构性下的时间漂移、可扩展隐私保护记忆机制等核心开放挑战。

Conclusion: FCL是实现分布式非平稳数据上终身自适应学习的关键范式，但领域仍面临极端异构性处理、隐私保护记忆机制设计和标准化基准建设等重大挑战。本文为该领域提供了系统性参考和未来研究路线图。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative and privacy-preserving model training across distributed clients, but most existing FL systems implicitly assume data stationarity. In real-world settings-such as healthcare, industrial IoT (IIOT), cybersecurity, and smart cities-data streams are inherently non-stationary, leading classical FL methods to suffer from performance degradation, instability, and catastrophic forgetting.
  Continual Learning (CL) addresses learning under evolving data distributions but has been largely studied in centralized settings, overlooking key constraints of federated systems, including privacy, limited communication, and client heterogeneity. Federated Continual Learning (FCL) emerges at the intersection of FL and CL, aiming to support lifelong, adaptive, and privacy-aware learning over distributed and non-stationary data.
  This survey provides a comprehensive and systematic overview of FCL. We first present a formal definition of the FCL problem and clarify its distinctive characteristics. We then analyze the limitations of classical FL under non-stationary conditions, highlighting how CL principles support long-term adaptation. To organize the rapidly growing literature, we propose a multi-dimensional taxonomy of FCL approaches. Furthermore, we review representative application domains and data modalities, summarize commonly used evaluation metrics, and discuss experimental perspectives for assessing long-term performance and forgetting. Finally, we highlight key open challenges, including handling extreme heterogeneity under temporal drift, designing scalable and privacy-preserving memory mechanisms, and establishing standardized benchmarks. This survey aims to serve as a reference and a roadmap for advancing FCL toward robust and deployable real-world systems.

</details>


### [228] [RoVE: Rotary Value Embeddings Attention for Relative Position-dependent Value Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11275)
*Alejandro García-Castellanos,Maurice Weiler,Erik J Bekkers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoVE 提出在旋转位置编码(RoPE)中同步旋转值(Value)与键(Key)，使注意力机制的值通路具备位置敏感性，从而将 RoPE 注意力转化为"注意力卷积"。该操作无需额外参数，在 GPT-2 规模模型的小样本上下文学习、分布外困惑度和长上下文检索任务上均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE 使注意力分数具有位置相对性，但值(Value)通路仍对位置不敏感——无论值 token 距离查询 token 多远，其传递的信息都相同。这限制了模型在长程聚合任务上的能力，因此需要一种使值通路也具备位置感知能力的方法。

Method: 提出 RoVE (Rotary Value Embedding)，一种无参数的修改方案：在计算注意力时，对值向量施加与键向量相同的旋转操作，使值通路也编码位置信息。作者进一步证明，这一操作将 RoPE 注意力转化为"注意力卷积"(attentive convolution)，统一了计算机视觉、机器人学和现代 LLM 架构中多种独立的等价操作表述。

Result: 在 124M 和 354M 参数的 GPT-2 模型上训练后，RoVE 相比原始 RoPE 在少样本上下文学习、分布外(out-of-distribution)困惑度和长上下文检索任务上均取得一致的性能提升，且在需要长程聚合的任务上改进最为显著。

Conclusion: RoVE 以零额外参数的成本弥补了 RoPE 值通路位置盲区的缺陷，其带来的"注意力卷积"视角为理解注意力机制提供了新的统一框架，并在多种任务上验证了有效性，尤其适用于长程依赖场景。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) make attention scores position-relative but leave the value pathway position-blind: the message sent by a value token is the same regardless of its distance from the query. We propose RoVE, a parameter-free modification that makes values position-sensitive by rotating them simultaneously with keys, and show that it turns RoPE attention into attentive convolution. This new perspective unifies several independent formulations of the same operation across computer vision, robotics, and modern LLM architectures. Trained 124M and 354M GPT-2 models show consistent empirical gains over RoPE on few-shot in-context learning, out-of-distribution perplexity, and long-context retrieval, with the clearest improvements on tasks that require long-range aggregation.

</details>


### [229] [Least-Action-Guided Diffusion for Physical Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11277)
*Zhongxin Yang,Yuanwei Bin,Xiang I. A. Yang,Shiyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAPG框架，将最小作用量原理转化为可微的推理时校正机制，结合条件扩散模型与物理引导分数，在时间、参数和几何外推中显著提升物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在计算物理中面临可靠外推的核心挑战：在有限时间、参数或几何范围内训练的模型，在训练分布之外往往产生物理上不一致的预测。现有方法依赖训练时约束，难以在推理阶段保证物理一致性。

Method: 提出LAPG（Least-Action-Principle-Guided diffusion）框架，分两阶段：第一阶段使用条件分数扩散模型生成分布内提案；第二阶段利用基于作用量的变分先验将提案精炼至目标分布外条件。该方法将最小作用量原理转化为可微的推理时校正机制，替代了需要经验性损失平衡的点态残差惩罚。

Result: 在自由落体、保守/耗散弹簧-质量系统、相互作用点涡、参数化翼型绕流等常微分和偏微分方程系统上，LAPG在时间、参数和几何外推测试中减少了相位漂移、保持了耗散衰减、准确捕捉涡旋运动，并改善了翼型升力响应，表现优于训练时物理信息基线方法。

Conclusion: LAPG通过推理时物理引导，为生成模型在计算物理中的可靠外推提供了一条新路径，其作用量驱动的校正机制可有效替代传统训练时约束方法。

Abstract: Reliable extrapolation remains a central challenge for generative models in computational physics, because models trained over finite ranges of time, parameters, or geometries may produce physically inconsistent predictions outside the training distribution. We introduce a least-action-principle-guided diffusion, LAPG, a framework that promotes physical consistency during inference rather than relying solely on constraints imposed during training. The method combines a conditional score-based diffusion model with an action-derived physical guidance score. In the first stage, the learned score model generates an in-distribution proposal; in the second, an action-based variational prior refines this proposal toward the target out-of-distribution condition. This formulation turns the principle of least action into a differentiable inference-time correction mechanism and provides an alternative to pointwise residual penalties that often require empirical loss balancing.
  We evaluate LAPG on representative ordinary- and partial-differential-equation systems, including free fall, conservative and dissipative spring-mass dynamics, interacting point vortices, and potential flow over parameterized airfoils. In temporal, parameter, and geometric extrapolation tests, LAPG reduces phase drift, preserves dissipative decay, captures vortex motion, and improves the lift response of airfoil flows compared with training-time physics-informed baselines.

</details>


### [230] [FreeBridge: Variational Schrödinger Bridges for Cellular Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11286)
*Xurui Wang,Qin Ren,Jun Ma,Haibin Ling,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreeBridge 是一种基于薛定谔桥的单细胞扰动建模方法，在仅给定起点和终点分布的条件下，通过在固定的细胞流形上引入经验潜在支持正则化，约束随机传输过程不偏离真实细胞形态，实现了更符合生物几何约束的中间轨迹推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量成像中细胞因化学固定而不可观察连续轨迹，现有生成模型虽能在起点/终点分布对齐上表现良好，但无法保证中间演化过程经过真实的单细胞形态区域，导致中间状态可能落在生物上不合理的区域。

Method: FreeBridge 将实例分割的单细胞表示作为原子状态，构建固定的细胞流形；采用薛定谔桥框架建模端点监督下的随机传输，并通过经验潜在支持正则化将传输约束在该流形几何内，避免中间状态溢出真实细胞分布支撑集。

Result: 在 BBBC021、RxRx1 和 JUMP 三个数据集上，FreeBridge 在统一的评估协议下保持竞争性或更优的端点保真度和作用机制保留能力；在 BBBC021 上进一步减少了中间支持集违规。

Conclusion: 几何扎根对于实现生物学上可解释的扰动动态至关重要，FreeBridge 通过将传输限定在真实的细胞形态流形上，为仅依赖端点观测的单细胞扰动建模提供了更合理的中间演化推断。

Abstract: High-content imaging assays quantify cellular responses to chemical and genetic perturbations, yet continuous trajectories of individual cells are unobservable because cells are chemically fixed at acquisition. Perturbation modeling therefore reduces to inferring stochastic transport between control and treated populations observed only as separate marginals. While recent generative models achieve strong end-point alignment, boundary consistency does not determine intermediate evolution: multiple stochastic processes may connect identical marginals while traversing regions unsupported by observed single-cell morphologies. We introduce \textbf{FreeBridge}, a Schrödinger Bridge formulation for single-cell transition modeling under endpoint-only supervision. FreeBridge defines atomic states as instance-segmented single-cell representations, establishing a fixed cellular manifold, and learns stochastic transport constrained within this geometry via empirical latent support regularization. Across BBBC021, RxRx1, and JUMP, FreeBridge maintains competitive or improved endpoint fidelity and mechanism-of-action retention under a unified evaluation protocol; on BBBC021, it further reduces intermediate support violations. These findings highlight the importance of geometric grounding for biologically interpretable perturbation dynamics. Project page: https://y-research-sbu.github.io/FreeBridge/.

</details>


### [231] [FlowBank: Query-Adaptive Agentic Workflows Optimization through Precompute-and-Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11290)
*Lingzhi Yuan,Chenghao Deng,Fangxu Yu,Souradip Chakraborty,Mohammad Rostami,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowBank提出三阶段框架（多样化生成、精炼压缩、自适应匹配），通过构建紧凑的互补工作流组合库并在推理时按查询路由，替代传统单一工作流或逐查询生成范式，在五个基准上取得最优平均得分，相对最强基线提升4.26%–14.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的多智能体系统在工作流优化上存在两难：任务级方法离线计算量大但只部署单一工作流，浪费了互补的候选方案；查询级方法为每个查询生成新工作流，推理成本高。分析表明这两种范式是互补的——离线搜索发现的工作流能解决不同查询子集，许多需昂贵查询级生成的查询其实可由廉价预计算工作流解决。因此需要一种新目标：构建紧凑可复用的互补工作流库，推理时自适应选择。

Method: 提出FlowBank三阶段框架：1）**Diversifying（多样化）**：提出DiverseFlow引导搜索覆盖未被充分处理的查询，生成高覆盖候选池；2）**Curating（精炼）**：提出CuraFlow将候选池压缩为紧凑、最小冗余的工作流组合；3）**Matching（匹配）**：将部署建模为查询-工作流二分图上的边值预测问题，为每个查询路由到预测效用最优的工作流。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，FlowBank在评估方法中取得最高平均得分，同时保持成本竞争力。相对最强自动化基线和手工设计基线分别提升4.26%和14.92%。

Conclusion: 通过构建互补工作流组合并在推理时自适应选择，FlowBank有效解决了任务级和查询级范式各自的局限，实现了性能与成本间更好的权衡。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems are increasingly powerful, but current agentic workflow optimization paradigms make an unsatisfying trade-off. Task-level methods spend substantial offline compute yet deploy only a single workflow, leaving complementary candidates unused, while query-level methods synthesize a new workflow per query at substantial inference cost. Our motivating analysis shows these paradigms are more complementary than competing: workflows discovered during offline search often solve different subsets of queries, and many queries handled by expensive query-level generation can already be solved by cheaper precomputed workflows. This suggests a different objective: rather than searching for one universally best workflow or regenerating one per instance, we should build a compact bank of reusable, complementary workflows and select among them adaptively at inference time. Doing so requires solving three coupled problems: generating complementary rather than redundant candidates, compressing them into a small deployable portfolio, and assigning each query to the right workflow under a performance-cost trade-off. To this end, we present FlowBank, a three-stage framework for portfolio-based agentic workflow optimization. Diversifying proposes DiverseFlow to steer search toward under-covered queries and produce a high-coverage candidate pool. Curating proposes CuraFlow to compress this pool into a compact portfolio with minimal redundancy. Matching casts deployment as edge-value prediction on a query-workflow bipartite graph and routes each incoming query to the portfolio member with the best predicted utility. Across five benchmarks, FlowBank achieves the highest average score among the evaluated methods while remaining cost-competitive, improving over the strongest automated and handcrafted baselines by 4.26% and 14.92% relative, respectively.

</details>


### [232] [Learning from almost nothing: How neural networks survive heavy input corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11319)
*Justin Tahmassebpur,Asadullah Bhuiyan,Hyejin Kim,Omri Lesser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究属性噪声（输入损坏、标签完好）下的神经网络鲁棒性，发现MLP在输入损坏超过90%时仍能保持远高于随机水平的准确率。通过平均场方法分析无限宽网络，推导出主导阶决策规则——最近类均值（原型规则），该规则对任意深度、多种激活函数和噪声分布普遍成立，为理解极端噪声下的学习提供了可解释的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注标签噪声，而对属性噪声（损坏输入、保留标签）的理论分析相对匮乏。实践中神经网络在极端输入损坏下表现出的鲁棒性远超人类，这一现象缺乏理论解释，亟需从学习理论角度揭示其背后的机制。

Method: 实验层面：在受损坏的分类数据集上训练多层感知机，测试不同损坏程度下的分类准确率。理论层面：采用平均场启发的方法分析无限宽网络在重度损坏区域的行为，推导主导阶决策规则，并验证该规则对网络深度、激活函数、噪声分布的普适性，同时与有限宽网络的实际行为进行对比。

Result: 实验表明神经网络极其鲁棒，即使输入损坏超过90%仍保持远高于随机水平的准确率。理论推导揭示无限宽网络的主导阶决策规则退化为最近类均值分类器——将测试点分配给训练集平均表示最接近的类别。该规则对任意深度的MLP、广泛的激活函数和噪声分布均成立，且与有限宽网络的实际行为高度吻合。

Conclusion: 神经网络在属性噪声下的鲁棒性源于其在高噪声极限下退化为简单的原型学习机制。这种最近类均值规则具有架构普适性，为理解为何即使单个训练样本几乎不含信号时学习仍能成功提供了可解释且可解析的理论基础。

Abstract: Learning from imperfect data is a central theme in machine learning, connecting practical questions of robustness to fundamental questions of learnability. Here we examine attribute noise: learning from corrupted inputs while keeping the labels intact, a setting that has received considerably less analytical attention than its label-noise counterpart. We consider two types of corruption models: additive noise and replacement noise. Through experiments with multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) on corrupted classification datasets, we find that neural networks remain robust, maintaining well-above-chance accuracy even when inputs are >90% corrupted -- far beyond human recognition. To understand this robustness, we analyze infinite-width networks in the heavy-corruption regime using a mean-field-inspired approach and derive a leading-order decision rule for the classification outcome: the network implements a prototype rule, the nearest-class-mean, assigning each test point to the class whose training-set average it most closely resembles. This leading-order decision rule is universal across a broad range of MLP architectures, holding for any depth, as well as a wide class of activation functions and noise distributions. The same centroid mechanism closely matches finite-width network behavior in our experiments and provides an interpretable and analytically tractable account of why learning can succeed even when individual training examples carry almost no signal.

</details>


### [233] [Energy-Conserved Neural Pipelines: Attenuating Error Propagation in Modular Neural Networks via Physical Conservation Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11341)
*David Young,Swan Yi Htet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出能量守恒硬约束替代软惩罚，解决模块化网络中的误差复合问题，在CIFAR-10及机器人流水线上显著提升噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化神经网络流水线存在误差复合问题：模块边界处的噪声会向下游传播并放大。现有方法多采用软能量惩罚，无法从根本上抑制噪声传播。

Method: 引入能量守恒作为模块间信息流的硬物理约束。具体将激活能量（特征向量的平方L2范数）强制在每一模块边界精确保持恒定。网络可跨神经元重新分配能量，但不可创造或销毁能量。论文还推导了形式化上界，证明守恒后的噪声能量严格小于输入噪声能量。

Result: （1）噪声σ=0.2时，守恒模型保留77.4%干净准确率，远超基线（35.1%）和能量惩罚模型（30.9%，p<0.001，5种子）；（2）流水线具备深度不变性，深度2至5层边界均有噪声时仍保持93.3%；（3）优势泛化至系统偏差（+45.1%）、高斯噪声（+40.4%）和对抗噪声（+4.8%），对dropout几乎无影响（-0.3%）；（4）ResNet-18上，无BatchNorm时优势达+26.2pp（σ=0.2），σ=0.5时达+58.0pp；（5）MuJoCo Franka Panda真实机器人流水线上，平均优势+18.9pp。

Conclusion: 能量守恒硬约束是一种简单有效的模块间正则化手段，能从根本上抑制噪声传播，使模块化流水线具备深度不变性，且优势随网络内在归一化程度降低而增强。

Abstract: Modular neural network pipelines suffer from error compounding: noise at any module boundary propagates and potentially amplifies through subsequent modules. We introduce energy conservation as a hard physical constraint on inter-module information flow. Activation energy (the squared L2 norm of feature vectors) is enforced to be exactly preserved at every module boundary. Unlike soft energy penalties, conservation is an inviolable law: the network may redistribute energy across neurons but cannot create or destroy it. Four experiments on CIFAR-10 demonstrate: (1) conservation retains 77.4% of clean accuracy at noise sigma=0.2, versus 35.1% for baselines and 30.9% for energy-penalized models (p<0.001, 5 seeds); (2) pipelines become depth-invariant, retaining 93.3% at depths 2 through 5 with noise at every boundary; (3) the advantage generalizes to systematic bias (+45.1%), Gaussian (+40.4%), and adversarial noise (+4.8%), with a principled non-effect on dropout (-0.3%); (4) on ResNet-18, the conservation advantage scales inversely with intrinsic normalization: +0.3 pp with BatchNorm, +26.2 pp without at sigma=0.2, reaching +58.0 pp at sigma=0.5. Experiment 5 validates the operator on a real modular robotic pipeline (MuJoCo physics, Franka Panda). Across three independent runs on separate machines (90 trials per cell), conservation provides +18.9 pp average advantage on monocular-depth-style noise. A formal bound proves conserved noise energy is strictly less than input noise energy.

</details>


### [234] [SwiftCTS: Fast Cross-Design Prediction and Pareto Optimization of Clock Tree Metrics via Few-Shot Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11348)
*Barsat Khadka,Kawsher Roxy,Md Rubel Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SwiftCTS 是一个物理信息引导的代理框架，通过轻量级物理统计特征与梯度提升集成模型耦合，实现5秒内CPU训练、亚毫秒级推理；引入K-shot乘法校准机制，无需重训练即可适应OOD设计，将功率预测误差从24.5%降至3.3%、线长误差从56.6%降至1%以下；结合进化优化器，10秒内评估10万种CTS配置，在OpenROAD流程中实现物理验证的Pareto最优前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTS是物理设计中计算昂贵的阶段，需要反复调用EDA工具在海量配置空间中搜索最优功耗、线长和时序偏差。现有ML方法面临两大局限：(1) 需要昂贵的重训练或微调来适应未见过的宏架构；(2) 架构上无法匹配穷举组合搜索所需的数百万次评估。

Method: 提出SwiftCTS框架：(1) 使用轻量级物理统计特征与梯度提升集成（GBDT）耦合，实现CPU上快速训练与亚毫秒推理；(2) 提出K-shot乘法校准机制，仅需1-2次物理参考运行即可将预测锚定到新设计上，无需重训练；(3) 将该代理引擎与进化优化器集成，实现CTS配置空间的高效搜索。

Result: 在OOD宏上，K-shot校准将功率预测误差从24.5%降至3.3%，线长误差从56.6%降至1%以下；进化优化器在10秒内评估10万种CTS配置；闭环验证确认功率和线长预测误差低于0.5%，时序偏差预测在5皮秒以内；在所有目标指标上持续优于默认工具启发式方法。

Conclusion: SwiftCTS通过物理信息引导的轻量级建模和K-shot校准机制，成功解决了CTS优化中OOD泛化和计算效率两大瓶颈，在不依赖GPU的情况下实现快速、准确的CTS配置空间探索，显著优于传统EDA工具默认策略。

Abstract: Clock Tree Synthesis (CTS) is a computationally expensive stage in the physical design flow, requiring iterative EDA tool invocations to navigate a vast configuration space for optimal power, wirelength, and timing skew. Existing machine learning approaches require computationally expensive retraining or fine-tuning cycles to adapt to unseen macro architectures and are architecturally mismatched to the millions of evaluations demanded by exhaustive combinatorial search. We present SwiftCTS, a physics-informed surrogate framework that addresses both limitations simultaneously. By coupling lightweight, physics-grounded statistical features with gradient-boosted ensembles, SwiftCTS trains in under five seconds on a CPU and delivers sub-millisecond inference without GPU support. To handle out-of-distribution (OOD) designs without retraining or fine-tuning, we introduce a K-shot multiplicative calibration mechanism that anchors predictions to just one or two physical reference runs, reducing power prediction error from 24.5\% to 3.3\% and wirelength error from 56.6\% to under 1\% on unseen macros. Integrating this engine with an evolutionary optimizer, SwiftCTS evaluates 100,000 CTS configurations in under ten seconds, yielding Pareto-optimal frontiers that are physically validated within the OpenROAD flow. Closed-loop validation confirms prediction errors below 0.5\% for power and wirelength, and timing skew predictions within five picoseconds on an OOD benchmark, consistently outperforming default tool heuristics across all target metrics. Code publicly available at: \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SwiftCTS-7E6E}{https://github.com/BarsatKhadka/SwiftCTS}

</details>


### [235] [GLACIER: A Multimodal Student-Teacher Foundation Model for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11382)
*Emily Nguyen,Yongchan Hong,Harsh Toshniwal,Yan Liu,Andreas Luttens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GLACIER框架，通过学生-教师架构融合分子图、SMILES字符串和理化描述符三种模态，并利用Finsler几何感知融合模块和对比学习从大型教师模型（MiniMol、MolFormer）蒸馏知识，在保持轻量高效的同时实现高精度分子性质预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模分子深度学习模型存在两个核心局限：（1）计算负担持续增长，限制了可扩展性；（2）大多数模型为单模态设计，未能利用互补的分子数据模态（图、序列、理化属性）。需要一种既能融合多模态信息、又能保持计算效率的分子表征学习框架。

Method: 提出GLACIER三阶段学生-教师框架：（1）在10万类药物分子上分别预训练三个学生编码器——用于分子图的消息传递神经网络、用于SMILES的Transformer编码器、用于理化描述符的多层感知机；（2）通过新颖的Finsler几何感知模块融合三种学生模态；（3）利用对比学习，将MiniMol和MolFormer等大型教师模型中的互补知识蒸馏到单一轻量模型中。

Result: GLACIER在复杂分子性质预测任务上展现出高预测性能与计算效率，验证了该框架的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: GLACIER通过多模态融合与知识蒸馏成功在轻量模型中整合了图、序列和理化描述符三种分子表示，证明该学生-教师框架能在保持高效推理的同时达到先进水平的预测能力，为大规模分子筛选提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models facilitate the discovery of molecules with tailored properties among billions of candidate compounds. However, the computational burden to develop and deploy state-of-the-art models continuously increases, limiting their scalability. Most large-scale models are unimodal in nature and overlook the potential to leverage complementary molecular data modalities. To address these shortcomings, this paper introduces the Graph-Language Alignment for Chemical Inference and Exploration using Representations (GLACIER) model, a student-teacher framework that integrates molecular graphs, SMILES strings, and physicochemical descriptors to learn rich molecular embeddings. Our framework consists of three stages: (1) we pretrain three student encoders on 100,000 drug-like molecules: a message-passing neural network for molecular graphs, a transformer-based encoder for SMILES strings, and a multilayer perceptron for physicochemical descriptors, (2) we fuse these student modalities using a novel Finsler geometry-aware module, and (3) distill complementary knowledge from large teacher models, including MiniMol and MolFormer, into a single lightweight model via contrastive learning. We demonstrate that GLACIER is a robust framework that delivers high predictive performance and computational efficiency in complex molecular property prediction tasks. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/eemokey/glacier.

</details>


### [236] [Recursive Binding on a Budget: Subspace Carving in Order-p Tensor Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11391)
*Travis Pence,Daisuke Yamada,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出正交子空间雕刻（OSC）架构，通过将填充项投影到角色基零空间后再聚合为固定阶张量，实现在恒定内存占用下进行深度递归绑定，解耦了张量阶数与结构深度，并在高叠加场景下展现出优越的内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量积表示（TPR）虽具有符号推理所需的结构保真度，但在编码深度递归结构时维度呈指数增长；向量符号架构（VSA）保持恒定维度，却因叠加带来的噪声压缩而牺牲容量与保真度。本文旨在弥合二者之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出OSC内存架构：将填充向量投影到角色基的零空间以强制几何正交性，再聚合为固定阶张量；检索通过识别（recognition）实现，使分量向量可远小于内存张量。同时证明TPR是Clifford代数中绑定的特例，并给出OSC的Clifford表述。

Result: OSC解耦了张量阶数与结构深度，实现恒定内存下的深度递归绑定。分量向量尺寸可比内存张量小若干个数量级，在高叠加场景下具有优越的内存效率。

Conclusion: OSC成功结合了TPR的结构保真度与VSA的恒定维度优势，为深度递归结构的神经符号绑定提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Tensor Product Representations provide the structural fidelity required for symbolic reasoning in models but suffer from exponential dimensionality growth when encoding deep recursive structures. Conversely, Vector Symbolic Architectures maintain constant dimensionality but sacrifice capacity and fidelity due to noisy compression via superposition. In this work, we propose Orthogonal Subspace Carving (OSC), a memory architecture that binds fillers to roles by projecting onto the null space of the role basis before aggregating into a fixed order-p tensor. OSC uses projections to enforce geometric orthogonality between bound structures within a static memory trace. We show that this mechanism decouples the tensor order from the structural depth, enabling deep recursive binding within a constant memory footprint. By performing retrieval via recognition, this construction allows for component vectors that are orders of magnitude smaller than the memory tensor, giving superior memory efficiency in settings involving high superposition. We also show that TPR is a special case of binding in Clifford algebra, and give a Clifford formulation of OSC.

</details>


### [237] [Risk Under Pressure: Compute-Aware Evaluation of Adversarial Robustness in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11409)
*Malikeh Ehghaghi,Boglárka Ecsedi,Marsha Chechik,Colin Raffel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于计算量（FLOPs）的对抗鲁棒性评估框架，通过风险-计算曲线揭示不同攻击在LLM上的真实代价，发现对齐训练对计算空间鲁棒性呈非单调影响、模型缩放对廉价攻击效果有限等关键结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对抗鲁棒性评估仅以固定查询预算下的攻击成功率（ASR）为指标，隐式假设所有攻击成本相同。然而实际中不同攻击策略的计算开销可相差数个数量级，固定预算的ASR无法反映攻击者真正付出的努力，也难以判断攻击成本是否值得其收益。因此需要一种计算感知的评估框架。

Method: 提出以累积浮点运算数（FLOPs）作为对抗努力的代理度量，构建"计算压力"概念。核心工具为风险-计算曲线（risk-compute curves），将计算预算映射为攻击风险，并从中导出两个汇总指标，衡量攻击成功所需的平均计算压力。在三个模型家族、四种训练/对齐阶段的十个模型上，使用三类攻击策略（基于梯度、迭代优化、模板注入）在两个越狱鲁棒性基准上进行评估。

Result: (1) 对齐训练对计算空间鲁棒性的影响是非单调的；(2) 扩大模型规模可降低基于梯度攻击的效果，但对更廉价的模板攻击影响有限；(3) 在替代模型上优化的梯度攻击可迁移至目标模型，降低攻击者成本；(4) 同一模型内不同危害类别的计算成本差异可达约5倍；(5) 安全对齐RL提升了总体攻击成本，但某些类别仍然不成比例地容易攻破。

Conclusion: 计算感知的评估框架能更真实地刻画LLM的对抗鲁棒性，揭示ASR指标掩盖的关键差异。该框架已开源，旨在推动计算感知的风险评估与评测实践。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness evaluations of large language models (LLMs) typically report attack success rate (ASR) under fixed query budgets, implicitly treating all attacks as equally costly. In practice, the computational expense of different attack strategies can vary by orders of magnitude. Consequently, ASR at a fixed budget can obscure the true effort required to jailbreak a model, thereby making it hard to determine whether an attack's cost justifies its payoff to the attacker. We propose a compute-aware evaluation framework based on computational pressure, measured in cumulative floating-point operations (FLOPs), as a proxy for adversarial effort. We introduce risk-compute curves, which map compute budgets to attack risk, and derive two metrics that summarize the average pressure required for a given attack to succeed. Across ten models spanning three families and four different stages in language model training and alignment, evaluated with three attack strategies (gradient-based, iterative refinement, and template-based) on two jailbreak robustness benchmarks, we find: (1) alignment training has non-monotonic effects on compute-space robustness; (2) scaling model size reduces gradient-based attack effectiveness but has limited impact on cheaper template-based attacks; (3) gradient-based attacks optimized on a surrogate model can transfer to a separate target model, providing a way to reduce attacker costs; (4) compute cost varies by up to ${\approx}5{\times}$ across harm categories within a single model; and (5) safety-aligned RL increases aggregate cost while leaving some categories disproportionately accessible. We release our framework to enable compute-aware risk assessment and evaluation.

</details>


### [238] [Mirror Descent Beyond Euclidean Stability: An Exponential Separation in Initialization Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11431)
*Shira Vansover-Hager,Matan Schliserman,Ofir Schlisselberg,Tomer Koren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了镜像下降(MD)在非二次正则化下的初始化敏感性：与梯度下降(GD)的稳定性形成鲜明对比，MD可将微小初始扰动指数级放大；作者进一步提出通过Bregman正则化锚定机制来稳定动力学，并指出锚点选择至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜像下降在强化学习和LLM后训练中作为KL正则化策略优化的工具重新流行，但其对输入的鲁棒性未被充分研究。初始化通常来自预训练模型，微小的初始化扰动是否会被放大，直接关系到可重复性和可靠性。

Method: 理论构造与数学分析：1）构造三维凸光滑目标函数和良条件强凸光滑正则化器，证明MD可将初始ε扰动放大至min{polylog⁻¹(1/ε), εe^{Ω(ηT)}}；2）分析单纯形上KL正则化MD在线性目标下的指数放大现象；3）提出Bregman正则化锚定方法，比较锚定在初始化点与锚定在固定点的稳定性差异。

Result: 1）非二次正则化下MD对初始化的敏感性可达指数级别，与GD的稳定性形成鲜明对比；2）单纯形上KL-MD即使在线性目标下，高维或近边界时初始扰动也被指数放大；3）Bregman锚定可有效稳定动力学，但锚定在初始化点仅部分缓解不稳定性，锚定在固定点则提供更稳定的机制。

Conclusion: 非二次正则化器使MD对初始化极其敏感，这对将MD用于LLM对齐等场景的可靠性提出警示；引入锚定正则化是有效的稳定策略，且锚点应选择固定点而非依赖当前初始化的点。

Abstract: Mirror Descent (MD) extends Gradient Descent (GD) beyond Euclidean geometry and has recently reappeared as a lens for KL-regularized policy optimization in reinforcement learning and LLM post-training. This raises a basic robustness question, crucial to reproducibility and reliability: how sensitive are MD dynamics to their inputs? We focus on initialization, often itself a pretrained or previously aligned model. Quadratic-regularized MD, including GD and Mahalanobis geometries, is well-known to be stable for convex smooth objectives. We show a sharp contrast: once the regularizer is non-quadratic, MD can be exponentially more sensitive to initialization than GD, even with a well-conditioned regularizer in Euclidean norm. We give a three-dimensional construction with a convex, smooth objective and a strongly convex, smooth, well-conditioned regularizer where an initial $\varepsilon$ perturbation is quickly amplified to $\min\{\text{polylog}^{-1}(1/\varepsilon), \varepsilon e^{Ω(ηT)}\}$ after $T$ iterations of MD with step size $η$. For canonical KL-regularized MD on the simplex, we show that even linear objectives can amplify an initial $\varepsilon$ perturbation exponentially fast in high-dimensional or near-boundary regimes. Finally, we show that adding a Bregman regularization term toward an anchor point can stabilize the dynamics while largely preserving the optimization guarantees, and that the choice of anchor is crucial: anchoring at the initialization only partially mitigates the instability, whereas anchoring at a fixed point yields a more stable mechanism.

</details>


### [239] [LSTM-Based Detection of Structural Breaks in Property Insurance Loss Reserving: A Climate-Informed Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11463)
*Thomas Mbrice,Shashwat Panigrahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以LSTM神经网络检测气候灾难导致的准备金结构突变，利用佛罗里达和路易斯安那州15年以上的监管发展三角数据及NOAA气候指标，目标将灾难年准备金精度提升15-20%，并构建了LSTM结构突变检测的概率理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化驱动的巨灾事件系统性地违背了传统精算方法（链梯法、Bornhuetter-Ferguson法、Cape Cod法）所依赖的稳定性假设，导致损失准备金评估失准，威胁保险公司偿付能力。现有文献中缺乏能够快速检测并适应此类结构突变的准备金方法。

Method: 采用LSTM神经网络建模。数据来源为佛罗里达州和路易斯安那州15年以上的监管发展三角数据，并融入NOAA飓风强度指数和海面温度作为外生协变量。理论层面构建了LSTM结构突变检测的概率形式化框架，推导出形式化的性能保证，以弥补测试期内灾难事件样本量有限的不足。

Result: 本文为研究计划/白皮书性质，尚未报告实证结果。研究假设在灾难暴露年份，LSTM相较于传统链梯法、BF法和Cape Cod法可实现15-20%的准备金精度提升，该目标基于先前神经网络准备金文献与文中推导的形式化收敛结果设定。

Conclusion: 该研究项目旨在验证LSTM在气候驱动结构突变下的准备金评估优势，同时在理论层面为小样本灾难事件下的模型性能提供概率保证。作者对研究局限性进行了坦诚评估，但具体局限性内容需参考全文。

Abstract: Accurate loss reserving is foundational to insurer solvency, yet accelerating climate driven catastrophes systematically violate the stability assumptions on which traditional actuarial methods depend. This white paper presents a research program testing whether Long Short Term Memory (LSTM) neural networks can detect and adapt to these structural breaks faster and more accurately than Chain Ladder, Bornhuetter Ferguson, and Cape Cod methods. Using 15 plus years of regulatory development triangle data from Florida and Louisiana, enriched with NOAA hurricane intensity indices and sea surface temperatures, we hypothesize a targeted improvement of 15, 20% in reserve accuracy for catastrophe exposed years, a threshold grounded both in the prior neural network reserving literature and in the formal convergence results developed here. Beyond empirical validation, we develop a theoretical framework grounding LSTM structural break detection in probabilistic terms, providing formal performance guarantees that compensate for the limited number of catastrophe events in the test period. We document the research design, methodology, expected contributions, and a candid assessment of limitations.

</details>


### [240] [Mahalanobis-Guided Latent OOD Detection for Hybrid ES-DRL Control in Time-Varying Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11474)
*Shaifalee Saxena,Alexander Scheinker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于马氏距离引导的变分自编码器（VAE）潜在空间分布外（OOD）检测方法，用于非线性时变系统中测试阶段的强化学习（RL）与极值搜索（ES）控制器切换，并在粒子加速器控制场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习控制器在训练分布内表现优异，但当系统出现时变动态导致未见过的观测时，其性能会严重退化。因此需要一个可靠的OOD检测机制，在测试时判断何时从RL控制器切换到鲁棒的、与模型无关的ES控制器，以确保安全关键系统的稳定运行。

Method: 训练阶段：仅在分布内（in-distribution）的束流轮廓观测数据上训练变分自编码器（VAE）。测试阶段：将实时观测通过VAE编码到潜在空间，计算潜在表示与分布内数据的马氏距离（Mahalanobis distance）作为OOD得分；当该距离超过阈值时，触发二值切换信号，将控制器从RL切换至有界极值搜索（ES）控制器。

Result: 在安全关键的粒子加速器控制任务中，空间磁铁运动产生了RL训练期间未见的OOD束流轮廓。VAE潜在空间的可视化表明，所提方法能够成功识别此类OOD场景，并提供可解释的切换信号，实现了RL与ES组合控制器的有效协同。

Conclusion: 基于VAE潜在空间马氏距离的OOD检测方法能够为非线性时变系统中的RL-ES组合控制器提供可靠、可解释的切换决策依据，在粒子加速器等安全关键应用中展现出良好的实用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Mahalanobis-guided latent out-of-distribution (OOD) detection for test-time RL controller switching in nonlinear time-varying systems. RL controllers can quickly control high-dimensional systems within the training distribution, but their performance can degrade when time-varying dynamics produce unseen observations. We consider a combined ES--DRL controller, where RL provides fast in-distribution actions and bounded extremum seeking (ES) provides robust model-independent control under OOD operation. The key challenge is deciding when to switch. We train a variational autoencoder (VAE) on in-distribution beam-profile observations and use Mahalanobis distance in the VAE latent space to detect OOD beam profiles at test time. This OOD decision sets a binary switch that selects either the RL controller or the ES controller. We evaluate the approach in safety-critical particle accelerator control. In this setting, spatial magnet motion creates OOD beam profiles that were not seen during RL training. Visualization of the VAE latent space shows that the proposed method identifies this OOD scenario and provides an interpretable signal for switching between RL and ES in the combined controller.

</details>


### [241] [Accurate and Resource-Efficient Federated Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11480)
*Jebacyril Arockiaraj,Dhruv Parikh,Jayashree Adivarahan,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor Prasanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedRAN 是一种资源感知的解析式联邦持续学习框架，通过截断 SVD 压缩 Gram 矩阵降低通信成本，结合服务端子空间合并与闭式岭分类器，并在标签稀缺时使用原型伪标签，在多项基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦持续学习方法依赖迭代优化、重放和全监督，资源开销大；分析式替代方案虽避免迭代，但使用高维随机特征需传输 Gram 矩阵（二阶统计量），通信成本随随机特征维度 M 呈平方增长，亟需一种资源感知的高效方案。

Method: 提出 FedRAN 框架：1) 客户端使用截断 SVD 压缩 Gram 矩阵，将上传通信量从关于随机特征维度 M 的二次级降至线性级；2) 服务端执行两级 QR-SVD 子空间合并（跨客户端空间合并与跨任务时间合并），并闭式求解岭分类器；3) 通过原型伪标签支持标签稀缺场景。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R 和 VTAB 数据集上，FedRAN 平均准确率比最强基线高 4.8 个百分点；每客户端通信量较基于优化的 FCL 减少 30.6–121.8 倍；平均速度比基于梯度的基线快 190.3 倍；在仅 20% 标签下伪标签使准确率提升高达 6.61 个百分点。

Conclusion: FedRAN 通过紧凑的随机特征统计和伪标签机制，在通信、计算和标签受限的场景下实现了高精度、高效率的联邦持续学习，展现了分析式方法在该领域的强大潜力。

Abstract: Federated continual learning (FCL) must learn from distributed task streams under limited resources, such as communication, computation, memory, and label availability. Existing FCL methods often rely on repeated local optimization, replay, and full supervision. Analytic alternatives avoid iterative training and replay, but using high-dimensional random features to improve accuracy requires a second-order feature statistic, the Gram matrix, which has a quadratic communication cost in the random feature size $M$. We propose FedRAN, a resource-aware analytic FCL framework that replaces gradient-based updates with compact random feature statistics. Each client transmits a truncated-SVD summary of its Gram matrix, reducing the dominant second-order upload from quadratic to linear in $M$ for fixed rank. The server performs a two-level QR-SVD subspace merge, spatially across clients and temporally across tasks, and solves a ridge classifier in closed form. FedRAN further supports label scarcity through prototype-based pseudo-labeling. Across CIFAR-100, ImageNet-R, and VTAB datasets, FedRAN improves average accuracy by up to 4.8 percentage points over the strongest baseline, uses 30.6-121.8$\times$ less per-client communication than optimization-based FCL, and is 190.3$\times$ faster on average than gradient-based baselines; with only 20% labels, pseudo-labeling improves average accuracy by up to 6.61 points. These results show that FedRAN enables accurate and resource-efficient FCL under communication, computation, and label constraints. The source code is available at https://github.com/JebacyrilArockiaraj/Fed-RAN-SSL.

</details>


### [242] [OmniLoc: A Geometry-Aware Foundation Model for Anchor-Free UE Localization Across Diverse Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11490)
*Lei Chu,Yuning Zhang,Omer Gokalp Serbetci,Anushka Katiyar,Bassel Abou Ali Modad,Andreas F. Molisch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniLoc 是一个基于无线测量的环境交互式基础模型，用于跨多样化室内环境的无锚点用户定位。它通过统一输入分词、几何感知 Transformer 和几何感知位置估计三大设计，在大规模数据集上显著优于现有方法，并展现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模室内定位面临建筑几何差异大、可检测 AP 集合变化、接收信号异质性强等挑战。现有基于学习的方法往往仅在有限场景下表现良好，环境变化时性能下降严重，因此亟需一种能够跨多样化室内环境进行鲁棒无锚点定位的方法。

Method: 提出 OmniLoc，首个直接基于无线测量构建的室内定位基础模型。核心包含三个设计：1）统一输入分词模块，将异构无线测量转换为统一表示；2）几何感知 Transformer，强调主导 AP 同时聚合辅助 AP 的互补信息进行 AP 感知特征提取；3）几何感知位置估计模块，以几何嵌入为条件回归，产生几何一致的位置预测。

Result: OmniLoc 在自建大规模数据集和公开基准数据集上进行评估，显著优于现有方法；其设计组件可集成到现有骨干网络中并持续提升性能；在跨环境评估中展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: OmniLoc 作为首个面向无线定位的基础模型，通过统一异构信号表示和注入几何先验，有效解决了跨环境室内定位的鲁棒性和泛化难题，为大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Indoor localization from wireless measurements remains challenging in large-scale deployments due to substantial variation in building geometry, the set of detectable access points (APs), and the heterogeneity of received signals. Existing learning-based methods often perform well only in limited settings and degrade under environmental shifts, making robust anchor-free localization across diverse indoor environments notoriously difficult. In this paper, we present OmniLoc, an environment-interactive foundation model for anchor-free user equipment localization across diverse indoor environments. To the best of our knowledge, OmniLoc is the first foundation-model-based approach built directly on wireless measurements for this task. OmniLoc is built on three key designs. First, a unified input tokenization module converts heterogeneous wireless measurements into a common representation that is more amenable to learning. Second, a geometry-aware Transformer performs AP-aware feature extraction by emphasizing dominant APs while aggregating complementary evidence from supporting APs. Third, a geometry-aware location estimation module conditions regression on geometric embeddings to produce geometrically consistent location predictions. We evaluate OmniLoc on both a large-scale in-house dataset and a public benchmark dataset. Results show that OmniLoc significantly outperforms existing methods, consistently improves existing backbones when its design components are integrated, and demonstrates strong generalization in cross-environment evaluations.

</details>


### [243] [Probabilistic Contrastive Pretraining for Multi-task ADME Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11508)
*Yifan Xue,Srimukh Prasad Veccham,Saee Paliwal,Tyler Shimko,Micha Livne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分子图-Transformer预训练框架，结合化学特定的自监督学习与对比互信息机器学习（cMIM），将重建、对比判别和化学监督统一为单一概率隐变量目标，并通过多任务GNN读出架构进行微调，在多个ADME基准上相比KERMT基线取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADME性质预测对药物发现至关重要，但面临三大挑战：(1) ADME终点数据噪声大；(2) 各终点间存在相互依赖关系；(3) 可用数据量有限。现有方法通常将辅助任务作为独立正则化项处理，需要单独调参，缺乏统一的概率框架。

Method: (1) 提出分子图-Transformer架构，将分子图编码为隐变量；(2) 从图衍生隐编码重建SMILES字符串；(3) 将重建、对比互信息判别和化学特定自监督任务统一为单一概率隐变量目标函数，各部分以单位权重对数概率因子形式组合；(4) 提出多任务GNN读出架构，使用任务特定的MLP头，在保持共享表征学习的同时缓解负迁移问题；(5) 在预训练语料中加入ADME邻近分子以增强迁移能力。

Result: 所提出的Contrastive KERMT预训练方法在Biogen、ExpansionRX和ChEMBL-MT三个ADME基准数据集上，相比KERMT基线分别平均提升7.6%、9.9%和9.5%（仅统计显著改善的终点）。对比组件使化学上有意义的隐空间邻域更加清晰，引入ADME邻近分子进一步改善了迁移效果。

Conclusion: 统一的概率隐变量框架有效整合了重建、对比学习和化学自监督任务，避免了多任务权重手动调参，在数据有限、噪声大的ADME预测场景中展现出显著优势，为药物发现中的分子性质预测提供了更鲁棒的预训练范式。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of absorption, distribution, metabolism, and excretion (ADME) properties is critical to drug discovery, but remains challenging because ADME endpoints are noisy, interdependent, and often data-limited. We propose a molecular graph-transformer pretraining framework that combines chemistry-specific self-supervision with contrastive mutual information machine learning (cMIM).
