Our method encodes molecular graphs into latent variables, reconstructs SMILES strings from the graph-derived latent codes, and augments the contrastive objective with domain-specific self-supervised chemistry tasks. Rather than treating these tasks as auxiliary regularizers with separately tuned loss weights, we formulate reconstruction, contrastive discrimination, and chemistry-specific supervision as unit-weighted log-probability factors in a single probabilistic latent-variable objective. For fine-tuning, we propose a multi-task GNN readout architecture with task-specific multilayer perceptron heads, preserving shared representation learning while mitigating negative transfer and improving the modeling of heterogeneous, nonlinear task relationships. Across Biogen, ExpansionRX, and ChEMBL-MT, the resulting Contrastive KERMT pretraining improves over the KERMT baseline by 7.6%, 9.9%, and 9.5% respectively (averaged over significantly-improved endpoints). Adding ADME-adjacent molecules to the pretraining corpus further improves transfer, and the contrastive component sharpens chemically meaningful latent neighborhoods.

</details>


### [244] [SirenFNO: Efficient and Full Frequency Learning of Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11518)
*Pengqing Shi,Jie Yin,Stephen Tierney,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SirenFNO 提出利用正弦表示网络（SIREN）替代 FNO 中的频率截断，实现全谱学习，消除频谱偏置，同时通过函数张量分解大幅减少参数量，在多个 PDE 基准上以更少参数超越 FNO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO 依赖频率截断维持学习效率，但由此产生对低频信息的频谱偏置（spectral bias），削弱了对强高频振荡 PDE 的学习能力。现有方法缺乏一种既能保持离散化不变性、又能以恒定参数量学习全频段频谱的方案。

Method: 提出 SirenFNO 框架：用 SIREN 学习隐式神经表示，以 mode-wise 方式对核进行参数化，实现全网格频谱学习且参数量与离散化无关（无需频率截断）。进一步引入函数张量分解（functional tensor decomposition）扩展 SirenFNO，提升参数效率与学习效率。

Result: SirenFNO 在多个 PDE 基准上持续优于 FNO，参数量减少约 4 至 15 倍且保持离散化不变性；函数分解变体最高以 73 倍更少参数获得性能提升。

Conclusion: SirenFNO 有效解决了 FNO 的频谱偏置问题，通过 SIREN 参数化和张量分解实现了高效的全谱学习，在参数效率与泛化性能上均显著优于原始 FNO。

Abstract: Fourier neural operators (FNOs) are effective and efficient surrogates for approximating solutions of PDEs and generalize across discretizations. However, owing to the reliance on frequency truncation to maintain learning efficiency of FNOs, empirical studies suggest that FNOs exhibit spectral bias toward low-frequency information, which may hinder the learning capability especially for certain PDEs with strong high-frequency oscillations. To address this limitation, we propose SirenFNO, a novel framework that leverages sinusoidal representation networks (SIRENs) to learn implicit neural representations and performs mode-wise kernel parameterization. Our SIREN parameterization learns a full-grid spectrum with a constant and discretization-independent parameter count, thereby eliminating the need for frequency truncation. We further extend SirenFNO with functional tensor decompositions to enhance parameter and learning efficiency. Empirical results show that our SirenFNO consistently outperforms FNO with approximately $4$ to $15$ times parameter reductions with preserved discretization invariance, and our functional decomposition variants obtain performance improvements with a maximum of $73$ times fewer parameters across multiple PDE benchmarks.

</details>


### [245] [Counterexample Guided Learning in the Large using Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11521)
*Hongyi Liu,Frederic Sala,Thomas Reps,Adithya Murali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过反例引导的反馈机制提升LLM在正则表达式归纳任务上的表现，提出的反例引导框架将成功率从3.2%提升至38.1%、从38.9%提升至74.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM及其智能体在接收反馈后应当改进，但反馈具有异构性、领域特异性且难以控制，导致难以判断LLM何时能真正从反馈中受益。本文选择正则表达式归纳这一经典符号学习问题作为测试平台，因为该领域存在精确的反例反馈机制，便于系统研究反馈对LLM的影响。

Method: 采用反例引导学习框架：LLM作为学习者根据正负标签字符串提出候选正则表达式，验证器作为教师返回反例以展示候选与目标语言之间的差异。提出两类关键策略：(1) 反例引导的精炼策略，包括正则化与符号化反例聚类；(2) 智能体策略，如反思与修复循环。

Result: 验证器反馈显著提升了困难正则表达式归纳任务的样本效率，减少了所需标注样本数量，并在标准提示方法失败时成功学习复杂目标表达式。在最困难的任务组中，反例引导框架在两个不同正则表达式领域将成功率分别从3.2%提升至38.1%、从38.9%提升至74.1%。

Conclusion: LLM能够从超越简单数据扩充的丰富反馈中受益，这为基于验证器引导的LLM程序合成与形式化推理方法开辟了道路。

Abstract: LLMs and LLM agents should improve when given feedback, but identifying when they are able to do so is difficult: feedback is heterogeneous, domain-specific, and difficult to control. We approach this challenge by asking LLMs to perform regular-expression induction, a classical symbolic learning problem where precise mechanisms for feedback exist in the form of counterexamples. In counterexample-guided learning, a learner (LLM) proposes candidate regular expressions from positive/negative-labeled strings, and the teacher (verifier) returns counterexamples showcasing the difference between the candidate and target languages. We identify novel counterexample-guided refinement strategies that enable effective regex learning, such as regularization and symbolic counterexample clusters. We also explore agentic strategies such as reflection and repair loops. Empirically, we find that verifier feedback substantially improves sample efficiency on challenging regex-induction tasks, reducing the number of labeled examples required and enabling learning of complex target expressions where standard prompting fails. For example, on the hardest task groups, our counterexample-guided framework improves success from 3.2% to 38.1% and from 38.9% to 74.1% on two different regex domains. These results suggest that LLMs can benefit from rich feedback beyond treating it as additional data, opening the door for robust verifier-guided methods for LLM-based program synthesis and formal reasoning.

</details>


### [246] [APEX: A Network-Native Time-Series Foundation Model for Forecasting and Anomaly Detection for Wireless Edge Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11553)
*Swadhin Pradhan,Niloo Bahadori,Peiman Amini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: APEX是一个面向企业无线网络遥测的decoder-only Transformer预测模型，通过在大规模生产网络数据上预训练，在DHCP退化预测任务上显著超越通用时序基础模型和传统统计方法，并支持云端和边缘端双形态部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用时序基础模型在无线网络遥测场景中迁移效果差，原因是网络信号具有突发性（bursty）、零膨胀（zero-inflated）以及跨协议层耦合（coupled across protocol layers）等独特特性，需要专门针对网络数据设计的预训练模型。

Method: 提出APEX——一个网络原生（network-native）的decoder-only Transformer架构，在约4,500个生产无线网络（约10万AP时间序列，每AP含34个指标）的10通道多变量遥测数据上进行预训练。提供两个版本：APEX-Large（269M参数，云端部署）和APEX-Edge（10.5M参数，边缘端部署）。

Result: 在192步（4天）的DHCP退化预测基准上，APEX-Large相比最强基础模型基线Toto的MAE降低18%，相比SARIMA降低38%；异常检测F1分数达到0.93。APEX-Edge可在AP级边缘硬件上实现亚秒级、隐私保护的推理。

Conclusion: 网络原生预训练是构建主动式无线运维（proactive wireless operations）的可行基础，APEX展示了领域特定预训练在无线网络遥测任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Generic time-series foundation models transfer poorly to wireless network telemetry whose signals are bursty, zero-inflated, and coupled across protocol layers. We present APEX, a network-native, decoder-only transformer for forecasting enterprise AP telemetry, and evaluate it on DHCP degradation as a representative network task. APEX is pre-trained on 10-channel multivariate telemetry from ~4,500 production wireless networks (~100K AP time series, 34 metrics per AP), and is available as APEX-Large (269M, cloud) and APEX-Edge (10.5M, edge). On a 192-step (4-day) DHCP degradation benchmark, APEX-Large reduces MAE by 18% over the strongest foundation-model baseline (Toto) and 38% over SARIMA, with anomaly-detection F1 = 0.93, while APEX-Edge enables sub-second, privacy-preserving inference on AP-class edge hardware. These results suggest network-native pre-training is a practical foundation for proactive wireless operations.

</details>


### [247] [GraphInfer-Bench: Benchmarking LLM's Inference Capability on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11562)
*Zhuoyi Peng,Jingzhou Jiang,Hanlin Gu,Lixin Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphInfer-Bench基准，用于评估LLM能否执行图推理（即生成无法从单一节点或路径检索的开放式答案）。现有图QA协议均无法测试该能力。该基准包含5种任务、42000个样本，覆盖6个真实图。评测显示，无论是图-令牌对齐模型、前沿LLM、Graph2Text SFT还是普通GNN，均无法完全弥合这一能力差距，其中普通GNN在多数任务上匹配或超越最强LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图分析中许多应用（如洗钱网络、药物重定位、用户偏好推断）需要从节点及其邻域进行推理，答案无法从单节点或单一路径获取。然而，现有的图QA协议（算法模拟、节点分类、单节点描述、KG-QA、GraphRAG等）本质上都允许从单节点或沿路径检索答案，无法真正测试LLM的图推理能力。因此需要一个新的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建GraphInfer-Bench基准：定义两类共五种任务——描述类（关系推断、主题识别、掩码节点预测）和比较类（离群检测、社区划分）。每个样本的ground truth均不驻留于任何单节点。通过自动化生成配合四层质量控制协议，从六个真实世界图中产生42,000个样本。评测四类方法：图-令牌对齐模型、零样本前沿闭源LLM、Graph2Text监督微调、以及作为结构参照的普通GNN。

Result: 无一方法族能闭合能力差距。图-令牌对齐模型部分处理描述任务（关系、主题），但在比较任务上崩溃；前沿LLM在离群检测和社区划分上领先于其他LLM方法，但在掩码节点预测上落后；Graph2Text SFT是描述任务上最强的LLM方法，但在比较任务上不如前沿LLM；普通GNN在每项任务上匹配或超越最强LLM方法，尤其在社区检测上优势最大。

Conclusion: 图推理是一个开放的能力缺口，而非某一特定架构的固有属性。GraphInfer-Bench揭示了当前LLM在图结构推理方面的系统性不足，呼吁社区关注这一未被充分探索的方向。

Abstract: Graph analysis underlies many applications whose answers cannot be looked up in a single record or retrieved along a path: laundering rings, drug repurposing, user preference, and scientific theme are all inferred from a node together with its neighbourhood. We introduce GraphInfer-Bench, a benchmark for whether LLMs can perform this graph inference: producing an open-ended answer that no single node supports and no path retrieves. Existing graph-QA protocols cannot test this capability: algorithm simulation, node classification, single-node description, KG-QA, and GraphRAG all admit answers retrievable from one node or along a path. GraphInfer-Bench defines five tasks along Description (what a region is) and Comparison (how regions differ), each constructed so the ground truth lives in no single node. The release contains 42,000 samples across six real-world graphs, produced automatically and screened by a four-layer quality-control protocol. We evaluate four method families against the same tasks: graph-token alignment models, zero-shot frontier closed-source LLMs, Graph2Text supervised fine-tuning, and plain GNNs as a structural reference. No method family closes the gap. Graph-token alignment partially handles description tasks (relational, theme) but collapses on comparison tasks. Frontier LLMs lead on outlier detection and community partition among LLM-based methods but lag on masked-node prediction. Graph2Text SFT is the strongest LLM-based method on the description side yet falls behind frontier LLMs on comparison. Across every task, plain GNNs match or beat the strongest LLM-based row, with the largest margin on community detection. GraphInfer-Bench surfaces graph inference as an open capability gap rather than a property of any one architecture.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond the Golden Teacher: Enhancing Graph Learning through LLM-GNN Co-teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11583)
*Zhuoyi Peng,Hanlin Gu,Lixin Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-GNN协同教学框架，摒弃传统"金牌教师"假设，通过双向伪标签交换和基于轮次的偏好优化(RPL-PO)，在少样本图学习任务上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-GNN方法依赖"金牌教师"假设，指定一个模型作为教师监督另一个。但在少样本稀疏监督下，GNN和LLM各有盲区：GNN对冷节点失效，LLM对文本歧义节点失效。将任一模型视为金牌教师会将其盲区传递给学生，因此需要一种不预设金牌教师的协同学习框架。

Method: 提出LLM-GNN Co-Teaching双向协同教学框架：(1)GNN和LLM在架构特定的小损失准则下交换各自最置信的伪标签，每轮同步更新；(2)提出Round-based Pseudo-Label Preference Optimization (RPL-PO)：当节点从第t轮的跨模型矛盾变为第t+1轮的跨模型一致时，LLM的两次回答构成偏好对(旧的矛盾自回答 < 新的同伴认可的回答)，用于DPO训练。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，LLM-GNN Co-Teaching一致优于GNN-as-Judge及所有先前方法。Cora数据集3-shot绝对提升7.86%，ogbn-arxiv提升7.73%；优势延续到5-shot场景和零样本跨数据集迁移。误差结构分析表明，放弃金牌教师假设显著提升了LLM对困难样本的图学习能力。

Conclusion: 放弃金牌教师假设、采用双向协同教学是解决少样本图学习中GNN与LLM互补短板的更优范式，RPL-PO机制有效利用了模型间的矛盾-一致轨迹来挖掘监督信号。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs) underlie real-world applications such as citation networks, social media, and e-commerce. Few-shot graph learning on TAGs is hard: with only a handful of labels per class and the rest of the graph unannotated, neither GNNs nor LLMs can learn well on their own. GNNs read topology and fail on cold nodes; LLMs read text and fail on text-ambiguous nodes. Existing LLM-GNN methods all follow the same recipe: designate one model as the golden teacher and use its outputs (e.g., features or pseudo-labels) to supervise the other. We argue this golden-teacher assumption breaks under sparse supervision: neither model is golden, and treating either as such transfers its blind spots into the student. We therefore ask: can we avoid designating either model as the golden teacher, and still perform effective graph learning? We answer with LLM-GNN Co-Teaching, a bidirectional co-teaching framework in which neither model is fixed as teacher. The GNN and LLM exchange their most confident pseudo-labels under an architecture-specific small-loss criterion, and both update every round. Supervision is then mined from the trajectory: whenever a node moves from cross-model contradiction at round t to cross-model agreement at round t+1, the LLM's two answers on the same input form a preference pair (old contradicting self < new peer-endorsed self) for DPO training. We call this Round-based Pseudo-Label Preference Optimization (RPL-PO). On six benchmarks, LLM-GNN Co-Teaching consistently outperforms GNN-as-Judge and all prior methods, with absolute 3-shot gains of 7.86% on Cora and 7.73% on ogbn-arxiv; improvements carry over to 5-shot and to zero-shot cross-dataset transfer. Error-structure analysis further shows that abandoning the golden-teacher assumption substantially improves the LLM's graph learning capability on challenging samples.

</details>


### [249] [Kuramoto Attention: Synchronizing Self-Attention on the Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11585)
*Joshua Nunley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kuramoto注意力机制，将自注意力的隐状态建模为角度（环面），利用门控余弦相似度评分、基于相位状态的注意力聚合，以及受Kuramoto模型启发的正弦耦合更新。该层在紧致群上定义了不变相似度与流形内均值，并在enwiki8字符级语言建模上取得了与RoPE+SwiGLU Transformer相当的性能，建立了自注意力与相位同步之间的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自注意力机制与相位同步动力学（Kuramoto模型）建立形式上的对应关系，探索在紧致流形（特别是环面）上定义注意力层的可能性，从而为Transformer架构提供几何结构化的替代方案，并揭示注意力与同步现象之间的深层联系。

Method: 提出Kuramoto注意力层：每个隐坐标为角度值（在环面上）。评分采用门控余弦相似度（gated cosine similarity），对前序相位状态做注意力加权，更新规则取注意力加权圆均值的切向分量，恰好等价于Kuramoto耦合项 ∑_u A_{t,u} sin(θ_u - θ_t)。注意力矩阵充当自适应、内容依赖的耦合核。门控评分可视为环面上的学习度量。该框架推广至任意紧致群，环面为阿贝尔情形，相似度与均值均有闭式解。softmax权重求解熵正则化相位恢复问题，旋转位置编码作为位置依赖的相位漂移融入评分。

Result: 在enwiki8字符级语言建模上，1M参数规模下Kuramoto注意力达到1.637±0.010 BPC，接近RoPE+SwiGLU Transformer的1.616±0.004（差距0.02以内）；5M参数规模下中位数相当（1.448 vs 1.452），均值略逊（1.468 vs 1.456）。消融实验分离了关键组件的作用。

Conclusion: 该几何约束结构在百万参数规模下是可行的语言模型，其核心贡献在于结构本身及其同步解读。Kuramoto注意力为自注意力与相位同步之间提供了紧凑的桥梁。

Abstract: We introduce Kuramoto attention, a self-attention layer in which each hidden coordinate is an angle. The layer scores tokens by gated cosine similarity, attends over previous phase states, and updates each token by the tangent component of the attention-weighted circular mean. Because the values are the raw phase states, this update is exactly the Kuramoto coupling term $\sum_u A_{t,u}\sin(θ_u-θ_t)$, with the attention matrix acting as an adaptive, content-dependent coupling kernel. Equivalently, the gated score is a learned metric on the torus that selects which tokens couple, and the update pulls each token toward the circular mean of the tokens it selects, tightening their phase agreement. The same two ingredients, an invariant similarity score and an on-manifold mean, define such a layer on any compact group; the torus is the abelian case, where both are closed-form. The softmax weights solve an entropy-regularized phase-retrieval problem, and rotary position enters as a position-dependent phase drift in the score. On enwiki8 character-level language modeling, the layer trains as a functional language model whose bits-per-character stays close to a strong matched RoPE+SwiGLU transformer: within $0.02$ BPC at one million parameters ($1.637\pm0.010$ versus $1.616\pm0.004$) and level on the median at five million ($1.448$ versus $1.452$ over five seeds) with the transformer ahead on the mean ($1.468$ versus $1.456$). These experiments establish that the constrained geometric structure is a viable language model at this scale; the structure itself, and its synchronization reading, is the contribution. Ablations isolate the load-bearing components, and the result gives a compact bridge between self-attention and phase synchronization.

</details>


### [250] [Physics-Distilled Neural Network enabled by Large Language Models for Manufacturing Process-Property Predictive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11605)
*Ge Song,Kiarash Naghavi Khanghah,Anandkumar Patel,Rajiv Malhotra,Hongyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种知识蒸馏框架，将大语言模型从科学文献中提取的解析物理先验融入特权教师模型，通过图掩码注意力层捕捉输入变量间的复杂物理依赖，再蒸馏至轻量级学生预测器，实现数据稀缺场景下的高精度、可解释的工艺-性能关系预测，且学生模型推理频率超6000Hz，适合工业边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业中预测工艺-性能关系面临两大挑战：一是高昂的实验成本导致数据稀缺，二是复杂"黑箱"模型缺乏可解释性。现有方法难以在少量数据下同时兼顾高精度与物理可解释性，亟需一种能利用已有物理知识辅助建模的框架。

Method: 提出一种知识蒸馏框架：(1) 利用大语言模型(LLM)从科学文献中系统提取解析物理先验；(2) 将物理先验融入特权教师模型，采用图掩码注意力层(Graph-Masked Attention layer)捕捉输入变量间的复杂物理依赖关系（如严格设定点、静态与高频时序特征的组合）；(3) 将特权知识蒸馏至轻量级学生预测器用于推理。验证采用重复K-fold交叉验证评估小数据集下的模型稳定性和泛化能力。

Result: 在五种不同制造工艺上的综合实验表明：(1) 该框架在所有评估领域均实现了高预测精度；(2) 即使LLM提取的解析先验为次优或不完整时，架构仍表现出显著的容错能力并维持鲁棒预测性能；(3) 学生预测器推理频率超过6000Hz，可在标准工业硬件上实现实时边缘部署。

Conclusion: 该工作为数据受限环境下弥合理论物理与实时工业监控之间的鸿沟提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，验证了将LLM提取的物理知识与图神经网络架构结合、并通过知识蒸馏实现高效推理的技术路线的可行性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Predicting process-property relationships in manufacturing is often challenged by high experimental costs and the limited interpretability of complex 'black-box' models. This paper proposes a novel knowledge distillation framework designed to achieve high-accuracy predictions in data-scarce scenarios. The framework integrates analytical physics priors, which are systematically extracted from scientific literature via Large Language Models, into a privileged teacher model. We employ a Graph-Masked Attention layer to capture the complex physical dependencies among input variables showing strict setpoints or a combination of static and high-frequency temporal signatures. This privileged knowledge is distilled into a lightweight student predictor for inference. The feasibility and robustness of the framework are evaluated through a comprehensive experiment across five diverse manufacturing processes. To ensure statistical reliability, given the small dataset sizes, a repeated K-fold cross-validation technique is employed to quantify model stability and generalization. Results indicate that the proposed framework consistently achieves high predictive accuracy across all evaluated domains. Most importantly, the architecture demonstrates significant fault tolerance by maintaining robust predictive performance even in scenarios where LLM-derived analytical priors are suboptimal or incomplete. Furthermore, the student predictor achieves an inference frequency exceeding 6000 Hz, which facilitates real-time edge deployment on standard industrial hardware. This work provides a scalable solution for bridging the gap between theoretical physics and real-time industrial monitoring in data-limited environments.

</details>


### [251] [Information-Theoretic Decomposition for Multimodal Interaction Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11614)
*Zequn Yang,Yake Wei,Haotian Ni,Zhihao Xu,Di Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的系统性分析，揭示了多模态学习中冗余、独特和协同信息等交互类型在样本层面动态变化的关键性，并指出了传统范式的不足。为此，作者提出了基于分解的多模态交互学习框架（DMIL），通过变分分解架构和新的学习策略，显式建模并学习样本特定的多模态交互，在多种任务上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习中，模态间的冗余、独特和协同信息构成了多模态交互，但这些隐式交互在不同样本间动态变化，传统方法未能有效捕捉这种样本特定的交互模式：模态集成方法难以捕获协同信息，而联合学习范式常未充分利用冗余信息。因此，亟需一种能够按样本自适应学习不同交互类型的方法。

Method: 提出DMIL框架：(1) 设计变分分解架构（variational decomposition architecture），将多模态信息显式分解为冗余、独特和协同等交互成分；(2) 提出新的学习策略，在微调过程中利用这些显式交互成分实现全面的交互学习，使模型能够按样本自适应地捕获和学习不同类型的多模态交互。

Result: 在多种任务和多种架构上的广泛实验表明，DMIL通过适应整体的样本特定交互，一致性地取得了优越的性能表现，验证了该框架的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: DMIL建立了一种以交互为中心的多模态学习范式，框架灵活且广泛适用，为多模态学习中的样本特定交互建模提供了有效解决方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal learning hinges on capturing redundant, unique, and synergistic information across modalities, which collectively constitute multimodal interactions. A critical yet underexplored challenge is that these implicit interactions vary dynamically across samples. In this work, we present the first systematic, information-theoretic analysis highlighting why learning these dynamic, sample-specific interactions is critical for effective multimodal learning. Our analysis further reveals deficits in conventional paradigms at learning these distinct interaction types: modality ensemble approaches struggle to capture synergy, while joint learning paradigms often under-utilize redundant information. This highlights the need for an approach that can adaptively learn from different interaction types on a per-sample basis. To this end, we propose Decomposition-based Multimodal Interaction Learning (DMIL), a novel paradigm that explicitly models and learns from sample-specific interactions. First, we design a variational decomposition architecture to isolate the constituent interaction components. Second, we employ a new learning strategy that leverages these explicit interaction components in a fine-tuning process to achieve comprehensive interaction learning. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks and architectures demonstrate that DMIL consistently achieves superior performance by adapting to holistic sample-specific interactions. Our framework is flexible and broadly applicable, establishing an interaction-centric paradigm for multimodal learning. The code is available at https://github.com/GeWu-Lab/DMIL.

</details>


### [252] [DeMix: Debugging Training Data with Mixed Data Error Types by Investigating Influence Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11616)
*Jiale Deng,Yanyan Shen,Xiaogang Shi,Chai Junjun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeMix 是一个训练数据调试框架，通过影响向量捕获不同错误类型在模型行为上的独特模式，将错误检测与错误类型识别建模为多标签分类问题，并引入基于干预的学习策略来提升分类器泛化能力，在数据调试F1分数上比现有方法提升22.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据集常混合存在标签错误、特征错误和虚假相关等多种错误类型，现有数据清洗和归因方法无法同时检测错误样本并识别其具体错误类型，导致难以进行针对性修复。因此需要一种能统一诊断错误样本及其错误类型的新方法。

Method: 提出DeMix框架，核心思路是不同错误类型会在模型行为上产生不同模式。首先通过影响向量（influence vectors）刻画每个训练样本对所有验证样本预测的影响，从而捕获错误特异性模式；然后将训练数据调试形式化为多标签分类问题，训练一个分类器直接从影响向量预测错误类型；进一步提出基于干预的学习策略（intervention-based learning），引导分类器捕获每种错误类型特有的不变原理（invariant rationales），确保泛化性。

Result: 在表格数据预测、推荐系统和LLM对齐共11个任务上的实验表明，DeMix显著优于现有最优方法：数据调试F1分数提升22.61%，数据修复后任务模型性能提升9.32%。

Conclusion: DeMix通过影响向量建模错误类型特异性模式，结合多标签分类和干预学习策略，有效实现了训练数据中错误样本检测与错误类型识别的统一框架，在多种任务和数据类型上均展现出优越的调试与修复能力。

Abstract: High-quality training data is essential for the success of machine learning models. However, real-world datasets often contain mixed types of errors arising from systematic flaws in data preparation pipelines, including label errors, feature errors, and spurious correlations. Effective debugging of training data requires both detecting erroneous samples and identifying their specific error types to enable targeted repair, yet existing data cleaning and attribution methods fail to adequately address this dual requirement. In this paper, we propose DeMix, a novel framework that simultaneously diagnoses erroneous samples and their error types. Our key insight is that different error types produce distinct patterns on model behavior. DeMix captures such error-specific patterns by influence vectors that characterize how each training sample affects model predictions across all validation samples. We formulate training data debugging as a multi-label classification problem where a classifier is developed to predict error types directly from influence vectors. We further introduce an intervention-based learning strategy that guides the classifier to capture invariant rationales specific to each error type, ensuring the learned classifier generalizes effectively. Empirical evaluations on 11 tasks across tabular data prediction, recommendation systems, and LLM alignment demonstrate that DeMix significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, achieving a 22.61% improvement in data debugging F1-score and a 9.32% gain in task model performance after data repair. Code is available at: https://github.com/SJTU-DMTai/DeMix.

</details>


### [253] [TimeRouter: Efficient and Adaptive Routing of Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11625)
*Kanghui Ning,Yushan Jiang,Kashif Rasul,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Dongjin Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeRouter 是一种轻量级路由框架，通过判别式路由、选择性门控和集成回退机制，在不依赖LLM的情况下从多个预训练时间序列基础模型（TSFM）中自适应选择最佳专家，在GIFT-EVAL排行榜上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFM）存在异质的归纳偏置，没有单一模型能在所有预测场景下持续占优。现有系统通常依赖LLM控制器进行专家选择，导致推理开销巨大。因此需要一种高效、轻量且不依赖LLM的TSFM路由机制。

Method: TimeRouter 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 可学习的路由头（discriminative routing），用于从TSFM池中识别最合适的专家；(2) 选择性门控（selective gating），判断路由置信度是否足够，避免低质量路由决策；(3) 集成回退（ensemble fallback），当门控判定路由不确定时，回退到集成预测以保证鲁棒性。整个推理过程无需调用LLM。

Result: TimeRouter 在 GIFT-EVAL 排行榜上取得最优性能（LB MASE = 0.6765）。消融实验揭示了TSFM路由设计中的关键因素，包括模型池组合和选择性门控的重要性。

Conclusion: TimeRouter 作为一个模块化、轻量级的路由层，验证了基于经验互补性的TSFM路由策略的有效性，为未来基于基础模型池的agentic时间序列系统提供了可复用的路由基础设施。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) are increasingly explored as predictive experts within emerging agentic time-series systems. However, TSFMs exhibit heterogeneous inductive biases, and no single model consistently dominates across forecasting regimes, making expert selection a critical challenge. Existing systems often delegate this decision to LLM-based controllers, incurring substantial inference overhead. We present TimeRouter, an efficient routing framework that leverages empirical complementarity across a pool of pretrained TSFMs through lightweight discriminative routing, selective gating, and ensemble fallback. Concretely, TimeRouter combines a learned routing head, a selective gate, and an ensemble fallback, enabling adaptive expert selection without invoking an LLM at inference time. TimeRouter achieves state-of-the-art performance on the GIFT-EVAL leaderboard, with an LB MASE of 0.6765. Beyond benchmark performance, our ablation studies provide empirical insights into TSFM routing design, highlighting the importance of pool composition and selective gating. Taken together, these results position TimeRouter as a modular and lightweight routing layer for future agentic time-series systems built upon foundation-model pools. Our code is available at https://github.com/UConn-DSIS/TimeRouter.

</details>


### [254] [When Context Returns: Toward Robust Internalization in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11627)
*Xun Wang,Ruishuo Chen,Zhuoran Li,Yu Chen,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别出"上下文诱发退化"现象——将特权上下文重新引入已蒸馏学生模型反而降低性能，并提出一种基于KL散度的一致性正则化方法来增强"上下文可移除性"，有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的on-policy蒸馏方法虽能让学生模型内化特权上下文（如系统提示、任务提示），从而在推理时不再需要上下文，但作者发现一个此前未被研究的现象：将原始上下文重新引入已蒸馏模型时，性能反而下降，甚至在模型已正确求解的样本上也是如此。这揭示了现有蒸馏方法在"上下文可移除性"上的不足，即学生模型不仅要匹配教师的条件化行为，还需在上下文重新引入时保持稳定。

Method: 提出一种轻量的一致性正则化方法：首先通过stop-gradient锚定学生模型的无上下文输出，然后以前向KL散度惩罚上下文条件化输出与无上下文输出之间的偏差。该方法每训练步仅需一次额外的前向传播。

Result: 在涵盖多种领域和模型家族的12种配置上进行实验，该方法在大多数设置中提升了上下文条件化准确率，在12种设置中的11种上减少了上下文诱发损害，并有效消除了回复长度膨胀。机制性案例研究表明，上下文可移除性在表示层面得以实现，无论上下文是否存在，隐状态几乎保持一致。

Conclusion: 上下文诱发退化是on-policy蒸馏中一个普遍且重要的问题，所提出的一致性正则化方法能有效缓解该问题，同时提升无上下文性能，为模型鲁棒内化特权信息提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that on-policy distillation can internalize privileged context, such as system prompts or task hints, into a student model so that the context is no longer needed at inference time. Although this approach successfully improves the student's no-context performance, we identify an interesting and previously unstudied phenomenon: in many settings, reintroducing the original privileged context to the distilled student actually degrades its performance, even on instances it already solves correctly without context. We term this context-induced degradation and argue that robust internalization demands not only matching the teacher's context-conditioned behavior, but also remaining stable when the context is reintroduced, a property we call context removability. Motivated by this observation, we propose a lightweight consistency regularizer that first anchors the student's no-context output via stop-gradient, then penalizes the context-conditioned output for deviating from it via forward KL divergence. This simple addition requires only one extra forward pass per training step, yet it effectively mitigates context-induced degradation and, in many cases, even improves no-context performance. Across 12 configurations spanning diverse domains and model families, our method improves context-conditioned accuracy in the majority of settings, reduces context-induced harm in 11 out of 12 settings, and effectively eliminates response-length inflation. A mechanistic case study further confirms that context removability is achieved at the representation level, with hidden states remaining nearly identical regardless of whether the context is present.

</details>


### [255] [TAROT: Task-Adaptive Refinement of LLM-prior Graphs for Few-shot Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11640)
*Ruxue Shi,Yili Wang,Mengnan Du,Hangting Ye,Yi Chang,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAROT提出了一种基于GNN的小样本表格学习框架，通过构建和优化任务自适应语义图来编码特征间的结构先验和语义先验，利用LLM推断特征语义关系并引入图精炼机制消除幻觉噪声，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小样本表格学习方法存在明显不足：传统方法需要额外训练数据，计算开销大；基于LLM的方法直接输入原始表格存在隐私与合规风险。更重要的是，两种范式都忽略了特征之间的语义关系——这种关系能为构建语义图提供结构和语义先验，对于建模小样本场景下有意义的特征交互至关重要。

Method: TAROT框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 统一语义表格节点编码器（USTNE），将异构表格数据编码为统一的节点语义表示；(2) 利用LLM根据任务描述和特征名称推断特征间语义关系，构建初始语义图；(3) 任务自适应语义图精炼机制，剪枝虚假或任务无关的边，补充缺失的任务相关边，使图结构与下游任务对齐；最后通过GNN在精炼后的图上进行消息传递，捕获任务相关的语义依赖关系进行预测。

Result: 在多个小样本表格学习基准上的广泛实验表明，TAROT取得了优越性能，确立了该领域的SOTA方法地位。

Conclusion: TAROT通过将结构先验与LLM推断的语义先验相结合，并引入图精炼机制缓解LLM幻觉问题，有效提升了小样本表格学习的预测性能，为该领域提供了新的SOTA方案。

Abstract: Few-shot tabular learning provides a cost-effective approach for real-world applications where annotation is costly and collecting sufficient samples for new tasks is difficult. Existing Traditional and LLM-based methods have demonstrated effectiveness in few-shot scenarios. However, traditional methods need additional training on unlabeled or generated data, which incur significant computational overhead. In addition, LLM-based methods that directly feed raw tabular data into LLMs raise privacy and compliance concerns. More importantly, both paradigms largely overlook the semantic relationships between features, which provide structural and semantic prior for constructing a semantic graph. Semantic graph is essential for modeling meaningful feature interactions in few-shot scenarios. In this paper, we propose TAROT, a GNN-based framework that encodes the structural and semantic prior by constructing and refining a task-adaptive semantic graph from this prior, thereby improving predictive performance in few-shot tabular learning. TAROT first encodes heterogeneous tabular data into unified node semantic representations via a Unified Semantic Tabular Node Encoder (USTNE). Then, it prompts LLMs to infer the semantic relationship between features based on the task description and feature names to construct a semantic graph. To mitigate structural noise introduced by the hallucination of LLMs, TAROT introduces Task-adaptive Semantic Graph Refinement that prunes spurious or task-unrelated edges and adds missing task-related ones, aligning the graph structure with the downstream objective. Finally, a GNN performs message passing over the refined graph to capture task-related semantic dependencies for prediction. Extensive experiments on various few-shot tabular learning benchmarks demonstrate the superior performance of TAROT, establishing it as a state-of-the-art approach in this domain.

</details>


### [256] [Structure-Preserving Neural Surrogates with Tractable Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11650)
*Handi Zhang,Adrienne M. Propp,Brooks Kinch,Houman Owhadi,Nathaniel Trask*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构保持的数据驱动降阶模型，将混合有限元空间与高斯过程回归相结合，在保持物理守恒结构的同时提供闭式后验不确定性估计，实现了偏微分方程的实时代理求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习虽然能实现PDE的近实时求解，但缺乏传统模拟器支持验证与确认(verification and validation)的理论基础。作者旨在构建既能实时运行又能保持物理结构、同时提供不确定性量化的代理模型。

Method: 作者利用外微分(exterior calculus)揭示的拓扑结构，在H(div)-L²子空间（基于Raviart-Thomas元和dgP₀元）上构建高斯过程表示状态-通量关系中的不确定性。通过轻量级Transformer预设子空间，将守恒律中的通量建模为GP。训练被表述为最优恢复问题(ORP)，转化为带等式约束的优化问题，并采用Schur补快速训练策略。最终得到Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射的闭式后验估计器。

Result: 模型可实时求解，并提供边界通量的闭式估计器。文中给出了线性泛函的RKHS后验误差界以支持不确定性量化，数值实验验证了后验分布作为误差估计代理的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过混合有限元空间与GP回归的新型接口，成功构建了结构保持、可实时运行且具备不确定性量化能力的降阶模型，弥补了科学机器学习在验证与确认理论方面的不足。

Abstract: Recent advances in scientific machine learning provide a means of near-real-time solution to partial differential equations (PDEs), but lack the theoretical underpinnings of conventional simulators that support contemporary verification and validation. In this work, we construct data-driven reduced-order models that serve as structure-preserving, real-time surrogates. Remarkably, the exterior calculus that imposes physical conservation structure also exposes topological structure that we use to build a Gaussian process (GP) representation of uncertainty in state-flux relationships, ultimately yielding a Dirichlet-to-Neumann map for quantities of interest with closed-form expressions for posterior uncertainty. We specifically propose structure-preserving $H(\mathrm{div})$--$L^2$ subspaces of conventional Raviart--Thomas and $dgP_0$ elements prescribed by a lightweight transformer. Reduced-order dynamics consistent with this subspace are learned by posing a conservation law in which a GP describes the fluxes between volumes. This work hinges on a novel interface between mixed FEM spaces and GP regression; when training is posed as the optimal recovery problem (ORP), the resulting GP regression can be written as an optimization problem with equality constraints that impose a conservation structure, amenable to a fast Schur-complement training strategy. The trained model can then be solved in real time with closed-form estimators for boundary fluxes driven by prescribed Dirichlet data. The paper includes RKHS posterior error bounds for linear functionals to support uncertainty quantification, as well as numerical experiments demonstrating the accuracy of the posterior distribution as a surrogate for error estimation.

</details>


### [257] [DeepRHP: A Hybrid Variational Autoencoder for Designing Random Heteropolymers as Protein Mimics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11651)
*Shuni Li,Zhiyuan Ruan,Andy Shen,Ivan Jayapurna,Ting Xu,Haiyan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepRHP，一种半监督改进变分自编码器模型，通过融合化学特征约束的潜空间来指导合成随机杂聚物（RHP）的设计，成功预测了稳定膜蛋白（如水通道蛋白Z）的单体组成，并与已发表实验结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成随机杂聚物（RHP）具有模拟蛋白质行为的潜力，但缺乏高效的计算工具来指导其设计。现有方法难以同时捕捉RHP序列模式与关键化学特征，亟需一种能够整合多维度信息的混合建模框架。

Method: 提出DeepRHP模型，在经典VAE基础上增加一个基于特征的VAE，构成半监督混合架构。该双VAE框架强制潜空间同时捕获关键化学特征的结构信息与个体RHP序列模式，允许以混合方式灵活纳入任意相关特征。

Result: 利用DeepRHP预测了能在非天然环境中稳定水通道蛋白Z（Aquaporin Z）等膜蛋白的单体组成，预测结果与已发表的实验数据高度一致，验证了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: DeepRHP模型与真实RHP功能之间的一致性表明，混合自编码器架构在指导蛋白质及其他生物化合物的RHP设计方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Synthetic random heteropolymers (RHPs), consisting of a predefined set of monomers, offer an approach toward the design of protein-like materials. These RHPs, if designed appropriately, can mimic protein behavior and function. As such, there is a need for computational tools to efficiently guide RHP design. We bridge this gap by developing DeepRHP, a modified variational autoencoder (VAE) model under a semi-supervised framework. By equipping a classical VAE with an additional feature-based VAE, DeepRHP forces the latent space to capture structures of critical chemical features as well as individual RHP sequence patterns. In this sense, our method is versatile by allowing any relevant features to be incorporated in a hybrid manner. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DeepRHP by suggesting potential monomer compositions that stabilize membrane proteins (e.g. Aquaporin Z) in non-native environments and cross-validating our prediction with published results. The concordance between our model and true RHP function suggests strong potential in utilizing hybrid autoencoder architectures to guide RHP design for proteins and other biological compounds.

</details>


### [258] [IAPO: Input Attribution-Aware Policy Optimization for Tool Use in Small Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11652)
*Yifan Yang,Zhen Zhang,Jiayi Tian,Liyan Tan,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IAPO算法，通过将小模型的输入归因与强教师模型对齐，以强化学习方式提升多模态小语言模型在工具调用中的决策能力，解决了传统精确匹配奖励在多模态场景下稀疏、脆弱的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态工具调用RL方法依赖精确匹配的二元奖励，存在两个核心问题：(1) 多模态任务中有效工具调用路径不唯一且缺乏标注轨迹，精确匹配假设不成立；(2) 稀疏的二元奖励无法为SLM的决策过程提供有效学习信号，训练尤其困难。

Method: 提出Input Attribution-Aware Policy Optimization (IAPO)，一种RL算法，通过将多模态SLM对输入各组件（图像区域、文本token等）的归因（attribution）分布与更强的教师模型对齐，引导模型关注最相关的输入证据，从而优化工具调用策略。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-3B上实验，IAPO在六个测试集上平均提升视觉问答准确率3%，优于现有视觉工具调用方法。

Conclusion: 通过归因对齐的方式为SLM提供更丰富的学习信号，相比传统稀疏奖励能更有效地提升多模态小模型的工具调用与视觉推理能力。

Abstract: This paper investigates reinforcement learning (RL) methods for improving tool-calling capabilities in multimodal small language model (SLM) agents. While existing works have explored various reward designs to improve agentic tool-calling ability, these approaches face inherent limitations for SLM training, especially under multimodal scenarios. First, many existing methods evaluate tool use correctness through exact matching against certain ground-truth or predefined formats. However, this assumption is often unsuitable for multimodal tasks, where multiple tool use paths may be valid and annotated tool trajectories are typically unavailable. Second, such sparse and brittle binary rewards provide little guidance on how to improve the underlying decision process, making them particularly difficult for multimodal SLM to learn from. To address these issues, we propose Input Attribution-Aware Policy Optimization (IAPO), an RL algorithm for improving tool use in multimodal SLM by aligning the model's attribution across input components with that of a stronger teacher. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B show that the proposed method improves visual question answering accuracy by an average of 3% across six test sets compared with existing visual tool use work, by helping the model attend to the most relevant input evidence.

</details>


### [259] [Sparse probes and murky physics: a case study of interpretability challenges in a foundation model for continuum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11657)
*Katherine Rosenfeld,Maike Sonnewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机制可解释性方法（稀疏自编码器SAE）探究科学基础模型Walrus在模拟连续介质动力学时的内部表征，发现特征使用呈现分段一致性与间歇性，且模型输出与数值模拟存在系统性偏差，讨论了科学基础模型在可解释性评估方面的开放性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当生成式AI仿真器能够复现已知的连续介质动力学时，其内部机制是否与已知物理一致、以及与仿真器成功/失败的关系尚不清晰，这构成了评估与可解释性的核心问题。

Method: 以剪切流为简单测试平台，对Polymathic的跨领域连续介质动力学基础模型Walrus的某一选定层应用稀疏自编码器（SAE）进行探测；使用基于物理的拟涡能（enstrophy）作为度量指标，对超过20,000个特征进行筛选分诊；对比不同参数设置下剪切流模拟中的特征招募模式，并将SAE特征使用变化与仿真器输出偏差进行关联分析。

Result: 特征使用存在分段一致性：部分特征子集在不同设置中以相似角色重复出现，但该结构呈间歇性且无法清晰映射到标准物理分解；仿真器与数值模拟之间存在系统性输出偏差（能量/结构过于弥散或过于集中），这些偏差与特定SAE特征使用的变化存在关联。

Conclusion: 科学基础模型面临三大开放性问题：如何鲁棒地优先筛选具有机制意义的特征；如何区分稳定结构与分析伪影（包括单层分析和SAE的局限性）；如何利用已有基准判断"不同"的内部表征何时是真正有信息量的，而非仅仅是有效的。

Abstract: Generative AI emulators are increasingly used in scientific domains where we already have strong theory, benchmarks, and physical intuition. This raises a central evaluation and interpretability question: when a foundation-style model can reproduce known continuum dynamics, what internal mechanism supports that behavior, is the internal behaviour consistent with known physics, and how does it relate to where the emulator succeeds or fails? We investigate a cross-domain foundation model for continuum dynamics, Walrus by Polymathic, using mechanistic interpretability guided by physical principles. We apply a sparse autoencoder (SAE) to probe a selected layer, and address the practical challenge of triaging a large feature set (over 20,000) using enstrophy as a physically grounded metric. As a deliberately simple testbed, we focus on shear flow and compare feature recruitment across multiple shear-flow setups, i.e. parameter values in the numerical simulation. Across setups we find evidence of piecewise consistency, with subsets of features recurring in similar roles, but this structure is intermittent and does not map cleanly onto standard physical decompositions. In parallel, direct comparisons between numerical simulation and the emulator reveal systematic output-level discrepancies, including regimes where energy/structures become too diffuse or too localized. We connect parts of these discrepancies to changes in specific SAE feature usage. Our work highlights open questions for scientific foundation models: how to robustly prioritize mechanistically meaningful features, how to separate stable structure from analysis artifacts (including single-layer and SAE limitations), and how to use established benchmarks to decide when "different" internal representations are genuinely informative rather than merely effective.

</details>


### [260] [Bergson: An Open Source Library for Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11660)
*Lucia Quirke,Louis Jaburi,David Johnston,William Z. Li,Gonçalo Paulo,Guillaume Martres,Girish Gupta,Stella Biderman,Nora Belrose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bergson 是一个开源数据归因库，面向大规模语言模型和预训练数据集，提供 MAGIC、SOURCE、TrackStar 三种前沿方法的首次开源实现，支持磁盘梯度存储和多节点分布式训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据归因虽在可解释性领域前景广阔，但大规模实施需要巨大工程投入，且许多前沿技术缺乏开源工具和支持，阻碍了该领域的快速发展。

Method: 发布 Bergson 开源库，原生支持磁盘梯度存储（on-disk gradient stores）和多节点分布式训练，集成 MAGIC、SOURCE 和 TrackStar 三种领先数据归因方法的首次开源实现，并提供面向研究人员的生活质量辅助工具。

Result: 提供了一个可扩展至超大规模语言模型和预训练数据集的统一数据归因工具库，降低了研究者使用前沿归因技术的工程门槛。

Conclusion: Bergson 通过开源生态填补了数据归因领域的工具空白，有望加速该领域的研究进展，使大规模数据归因更加易用和可复现。

Abstract: Data attribution is a promising field in interpretability that aims to explain model behavior through the influence of its training data, with applications including debugging undesirable model behavior and training dataset curation. However, significant engineering effort is required to perform it at scale, and many cutting edge techniques lack open-source tooling and support. Bergson is an open source library that aims to enable faster progress in the field by providing a host of techniques that scale to very large language models and pre-training datasets. The library natively supports on-disk gradient stores and multi-node distributed training, and provides quality of life tools for researchers. Finally, we introduce the first open-source implementations of three leading data attribution methods: MAGIC, SOURCE, and TrackStar. The library is available at https://github.com/EleutherAI/bergson .

</details>


### [261] [Spectrally Regularized Latent Flow Matching for Turbulence Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11691)
*Khalid Rafiq,Aditya G. Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种谱正则化的潜在流匹配框架，通过在压缩阶段引入分区加权对数谱目标函数，解决了现有潜在扩散/流匹配模型系统性低估耗散区振幅的问题。在Re_f≈2250的256² DNS数据集上，该方法将深层耗散区保留谱功率从25%提升至94%（重建）和从20%提升至79%（无条件生成），并显著改善了采样代价-保真度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在扩散和流匹配方法在合成湍流时，系统性地低估了耗散区（高波数）的振幅。这一问题根源于传统MSE训练的VAE在压缩阶段对小尺度结构的保真度不足，导致生成模型存在根本性的质量天花板，无法通过增加采样步数或更换积分器来克服。

Method: 提出潜在流匹配框架，核心创新在于压缩阶段使用分区加权对数谱目标函数替代传统MSE损失来训练VAE。具体而言：(1) 构建谱正则化VAE，按波数分区加权对数谱损失，强制保留高波数分量；(2) 在正则化潜在空间中执行流匹配生成；(3) 通过编码器-解码器交换实验和支持-振幅分解进行机制分析，揭示改进来源为编码器诱导的潜在重组织，并将MSE模型定性为"保守抑制模型"。

Result: 在Re_f≈2250的256² DNS数据集上：(1) 重建任务中深层耗散保留谱功率从25%提升至94%；(2) 无条件生成中从20%提升至79%；(3) MSE潜在空间存在DD bias≈-0.70的质量天花板，而谱正则化潜在空间在仅20次函数评估即达DD bias=-0.117；(4) 两种管线均能恢复二阶结构函数及正确的S_3符号，表明无需显式监督即可捕捉正确的级联方向；(5) S_3幅度存在残余差距，表明相位相干的三角结构仍是未来改进方向。

Conclusion: 谱正则化压缩阶段是解决生成湍流模型耗散区振幅失真的有效手段，其改进主要来自编码器诱导的潜在空间重组织。MSE-VAE本质上是"保守抑制模型"——通过衰减间歇性高波数结构来最小化逐点误差。正确的级联方向可无监督习得，但振幅保真度与相位相干的三角组织需作为互补维度共同关注。

Abstract: Latent diffusion and flow matching have emerged as leading approaches for synthetic turbulence generation, yet they systematically under-represent dissipation-range amplitudes. We introduce a latent flow matching framework with a spectrally regularized compression stage that directly targets this failure mode. On a 256^2 DNS dataset at Re_f \approx 2250, replacing an MSE-trained VAE with a zone-weighted log-spectral objective raises deep-dissipation retained spectral power from 25% to 94% in reconstruction and from 20% to 79% in unconditional generation. The improved latent representation also yields a substantially better sampling cost-fidelity tradeoff: the MSE-trained latent space imposes a fundamental quality ceiling near DD bias -0.70 that no integrator or step-count can overcome, while the spectrally regularized latent space reaches DD bias -0.117 at just 20 function evaluations. Mechanistically, encoder-decoder swap experiments show that the improvement is driven primarily by encoder-induced latent reorganization rather than decoder capacity, while a support-amplitude decomposition reveals that MSE-trained models behave as conservative suppression models, minimizing pointwise error by attenuating intermittent high-wavenumber structure. Both pipelines recover the second-order structure function and the correct sign of S_3, indicating the correct cascade direction without explicit supervision. A small residual gap in the magnitude of S_3 suggests that phase-coherent triadic organization remains a complementary axis to amplitude fidelity for future generative turbulence models.

</details>


### [262] [Noise-Aware Framework for Correcting Corrupted Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11695)
*Ha-Linh Nguyen,Hong-Anh Nguyen,Minh-Duc La,Phong Lam,Thu-Trang Nguyen,Son Nguyen,Hieu Dinh Vo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CANOLA 是一种通过噪声感知学习和迭代标签精炼来纠正数据集中损坏标签的框架。它显式估计数据集的噪声分布，并将其融入深度神经网络训练，使模型能够弱化不可靠监督信号。通过谨慎的迭代软标签精炼，逐步修复数据集。在六个数据集上的实验表明，CANOLA 始终优于现有标签校正方法，错误减少相对提升 19% 至 52%，且校正后的数据可显著提升下游模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据集常含有大量损坏标签，严重降低 ML/DL 模型性能。现有方法在标签纠错过程中缺乏对噪声特性的显式建模，容易过早做出错误修正。需要一个能稳健估计噪声分布并渐进式修复标签的框架。

Method: 提出 CANOLA 框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 噪声感知学习——显式估计数据集的底层噪声分布（如噪声转移矩阵），将该信息注入深度神经网络训练，使模型自动降低不可靠监督信号的权重；(2) 谨慎的迭代软标签精炼——将模型预测与原始观察标签进行混合（软标签形式），以渐进、受控的方式更新标签，避免过早或错误的修正，实现数据集的稳定修复。

Result: 在六个广泛使用的数据集上，于真实噪声标注场景下进行评估。CANOLA 始终优于当前最先进的标签校正方法，误差减少方面取得 19% 至 52% 的相对提升。此外，使用 CANOLA 校正后数据训练的模型在下游任务中获得显著性能增益——即使是简单分类器在 CANOLA 校正数据上训练后，也能以高达 67% 的优势超越复杂的以模型为中心的方法。

Conclusion: CANOLA 证明了显式噪声分布建模与迭代软标签精炼相结合是处理标签噪声的有效范式。其数据中心的标签修复思路能以较小的模型复杂度换取大幅性能提升，为噪声标签场景下的鲁棒学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: High-quality labeled data is essential for training reliable ML/DL models. However, real-world datasets often contain a considerable proportion of corrupted labels, which can severely degrade model performance. To address this problem, we propose CANOLA, a novel framework for correcting corrupted labels through noise-aware learning and iterative label refinement. CANOLA explicitly estimates the underlying noise distribution of the dataset and incorporates this information into the training of a noise-aware Deep Neural Network. By incorporating noise characteristics during learning, CANOLA enables the model to down-weight unreliable supervision signals and focus on trustworthy patterns, thereby improving robustness and generalization. Label correction is performed via cautious, iterative soft label refinement, in which model predictions are blended with observed labels to prevent premature or erroneous updates. This progressive refinement allows the dataset to be repaired in a stable and controlled manner. We evaluate CANOLA on six widely used datasets under realistic noisy labeling scenarios. Experimental results show that CANOLA consistently outperforms SOTA label correction methods, achieving relative improvements ranging from 19% to 52% in error reduction. Moreover, models trained on datasets corrected by CANOLA obtain substantial downstream performance gains. Even simple classifiers trained on CANOLA's corrected data can outperform complex model-centric approaches by margins of up to 67%.

</details>


### [263] [A Data-Centric Framework for Detecting and Correcting Corrupted Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11699)
*Ha-Linh Nguyen,Hong-Anh Nguyen,Minh-Duc La,Thu-Trang Nguyen,Son Nguyen,Hieu Dinh Vo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Relabeler 是一个端到端的数据中心框架，通过联合利用局部和全局数据关系来检测并纠正噪声标签，在多个数据集和噪声条件下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界数据集的标签质量常因噪声标签而受损，严重影响模型准确性和可靠性，现有方法在检测和纠正噪声标签方面仍有不足。

Method: 提出 Relabeler 框架：(1) 噪声检测阶段，联合利用数据实例间的局部关系（临近样本相似性）和全局关系（整体数据结构）识别可疑噪声样本；(2) 标签纠正阶段，基于输入特征和观测噪声标签，估计每个实例最可能的干净标签进行修正。

Result: 在多个数据集、多种噪声类型和噪声率下，Relabeler 一致优于最先进基线方法，标签纠正精度最高提升58%，下游任务性能提升6%。

Conclusion: Relabeler 作为一种数据中心方法，能够有效检测并纠正噪声标签，显著提升下游模型性能，证明了联合利用局部与全局关系进行标签质量治理的有效性。

Abstract: The performance of machine learning and deep learning models largely depends on the quality of the training data. However, the quality of the real-world datasets is often compromised by noisy labels, which can substantially degrade model accuracy and reliability. To address this challenge, we propose Relabeler, an end-to-end data-centric framework for detecting and correcting corrupted labels. For corrupted label detection, Relabeler jointly leverages both local and global relationships among data instances to identify potentially noisy samples. After detecting suspicious instances, Relabeler further performs label correction by estimating the most probable clean label for each instance based on both its input features and observed noisy label. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, noise types, and noise rates demonstrate that Relabeler consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 58% improvement in label correction precision and 6% improvement in downstream task performance.

</details>


### [264] [RLCSD: Reinforcement Learning with Contrastive On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11709)
*Leyi Pan,Shuchang Tao,Yunpeng Zhai,Lingzhe Zhang,Zhaoyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Aiwei Liu,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了On-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）中存在"特权诱导的风格漂移"问题——学习信号集中在风格token而非任务token上，并提出RLCSD方法，通过对比正确提示与错误提示下的师生分布差距来抑制风格漂移，使信号聚焦于任务关键token，在多个模型和推理任务上超越了GRPO及现有OPSD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD虽然通过将模型自身分布与特权上下文下的分布对齐来提供token级监督，但作者发现学习信号主要来源于风格token（如输出更直接、更短）而非任务核心token，这种"特权诱导的风格漂移"会破坏训练稳定性或导致回复长度异常缩短，亟需一种方法来抑制这种风格层面的虚假信号。

Method: 提出RLCSD（Reinforcement Learning with Contrastive on-policy Self-Distillation）。核心思路是构建对比：计算正确提示下的师生分布差距（teacher-student gap），同时计算错误提示下的同样差距，将二者进行对比，从而抵消掉"只要有提示就会产生的风格偏移"这一共同成分，使得剩余的学习信号更集中于与任务推理真正相关的token上。该方法可即插即用地集成到现有OPSD方法中。

Result: 在Qwen3（1.7B/4B/8B）和Olmo-3-7B-Think模型上，于数学推理和逻辑推理任务中，RLCSD一致地超越了GRPO基线及先前的OPSD方法。此外，对比原则具有良好的泛化性：可直接嵌入现有OPSD方法以提升其性能，且其核心洞察可扩展到更广泛的跨模型在线蒸馏场景。

Conclusion: 特权诱导的风格漂移是OPSD方法中的一个关键但此前未被充分认识的问题。通过对比式在线自蒸馏（RLCSD）可以有效抑制风格层面的虚假学习信号，将监督集中在任务相关token上，从而稳定训练并提升推理性能。该对比原则具有普适性，有望推动在线蒸馏方法的进一步发展。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense, token-level supervision for reasoning models by aligning a model's own distribution with the distribution it produces under privileged context, typically a verified solution. However, we show that the learning signal drawn from this distributional gap concentrates on style tokens rather than task-bearing ones, as the hinted model tends to produce more direct, shorter outputs. We term this pathology \emph{privilege-induced style drift}, which destabilizes training or causes response length to shrink. To address this, we propose \textbf{RLCSD} (Reinforcement Learning with Contrastive on-policy Self-Distillation), which mitigates this drift by contrasting the teacher-student gap under a correct hint against that under a wrong hint, suppressing the style shift that conditioning on a hint tends to induce regardless of correctness, and yielding a signal that is more concentrated on task-bearing tokens. Experiments on Qwen3 (1.7B/4B/8B) and Olmo-3-7B-Think across mathematical and logical reasoning show that RLCSD consistently outperforms GRPO and prior OPSD methods. We further show that the contrastive principle is general: it plugs into existing OPSD methods to improve them, and its underlying insight extends to the broader cross-model on-policy distillation setting.

</details>


### [265] [ICA Lens: Interpreting Language Models Without Training Another Dictionary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11722)
*Sida Liu,Feijiang Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了独立成分分析（ICA）在大语言模型可解释性中的应用，提出 ICALens——一个结合 GPU 并行 FastICA、稳定性配方和诊断工具的实用工作流。在 GPT-2 Small、Gemma 2 2B 和 Qwen 3.5 2B 上，ICA 无需逐层梯度训练即可恢复紧凑、人类可解释的方向，且在 SAEBench 基准上，稀疏探测与公开 SAE 竞争，在小中预算的目标探测扰动中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）已成为模型可解释性的标准工具，但其需要训练、存储和评估大型过完备字典，成本高昂，限制了快速探索。核心直觉是：许多可解释方向在 token 上具有选择性，应呈现非高斯分布。因此，作者重新审视 ICA 这一经典非高斯方向发现方法，认为其在 LLM 可解释性中被低估。

Method: 提出 ICALens，首个面向 LLM 表征的实用 ICA 分析工作流：1）优化的 GPU 并行 FastICA 管线；2）LLM 特定的稳定性配方（处理激活的脆性问题）；3）改进的拟合诊断工具；4）支持高效的逐层分析。

Result: 在 GPT-2 Small、Gemma 2 2B 和 Qwen 3.5 2B Base 上，ICALens 无需逐层梯度字典训练即可恢复紧凑、人类可解释的方向。在 SAEBench 上，稀疏探测与公开 SAE 竞争，在小到中等预算的目标探测扰动中超越 SAE。

Conclusion: ICA 不应被视为弱基线，而应作为探索语言模型表征的高效且互补的第一视角。ICALens 为快速、可审计的 LLM 可解释性分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Finding interpretable directions in language-model representations is critical for understanding and controlling model behavior. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become the standard tool for this purpose, but using them as the default first lens often requires training, storing, and evaluating large overcomplete dictionaries. This bottleneck limits rapid exploration and raises a fundamental question: how much interpretable structure is already visible from activation geometry before training another neural dictionary? Our intuition is simple: many interpretable directions are selective on tokens, and these directions should look less Gaussian than random directions. We therefore revisit independent component analysis (ICA), a classical method for finding non-Gaussian directions, as a compact lens for language-model interpretability. We find that ICA has been underestimated for LLM interpretability, because prior uses often relied on off-the-shelf ICA implementations that are brittle on LLM activations and lacked systematic tools for inspecting and evaluating the recovered directions. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ICALens, the first practical workflow for stable, efficient, and auditable ICA analysis of LLM representations. It combines an optimized GPU-parallel FastICA pipeline with LLM-specific stability recipes and better fitting diagnostics, enabling efficient and reliable layer-wise analysis. Across GPT-2 Small, Gemma 2 2B, and Qwen 3.5 2B Base, ICALens efficiently recovers compact, human-interpretable directions without per-layer gradient-based dictionary training. On SAEBench, ICA is competitive with public SAEs in sparse probing and outperforms them in targeted probe perturbation under small-to-medium budgets. These results suggest that ICA should not be viewed as a weak baseline, but as an efficient and complementary first lens for exploring language-model representations.

</details>


### [266] [RCAP: Robust, Class-Aware, Probabilistic Dynamic Dataset Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11761)
*Atif Hassan,Swanand Khare,Jiaul H. Paik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RCAP 是一种鲁棒的、类别感知的概率动态数据剪枝算法，通过闭式解估计每个类别应保留的样本比例，并利用自适应采样策略优先选取高损失样本，在平衡与不平衡数据集上均能保持强最差组准确率，仅用10%数据即可在不平衡数据集上超越全数据训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态数据剪枝方法在高剪枝率下难以维持良好的最差组准确率（worst-group accuracy），尤其在类别不平衡数据集中表现不佳。因此需要一种能够在平衡与不平衡数据集上均保持鲁棒性的动态剪枝方法。

Method: 提出 RCAP 算法：(1) 采用闭式解（closed-form solution）按类别估计每轮训练应保留的样本比例，该比例通过类别聚合损失（class-wise aggregated loss）在每个 epoch 自适应调整；(2) 使用自适应采样策略，在每个类别子集中优先选取高损失样本进行填充。

Result: 在六个数据集（从类别平衡到高度不平衡）、五种模型、三种训练范式（从头训练、迁移学习、微调）上评估，RCAP 在所有剪枝率下均优于现有最优方法。仅用 10% 数据即可在不平衡数据集上比全数据训练提升 >1% 性能，同时平均加速 8.69 倍。

Conclusion: RCAP 通过类别感知和概率剪枝策略，有效解决了动态数据剪枝在最差组准确率上的瓶颈，在不平衡场景下以极低数据量即可超越全数据训练，显著降低了计算开销。

Abstract: Dynamic data pruning techniques aim to reduce computational cost while minimizing information loss by periodically selecting representative subsets of input data during model training. However, existing methods often struggle to maintain strong worst-group accuracy, particularly at high pruning rates, across balanced and imbalanced datasets. To address this challenge, we propose RCAP, a Robust, Class-Aware, Probabilistic dynamic dataset pruning algorithm for classification tasks. RCAP applies a closed-form solution to estimate the fraction of samples to be included in the training subset for each individual class. This fraction is adaptively adjusted in every epoch using class-wise aggregated loss. Thereafter, it employs an adaptive sampling strategy that prioritizes samples having high loss for populating the class-wise subsets. We evaluate RCAP on six diverse datasets ranging from class-balanced to highly imbalanced using five distinct models across three training paradigms: training from scratch, transfer learning, and fine-tuning. Our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art dataset pruning methods, achieving superior worst-group accuracy at all pruning rates. Remarkably, with only $10\%$ data, RCAP delivers $>1\%$ improvement in performance on class-imbalanced datasets compared to full data training while providing an average $8.69\times$ speedup. The code can be accessed at https://github.com/atif-hassan/RCAP-dynamic-dataset-pruning

</details>


### [267] [AI4Land: Scalable Deep Learning for Global High-Resolution Land Use Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11793)
*Amirpasha Mozaffari,Marina Castaño,Stefano Materia,Etienne Tourigny,Oscar Molina-Sedano,Jordi Varela-Agrelo,Dario Garcia-Gasulla,Miguel Castrillo Melguizo,Mario Acosta,Amanda Duarte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI4Land 是一个基于 U-Net 的数据驱动框架，通过融合粗分辨率情景数据与静态地球物理特征，重建高分辨率土地利用/土地覆盖历史数据并预测未来动态生物物理变量，最终以开源模拟器形式为数字孪生平台提供实时陆地表面条件，旨在降低气候预测中的碳循环不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陆地碳循环的不确定性是气候预测的主要制约因素之一，其根源在于地球系统模式中陆地表面表征和变率的局限性。现有模式难以生成高分辨率、时空连续的陆地表面变量数据，尤其在缺乏直接观测的历史时段和未来情景中。因此，迫切需要一种能够重建历史、预测未来、且与数字孪生平台实时耦合的高分辨率陆地表面模拟方法。

Method: AI4Land 框架采用两阶段 U-Net 架构：第一阶段聚焦于土地利用/土地覆盖的逐年高分辨率重建，通过将粗分辨率情景数据与静态地球物理特征（如地形、土壤等）进行融合，模型基于地球观测数据训练，学习空间显式且物理一致的陆地表面模式，并外推至无直接观测时段；第二阶段（计划中）将利用第一阶段生成的高分辨率地图，预测更精细时间尺度的动态生物物理变量，特别是叶面积指数（LAI）。整个框架在 GPU 加速的 HPC 平台 MareNostrum5 上开发与训练。

Result: AI4Land 在 MareNostrum5 上成功实现了全球尺度的气候 AI 流程，验证了 GPU 加速 HPC 基础设施支撑大规模陆地表面模拟的可行性。最终产出为一套开源模拟器，能够按需生成真实且动态演化的陆地表面条件，并具备与 Destination Earth 等数字孪生平台实时耦合的能力。

Conclusion: AI4Land 展示了一种结合深度学习与 HPC 的有效范式，能够生成高分辨率、物理一致的陆地表面重建与预测数据。通过开源模拟器与数字孪生平台的实时耦合，该工作有望显著降低陆地碳循环模拟中的关键不确定性，提升下一代气候模拟的预测能力。

Abstract: Uncertainty in the terrestrial carbon cycle remains a major constraint in climate projections, partly driven by the uncertainties affecting the land surface representation and variability in Earth system models. To address this limitation, we present a data-driven framework AI4Land, for generating high-resolution historical reconstructions and future projections of key land surface variables. The framework follows a two-phase approach using a U-Net architecture. In the first phase, which is the focus of this work, it reconstructs annual land use and land cover by integrating coarse-resolution scenario data with static geophysical features. In a planned second phase, the resulting high-resolution maps will be used to predict dynamic biophysical variables, particularly leaf area index, at finer temporal scales. Trained on Earth observation data, the models learn to reproduce spatially explicit and physically consistent land surface patterns, extending temporal coverage to periods lacking direct observations. AI4Land was developed and trained on MareNostrum5, demonstrating how GPU-accelerated HPC infrastructure enables global-scale climate AI pipelines. The final product is a suite of open-source emulators designed for real-time coupling with digital twin platforms, such as those developed under the Destination Earth initiative. By delivering realistic and evolving land surface conditions on demand, this work aims to reduce critical uncertainties and improve the predictive power of next-generation climate simulations.

</details>


### [268] [Multimodal Ordinal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Severity Using Structural MRI and Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11794)
*Boris-Stephan Rauchmann,Jonathan Laib,Buse Ercik,Robert Perneczky,Sergio Altares-López*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合注意力机制与序数回归的多模态机器学习框架，整合T1加权MRI、人口统计学和遗传数据，用于阿尔茨海默病严重程度的自动化、可解释分期，在多个数据集上验证并展示了优异的预测性能和临床一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前阿尔茨海默病临床分期工具（如CDR量表）耗时长、主观变异性大，亟需准确、可扩展且可解释的自动化分期方法。

Method: 提出注意力增强的多模态机器学习框架，整合T1加权MRI与人口统计学/遗传变量，对比单模态/多模态架构及序数/非序数预测头；采用ADNI、AIBL、NIFD数据集的队列分层划分，严格保留测试集，全部使用受试者级别划分防泄漏；可解释性分析采用Grad CAM++和SHAP。

Result: 单模态中MRI模型相邻阶段准确率0.963、QWK 0.444，略优于表格模型（QWK 0.433）；多模态非序数基线MAE最低（0.340），多模态序数模型相邻阶段准确率最高（0.970）、QWK最强（0.549）。可解释性分析显示解剖学和临床合理的模型行为。

Conclusion: 序数回归形式能更好捕捉CDR量表的序数结构，注意力多模态学习结合序数回归是AD自动化分期的稳健、可解释且可扩展的方案，适用于AI辅助临床决策。

Abstract: Neurodegenerative diseases such as Alzheimer's disease (AD) require accurate and scalable tools for assessing disease severity, yet current clinical staging remains time-intensive and prone to variability. We propose an attention-enhanced multimodal machine learning framework with ordinal regression for automated and interpretable AD severity staging. The framework integrates T1-weighted MRI with demographic and genetic variables and compares unimodal and multimodal architectures using ordinal and non-ordinal prediction heads. Models were trained and validated using cohort-stratified splits derived from the ADNI, AIBL, and NIFD datasets. A strictly held-out test set was constructed using subjects excluded from all training, validation, preprocessing, and hyperparameter tuning procedures, with subject-level splitting employed throughout to prevent data leakage. Among unimodal approaches, the T1-weighted MRI model achieved slightly higher adjacent-stage accuracy (0.963) and agreement with clinical staging (QWK 0.444) than the tabular model (QWK 0.433). Integrating imaging, demographic, and genetic information improved overall performance. The multimodal non-ordinal baseline achieved the lowest prediction error (MAE 0.340), whereas the ordinal multimodal model achieved the highest adjacent-stage accuracy (0.970) and strongest agreement with clinical staging (QWK 0.549). These findings indicate that ordinal formulations better capture the ordered structure of the CDR scale and yield predictions more consistent with clinical staging. Explainability analyses using Grad CAM++ and SHAP demonstrated anatomically and clinically plausible model behavior, supporting transparent decision-making. Overall, attention-based multimodal learning with ordinal regression represents a robust, interpretable, and scalable approach for automated AD severity staging and AI-assisted clinical decision support.

</details>


### [269] [Space-sampled Value Decay: Forgetting Mechanisms for Non-stationary Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11797)
*Felix Störck,Fabian Hinder,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为“空间采样值衰减”（Space-sampled Value Decay）的显式遗忘机制，用于应对非平稳强化学习中的环境漂移问题，在DQN和SAC上验证了其有效性及局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前非平稳强化学习方法通常需要任务ID或上下文等漂移的（部分）完美信息，而啮齿动物实验表明在无漂移信息（不确定性）下也能通过遗忘机制适应环境变化。因此，作者希望开发一种无需漂移先验知识的显式遗忘机制来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出Space-sampled Value Decay方法，作为一种显式遗忘机制嵌入基于值的深度强化学习架构（DQN和SAC），通过对价值函数进行空间采样的衰减来缓解环境漂移的影响。

Result: 在非平稳环境中，改进后的DQN和SAC展示了正向效果，但也存在回报表现上的局限性。

Conclusion: Space-sampled Value Decay是一种简单而有效的显式遗忘方法，能够在不依赖漂移先验信息的情况下提升深度RL在非平稳环境中的适应性，但仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Studies on rodents such as mice have shown the capabilities to adapt their behavior when dealing with changing parameters (``drift'') of the environment even if no information about change is provided (uncertainty) -- a behavior that can be modeled by forgetting mechanisms. Non-stationary Reinforcement Learning (NSRL) deals with adapting state-of-the-art RL methods to deal with changing environments: these however usually require (partially) perfect information about the drift such as ``task IDs'' or ``context''. To mitigate the effects of drift, this work develops \emph{Space-sampled Value Decay} as an explicit forgetting mechanism for value-based deep RL architectures as a simple yet effective approach. In particular we demonstrate and discuss positive effects but also limitations in achieved returns for modifications of Deep Q-networks (DQN) and Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) when evaluated on non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [270] [From Uniform to Learned Graph Priors: Diffusion for Structure Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11831)
*Qi Shao,Hao Guo,Jiawen Chen,Duxin Chen,Wenwu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Diff-prior，一种基于扩散模型的自适应先验，用于校准神经关系推断（NRI）中的潜在图分布，将先验整合重构为可学习的去噪式校准过程，使边后验更加确定，提升结构发现的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NRI方法使用过于简化的因子化图先验（接近均匀分布），将边视为独立实体，与现实系统不匹配，导致边后验模糊且不确定，限制了结构发现的可靠性。

Method: 提出Diff-prior，将先验整合重新定义为可学习的去噪式校准：在结构采样前，扩散模型作为去噪校准器直接作用于编码器边分布，将分散不确定的边后验组织为更可靠的全局结构，可通用于多种NRI架构的结构变量训练。

Result: 在标准基准上验证，Diff-prior在多个NRI家族架构上均提升了结构推断性能，并生成更确定的边后验。

Conclusion: Diff-prior通过扩散模型参数化的自适应先验，有效校准了边后验分布，解决了现有NRI方法先验失配问题，为结构化变量提供了通用训练范式。

Abstract: Neural relational inference (NRI) methods discover interaction graphs from trajectories through variational reasoning on discrete potential edges. However, these methods typically rely on oversimplified, factorized graph priors. Such priors, typically nearing uniform distributions, treat edges as independent entities. This systemic misalignment does not match the real-world systems and yields diffuse and indecisive edge posteriors limiting the reliability of structural discovery. To address this, we propose \textit{Diff-prior}, a diffusion-parameterized adaptive prior used to calibrate latent graph distribution rather than generate graphs. Our core insight is to reframe prior integration as a learnable denoising-style calibration that organizes scattered, uncertain edge posteriors into a more reliable overall structure which can be trained by the diffusion model. Diff-prior learns an adaptive structure prior that performs structured calibration on the edge posteriors during inference, guiding it towards a distribution closer to the underlying structure. The diff-prior operates before structural sampling and acts as a denoising calibrator directly on the encoder edge distribution, which provides a generic training paradigm over structured variables. Experiments on standard benchmarks validated our framework, and the results indicate that Diff-prior improves the performance of structure inference and generates more decisive edge posteriors across multiple NRI-family architectures. The code is available on https://github.com/Hardy158118/Diffprior.

</details>


### [271] [Flow Matching with In-Context Priors for Out-of-Distribution Brain Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11833)
*Sam Gijsen,Michał Łukomski,Marc-André Schulz,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向未见认知任务的全脑皮层fMRI动态生成模型——一种基于每时间步条件化的扩散Transformer架构。该模型通过注入组合式语言描述和可选的空间先验，实现了零样本fMRI脑动态生成，为反事实神经科学与数据驱动实验设计开辟了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经时间序列生成模型大多局限于类别条件化（categorical conditioning），无法实现组合式（compositional）和零样本（zero-shot）泛化。这阻碍了反事实神经科学的发展——即在经验验证前，通过计算机模拟设计与评估新型认知实验。因此，需要一种能够对未见认知任务生成逼真fMRI脑动态的生成模型。

Method: 提出一种每时间步条件化的扩散Transformer（per-timestep conditioned diffusion transformer）。核心思路是将两种条件信号注入生成过程：(1) 组合式语言描述——用于零样本指定未见认知任务；(2) 可选的空间先验——在语言表征不足的任务空间区域提供锚定。模型在大规模训练后，可在推理时仅凭语言描述（或辅以空间先验）生成对应认知任务的全脑fMRI动态。

Result: 在数百个保留任务条件上评估显示：(1) 仅凭语言输入，模型即可恢复各任务对应的脑区特异性招募模式（region-specific recruitment）及保留空间激活模式；(2) 空间先验在语言表征退化的任务空间区域补足了生成质量；(3) 空间先验的引入并未破坏语言通路所保持的组合结构，从而保留了反事实任务规范所需的关键特性。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个面向未见认知任务的全脑皮层fMRI动态生成模型，成功实现了零样本条件下的逼真脑动态生成。这为反事实神经科学（counterfactual neuroscience）和数据驱动的实验设计（data-driven experimental design）提供了新的技术基础，有望支持在计算机中预先设计与评估新型认知实验。

Abstract: Flow matching and diffusion models enable conditional generation across domains ranging from images to proteins, with recent extensions to out-of-distribution contexts. Yet generative models of neural time series have largely remained restricted to categorical conditioning, precluding compositional and zero-shot generalization. In this work, we propose a per-timestep conditioned diffusion transformer for generating realistic fMRI brain dynamics during unseen cognitive tasks by injecting both compositional language and optional spatial priors in-context. Such zero-shot generation could enable counterfactual neuroscience by supporting in-silico design and evaluation of novel cognitive experiments before empirical validation. Leveraging this model, we evaluate across hundreds of held-out task conditions and characterize predictive performance in relation to the training manifold. From language alone, the model recovers region-specific recruitment across tasks and held-out spatial activation patterns. Spatial priors, when available, complement the text pathway by anchoring generation in regions of task space where language alone degrades, while retaining the compositional structure needed for counterfactual task specification. To our knowledge this is the first generative model of whole-cortex fMRI dynamics for unseen cognitive tasks, advancing counterfactual neuroscience and data-driven experimental design.

</details>


### [272] [TaskFusion: Continual Anomaly Detection for Heterogeneous Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11844)
*Dayananda Herurkar,Federico Raue,Joachim Folz,Jörn Hees,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向异构表格数据的持续异常检测方法，包含AGF模型（共享空间映射与分布对齐）、Taskfusion增强（边界感知插值与跨任务混合）以及表格数据集蒸馏（压缩重放样本与异常暴露），在21个多领域异构数据集上显著优于序列微调及其他持续学习基线，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中数据从不同领域顺序到达，存在特征模式异构、分布偏移和严重类别不平衡等挑战。传统持续学习方法依赖固定输入空间，无法处理异构表格数据的持续异常检测任务。

Method: 1) AGF模型：将任务特定特征映射到共享空间，对齐分布以减少表征漂移，并在对齐空间中学习异常决策边界；2) Taskfusion增强：结合任务内边界感知插值以细化异常边界，以及跨任务混合以跨数据集迁移异常结构；3) 表格数据集蒸馏：存储压缩的合成重放样本，与增强数据联合用于异常暴露目标，实现鲁棒异常检测。

Result: 在21个多领域异构数据集上评估，方法在持续异常检测性能上大幅优于序列微调和其他持续学习基线，同时减少灾难性遗忘，保持跨异构数据集的稳定检测。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效解决了异构表格数据场景下的持续异常检测难题，通过共享空间对齐、数据增强与蒸馏重放三大组件的协同，实现了高稳定性、低遗忘的持续学习能力。

Abstract: Continual anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging and remains largely underexplored, particularly in settings with heterogeneous feature schemas, distribution shifts, and severe class imbalance. In many real-world applications, data arrive sequentially from diverse domains, rendering conventional continual learning methods ineffective due to their reliance on a fixed input space. We propose a continual learning (CL) method, which can overcome these challenges and continually learn from different tasks. Our method consists of three main parts: our AGF model, Taskfusion augmentation, and outlier exposure. The AGF-model maps task-specific features into a shared space, then aligns distributions to reduce representation drift, and learns anomaly decision boundaries in the aligned space. To improve stability, we introduce Taskfusion augmentation, combining boundary-aware interpolation within tasks to refine the model anomaly boundaries and cross-task mixing to transfer anomaly structure across datasets. To handle class imbalance and memory constraints, we employ tabular dataset distillation to store compact synthetic replay samples, which are jointly used with augmented data in an outlier exposure objective for robust anomaly detection. We evaluate the approach on 21 heterogeneous datasets across multiple domains. Results show that our approach substantially improves continual anomaly detection performance over sequential fine-tuning and other CL baselines while reducing catastrophic forgetting and maintaining stable detection across heterogeneous datasets.

</details>


### [273] [Fine-tuning Multi-modal LLMs with ART: Art-based Reinforcement Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11854)
*Michal Chudoba,Sergey Alyaev,Petra Galuscakova,Tomasz Wiktorski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ART（基于艺术的强化训练），一种新的参数高效微调方法：通过仅优化冻结多模态大语言模型的原始视觉输入（像素数组），将软提示信息注入模型，从而在预编译计算图上实现软令牌方法。该方法无需修改计算图，兼容高通量推理引擎如vLLM，在数学和结构化工具使用基准上达到与LoRA竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的PEFT方法（LoRA和Soft Prompting）都需要修改预编译、预优化LLM的计算图，导致它们无法在高吞吐量推理引擎（如vLLM）中得到完全支持。因此需要一种无需修改计算图即可实现参数高效微调的新方法。

Method: ART方法将信息注入冻结的MLLM，通过仅优化其原始视觉输入（纯像素数组）。具体而言，该方法通过将梯度反向传播到像素数组来实现优化，支持任意微调目标。优化后的视觉输入可被风格化为与任务相关的"计算艺术品"（computational artworks）。

Result: 在流行的开源Qwen架构的不同规模模型上，以及在多个文本基准测试中验证了方法的有效性。ART在数学和结构化工具使用基准上达到了与LoRA相竞争的准确率。

Conclusion: ART作为一种无需修改计算图的PEFT方法，通过优化视觉输入实现软提示的效果，成功兼容预编译推理引擎，同时保持与LoRA竞争的性能，为LLM的高效微调提供了新的实用方向。

Abstract: There are two main Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) techniques for Large Language Models (LLMs). While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) introduces additional weights between the LLM layers, Soft Prompting introduces additional fine-tuning-specific raw tokens to an LLM input. However, both require modification to the computational graphs of precompiled, preoptimized LLMs. As a result, neither is fully supported in high-throughput engines like vLLM. We propose fine-tuning with ART (Art-based Reinforcement Training). The method injects information into a frozen Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) by optimizing only its raw visual input, thus enabling the soft-token approach on pre-compiled computational graphs. It relies on backpropagation of gradients back into a plain pixel array and thus supports any fine-tuning objective. Moreover, the optimized visual input can be stylized as task-relevant computational artworks. The approach's effectiveness is confirmed for different sizes of a popular open Qwen architecture and for several textual benchmarks. Specifically, ART reaches accuracy competitive with LoRA across mathematics and structured-tool-use benchmarks.

</details>


### [274] [RePAIR: Predictive Self-Supervised Representation Learning in Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11860)
*Christoph Koller,Johannes Fürnkranz,Timo Bertram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RePAIR架构，融合MAE、JEPA和BERT的思想，通过迭代细化实现自监督表示学习，在象棋领域验证了其能学习到有意义的棋子移动概念，并支持直观的游戏轨迹分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将序列数据（如连续象棋棋局）中的对象编码为紧凑且有意义的表示，同时避免依赖昂贵的强化学习方法，使模型能够在低维嵌入空间中推理棋子移动。

Method: 提出RePAIR架构：(1) 类似BERT和MAE，对序列中的大部分潜在状态进行掩码；(2) 使用轻量级预测器（Predictor），在低维嵌入空间中修复序列中的空缺，类似于JEPA的方式；(3) 通过迭代细化（Iterative Refinement）进行自监督表示学习。

Result: 在象棋领域实验中：(1) 编码器能将棋盘表示细化为有意义的象棋概念，并在潜在空间中形成聚类；(2) 模型能推理棋子移动并重建被掩码的棋盘状态，无需强化学习；(3) 通过在语义丰富的表示空间中观察游戏路径轨迹，可实现快速直观的棋局分析。

Conclusion: RePAIR成功融合了多种自监督学习范式的优势，在象棋领域证明了其在无强化学习条件下学习有意义表示和推理序列动态的能力，为序列数据的表示学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Representation Prediction via Autoencoding using Iterative Refinement (RePAIR) - a novel self-supervised representation learning architecture that synthesizes Masked Autoencoders (MAE), Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA), and Bidirectional Encoder Representations from Transformers (BERT). We demonstrate how it can be used to encode objects in sequential data like consecutive chess positions into compact yet meaningful representations. The basic principle of the architecture is to mask large portions of a sequence of latent states, similar to BERT and MAE. Then, we apply a lightweight Predictor to the latent representations that repairs gaps in the sequence in a lower-dimensional embedding space akin to JEPA. Our experiments in the domain of chess show that the Encoder refines the board representations such that meaningful chess concepts emerge clustered in the latent space. Furthermore, reconstructions of the masked board states show that the model is able to reason about the piece movements without relying on costly reinforcement learning methods. Lastly, we find that the resulting representation space allows for quick and intuitive dissections of chess games by observing the game path trajectories in this semantically rich space.

</details>


### [275] [MemNovo: Look Back at the Spectrum for Balanced De Novo Peptide Sequencing from Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11868)
*Dongxin Lyu,Jingbo Zhou,Hongxin Xiang,Yuqiang Li,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有Transformer编解码器在肽段从头测序中的推理病理——过度依赖已生成序列先验而逐步忽视质谱输入，并提出一种无需训练的即插即用机制MemNovo，通过构建持久化频谱记忆库和超保守残差连接恢复解码器与原始频谱之间的互信息，显著提升了肽段测序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的肽段从头测序模型虽表现优异，但推理时存在严重缺陷：自回归解码器会越来越依赖已生成的序列先验，而逐步弱化对输入质谱中精细物理证据的利用，导致生成的肽段虽在生物学上看似合理却与输入谱图不忠实匹配。

Method: 提出MemNovo方法：(1)构建持久化频谱记忆库，保留整个输入频谱的特征；(2)在最终解码阶段通过超保守残差连接将检索到的频谱特征直接注入解码器状态；(3)该机制无需训练、即插即用；(4)理论分析证明该方法恢复了解码器状态与原始频谱之间的互信息。

Result: 在Nine Species基准数据集上，以Casanovo和InstaNovo为基线模型，MemNovo一致提升了氨基酸精度和肽段精度。Casanovo的肽段精度相对提升最高达39.1%，InstaNovo最高达3.9%，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: MemNovo有效解决了现有肽段解码器中序列先验与频谱证据之间的信息失衡问题，作为一种训练无关的轻量级插件，为提升肽段从头测序的可靠性提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: De novo peptide sequencing from tandem mass spectrometry is pivotal in proteomics, enabling identification of novel peptides without reference databases. While recent Transformer-based encoder-decoder models have achieved remarkable performance, we uncover a critical pathology in their inference dynamics. Through comprehensive feature scaling experiments, we demonstrate that existing auto-regressive peptide decoders tend to over-rely on generated-sequence priors while progressively under-utilizing fine-grained physical evidence from the input mass spectrum. This phenomenon leads to suboptimal results, where generated peptide sequences are biologically plausible yet not faithful to the input spectrum. To rectify this, we propose MemNovo, a training-free and plug-and-play mechanism that re-balances peptide and spectral contributions at inference time. MemNovo alleviates the information bottleneck by establishing a persistent spectral memory bank and injecting retrieved features directly into the final decoding stage via an ultra-conservative residual connection. Theoretical analysis confirms that this mechanism restores the mutual information between the decoder state and the raw spectrum. Extensive experiments on the Nine Species benchmark with two representative baselines, Casanovo and InstaNovo, demonstrate that MemNovo consistently improves both amino acid precision and peptide precision, achieving up to 39.1% relative improvement in peptide precision for Casanovo and up to 3.9% for InstaNovo, with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [276] [Beyond representational alignment with brain-guided language models for robust reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11893)
*Mingqing Xiao,Kai Du,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型（LLM）内部表征与人类大脑推理相关区域的神经信号既有对齐也存在分歧，并进一步提出了一种脑信号引导框架，可直接利用任务态fMRI信号增强LLM的演绎推理能力，在多个模型上实现了显著的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对大语言模型与人类高级认知神经机制之间的对应关系理解不足。已知语言与推理在人脑中可分离，因此一个开放问题是：LLM是否与推理相关脑区的神经信号对齐，以及这些信号能否反过来增强LLM的推理能力。

Method: 首先使用神经预测性指标，量化LLM内部表征对推理相关脑区fMRI活动的可解释方差。然后提出脑信号引导框架：利用模型表征与脑表征的联合结构导出方向，对模型表征进行引导——在推理阶段施加干预，在训练阶段进行微调。

Result: 在聚合层面，LLM解释了推理相关脑区中可观的可解释方差，但在特定推理类型上预测性较低，表明对齐与分歧并存。脑信号引导框架在10个LLM（1.5B-72B）上实现了推理增益，与纯语言监督正交，可跨推理类型迁移，绝对准确率提升最高达13%。

Conclusion: 该工作将LLM与大脑的对应关系从相关性层面推进到引导层面，建立了一条以脑信号为驱动的路径，朝着更鲁棒、更认知对齐的AI迈进。

Abstract: The correspondence between large language models (LLMs) and the neural mechanisms underlying human higher-order cognition remains insufficiently characterized. Given that language and reasoning in the human brain appear dissociable, an open question is whether LLMs align with neural signals from reasoning-related regions and whether such signals can improve them. Here, focusing on deductive reasoning, we show that LLM internal representations are not only partially aligned with task-fMRI activity but can also be directly enhanced by these signals.
