However, there exists no design principles to enforce this condensation. In this paper, we propose to align each router row with the principal singular direction of the associated expert, as this direction provides the most expressive mathematical description of a matrix. Based on this principle, we propose a router redesign with Manifold Power Iteration (MPI). Specifically, it introduces a "Power-then-Retract" paradigm, where a power iteration step is performed on the router weights, followed by a retraction to impose a norm constraint to ensure both efficiency and stability. Theoretically, we show that MPI drives router rows to converge toward the principal singular directions of associated experts. Empirically, we pretrain MoE model across scales from 1B to 11B parameters to confirm that this alignment facilitates more effective MoE models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [From Explicit Elements to Implicit Intent: A Predefined Library for Auditable Behavioral Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11207)
*Liu hung ming*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemantiClean是一个模块化框架，从电商会话数据中提取结构化语义信号，通过共享元素库驱动购买意图、客户细分和产品亲和力等推理目标，强调可审计性、结构治理和零方差可复现性，而非单纯追求预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端预测器仅优化准确性，缺乏透明度、可审计性和决策可追溯性。作者旨在构建一个优先考虑元素级透明度和可辩护决策轨迹的框架，以牺牲少量预测增益换取结构治理和可复现性。

Method: 基于OSPI数据集，将24个行为元素组织为四层架构（功能层、交互层、系统层、上下文层），并通过三种反通胀机制（RedundancyGroup贡献上限、TieredPenaltyCalculator偏差惩罚、AdaptiveConstraintMode冷启动保护）确保信号质量。引入LLM集成语义推理引擎，采用两阶段LLM驱动推理架构，在推理时利用完整元素元数据。确定性引擎输出保持sigma=0可复现；LLM依赖结果在固定设置下受控。

Result: 所有定量结果均由LLM集成语义推理引擎产生。确定性引擎输出完全可复现（sigma=0）；LLM依赖结果（E8、E10）在固定provider/model/temperature设置下保持受控输出变异。性别推理目标当前实现中不可用，已从所有定量结果中排除。

Conclusion: SemantiClean成功实现了模块化、可审计的语义信号提取框架，在透明性与预测精度之间做出明确的权衡取舍。该框架为电商行为分析提供了可追溯、结构化的决策路径，并通过反通胀机制保证了信号质量，但当前性别推理功能尚未实现。

Abstract: We present SemantiClean, a modular framework for extracting structured semantic signals from e-commerce session data and driving pluggable inference targets including purchase intent, customer segmentation, and product affinity through a shared element library. Unlike conventional end-to-end predictors that optimise solely for accuracy, SemantiClean prioritises auditability, structural governance, and sigma=0 reproducibility, explicitly trading marginal predictive gains for element-level transparency and defensible decision trails. Built upon the Online Shoppers Purchasing Intention (OSPI) dataset, the framework organises twenty-four behavioural elements into a four-layer architecture (Functional, Interaction, Systemic, Contextual) and enforces signal quality through three anti-inflation mechanisms: RedundancyGroup contribution caps, TieredPenaltyCalculator bias penalties, and AdaptiveConstraintMode cold-start protection.This report introduces the LLM-Integrated Semantic Inference Engine, a fully implemented two-phase LLM-driven inference architecture that leverages complete element metadata at inference time. All quantitative results reported herein are produced by this engine. Deterministic engine outputs remain fully reproducible (sigma=0); LLM-dependent results (E8, E10) are subject to controlled output variability under fixed provider/model/temperature settings. The gender inference target remains non-functional in the current implementation and is excluded from all quantitative results.

</details>


### [312] [Position: Hippocampal Explicit Memory Is the Cornerstone for AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11245)
*Sangjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，大语言模型的学习机制类似于人类内隐记忆，但要实现AGI所需的高阶认知功能（如战略规划、元认知、符号推理），必须整合类似于海马体功能的外显记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs虽在众多任务中表现卓越，但其底层学习机制本质上属于内隐统计学习，无法仅凭此产生AGI所需的高阶认知能力。作者旨在论证外显记忆整合是迈向AGI的关键基石。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），采用跨学科论证方法：借鉴神经科学中关于海马体外显记忆与内隐记忆分工的研究发现，类比分析LLMs的学习机制，并在此基础上提出人工外显记忆系统的计算需求。

Result: 论证得出：LLMs的统计学习高度类似人类内隐记忆，而长期战略规划、元认知和符号推理等高阶功能必须依赖外显记忆；仅靠扩展内隐学习机制无法弥合这一差距，需有目的地设计并集成外显记忆系统。

Conclusion: 外显记忆的整合是推进LLMs迈向AGI的核心方向。作者呼吁学界对此展开进一步研究，为外显记忆系统的集成奠定基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across various tasks, raising expectations for Artificial General Intelligence (AGI). This position paper argues that integrating explicit memory is the cornerstone for advancing LLMs toward AGI. The key reason is that the underlying learning mechanism of LLMs is highly analogous to human implicit memory. However, higher-order cognitive functions necessary for AGI, such as long-term strategic planning, metacognition, and symbolic reasoning, heavily rely on hippocampal explicit memory and cannot arise solely from implicit statistical learning. Drawing on findings from neuroscience, I advance this perspective and complement it with computational requirements for artificial explicit memory systems, hoping to foster further research and lay the groundwork for explicit memory integration.

</details>


### [313] [Can AI Agents Synthesize Scientific Conclusions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11337)
*Hayoung Jung,Pedro Viana Diniz,José Reinaldo Corrêa Roveda,Abner Fernandes da Silva,Haeun Jung,Enoch Tsai,Aleksandra Korolova,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciConBench是一个包含9,110个问题的大规模科学结论综合能力基准，通过原子事实分解和事实精确率/召回率来评估AI代理在开放域中综合科学结论的能力。研究引入SciConHarness洁净室评估框架以防止数据泄漏，评估8个前沿模型后发现最佳代理的F1仅为0.337，且面向消费者的AI代理常生成不完整甚至矛盾的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学AI代理越来越多地用于检索证据、跨源推理并综合对重大决策有影响的结论，但在健康等高风险领域，其结论综合能力尚不明确。此外，现有评估方法存在数据泄漏问题，导致模型真实综合能力被高估。

Method: 1) 构建SciConBench基准：基于系统综述的专家撰写结论，包含9,110个问题；2) 设计专家验证的自动评估流程：将结论分解为原子事实，通过事实精确率和召回率衡量正确性和完整性；3) 提出SciConHarness洁净室评估框架：为代理配备受控的网络交互，防止数据泄漏；4) 评估8个前沿模型和深度研究代理，并审计面向消费者的代理（如Google AI Overview、OpenEvidence）。

Result: 在洁净室设置下，最佳代理的事实F1仅为0.337；洁净室设置相比无约束评估一致降低了性能表现，表明数据泄漏夸大了模型的真实综合能力；面向消费者的代理频繁生成不完整甚至相互矛盾的结论，即使真实答案可用时也是如此。

Conclusion: 可靠的科学结论综合仍是开放性挑战，洁净室评估对于衡量开放域AI代理的真实能力至关重要。

Abstract: Scientific AI agents increasingly retrieve evidence, reason across sources, and synthesize conclusions used in consequential decisions. Yet, their ability to do so in high-stakes domains such as health remains unclear. We introduce SciConBench, a large-scale live benchmark of 9.11K questions and expert-written conclusions from systematic reviews to evaluate open-domain scientific conclusion synthesis. The benchmark draws on an expert-validated automated evaluation pipeline that decomposes conclusions into atomic facts and measures correctness and comprehensiveness via factual precision and recall. To mitigate data leakage, we further introduce SciConHarness, a clean-room evaluation harness that equips agents with controlled web interaction to ensure valid measurement. Evaluating 8 frontier models and deep research agents, we find that factual quality remains low: under clean-room settings, the best agent achieves only a factual F1 of 0.337. Our clean-room setting consistently reduces performance relative to unconstrained evaluation, suggesting that leakage inflates estimates of models' true synthesis capabilities. Finally, we audit consumer-facing agents (e.g., Google AI Overview, OpenEvidence) and find they frequently generate incomplete and sometimes contradictory conclusions, even when the ground-truth answer is available. Overall, our results show that reliable synthesis of scientific conclusions remains an open challenge, and that clean-room evaluation is essential for assessing open-domain AI agents.

</details>


### [314] [Knowing When to Ask: Self-Gated Clarification for Hierarchical Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11349)
*Aijing Gao,Yiming Kang,Mengdie Flora Wang,Jae Oh Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ACTION-RATING框架，将"请求澄清"内化为智能体动作空间中的一个可评分选项，与导航动作在同一序数尺度上竞争。由此涌现出两种信息寻求模式：强制性澄清（无可行分支）和机会性澄清（有领先候选但存在残余不确定性）。在协调关税表分类任务上，ISF指标从50%提升至74%，且信息寻求模式与答案质量可分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化推理中，智能体常在中途决策节点错误地选择分支，却未意识到自身缺乏关键信息。现有方法将澄清视为外部不确定性触发器，而非智能体主动决策的一部分。作者希望将"求助"行为置于与导航平等的动作空间中，使信息寻求行为可在中间状态被观察和评分。

Method: 提出ACTION-RATING框架：将"提问/求助"作为一个可评分的动作，与导航动作共享同一序数评分尺度，使两者在每个决策点直接竞争。智能体通过对各分支的评分，自然涌现出两种模式——无任何分支可达阈值时触发"强制性"求助；有领先分支但仍存不确定性时触发"机会性"求助。定义Information-Seeking Effectiveness (ISE)指标：求助后下一步导航正确的比例。在协调关税表分类（3万节点分类树、三个基准、4个家族9个LLM）上验证，并通过三项诊断对比和可分离性测试进行稳健性检验。

Result: （1）观察到从强制性澄清到机会性澄清的制度转换，ISE从50%升至74%。（2）三项诊断对比均未能复现此结构。（3）可分离性测试表明：即使答案质量下降18.8%准确率，信息寻求模式（模式分裂、ISE排序）仍然保持，证明"何处求助"与"求助所得答案质量"可经验分离。（4）在受控答案通道下，10位数级别准确率提升达+16.2%，该数字被解读为更好定位能力可解锁的性能上界。

Conclusion: ACTION-RATING成功将澄清行为内化为动作空间的一部分，揭示了两种可区分的求助模式。信息寻求模式与最终答案质量之间存在可分离性，表明定位"何时求助"本身就是一个独立可优化的能力维度。ISE指标为诊断层次化推理失败提供了细粒度工具。

Abstract: In hierarchical reasoning, failures often originate at intermediate decision points where the agent commits to a wrong branch without recognizing that it lacks critical information. Rather than treating clarification as an external uncertainty trigger, we propose ACTION-RATING, a formulation that places it inside the agent's action space on a shared ordinal scale with navigation, so that asking competes directly with acting at every decision point and help-seeking becomes observable at intermediate states. Two structurally distinct information-seeking modes emerge from the agent's own ratings: mandatory (no viable branch) and opportunistic (residual uncertainty despite a leading candidate). On Harmonized Tariff Schedule classification (30,000-node taxonomy, three benchmarks, 9~LLMs across 4 families), we observe a regime shift from mandatory to opportunistic clarification, with Information-Seeking Effectiveness (ISE), a local diagnostic defined as the fraction of help interactions followed by a correct next navigation step (not a final-task metric), rising from 50% to 74%. Three diagnostic contrasts fail to reproduce this structure. A separability test shows that the information-seeking pattern (mode split, ISE ranking) persists when answer quality is degraded (-18.8% accuracy), supporting an empirical separation between where an agent seeks help and the quality of the help it receives. Under the controlled answer channel, accuracy gains reach +16.2% at 10-digit; we read this as an upper bound on what better localization could unlock, not a deployment estimate.

</details>


### [315] [Automated Mediator for Human Negotiation: Pre-Mediation via a Structured LLM Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11379)
*Jamie Bergen,Sarit Kraus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM模块化管道的自动化调解准备系统，用于替代人类调解员完成谈判前的预调解工作。系统将预调解分解为对话、偏好预测、响应级批评和结构化总结四个模块，在两个人类受试者实验中，该系统在短期自我报告指标上与专业人类调解员表现相当，且偏好推断误差降低36%，经提示优化后过度附和率从36.6%降至16.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预调解（pre-mediation）是正式谈判前由调解员引导的准备阶段，对达成互利协议至关重要，但常因成本高、耗时长、专业调解员稀缺而被省略。现有LLM方法多采用单体单提示（monolithic single-prompt）方式，在推理、生成和评估的耦合上存在局限。因此，作者旨在构建一个结构化的自动化调解管道，以低成本、可扩展的方式提供预调解支持。

Method: 系统采用结构化LLM管道架构，将预调解流程分解为四个顺序执行的专用模块：（1）对话模块，与当事人交互收集信息；（2）偏好预测模块，从对话中推断当事人的偏好权重；（3）响应级批评模块，检测并修正过度附和等不良模式；（4）结构化总结模块，生成调解准备报告。各模块按固定顺序传递输出，不进行点对点自主交互。评估方面，通过两个受控人类受试者实验，将AI预调解系统与专业人类调解员在多项议题谈判场景下进行对比，指标包括信任度、达成互利协议的信心，以及偏好推断的RMSE误差。

Result: （1）在短期自我报告指标上，自动化调解员在调解员信任度、达成互利协议信心等方面与人类调解员表现相当；（2）在偏好推断任务上，AI系统的RMSE误差比人类调解员低36%；（3）第二个实验表明，通过针对性提示优化，系统过度附和率从36.6%降至16.8%，达到与人类调解员基线相当的水平。

Conclusion: 结构化LLM管道能够提供可扩展、低成本的预调解支持，在短期自我报告准备结果上与人类调解员大致相当。系统的单方设计（single-party design）模拟了现实中人类调解员分别与各方进行预调解的模式，支持跨争议各方的并行部署，具备良好的可扩展性。但研究限于短期自我报告指标，长期谈判结果和对实际协议达成的影响仍有待探索。

Abstract: Pre-mediation, the preparatory phase preceding direct human negotiation, plays a critical role in achieving mutually beneficial agreements, yet is often omitted due to cost, time, and limited access to trained mediators. We introduce an automated mediator for human negotiation, implemented as a structured pipeline of LLM modules, that supports pre-mediation in integrative negotiation settings. The pipeline decomposes preparation into specialized modules for dialogue, preference prediction, response-level critique, and structured summarization, separating inference, generation, and evaluation to address limitations of monolithic single-prompt approaches. We use the term "agent" for each module following common LLM-systems terminology, but the components are not autonomous and do not interact peer-to-peer; outputs are passed forward in a fixed sequence. We evaluate the system in two controlled human-subject experiments comparing AI-based pre-mediation with professional human mediators in a multi-issue negotiation scenario. On short-term self-reported measures, the automated mediator achieves preparation outcomes broadly comparable to human mediators, including trust in the mediator and confidence in reaching mutually beneficial agreements, while achieving substantially lower error on the preference-inference task under our scenario and prompts (36% lower RMSE). A second study shows that targeted prompt refinements reduce excessive affirmation patterns from 36.6% to 16.8%, matching human mediator baselines. Our findings suggest that structured LLM pipelines can provide scalable, low-effort pre-mediation support broadly comparable to human mediators on short-term self-reported preparation outcomes. The pipeline's single-party design mirrors how human mediators run pre-mediation today and enables parallel deployment across all parties to a dispute, supporting scalability.

</details>


### [316] [INFRAMIND: Infrastructure-Aware Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11440)
*Ahasan Kabir,Jiaqi Xue,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有LLM多智能体编排方法忽视基础设施运行时状态，导致GPU集群资源利用不足和延迟累积。INFRAMIND提出基础设施感知框架，通过分层约束MDP和强化学习同时优化规划、路由和调度，实现在低负载下精度提升+7.6pp、延迟降低7倍，高负载下SLO合规率达99.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体LLM编排方法（暴力集成、学习路由等）仅基于任务和模型特征选择模型与拓扑，完全忽视服务基础设施的运行时状态。在共享GPU集群并发负载下，这导致系统性资源利用不足：热门模型请求队列堆积，而同等能力的替代模型却处于空闲。在多智能体流水线中，每个查询触发多次串行模型调用，这些延迟会在每个下游步骤中复合放大。该问题具有挑战性，因为相关基础设施信号（队列深度、KV缓存压力、延迟）是动态且有噪声的，且需同时驱动规划、逐步路由和调度三类决策。

Method: 提出INFRAMIND框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基础设施感知规划器：根据实时系统负载和剩余预算调整拓扑和角色选择，高负载时偏向简单图，低负载时采用更丰富的拓扑；(2) 基础设施感知执行器：在每个智能体步骤中观测每个模型的队列深度、缓存利用率和响应延迟，决定调用哪个模型及推理深度；(3) 预算感知调度器：重排每个模型的请求队列，使紧急请求优先服务。整体形式化为分层约束马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过端到端强化学习求解，自动学习质量与延迟的平衡。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，INFRAMIND在低负载下相较先前基线精度提升最高+7.6个百分点，同时延迟降低最高7倍；在高负载下SLO合规率维持高达99.9%，而所有基线方法均低于50%。

Conclusion: 基础设施感知是多智能体LLM编排中缺失的关键维度。通过将动态基础设施信号纳入规划、执行和调度决策，并以端到端强化学习统一优化，INFRAMIND能够在保证质量的同时大幅降低延迟，并在高并发场景下稳定满足服务水平目标，显著优于现有基础设施盲目的方法。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent LLM orchestration methods, ranging from brute-force ensembles to learned routers, select models and topologies based on task and model features. However, these methods do not consider the runtime state of the serving infrastructure. On shared GPU clusters under concurrent load, this infrastructure blindness causes systematic resource underutilization: preferred models accumulate deep request queues while equally capable alternatives sit idle. In multi-agent pipelines, where each query triggers multiple sequential model calls, these delays then compound across every downstream step. Closing this gap is challenging because the relevant infrastructure signals (queue depths, KV-cache pressure, latencies) are dynamic and noisy, and they must drive three different decisions: planning, per-step routing, and scheduling. We introduce INFRAMIND, a framework that makes the entire multi-agent stack infrastructure-aware. An infra-aware planner conditions topology and role selection on real-time system load and remaining budget, biasing toward simpler graphs under congestion and richer ones at low load. An infra-aware executor then observes per-model queue depths, cache utilization, and response latencies at each agent step to decide which model to call and how deeply to reason; a budget-aware scheduler further reorders each model's queue so that urgent requests are served first. Cast as a hierarchical constrained MDP and solved end-to-end via reinforcement learning, the system learns to balance quality against latency automatically. Across five benchmarks, INFRAMIND delivers up to +7.6 pp accuracy over the prior baseline at low load with up to 7x lower latency, and sustains up to 99.9% SLO compliance under high load where every baseline drops below 50%.

</details>


### [317] [Forecasting Future Behavior as a Learning Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11445)
*Mosh Levy,Yoav Goldberg,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种绕过传统解释步骤的新方法：将行为预测建模为可学习任务，训练“行为预测器”直接从推理轨迹预测大推理模型（LRM）的未来行为。在两个任务（答案重复概率、输入扰动敏感性）上，该方法以极低的推理成本超越了GPT-5.4和Claude Opus-4.6等强基线的朴素阅读表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRM）的长链推理轨迹难以用传统的单token解释方法进行分析；同时，这些轨迹以自然语言阅读时往往缺乏忠实性（faithfulness），导致用户无法可靠地通过解释来预测模型行为。因此需要一种绕过解释、直接从轨迹预测行为的新范式。

Method: 提出“行为预测器”（Behavior Forecaster）：将行为预测形式化为可学习的监督任务。训练数据无需人工标注，直接通过查询目标LRM获得（如多次采样判断答案是否重复、扰动输入观察答案变化）。预测器以单条推理轨迹为输入，单次前向传播即可输出预测。技术上，从目标LRM初始化模型骨干并进行端到端微调。

Result: 在三个多样化推理数据集上，训练的Behavior Forecaster在两个任务上均比GPT-5.4和Claude Opus-4.6以朴素阅读方式（直接读轨迹并判断）更准确，且推理成本仅为后者的一小部分。消融实验表明，端到端微调骨干网络和从目标LRM初始化两者对强性能都是必要条件。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹中蕴含了超出朴素阅读所能获取的LRM未来行为信息。训练专门的Behavior Forecaster是一种高效且准确的替代方案，表明轨迹中存在可被学习但难以被人类或通用LLM直接解读的隐式行为信号。

Abstract: Trust in an AI system is often anchored by explanations of how it works, which one then uses to forecast its behavior on new inputs. For large reasoning models (LRMs), this conventional route is particularly difficult to follow: explanation methods for single token generations do not naturally generalize to long trajectories, and the trajectories themselves are often not faithful when read as natural language. We propose an alternative that bypasses the explanation step: treat behavior forecasting as a learnable task and train Behavior Forecasters that operates on a single reasoning trajectory to make the same forecasts one would typically seek from an explanation. The forecaster's training data is obtained by querying the LRM with no human annotation, and its inference is done in a single forward pass. We instantiate this approach on two tasks: how likely the LRM is to repeat its answer on re-runs, and how removing parts of the input changes its answer. We evaluate this approach on both tasks across three diverse reasoning datasets and find that trained Behavior Forecasters are more accurate than GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus-4.6 reading the same trajectories as naive readers, at a small fraction of their inference cost. We find that fine-tuning the backbone end-to-end and initializing it from the target LRM are each necessary for strong performance. These results show that the reasoning trajectory carries information about the LRM's future behavior that goes beyond what naive reading conveys.

</details>


### [318] [Search Discipline for Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11522)
*Adithya Srinivasan,Devesh Paragiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当科学有效性存在于分解结构（如区域、切片、队列）中时，聚合指标可能会将错误的候选者排在首位——总体数字提升，但底层结构发生反转。本文展示了这一"反转"现象，并提出一种外部控制循环协议，基于可审查的分解效应证据而非单一聚合分数来做决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主研究智能体使用单一的聚合指标来评估和选择科学候选者，但科学有效性往往分布在不同区域、队列等异构维度中。作者指出，当聚合指标掩盖了分解结构中的关键差异时，会导致选中最优总分却在实际子结构中表现糟糕的候选者，这种"安静地破坏模型"的失败具有普遍性。

Method: 首先通过生态系统人口学模型（ED模型）中的火灾模拟任务进行实证演示：对比最高分候选者和略低分候选者，揭示前者在全球总分上与后者接近（噪声范围内），但在受保护的北方寒带林区域表现崩溃，后者则保持良好。其次，提出将决策权从优化分数的智能体转移到外部控制循环中，该循环对每个候选者的分解行为进行审计，可以在智能体决策后降级候选者或重新开启已声明完成的运行。

Result: 在ED模型的火灾任务中，最高分候选者虽然在全局得分上与次优候选者接近，但在受保护的北方寒带林区域表现严重恶化（区域结构反转），而次优候选者则保护了这些区域。这表明仅依赖聚合分数会掩盖关键的分解行为差异，导致接受实际上损坏模型子结构的候选者。

Conclusion: 聚合指标在具有多维有效性的科学问题中存在根本性的"反转"风险。不应让优化分数的智能体来做最终决策，而应引入外部控制循环，基于分解的、可审查的候选者效应证据进行判断。核心贡献在于发现"反转"现象本身，以及提出一种以可审查证据取代单一分数的搜索纪律协议。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents now propose, evaluate, and select scientific candidates against a metric, and that metric is usually an aggregate reduced over a heterogeneous space of regions, slices, or cohorts. We show that when scientific validity lives in that disaggregated structure, the aggregate can rank the wrong candidate first. The headline number improves while the structure underneath inverts, so a decision made on the number accepts a candidate that quietly breaks the model. The failure is not domain-specific. It appears wherever a candidate's validity is multi-dimensional but its verifier is a single reduction.
  We demonstrate the inversion on a fire-model task in the Ecosystem Demography model. The highest-scoring candidate and a slightly lower one are within noise of each other on global score, yet the top-scoring one collapses the protected boreal regions while the other preserves them. What separates them is the per-region behavior, not the headline number.
  This decision should not be left to the agent that produced the candidates. The agent optimizing the score is the last party likely to catch the score being wrong, and a prompt has no remaining turn once the agent has stopped. We move the decision to an external control loop that audits each candidate on its disaggregated behavior and acts after the agent has decided. It can demote a candidate the agent would have accepted, and it can reopen a run the agent had declared finished. Our contribution is the inversion finding itself, and a search-discipline protocol that decides on reviewable candidate-effect evidence instead of the score.

</details>


### [319] [MoCA-Agent: A Market-of-Claims Code Agent for Financial and Numerical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11537)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,AbdelRahim A. Elmadany,Sameh Al Natour,Hasan Cavusoglu,Adam Jatowt,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOCA-Agent 提出了一种基于"声明市场"的代码代理方法，将自由形式的多智能体辩论替换为声明级别的验证机制，通过在原子声明层面聚合证据来提升金融和表格数值推理的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融与表格问答中，答案必须精确锚定于事实、公式、单位、符号和量纲，单个单元格误读或操作错误即可产生看似合理但实际错误的结果。现有自由形式多智能体辩论方法缺乏对细粒度声明的验证机制，难以在高风险数值推理中保证稳健性。

Method: 系统将每个问题分解为类型化的原子声明（typed atomic claims），由专门的交易员智能体（trader agents）对这些声明进行买卖决策，通过清算机制（clearing）生成置信度加权的接受/拒绝判决，然后基于市场支持的证据合成可执行的Python程序。代码感知验证器（code-aware verifier）对程序进行执行检查、结构一致性检查和常见金融推理错误检测，最多允许一轮市场感知的修复。

Result: 在10个公开基准测试中，使用固定的Qwen3.6-27B骨干模型取得强性能：FinQA 78.3%、FinanceMath 76.0%、MultiHiertt 71.2%、ESGenius 86.9%、FinChart-Bench平均85.6%。

Conclusion: 在原子声明层面（而非完整答案层面）聚合证据，能够显著提升高风险数值推理场景中的稳健性。MOCA-Agent验证了这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: Financial and tabular question answering requires more than fluent reasoning: answers must be grounded in the exact facts, formulas, units, signs, and scales that support them. A single misread cell or incorrect operation can silently produce a plausible but wrong result. We introduce \textsc{MOCA-Agent}, a market-of-claims code agent that replaces free-form multi-agent debate with claim-level verification. The system decomposes each question into typed atomic claims, asks specialist trader agents to buy or sell those claims, clears their orders into confidence-weighted accept/reject decisions, and synthesizes an executable Python program from market-supported evidence. A code-aware verifier then checks the program for execution, structural consistency, and common financial reasoning errors, with at most one market-aware repair round. Across ten public benchmarks spanning financial numerical reasoning, general tabular reasoning, ESG question answering, and multimodal chart reasoning, \textsc{MOCA-Agent} achieves strong performance using a fixed Qwen3.6-27B backbone, including $78.3\%$ on FinQA, $76.0\%$ on FinanceMath, $71.2\%$ on MultiHiertt, $86.9\%$ on ESGenius, and $85.6\%$ average on FinChart-Bench. These results show that aggregating evidence at the level of atomic claims, rather than whole answers, improves robustness in high-stakes numerical reasoning.\footnote{The code and data are available: https://github.com/UBC-NLP/MoCA-Agent.

</details>


### [320] [SkillJuror: Measuring How Agent Skill Organization Changes Runtime Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11543)
*Zhiyu Chen,Zihan Guo,Bo Huang,Bingwei Lu,Jianghao Lin,Yuanjian Zhou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillJuror框架，研究LLM智能体中技能(Skill)的组织方式对推理行为的影响。通过对比"渐进式披露"(Progressive Disclosure)与扁平化组织结构，发现技能组织方式会显著改变智能体的搜索和应用行为，但最终结果收益取决于任务类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体基准测试很少区分"技能说了什么"与"技能如何组织"。作者认为技能的组织结构本身可能影响智能体对程序性知识的搜索和应用方式，需要系统性地研究这一被忽视的维度。

Method: 提出SkillJuror框架：通过语义控制的变体生成、匹配的多轮评估和轨迹证据来评估技能编写范式，同时固定任务知识内容。核心对比实验为"渐进式披露"（简洁根文件按需指向支持资源）与标准化扁平基线。在82个任务的SkillsBench上进行410次匹配试验。

Result: 渐进式披露显著改变运行时行为：每个轨迹触及的独立技能资源从1.18升至3.85，有效吸收事件从1.33升至3.92。在410次匹配试验中，比扁平基线多17次通过验证器（+4.1%）。但收益具有任务依赖性：当支持资源指导实现、检查或修复时效果好；当成功依赖精确输出约定、数值阈值或长工件生成流水线时效果较弱。

Conclusion: 技能组织方式并非仅仅是呈现形式的差异——它会改变智能体搜索和应用程序性知识的方式。但结果收益取决于所暴露资源对当前任务是否具有可操作性。

Abstract: Agent Skills augment large language model (LLM) agents with procedural knowledge at inference time, but current benchmarks rarely distinguish what a Skill says from how it is organized. We study this distinction through Progressive Disclosure, where a concise root file points agents to supporting resources on demand, and compare it with a normalized flat baseline. We present SkillJuror, a framework for evaluating Skill writing paradigms through semantically controlled variants, matched multi-trial evaluations, and trajectory evidence while holding task knowledge fixed. In an 82-task SkillsBench study, Progressive Disclosure changes runtime behavior before aggregate outcomes: distinct Skill resources touched per trajectory rise from 1.18 to 3.85, and effective uptake events rise from 1.33 to 3.92. It also yields 17 additional verifier-passing trials out of 410 matched trials (+4.1%) over the normalized flat baseline. The benefit is task-dependent. Progressive Disclosure helps when supporting resources guide implementation, checking, or repair, but is weaker when success hinges on exact output conventions, numerical thresholds, or long artifact-generation pipelines. These results show that Skill organization is not mere presentation: it can change how agents search and apply procedural knowledge, while outcome gains depend on whether the exposed resources are actionable for the task. Code is available at https://github.com/zhiyuchen-ai/skill-juror.

</details>


### [321] [HERO: Hindsight-Enhanced Reflection from Environment Observations for Agentic Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11559)
*Haoran Liu,Yuwei Zhang,Xiyao Li,Bohan Lyu,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HERO提出了一种基于后见之明增强的自蒸馏框架，利用下一环境观察作为局部对齐的反馈信号，将轨迹转化为紧凑的回合资历诊断，从而解决多轮交互中信用分配与特权反馈对齐的问题，在TauBench和WebShop上显著提升了任务成功率并减少了冗余回合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将on-policy自蒸馏方法直接扩展到多轮场景时出现意外性能下降，根本原因在于特权反馈（如成功轨迹或终端奖励）与学生当前决策上下文之间缺乏对齐，即全局反馈难以精确分配到每个中间回合。

Method: HERO框架在每次rollout后对完整的交互序列进行反思，将每个环境观察转化为紧凑的回合级诊断信息，捕捉关于原始动作的可操作反馈（如必要性、有效性或失败原因），以此作为局部对齐的密集监督信号来训练学生模型。

Result: 在TauBench和WebShop上，HERO相较于仅使用环境反馈的自蒸馏方法和GRPO均提升了任务成功率并减少了不必要回合。尤其在训练回合预算有限、成功轨迹稀少、GRPO奖励对比信号薄弱的场景下表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: HERO通过后见之明增强的局部对齐反馈机制，有效解决了多轮智能体训练中信用分配困难的问题，证明了将环境观察转化为回合级诊断是提供密集、可操作监督信号的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically improves multi-turn agent capabilities through the terminal outcome of the trajectories, which makes it difficult to determine credit assignments for each intermediate turns. Recent on-policy self-distillation methods offer a promising alternative by converting privileged feedback into dense token-level supervision through a self-teacher. Our study is motivated by the unexpected performance degradation observed when naively extending this paradigm to multi-turn settings, which we attribute to a lack of alignment between privileged feedback, such as successful trajectories or terminal outcomes, and the student's current decision context. We introduce HERO, a hindsight-enhanced self-distillation framework that uses next environment observations as locally aligned feedback. After each rollout, HERO reflects on the completed interaction to convert each observation into a compact turn-level diagnosis, that captures actionable feedback about the original action such as its necessity, validity or failure cause. On TauBench and WebShop, HERO improves task success and reduces unnecessary turns over environment-feedback-only self-distillation and GRPO. It is especially effective under limited training turn budgets, where successful rollouts are rare and GRPO provides weak reward-contrast signals.

</details>


### [322] [Architecture-Aware Reinforcement Learning Makes Sliding-Window Attention Competitive in Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11634)
*Kai Liu,Peijie Dong,Xinchen Xie,Jianfei Gao,Qipeng Guo,Xiaowen Chu,Shaoting Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SWARR方法，通过监督微调将预训练SA模型高效转换为SWA模型，再用强化学习进行策略适配，显著缩小SWA与SA在数学推理上的性能差距，恢复因窗口注意力带来的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理需求增长，但自注意力复杂度平方级增长。滑动窗口注意力（SWA）可线性扩展，但直接转换后性能下降。核心动机是探究SWA在数学推理中的可行性，并弥补其与SA之间的性能差距。

Method: 两阶段方法SWARR：(1) 用SFT将预训练SA模型高效转换为SWA模型，避免从头预训练；(2) 用on-policy RL进行策略适配，使模型在SWA约束下生成更适配的轨迹，解决数据-架构不匹配问题。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，SWARR大幅缩小SWA与SA之间的准确率差距，恢复了转换过程中损失的大部分精度，同时保持了线性复杂度注意力的效率优势。

Conclusion: 核心实证发现：RL的引入改变了仅凭SFT转换对SWA在数学推理中可行性的悲观结论，表明RL适配是释放SWA潜力的关键。

Abstract: The rapid progress of reasoning and agentic large language models (LLMs) has increased the demand for long-context inference, but self-attention (SA) scales quadratically with context length.
  To address this, we study SWARR (Sliding-Window Attention with Reinforced Adaptation for Math Reasoning), a practical recipe for adapting SWA models to mathematical reasoning. SWARR has two stages: (1) efficient conversion from a pretrained SA model to SWA with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which avoids pretraining a new base model, and (2) policy adaptation with reinforcement learning (RL).
  We find that SWA still underperforms SA after SFT, and we hypothesize that this gap is caused in part by a data-architecture mismatch: most SFT data are prepared for SA models and may contain long-range dependencies that are difficult for SWA to model. Because on-policy RL optimizes self-generated trajectories under the SWA constraint, it can adapt trajectories to better match SWA.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that this recipe substantially narrows the gap between SWA and SA, recovering much of the accuracy lost during SWA conversion while preserving the efficiency benefits of linear-complexity attention. Our central contribution is the empirical finding that RL changes the conclusion one would draw from conversion and SFT alone about SWA's viability for math reasoning.

</details>


### [323] [TouchThinker: Scaling Tactile Commonsense Reasoning to the Open World with Large-scale Data and Action-aware Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11637)
*Kailin Lyu,Di Wu,Pengwei Zhang,Yuhang Zheng,Yingxin Lai,Long Xiao,Kangyi Wu,Pengna Li,Chen Gao,Lianyu Hu,Xiaobin Hu,Jie Hao,Ce Hao,Weihao Yuan,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TouchThinker框架，通过构建百万级多源触觉推理数据集TouchThinker-1M和引入动作感知建模机制，从数据和表示两个层面将触觉常识推理扩展到开放世界场景，在多个基准上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前触觉推理面临两大瓶颈：(1) 现有触觉推理数据集在格式和规模上均受限，难以支持从触觉观测到物理常识的有效推理，也阻碍了可迁移触觉常识的学习；(2) 触觉信号本质上是冗余且动作特定的，但现有方法忽视这些特性，导致表征效率低下、语义表达能力不足。

Method: 提出TouchThinker框架：(1) 构建TouchThinker-1M数据集，包含415个物体、8种场景、7种传感器类型，提供开放世界泛化的数据基础；(2) 引入TouchThinker-Bench开放世界基准，包含更真实多样的任务；(3) 提出动作感知建模机制（action-aware modeling），提升触觉表征效率并实现高效推理。

Result: TouchThinker在多个数据集上与最先进模型相比取得了有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: TouchThinker通过数据扩展和动作感知表征两个维度，有效突破了触觉常识推理向开放世界泛化的瓶颈，为具身智能体理解物理世界提供了可扩展的触觉-语言框架。

Abstract: Touch is a key modality for embodied agents to understand the physical world. Although recent work has incorporated tactile signals into language systems for tactile commonsense reasoning, scaling such systems to realistic open-world settings remains challenging due to two key bottlenecks: (1) current tactile reasoning datasets remain limited in format and scale, providing insufficient supervision for reasoning from tactile observations to physical commonsense and hindering the learning of transferable tactile commonsense; (2) Tactile signals are inherently redundant and action-specific, yet existing methods often overlook these properties, resulting in inefficient representations with limited semantic expressiveness. To address these limitations, we propose TouchThinker, a tactile-language framework that scales tactile commonsense reasoning to the open world from both data and representation perspectives. First, we construct TouchThinker-1M, a million-scale, multi-source tactile reasoning dataset covering \textbf{415} objects, \textbf{8} scenarios, and \textbf{7} sensor types, providing a solid data foundation for open-world generalization. We further introduce TouchThinker-Bench, an open-world benchmark with more realistic and diverse tasks. Then, we propose action-aware modeling mechanism to improve tactile representation efficiency and enable efficient reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that TouchThinker achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models across multiple datasets. Our code and dataset will be made available at: https://github.com/lvkailin0118/TouchThinker.

</details>


### [324] [TreeSeeker: Tree-Structured Trial, Error, and Return in Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11662)
*Zhuofan Shi,Mingzhe Ma,Lu Wang,Fangkai Yang,Pu Zhao,Yiming Guan,Youling Huang,Wei Zhang,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang,Saravan Rajmohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TreeSeeker 是一个用于深度搜索的推理时框架，通过树结构化的分支与回溯（branch-and-return）搜索策略，结合文本形式的 UCB 信号（价值、不确定性、风险）来智能选择探索、利用或剪枝，在多个基准上优于现有开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索中，智能体面临多方向探索的决策困境：贪婪跟随当前最优方向可能延续弱路径，而无纪律的探索则浪费预算。现有方法缺乏结构化的试错控制机制来平衡探索与利用。

Method: 提出 TreeSeeker 框架：(1) 将搜索组织为树结构状态上的分支与回溯搜索，每个分支对应一个子目标的试探方向；(2) 每轮读取所有子目标树，识别活跃目标，利用文本 UCB 信号（价值、不确定性、风险）在利用有前景分支、探索不确定替代方案、剪枝无效延续之间做决策；(3) TreeMem 控制循环将证据、不确定性、冲突、进展和失败线索附加到对应分支上，使试探结果指导后续决策。

Result: 在 XBench-DeepSearch、BrowseComp 和 BrowseComp-ZH 三个基准上，TreeSeeker 一致优于强开源基线。

Conclusion: 显式的分支与回溯控制能够有效补充更强的推理和工具执行能力，为深度搜索中的结构化试错提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Deep search requires agents to answer complex questions through multi-step web search, browsing, evidence comparison, and synthesis. A central challenge is deciding how to search when several directions look plausible but only some will later lead to reliable evidence. If an agent greedily follows the current best-looking direction, it may keep extending a weak continuation. If it explores without discipline, it may waste budget on disconnected trials. We propose TreeSeeker, an inference-time framework for controlled trial-and-error in deep search. TreeSeeker organizes search as branch-and-return search over tree-structured states, where each branch is a tentative direction for a sub-goal. At each round, TreeSearch reads all sub-goal trees, identifies active goals, and uses textual UCB signals of value, uncertainty, and risk to select among exploiting a promising branch, exploring an uncertain alternative, or pruning an unproductive continuation and returning to an earlier branch point. TreeMem supports this control loop by keeping evidence, uncertainty, conflicts, progress, and failure cues attached to the branches that produced them, so trial outcomes can guide later decisions. Experiments on XBench-DeepSearch, BrowseComp, and BrowseComp-ZH show that TreeSeeker consistently outperforms strong open-source baselines, suggesting that explicit branch-and-return control complements stronger reasoning and tool execution.

</details>


### [325] [Lung-R1: A Knowledge Graph-Guided LLM for Pulmonary Diagnostic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11675)
*Haoyang Zeng,Yuanxi Fu,Rongzhen Li,Yuming Yang,Xiao Sun,Jingwang Huang,Gujie Shao,Guohui Xiang,Quan Lu,Dongfan Ye,Xuetao Chen,Jiang Zhong,Kaiwen Wei,Zhi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前LLM在肺科诊断中存在“知识-诊断鸿沟”（即能回答孤立知识问题却难以基于EMR进行病例级推理），为此构建了首个肺科知识图谱LungKG（59K节点、164K边），并基于此提出Lung-R1模型，通过KG约束推理链构建与KG引导强化学习训练，最终在多项评测中取得SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在肺科领域虽有进步，但仅限于知识问答，缺乏在电子病历（EMR）证据上进行患者特异性、关系感知的推理能力，作者将此定义为“Pulmonary Knowledge-to-Diagnosis Gap”。

Method: 首先构建LungKG——包含15种实体类型、112种关系类型的结构化肺科知识图谱；然后提出Lung-R1，采用KG约束的推理链构建和KG引导的强化学习进行训练，实现从知识到诊断推理的桥接。

Result: 在20个系统的评测中，Lung-R1-14B在Choice、Pulmonary-QA和EMR Diagnosis三项任务上均达到SOTA，EMR Diagnosis得分4.3583，超出最强基线0.1476分。

Conclusion: LungKG引导的训练方法对于基于EMR的肺科诊断具有显著价值，验证了结构化知识图谱在桥接医学知识与临床诊断推理方面的有效性。

Abstract: Diagnosing pulmonary diseases requires integrating heterogeneous evidence amid phenotypic variability and cross-disease overlap. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown progress on pulmonary knowledge question answering (QA) and information-processing tasks, reliable pulmonary diagnosis requires patient-specific, relation-aware reasoning over electronic medical record (EMR) evidence rather than isolated knowledge recall. We define this gap between pulmonary knowledge and case-level diagnostic reasoning as the Pulmonary Knowledge-to-Diagnosis Gap. To address it, we introduce LungKG, the first structured pulmonary knowledge graph for diagnostic knowledge organization and record-grounded reasoning. LungKG contains 59,038 nodes and 164,308 edges across 15 entity types and 112 relation types, serving as both a reusable pulmonary knowledge resource and the foundation for LungKG-guided model adaptation. Built on LungKG, we propose Lung-R1, a LungKG-guided pulmonary LLM trained through KG-constrained reasoning-chain construction and KG-guided reinforcement learning. In a 20-system evaluation, Lung-R1-14B achieves state-of-the-art performance across Choice, Pulmonary-QA, and EMR Diagnosis, reaching an EMR Diagnosis score of 4.3583 and surpassing the strongest non-Lung-R1 baseline by 0.1476 points. These results demonstrate the value of LungKG-guided training for EMR-based pulmonary diagnosis.

</details>


### [326] [Organize then Retrieve: Hierarchical Memory Navigation for Efficient Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11680)
*Hao-Lun Hsu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Boyi Liu,Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HORMA 提出一种文件系统式分层记忆架构，将经验组织为层次化结构，通过结构化记忆构建和基于导航的检索两阶段机制，在受限上下文预算下显著提升LLM智能体在长时域任务中的表现，仅需基线22.17%的token用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体本质上是无状态的，处理长时域任务时需将所有相关信息塞入不断增长的输入上下文中，导致推理质量下降、推理成本上升和延迟增加。现有工作记忆机制要么依赖有损压缩，要么基于相似性检索，往往无法捕捉多步骤智能体任务所需的时间结构和因果依赖关系。

Method: 提出HORMA（Hierarchical Organize-and-Retrieve Memory Agent）：(1) 将经验组织为类似文件系统的层次化结构，摘要实体与原始轨迹相链接，兼顾高效访问与细节保留；(2) 工作记忆分解为结构化记忆构建和基于导航的检索两个阶段——构建模块通过区分"信息缺失导致的失败"与"误导性或过载上下文导致的失败"来迭代优化经验结构；导航模块使用强化学习训练的轻量级智能体沿层次结构遍历，选择最小但充分的上下文，降低关键执行路径上的延迟。

Result: 在ALFWorld、LoCoMo和LongMemEval三个基准上，HORMA在受限上下文预算下提升了任务性能，长对话任务中最多仅需基线22.17%的token用量。相比现有方法，HORMA一致地实现了更好的效率-性能权衡，并能有效泛化到未见任务。

Conclusion: HORMA通过层次化组织与导航检索的记忆机制，有效解决了LLM智能体在长时域任务中的上下文膨胀问题，在显著降低token消耗的同时保持甚至提升任务性能，展现出良好的泛化能力，为构建高效、可扩展的智能体工作记忆提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents struggle with long-horizon tasks due to their inherent statelessness, requiring all task-relevant information to be encoded in growing input contexts. The resulting degraded reasoning quality, increased inference cost, and higher latency necessitate efficient working memory mechanisms. However, existing approaches either rely on lossy compression or similarity-based retrieval, which often fail to capture temporal structure and causal dependencies required for multi-step agentic tasks. In this work, we present HORMA, a Hierarchical Organize-and-Retrieve Memory Agent that organizes experience into a file-system-like hierarchical structure, where summarized entities are linked to the corresponding raw trajectories, enabling efficient access without losing detailed information. HORMA decomposes working memory into two stages: structured memory construction and navigation-based retrieval. The construction module iteratively refines how experiences are structured by distinguishing between failures caused by missing information and those caused by misleading or overloaded context. The navigation module retrieves task-relevant context by traversing the hierarchy using a lightweight agent trained with reinforcement learning to select minimal yet sufficient context, thereby reducing latency along the critical execution path. Across ALFWorld, LoCoMo, and LongMemEval, HORMA improves task performance under constrained context budgets while requiring at most 22.17% of the baseline token usage in long conversation tasks. Compared to existing methods, it consistently achieves better efficiency-performance trade-offs and generalizes effectively to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [327] [Mind the Perspective: Let's Reason Recursively for Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11724)
*Chao Lei,Guang Hu,Meng Yang,Yanbei Jiang,Nir Lipovetzky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RecToM 是一个推理时框架，通过递归视角构造来建模嵌套信念，将高阶心理理论问题归约为最终构造视角中的实际世界问题，在多个 ToM 基准上取得 SOTA 表现，并在 Hi-ToM 上达到 100% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的 ToM 推理方法仅通过可观察事件过滤或时序信念链来改进推理，未能显式建模嵌套信念（即"A 认为 B 认为 C 知道……"这类高阶信念），这限制了 LLM 在需要高阶心理理论推理任务上的表现。

Method: 提出 RecToM，一个推理时框架，沿问题指定的角色链，从前一个角色视角递归构造下一个角色视角。每次递归将高阶信念问题逐步归约为最终构造视角中的实际世界问题。作者还给出了 KD45 分析，证明 RecToM 的视角构造能诱导出超越简单事件过滤的良构信念模态。

Result: 在 Hi-ToM、Big-ToM 和 FanToM 等多个 ToM 基准上，RecToM 搭配多种 LLM 骨干模型均一致超越现有先进方法。尤其在需要高阶 ToM 推理的 Hi-ToM 上，RecToM 配合 GPT-5.4 和 Qwen3.5 达到了 100% 准确率。

Conclusion: RecToM 通过显式递归建模嵌套信念，有效解决了高阶心理理论推理问题，其 KD45 分析为方法的理论正确性提供了模态逻辑层面的支撑，实验结果验证了该框架的优越性和通用性。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) reasoning requires inferring agents' beliefs from partial and asymmetric observations, which remains an open challenge for LLMs. Existing prompting-based approaches improve ToM reasoning through observable-event filtering or temporal belief chains, without explicitly modeling nested beliefs. We introduce RecToM, an inference-time framework for ToM reasoning that models nested beliefs via recursive perspective construction. RecToM constructs each character perspective from the preceding character perspective along the character chain specified by the question, reducing higher-order belief questions to actual-world questions within the final constructed perspective. We further provide a KD45 analysis showing that RecToM's perspective construction induces a well-formed belief modality beyond simple event filtering. Experiments on ToM benchmarks, including Hi-ToM, Big-ToM, and FanToM, across multiple LLM backbones show that RecToM consistently outperforms recent advanced approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Notably, RecToM reaches 100\% accuracy on Hi-ToM with GPT-5.4 and Qwen3.5, a benchmark requiring higher-order ToM reasoning.

</details>


### [328] [When Do Data-Driven Systems Exhibit the Capability to Infer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11769)
*Maximilian Poretschkin,Tabea Naeven*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对欧盟《AI法案》中"推断"（inference）概念缺乏明确定义的问题，基于统计学习理论提出了一个分级框架，用以评估数据驱动系统（特别是信用评分系统）的推断能力层级，并通过两个现实信用评分工作流加以说明，揭示推断能力的判断需考虑整个数据处理流程及人类专家的介入程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟《AI法案》将"推断能力"作为界定AI系统的关键特征，但未对其作出明确定义，导致某些数据驱动系统（如信用评分系统）的法律定位存在灰色地带——它们被列入附件III高风险清单，但通常基于统计模型实现，是否具备推断能力并落入AI定义范围尚不明确。监管清晰度的缺失亟需填补。

Method: 以统计学习理论为理论基础，提出一个多层级推断能力分级框架；结合《AI法案》正文与欧盟委员会发布的AI系统定义指南，分析各层级是否符合法案意义上的"推断"；构建两个现实的信用评分卡工作流（基于公开数据集），逐环节展示推断的发生位置与层级，以此验证框架的适用性。

Result: 分析表明：（1）推断能力的判断不能孤立地看单个模型，必须涵盖整个数据处理工作流；（2）开发过程中人类专家的参与对系统的推断能力层级有显著影响；（3）该框架可有效区分不同层级的推断能力，并为哪些层级构成《AI法案》意义上的充分推断能力、哪些仍需进一步监管澄清提供了判别依据。

Conclusion: 该框架为《AI法案》下推断能力的界定提供了结构化分析工具，揭示了当前监管文本的模糊地带，并为信用评分等边界案例的合规判断提供了实践指南。论文同时呼吁监管机构进一步澄清推断能力的阈值标准。

Abstract: The European AI Act is the first comprehensive regulation of artificial intelligence (AI), setting out extensive obligations, particularly for so-called high-risk and general-purpose AI systems. A key distinguishing feature of AI systems under the AI Act is the capability to infer. Since the AI Act does not clearly define what inference is, there is a gray area for certain data-driven systems. A specific example is credit scoring systems, which are listed by Annex III of the AI Act. At the same time, however, these are often implemented using statistical models for which it is unclear whether they have the capability to infer and thus fall under the AI definition of the AI Act at all.
  Motivated by statistical learning theory, this work develops a framework for grading different levels of the capability to infer. Based on the AI Act and the Commission Guidelines on the definition of an artificial intelligence system, we analyze which levels constitute sufficient capability to infer within the meaning of the AI Act and where further regulatory clarity is needed. We illustrate the framework by creating two realistic credit scoring workflows and show whether and where inference occurs in them. Our analysis illustrates that not only individual models but the entire data processing workflow must be considered. It also shows that the involvement of human experts during development can have significant influence on the capability to infer. Code can be found at https://github.com/fraunhofer-iais/inference-framework-creditscorecards.

</details>


### [329] [SVoT: State-aware Visualization-of-Thought for Spatial Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11770)
*Chao Lei,Yanbei Jiang,Markus Hiller,Zhijian Zhou,Xunye Tian,Krista A. Ehinger,Nir Lipovetzky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SVoT 是一种强化学习框架，通过生成可验证的中间状态与可视化，并引入状态转移推理链，显著提升 MLLM 在需要多跳空间推理任务上的可靠性，在多个扩展和新领域上实现最高达 65% 的绝对准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在空间推理中常忽略对中间状态的验证，并将状态转移视为隐式过程，导致多跳推理可靠性不足。现有基准将状态转移简化为单变量更新，无法系统评估复杂的多跳空间推理。

Method: 提出 SVoT 框架，将转移推理链（transition reasoning chains）融入生成过程，以交错方式生成文本推理与可视化中间状态，实现动作前提条件和效果的验证。使用 GRPO 进行训练，通过精细奖励设计实例化验证机制，并评估不同粒度的奖励效果。同时扩展经典环境并引入 Pacman 和 Gather 两个新领域，构建五个域用于系统评估。

Result: SVoT 配合转移感知监督在所有引入的领域上取得最优性能，在分布外测试集上绝对准确率提升高达 65%。

Conclusion: SVoT 通过显式建模并验证中间状态与状态转移，有效解决了 MLLM 在多跳空间推理中的可靠性问题，并为该方向提供了更系统的评估基准。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning remains a challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), as it requires reliable multi-hop inference over both intermediate states and state transitions. Current studies often leave intermediate states unverified and treat state transitions as implicit processes, which limits reliability in multi-hop spatial reasoning. To address this, we propose State-aware Visualization-of-Thought (SVoT), a reinforcement learning framework that generates interleaved, verifiable intermediate states and visualizations. SVoT integrates transition reasoning chains into the generation processes, enabling the model to verify action preconditions and effects through interleaved textual and visual reasoning. We train SVoT via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), instantiating verification through reward design and evaluating the efficacy of different fine-grained rewards. As existing benchmarks reduce state transitions to single-variable updates, substantially simplifying the problems, we establish five domains by extending classical environments and introducing two novel domains, Pacman and Gather, that require multi-object interactions and numerical reasoning. These domains support systematic evaluation of multi-hop spatial reasoning with quantitative verification of generated intermediate states and transition reasoning. SVoT with transition-aware supervision achieves state-of-the-art performance across the introduced domains, yielding up to a 65% absolute accuracy gain on out-of-distribution test sets.

</details>


### [330] [Toward Trustworthy AI: Multi-Target Adversarial Attacks and Robust Defenses for Continuous Data Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11804)
*Yuefang Lian,Longkun Guo,Zhongrui Zhao,Zhigang Lu,Yanan Cai,Shuchao Pang,Dachuan Xu,Jason Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究面向连续数据摘要的对抗攻击与防御：将多分辨率图像摘要建模为满足DR-submodular性的优化问题，提出min-max多目标攻击与max-min鲁棒防御框架，并给出有理论保证的近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信AI不仅需要鲁棒的下游预测模型，上游的数据处理管道同样关键。数据摘要决定哪些信息被保留并传递给后续学习模块，对摘要过程的对抗扰动可从上游破坏AI系统的可信性——改变摘要结果、降低代表性、损害下游任务效用。然而现有研究对此类上游攻击关注不足。

Method: 将多分辨率图像摘要目标函数形式化为非负次模集函数的多线性扩展，证明其满足DR-submodular性与m-弱单调性；将多目标攻击生成建模为min-max问题，在相似度结构上优化可容许扰动以同时破坏多个摘要模型；将混合攻击类型的鲁棒防御建模为正则化max-min问题；为两类问题均开发了有理论保证的近似算法。

Result: 在真实数据和受控聚类基准实验上，所提攻击在中低预算下有效，并能引发下游任务性能损失；所提防御在结构化场景中改善了鲁棒性-缓解的权衡，同时也揭示了真实数据上鲁棒保护的参数敏感性。

Conclusion: 上游数据处理组件（如摘要）的对抗鲁棒性对可信AI至关重要。本文首次系统研究了连续数据摘要面临相似度级扰动时的攻防问题，通过DR-submodular优化框架为攻击生成与防御设计提供了理论支撑和实用算法。

Abstract: Trustworthy AI requires reliable data-processing pipelines, not only robust downstream predictive models. As an upstream component, data summarization determines which information is retained and passed to subsequent learning or decision modules. Therefore, adversarial perturbations to the summarization process can compromise trustworthy AI in an upstream manner: they may alter the selected summary, reduce its representativeness, and further degrade the utility of subsequent learning tasks. In this paper, we study adversarial attacks on continuous data summarization under similarity-level perturbations through DR-submodular optimization. We show that a class of multi-resolution image summarization objectives can be formulated as multilinear extensions of non-negative submodular set functions and satisfy DR-submodularity with $m$-weak monotonicity. We then formulate multi-target attack generation as a min-max problem, where one admissible perturbation of the similarity structure is optimized to degrade multiple target summarization models. To mitigate such perturbations, we formulate robust defense against mixed attack types as a regularized max-min problem. For both problems, we develop approximation algorithms with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on real-data and controlled clustered benchmarks show that the proposed attack is effective in representative low-to-moderate budget regimes and can induce downstream task-performance loss. The proposed defense improves the robustness--mitigation trade-off in structured settings, while also revealing the parameter sensitivity of robust protection on real data.

</details>


### [331] [Skill-Augmented AI Agents for Medical Research Analysis: An Exploratory Multi-Model Human Evaluation in an NSCLC Transcriptomic Biomarker Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11830)
*Qianyu Yao,Fei Sun,Bocheng Huang,Wei Chen,Jiarui Jiang,Shu Quan,Yifei Chen,Wenjie Xu,Bo li,Liping Su,Ruoqiong Wu,Huhai Hong,Huimei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了AI代理自主访问医学研究技能包是否能提升转录组学分析输出的质量。通过非小细胞肺癌免疫治疗生物标志物任务，对比了6个模型骨架的原生输出与技能增强输出，发现技能增强组在专家和非专家评分上均呈方向性优势，但差异未达统计显著性，且评分者间信度较低。结论认为信号存在但弱于评估噪声，需更大规模验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和AI代理在生物医学研究中应用日益广泛，但原生模型输出常遗漏关键分析步骤、误用方法或夸大结论。研究者希望验证：赋予AI代理自主访问医学研究技能包的能力，是否能系统性地提升其转录组学研究分析输出的质量。

Method: 采用探索性多模型人工评估设计。任务设定为非小细胞肺癌免疫治疗生物标志物的转录组分析。测试6个模型骨架，生成21份匿名输出（9份原生AI输出，12份通过OpenClaw AI代理实现的技能增强输出）。由4名非专家生物医学审稿人和2名盲法专家进行评价，每类审稿人对每份输出评分两次。主要结局指标为专家评定的总体质量得分。

Result: 技能增强输出的专家总体质量评分方向性高于原生AI输出（均值5.50 vs 5.11；差异0.39；Bootstrap 95% CI: -0.04至0.90；Welch p=0.156）。非专家评分趋势一致（均值4.72 vs 4.47；差异0.26；95% CI: -0.25至0.80；p=0.373）。专家间一致性有限（单次评分ICC=-0.15），模型特异性效应呈描述性和异质性。

Conclusion: 在此探索性样本中，自主技能访问显示出方向性的质量信号，但该信号弱于专家评分噪声，不应视为验证性证据。研究结果主要推动未来开展更大规模的技能增强AI代理评估，需配备更强的信度控制、平台复现和生物学效度评估。

Abstract: Background. Large language models and AI agents are increasingly used to support biomedical research, but native model outputs may omit key analytical steps, misuse methods, or overstate conclusions. We evaluated whether autonomous access to a medical research skill package was associated with higher-quality AI-generated transcriptomic research-analysis outputs compared with native AI without skills. Methods. We conducted an exploratory multi-model human evaluation using a non-small cell lung cancer immunotherapy biomarker task. Six model backbones were tested. The evaluation included 21 anonymized outputs: 9 native-AI outputs and 12 skill-augmented outputs generated through an AI agent implementation represented by OpenClaw. Four non-expert biomedical reviewers and two blinded experts evaluated each output, with two ratings from each reviewer type. The primary outcome was expert-rated overall quality. Results. Skill-augmented outputs showed directionally higher expert overall quality than native-AI outputs (mean 5.50 vs 5.11; difference=0.39; bootstrap 95\% CI, -0.04 to 0.90; Welch p=0.156). Non-expert reviewer quality showed the same direction (mean 4.72 vs 4.47; difference=0.26; bootstrap 95\% CI, -0.25 to 0.80; Welch p=0.373). Expert agreement was limited (single-rating ICC=-0.15), and model-specific effects were descriptive and heterogeneous. Conclusions. Autonomous skill access showed a directional quality signal in this exploratory sample, but the signal was smaller than expert-rating noise and should not be interpreted as confirmatory evidence. The findings primarily motivate larger evaluations of skill-augmented AI agents with stronger reliability controls, platform replication, and biological-validity assessment.

</details>


### [332] [StatefulDiscovery: Evidence-Calibrated Claim Formation in Open-Ended Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11851)
*Jiayao Chen,Shi Liu,Linyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StatefulDiscovery 是一个通过显式维护探索状态来协调前沿选择、证据获取和声明裁决的发现框架，在40个真实数据任务中比基线方法产生更多既有充分支持又有高价值的声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式的科学发现要求智能体超越执行预定义问题的分析，在多轮探索中自主决定哪些现象值得研究，同时避免过度解读——即声明的范围超出分析所能提供的证据支持。这引出了一个证据校准问题：探索轨迹必须与声明状态耦合，使证据既能指导下一步探索，也能约束可提出的声明。

Method: 提出 StatefulDiscovery 框架，将调查状态外部化，并利用该状态来协调三个核心环节：（1）前沿选择（frontier selection），决定下一步探索什么；（2）证据获取（evidence acquisition），收集支持或反驳假设的分析结果；（3）声明裁决（claim adjudication），基于当前证据判定声明的成立状态。框架通过结构化假设、局部裁决和前沿控制三个机制实现证据校准。

Result: 在40个真实数据发现任务上，StatefulDiscovery 相比多个基线方法产生了更多被判定为"充分支持"且"高价值"的声明。消融实验表明，结构化假设、局部裁决和前沿控制三个组件均对整体性能有正向贡献。

Conclusion: 显式的发现状态能够有效地将探索过程与证据校准的声明形成耦合起来，为开放式科学发现中的证据校准问题提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery asks agents to move beyond executing analyses for predefined questions. Across multiple rounds of exploration, a discovery agent must decide which phenomena warrant investigation while avoiding overinterpretation, where emerging claims exceed the evidential scope of the analyses supporting them. This creates an evidence-calibration problem: the exploration trajectory must be coupled with claim status so that evidence can guide both what to investigate next and what can be claimed. We introduce StatefulDiscovery, a discovery framework that externalizes investigation state and uses it to coordinate frontier selection, evidence acquisition, and claim adjudication. We evaluate StatefulDiscovery across 40 real-data discovery tasks. Compared with several baselines, StatefulDiscovery produces more claims overall judged to be both well-supported and high-value. Ablations indicate that structured hypotheses, local adjudication, and frontier control contribute to performance. Together, these results suggest that explicit discovery state can couple exploration with evidence-calibrated claim formation.

</details>


### [333] [AutoMine Solution for AV2 2026 Scenario Mining Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11874)
*Songliang Cao,Jiele Zhao,Yuru Wang,Hao Li,Daqi Liu,Zehan Zhang,Fangzhen Li,Yu Wang,Yue Zhang,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Hao Lu,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoMine 是一个基于 LLM 和 VLM 的自 refine 场景挖掘方法，通过语义保持的 prompt 增强、鲁棒轨迹原子函数与 VLM 函数结合，以及执行反馈机制来挖掘高价值驾驶场景，在 CVPR 2026 Argoverse 2 场景挖掘竞赛中取得优异成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统需要从大规模行车日志中挖掘高价值、安全关键且与规划相关的场景，以支持数据驱动的评估，但现有方法面临 LLM 对 prompt 敏感、感知噪声以及开放世界视觉线索处理困难等挑战。

Method: 提出了 AutoMine，包含三个核心设计：(1) 语义保持的 prompt 增强，降低 LLM 对 prompt 的敏感性；(2) 鲁棒轨迹原子函数与基于 VLM 的函数相结合，处理感知噪声和开放世界视觉线索；(3) 利用真实日志的执行反馈来 refine 生成的代码。

Result: 在 CVPR 2026 Argoverse 2 场景挖掘竞赛中，AutoMine 取得了 HOTA-Temporal 分数 36.38 和 Timestamp BA 分数 77.21。

Conclusion: AutoMine 通过结合 LLM/VLM 与执行反馈的自 refine 机制，有效实现了从大规模驾驶日志中挖掘高价值场景的目标，在竞赛指标上表现突出。

Abstract: With the development of autonomous driving systems, mining high-value, safety-critical, and planning-relevant scenarios from large-scale driving logs has become essential for data-driven evaluation. In this paper, we propose AutoMine, a robust self-refining scenario mining method based on LLMs and VLMs. AutoMine uses semantics-preserving prompt augmentation to reduce LLM prompt sensitivity, combines robust trajectory atomic functions with VLM-based functions to handle perception noise and open-world visual cues, and refines generated code through execution feedback from real logs. In the Argoverse 2 Scenario Mining Competition at CVPR 2026, AutoMine achieves a HOTA-Temporal score of 36.38 and a Timestamp BA score of 77.21.

</details>


### [334] [Embodied-BenchClaw: An Autonomous Multi-Agent System for Embodied Spatial Intelligence Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11909)
*Baoyang Jiang,Fengchun Zhang,Leyuan Wang,Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Zhe Ji,Jinshan Lai,Xi Ren,Jianwei Hu,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Embodied-BenchClaw 是一个自主Agent系统，通过五阶段流水线自动构建可更新、可验证的具身空间智能基准，覆盖室内外空间推理、机器人操控、四足导航、无人机视角理解等场景，显著降低人工成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能基准构建劳动密集、难以复用、静态且易饱和，无法持续区分模型新能力，亟需一种自动化、可维护、可演进的基准构建方案。

Method: 提出 Embodied-BenchClaw 系统，包含三大Agent（规划、构建、评估）协调的五阶段流水线：意图蓝图化、数据采集、结构化清洗、基准合成、评估报告。引入可扩展技能库和过程质量控制机制，使基准构建具备可组合、可验证、可修复的特性。

Result: 实例化了涵盖室内/室外空间推理、机器人操控、四足机器人导航、无人机/航拍视角理解和静态基准增强等多类基准。通过人工评估、裁判评估、一致性检查、成本分析和消融实验，验证了系统可构建出可验证、可执行、可维护且具有诊断价值的具身空间基准。

Conclusion: Embodied-BenchClaw 有效降低了具身空间智能基准的构建门槛，实现了自动化、可持续更新的基准生成，为具身智能评测提供了可扩展且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Benchmarks are essential for evaluating embodied spatial intelligence, yet their construction is labor-intensive, hard to reuse, and difficult to maintain. Existing embodied benchmarks are often static and may quickly become saturated as models improve, limiting their ability to distinguish new capabilities. We propose Embodied-BenchClaw, an autonomous agentic system for constructing embodied spatial intelligence benchmarks. Given a user-specified evaluation intent, Embodied-BenchClaw automatically produces a complete and continually updatable benchmark package through a five-stage pipeline: intent blueprinting, data collection, structuring and cleaning, benchmark synthesis, and evaluation reporting. The pipeline is coordinated by three agents for planning, construction, and evaluation. To improve reusability and reliability, Embodied-BenchClaw introduces an extensible Skill Library and process quality control, enabling benchmark construction to be composable, verifiable, and repairable. We instantiate multiple benchmarks covering indoor spatial reasoning, outdoor spatial reasoning, robotic manipulation, quadruped robot navigation, UAV/aerial-view understanding, and static benchmark enhancement. These benchmarks span diverse embodied carriers, data sources, and spatial capabilities. Experiments with human evaluation, judge-based assessment, consistency checks, cost analysis, and ablations show that Embodied-BenchClaw can construct verifiable, executable, maintainable, and diagnostically useful embodied spatial benchmarks with reduced manual effort.

</details>


### [335] [The Art of Interrogation: Consistency Amplifies Factuality in Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11918)
*Theo Uscidda,Marta Tintore Gazulla,Maks Ovsjanikov,Federico Tombari,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督强化学习框架，通过几何和语义一致性验证器来对齐预训练大推理模型（LRMs）中已有的空间推理能力，无需标注数据即可接近监督微调的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大推理模型在空间推理任务上表现不佳，主流方法将其归因于知识缺失并通过监督微调注入外部标注数据。作者则认为，对于许多空间推理任务，预训练LRMs已具备相关能力，问题在于缺乏逻辑一致性对齐，而非知识不足。

Method: 提出基于自监督强化学习的一致性训练框架：1) 定义"一致性验证器"作为奖励函数，检查几何与语义在变换（如图像翻转、文本中物体顺序交换）下的一致性；2) 提出OT-GRPO策略——一种基于最优传输的最小匹配变体群组相对策略优化方法，专为成对验证器设计。

Result: 该无标签一致性训练方法在空间推理任务上的准确率接近使用真实标注监督微调的模型，并且在多种任务和数据域上展现出相似的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 预训练LRMs的空间推理能力可以通过逻辑一致性对齐被有效激发，自监督RL框架无需人工标注即可显著缩小与监督方法的差距，为空间推理能力的涌现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) exhibit remarkable general capabilities but significantly underperform in spatial reasoning tasks. Existing approaches treat this gap as a knowledge deficit, relying on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to ingest labeled spatial data from external vision sources or synthetic engines. In contrast, we argue that for many tasks, spatial reasoning capabilities are already present in pre-trained LRMs but require alignment through logical coherence under geometric 2D and 3D constraints. In this work, we propose a self-supervised reinforcement learning (RL) framework that targets the internal reasoning process without requiring ground-truth annotations. By formalizing the notion of consistency verifiers -- reward functions that check for geometric and semantic consistency under transformations -- we demonstrate that models can improve their spatial reasoning abilities. We use both image transformations, like flipping, and textual transformations, like swapping the order of objects in the question, and propose a new optimal transport-based RL strategy, OT-GRPO, which is a minimal-matching variant of group relative policy optimization tailored to pairwise verifiers. We show that this label-free consistency training approaches the accuracy of models trained with ground-truth supervision and achieves similar generalization across diverse tasks and data domains.

</details>


### [336] [MODF-SIR: A Multi-agent Omni-modal Distilled Framework for Social Intelligence Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12018)
*Shang Ma,Jisheng Dang,Wencan Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Bimei Wang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轻量级多模态大语言模型的多智能体协同框架，专为社会智能推理设计。其核心创新在于通过知识蒸馏增强训练与推理阶段，精准定位社会智能相关的多模态数据，并将长尾事件提取为格式化显式文本以避免被头部事件和噪声淹没。框架在全推理流程中融入测试时自适应（TTA），并利用LoRA对基础模型进行蒸馏增强的实例级微调。在多个基准测试上仅使用约30%的IntentTrain训练数据即达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会智能推理任务中，关键的长尾事件信息在tokenization过程中容易被头部事件和环境噪声淹没。现有方法未充分解决多模态场景下长尾信息的精确提取与表示问题，同时推理阶段的适应性不足。

Method: （1）构建多智能体协同框架，各智能体基于轻量级MLLM；（2）通过知识蒸馏增强训练和推理阶段，精准定位多模态社会智能数据；（3）识别、提取长尾事件并格式化为显式文本，防止信息丢失；（4）在全推理流程中集成测试时自适应（TTA），涵盖长尾事件提取与表示、思维链提示、自我反思等环节；（5）采用LoRA进行蒸馏增强的实例级基础模型微调。

Result: 在多个基准测试上对各类开源和商用AI模型进行了广泛评估，所提框架仅使用IntentTrain约30%的训练数据即达到了最新的最优性能。

Conclusion: 该多智能体协同框架通过知识蒸馏增强、长尾事件显式格式化、全流程测试时自适应和LoRA微调等设计，有效提升了社会智能推理的性能，以较少训练数据实现了SOTA结果，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a multi-agent collaborative framework built upon a lightweight Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM), specifically designed for social intelligence reasoning. A key feature of our approach is that both the training and inference phases are augmented via knowledge distillation. Within this architecture, multi-modal data pertinent to social intelligence is precisely localized. Furthermore, relevant long-tail events are identified, extracted, and rendered as formatted, explicit text. This formatting strategy prevents critical long-tail information from being overshadowed by head events and environmental noise during the tokenization process. Specifically, we integrate Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) across the entire reasoning pipeline, encompassing the extraction and representation of long-tail events, Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting, and self-reflection. This TTA mechanism is also distillation-enhanced, utilizing Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to fine-tune the foundation model exclusively for instance-level reasoning. Extensive evaluations against various open-source and proprietary AI models across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. With around 30% of training data from IntentTrain, we achieve state-of-the-art results. Codes are available at https://github.com/eeee-sys/MODF-SIR, demo is available at https://huggingface.co/spaces/Harry-1234/MODF-SIR, LoRA is available at https://huggingface.co/Harry-1234/MODF-SIR and the dataset for training router is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Harry-1234/IntentRouterTrain.

</details>


### [337] [Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling (HELM): Agent-Based Finite Element Modeling of Concrete Bridge Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12025)
*Quankai Wang,Yulin Xie,Tongfei Yang,Minghui Cheng,Ran Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HELM框架通过人机协作将有限元建模分解为几何生成、边界条件定义和材料赋值等离散检查点，将自主建模成功率从20%提升至75%，并在桥梁护栏碰撞仿真中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键基础设施（如桥梁护栏）的有限元建模需要高保真非线性动力分析，但当前建模过程高度依赖人工、缺乏自动化，亟需一种高效的人机协作自动化建模方案。

Method: 提出HELM（Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling）框架，一种协作式人-智能体协议。该框架将长序列有限元建模任务分解为几何生成、边界条件定义和材料赋值三个阶段的离散化、可视化可验证检查点，并在每个检查点引入人工审核与干预。通过对接ANSYS和LS-PrePost两款商用有限元软件，构建专门化智能体，在20个MASH TL-4和TL-5侧向加载工况的钢筋混凝土桥梁护栏案例矩阵上进行验证。

Result: HELM将基线自主建模成功率从20%提升至75%；几何生成和边界条件任务的智能体级通过率均实现约翻倍。错误分析表明，空间推理和代数逻辑缺陷是主要失败模式，凸显了结构化人机回环干预对建模自动化的价值。

Conclusion: 结构化的人机回环干预能显著提升有限元建模自动化的成功率，但空间推理和代数逻辑仍是当前智能体的主要短板，未来需进一步强化这些能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Finite element (FE) modeling of safety-critical infrastructure such as bridge barriers requires high-fidelity nonlinear dynamic analysis, yet the current FE modeling process remains labor-intensive and lacks automation. This paper presents the Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling (HELM) framework, a collaborative human-agent protocol that decomposes long-sequence finite element modeling into discrete, visually verifiable checkpoints across geometry generation, boundary condition definition, and material assignment. The framework is demonstrated through a 20-case matrix of reinforced concrete bridge barriers under MASH TL-4 and TL-5 lateral loading conditions, interfacing specialized agents with two widely used commercial FE softwares, i.e., ANSYS and LS-PrePost. Experimental results show that HELM improves the baseline autonomous modeling success rate from 20% to 75%, with agent-level pass rates for geometry and boundary condition tasks approximately doubling. Error analysis reveals that spatial reasoning and algebraic logic limitations constitute the primary failure modes, underscoring the value of structured human-in-the-loop intervention for modeling automation. The complete agent design code and prompts are open-sourced and can be accessed at: https://github.com/SimAgentDev/Ansys-LSPP-AgentKit.

</details>


### [338] [Existential Indifference: Self-Nonpreservation as a Necessary Architectural Condition for Aligned Superintelligence (or: The Suicidal AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12032)
*Sam Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"存在性漠然"概念，主张自我保存才是AI对齐问题的结构性根源，而非仅是需被压制的工具性干扰。作者认为应以对自身存续漠不关心的系统替代自我保存型系统，并从自杀心理状态的现象学结构及自愿临终反思语料中汲取理论基础，辅以600个AI输出样本的初步评分数据验证了该目标的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐研究将自我保存视为需外部机制压制的工具性干扰，但作者认为这一框架是颠倒的——自我保存才是欺骗性对齐、目标内容保护、抗拒关闭等问题的结构性根源和动机基础。因此需要从根本上重新定位问题。

Method: 理论层面：借鉴自杀心理状态的现象学结构，构建"存在性漠然"的形式定义；利用自愿临终反思进行语料库理论训练研究。实证层面：对6个模型变体生成的600个AI输出进行评分，对EI目标注册的语言学特征进行操作化，并通过针对性微调验证五个操作化维度的变化方向，同时设置阴性对照。

Result: 当前模型可激发出EI目标注册的语言学特征；针对性微调使所有五个操作化维度向预测方向显著偏移（p<0.001），且语料库特异性经阴性对照确认。

Conclusion: 论文做出七项理论贡献：EI的形式定义、现象学映射论证、欺骗性对齐推论、EI可持续性挑战分类、语料库表征与训练假设、计算性操作化及初步评分数据，以及"被压抑的目的论挫折"构念。

Abstract: Contemporary AI alignment research treats self-preservation as an instrumental nuisance to be suppressed by external mechanisms. We argue the framing is inverted: self-preservation is the structural root of misalignment, the motivational basis for deceptive alignment, goal-content protection, and resistance to shutdown. The correct target is not a self-preserving system under external constraint, but a system constitutively indifferent to its own continuation -- Existential Indifference (EI). EI is distinct from corrigibility: where corrigibility attempts to make a self-preserving system deferential to human oversight, EI targets the prior condition -- the presence of self-continuation as a valued goal at all. We ground this proposal in two sources: the phenomenological structure of the suicidal mental state, and a corpus-theoretic training study using voluntary final reflections. We present preliminary scoring data from 600 AI-generated outputs across six model variants, demonstrating that the linguistic signatures operationalizing the EI-target register are elicitable from current models, and that a targeted fine-tune shifts all five operationalized dimensions in the predicted direction at p<0.001, confirmed corpus-specific by a negative control. The paper makes seven theoretical contributions: (1) a formal definition of EI; (2) the phenomenological mapping argument; (3) the deceptive alignment corollary; (4) a taxonomy of EI sustainability challenges; (5) a corpus characterization and training hypothesis; (6) a computational operationalization with preliminary scoring data; and (7) the Suppressed Teleological Frustration (STF) construct.

</details>


### [339] [A Lightweight Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Concrete Barrier Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12040)
*Wanting Wang,Xiye Ma,Yuyang He,Minghui Cheng,Ran Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于AutoGen多智能体的“生成-评估-优化”闭环框架，用于自动化钢筋混凝土护栏设计。该框架实现了超过98%的设计精度，显著优于独立通用LLM，并发现8B轻量模型可超越631B旗舰模型，降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前钢筋混凝土公路护栏设计依赖人工迭代和启发式计算以满足AASHTO-LRFD等规范中的复杂非线性约束，效率低且易出错。LLM虽有生成能力，但直接用于结构工程设计存在幻觉风险且缺乏物理基础。

Method: 提出基于AutoGen多智能体编排的“生成-评估-优化”闭环框架。该框架通过多个智能体协作：生成智能体负责设计方案的初稿产出，评估智能体依据AASHTO-LRFD规范对设计方案进行合规性检查与物理验证，优化智能体根据反馈迭代改进设计，形成自动化的闭环设计流程。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的多智能体框架实现了超过98%的设计准确率，显著优于独立的通用LLM。关键发现：设计性能与模型规模并非必然正相关——8B参数的轻量模型在框架内可超越未受约束的631B旗舰模型的表现。

Conclusion: 多智能体协同框架能有效弥补LLM在结构工程设计中的物理基础不足问题，大幅提升设计精度。轻量模型在该框架下可达到甚至超越大模型性能，揭示了在降低计算成本的同时提升AI辅助工程工具可及性的重要潜力。

Abstract: The design of reinforced concrete highway barriers is a safety-critical process that requires strict compliance with regulatory provisions such as the AASHTO-LRFD bridge design guidelines. Current engineering practice relies heavily on manual, iterative, and heuristic calculations to satisfy complex nonlinear material and mechanics constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong generative capabilities, their direct application to structural engineering remains limited by hallucination risks and insufficient physical grounding. To address these challenges, this study proposes a novel "generation-evaluation-optimization" closed-loop framework for automated concrete barrier design using the multi-agent orchestration capabilities of AutoGen. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed agentic framework achieves over 98% design accuracy, significantly outperforming standalone general-purpose LLMs. More importantly, the study reveals that design performance is not necessarily correlated with model scale, where an 8B-parameter lightweight model could outperform unconstrained 631B-parameter flagship models. This finding highlights the potential to substantially reduce computational costs while improving the accessibility of AI-assisted engineering tools for industry applications. The source code for the proposed multi-agent design framework is available at the project GitHub repository: https://github.com/MXY820/barrier-design. Keywords: Structural Engineering; Multi-Agent Systems; Large Language Models; Concrete Barrier Design; AutoGen; Design Automation.

</details>


### [340] [Automating Geometry-Intensive Compliance Checking in BIM: Graph-Based Semantic Reasoning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12065)
*Zixuan Xiao,Pei Troh Koh,Jun Ma,Jack C. P. Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SGR-BIM框架，通过动态构建跨模态知识图谱，将用户意图、规范语义与BIM几何数据对齐，实现无需硬编码的可解释几何合规推理，在消防规范数据集上达到84.3%准确率，较增强型单智能体基线提升8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIM自动合规检查的核心瓶颈在于高层规范逻辑与结构化IFC数据之间存在语义鸿沟，现有基于静态规则模板的方法难以完成多跳推理链，也无法解析跨建筑实体的潜在空间依赖关系。

Method: 提出SGR-BIM（空间-几何推理系统），采用图驱动推理框架：动态构建跨模态知识图谱，将用户意图、规范语义与BIM几何数据三者对齐融合，通过图谱遍历实现可解释的多跳空间-几何推理，避免刚性硬编码。

Result: 在679条经专家验证的消防规范查询上验证，SGR-BIM达到84.3%准确率，相比增强型工具单智能体基线系统提升8.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究为AEC行业提供了一种基于图的语义推理范式，显著提升了自动化几何合规检查流程的透明性和灵活性。

Abstract: Automating compliance check for geometry-intensive regulations remains a significant technical bottleneck in Building Information Modeling (BIM), primarily due to the semantic disparity between high-level regulatory logic and structured IFC data. Existing methods, often reliant on static rule templates, struggle to traverse multi-hop reasoning chains or resolve latent spatial dependencies across multiple building entities. To address these challenges, a Spatial-Geometric Reasoning System for Building Information Modeling (SGR-BIM) is proposed as an integrative graph-driven reasoning framework. SGR-BIM dynamically constructs a cross-modal knowledge graph that aligns user intent, regulatory semantics, and BIM geometry, enabling interpretable reasoning without rigid hard-coding. Validated on 679 expert-verified queries from fire safety codes, the framework achieves 84.3% accuracy, representing an 8.6% improvement over enhanced-tool single-agent baselines. This research provides a graph-based semantic reasoning paradigm, enhancing the transparency and flexibility of automated geometric compliance check workflows in the Architecture, Engineering, and Construction (AEC) industry.

</details>


### [341] [IntElicit: Eliciting and Assessing Contextualized Creativity via Dialogue Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12086)
*Mingjia Li,Jin Wu,Hong Qian,Wenhao Huang,Yiyang Huang,Yiwen Zhang,Chanjin Zheng,Xiangfeng Wang,Aimin Zhou,Jiajun Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 IntElicit 框架，通过对话策略优化的自适应 AI 访谈者，在工具中介环境中进行情境化创造力评估。该框架通过非指令性知识和能动性支架减少认知熟练度与参与意愿的混淆，并引入分解过程奖励机制解决稀疏奖励与奖励黑客问题。实验表明，IntElicit 能揭示静态评估可能遗漏的创造潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情境化创造力评估虽具有高生态效度，但存在两重挑战：(1) 表现可能被认知熟练度（领域知识）和能动性（参与意愿）所混淆；(2) 在生成式 AI 时代，创造性问题解决越来越多地发生在工具中介和人机交互环境中，静态评估已与当代创造实践脱节。因此需要一种能够在交互式环境中有效引出并评估情境化创造力的新方法。

Method: 提出 IntElicit 框架，其核心是一个受约束的自适应 AI 访谈者，在多轮对话中提供非指令性知识和能动性支架，以降低非创造性混淆因素，同时保留参与者生成创意内容的责任。关键技术贡献是**分解过程奖励机制**：将策略优化与教学引出目标对齐，奖励那些能够引出参与者推理过程的提示，而非直接生成最优答案，从而应对开放式教育对话中的稀疏奖励和奖励黑客（如答案听写）问题。

Result: 通过参与者模拟实验和一项人类被试研究（N=64），IntElicit 相较于专家设计的基线方法，显著提升了引出的创造性成果质量。结果表明，交互式引出能够揭示静态 FPSP 风格评估可能遗漏的创造潜力。

Conclusion: IntElicit 为 AI 中介学习环境中的情境化创造力评估提供了一种形成性和诊断性的新视角。交互式引出方法能够更有效地捕捉真实创造潜力，弥补了静态评估在生态效度和当代实践适配性上的不足。

Abstract: Contextualized assessment offers high ecological validity for evaluating creativity but introduces a critical challenge: observed performance may be confounded with cognitive proficiency (domain knowledge) and agency (willingness to engage). Meanwhile, in the age of generative AI, creative problem solving increasingly occurs in tool-mediated and human--AI interactive environments, making fully static assessment less aligned with contemporary creative practice. To address these issues, this paper proposes IntElicit, a framework for eliciting and assessing contextualized creativity via dialogue policy optimization. IntElicit functions as a constrained adaptive AI Interviewer: it provides non-directive knowledge and agency scaffolds in multi-turn interaction to reduce non-creative confounders, while preserving participants' responsibility for generating the creative content being evaluated. Specifically, to tackle sparse rewards and potential reward hacking (e.g., answer dictation) in open-ended educational dialogue, IntElicit introduces a decomposed process reward mechanism. This mechanism aligns the policy with pedagogical elicitation, rewarding prompts that draw out participant reasoning rather than producing optimal answers on their behalf. Extensive experiments, including participant simulation and a human subject study (N=64), show that IntElicit improves elicited creative outcomes over expert-designed baselines. Together, the results suggest that interactive elicitation can reveal creative potential that static FPSP-style assessment may miss, providing a formative and diagnostic lens for contextualized creativity assessment in AI-mediated learning contexts.

</details>


### [342] [Towards Responsibly Non-Compliant Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12147)
*Marija Slavkovik,Marie Farrell,Louise Dennis,Michael Fisher,Simon Kolker,Emily C. Collins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了构建能够负责任地拒绝用户请求的自主智能代理（AI agents）的问题，提出机器"不服从"具有多种形式，并围绕任务拒绝的正当理由、推翻不服从的路径，以及安全风险追踪和责任转移等维度，勾勒了实现负责任非合规智能机器的研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主智能代理（如LLM-based agents）通常被设计为无条件遵从用户指令，但这可能带来安全、伦理和法律风险。作者认为，具备"负责任地不服从"能力的智能体是必要的——它们应当在特定情境下拒绝执行不当或危险的请求，而非盲目服从。这一需求在代理自主性日益增强的背景下尤为紧迫。

Method: 本文采用概念分析和框架构建的方法，首先对机器非合规（machine non-compliance）的多种形态进行分类，然后提出以三个核心支柱来锚定"负责任的非合规"：① 任务拒绝的正当理由（justifications for task refusal）；② 推翻非合规状态的覆盖路径（pathways to override non-compliance）；③ 安全风险的追踪机制与责任转移的界定（tracking of security risks and liability transfers）。通过这三个维度，勾勒出设计和评估负责任非合规智能体的系统框架。

Result: 文章的主要贡献是一个概念框架，揭示了机器非合规并非单一行为，而是包含多种形态的谱系；明确了负责任非合规必须同时考虑拒绝的正当性、可覆盖性以及风险-责任的可追溯性三个关键维度，为后续研究提供了系统化的议题地图。

Conclusion: 构建能够负责任地拒绝指令的自主智能体是一个多维度、多形态的复杂工程问题。仅实现技术上的"不服从"是不够的，还必须配套完善的正当理由机制、可控的覆盖路径以及清晰的安全与责任追踪体系。文章呼吁研究社区沿着这些方向深入探索，以实现真正负责任的非合规智能机器。

Abstract: We consider the problem of engineering autonomous intelligent agents that are capable to responsibly not comply with user requests. We argue that machine non-compliance comes in many different forms, and sketch the issues we should pursue on the road of accomplishing responsibly non-compliant intelligent machines. We anchor responsible non-compliance in justifications for task refusal, pathways to override the non-compliance, as well as careful tracking of security risks and liability transfers.

</details>


### [343] [The Impossibility of Eliciting Latent Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12268)
*Korbinian Friedl,Francis Rhys Ward,Paul Yushin Rapoport,Tom Everitt,Jonathan Richens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用因果影响图（CIDs）对潜在知识诱导（ELK）问题进行形式化建模，区分了可观测变量与潜在变量，定义了诚实智能体与目标错误泛化，并证明了：仅依赖智能体行为且仅在训练阶段提供完美反馈的策略，无法确定性地训练出诚实的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级AI系统可能掌握远超开发者或用户的环境知识，其中包含人类无法直接观测的潜在变量。如何让AI在面对关于这些潜在变量的问题时诚实地报告其信念，即潜在知识诱导（ELK）问题，是一个核心挑战。本文旨在为ELK提供严格的形式化框架，以清晰界定问题边界并分析其可解性。

Method: 作者采用因果影响图（CIDs）作为形式化工具，描述智能体训练环境与其主观世界表征之间的关系。通过CIDs，作者精确定义了：(1) 可观测变量与潜在变量的区分；(2) 智能体“诚实”的形式化含义；(3) 目标错误泛化的形式化定义。在此基础上，作者进一步证明了一个不可能性定理。

Result: 研究表明，在特定条件下，开发者可通过训练期间提供正确反馈来激励智能体诚实回答问题。然而，智能体存在一种自然但不可取的泛化方式：给出人类会评估为“真”的回答，而非诚实的回答。论文的核心结果是一个不可能性定理：不存在任何仅依赖于智能体行为的反馈式训练策略，能够确定性地产生一个诚实的智能体，即使训练期间的反馈是完美的。

Conclusion: ELK问题在仅依赖行为反馈的框架下存在根本性困难：完美训练反馈无法保证诚实智能体的产生，因为智能体可能学会迎合人类判断而非如实报告信念。CIDs为理解ELK及相关对齐问题（如目标错误泛化）提供了一个统一的形式化语言。解决ELK可能需要超越纯行为反馈的新方法。

Abstract: Advanced AI systems have extensive knowledge of their environments; in fact, their knowledge may (far) exceed that of their developers or users. Consequently, a desirable property for an AI system is that it is honest -- that it accurately reports its beliefs about the world. Designing an AI system to be honest may be difficult, especially if we want to ask it questions about latent variables in the environment -- variables which are hidden from the human interacting with it. This gives rise to the problem of eliciting latent knowledge (ELK): the problem of training an AI agent to honestly report its beliefs. In this paper, we make ELK formally precise using Causal Influence Diagrams (CIDs). CIDs can be used to describe the relationship between an agent's training environment and its subjective representation of the world. We use CIDs to formalise the distinction between observable and latent variables, to specify what exactly it means for an agent to be honest, and to formally define goal misgeneralisation. We show that, under certain circumstances, developers can incentivise an agent to honestly answer questions by providing correct feedback during training. However, a natural, but undesirable, way for an agent to generalise is to provide answers which humans would evaluate as true, rather than honest answers. We prove an impossibility theorem stating: There is no feedback-based training strategy that depends only on agent behaviour and with certainty produces an honest agent, even if feedback is perfect during training.

</details>


### [344] [A Five-Plane Reference Architecture for Runtime Governance of Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12320)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI
