TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向生产环境AI代理的运行时治理参考架构，通过五平面分解、随时可中断的中介机制、带能力衰减的复合主体以及结构化审计基底四个原语，解决了传统企业安全策略引擎无法处理代理系统中有状态、复合主体及委托链授权的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业安全架构原本围绕"静态与传输中的数据边界"构建，但生产AI代理溶解了这一假设——代理在业务流程内部读取上下文、调用工具并修改系统记录，风险从边界转移至工作流内部。现有策略引擎仅针对原子主体做请求时决策，无法应对代理系统中有状态的复合主体及委托链中的权限衰减问题。

Method: 提出四个可组合的治理原语：(1)五平面分解，包含一个裁定意图的推理平面和四个执行平面（网络、身份、端点、数据）；(2)随时可中断的中介机制；(3)带能力衰减的复合主体模型；(4)以审计作为结构化证据基底。在此基础上定义六种中断原语（泛化allow/deny），提出四项正确性不变量，并通过五个具体工作流展示七类生产代理威胁的阻断。参考实现给出了性能与正确性测量数据。

Result: 在参考实现中，能力衰减正确性与证据可重建性在每次试验中均成立；裁定延迟在个位数微秒级别；审计基底的防篡改特性完全符合设计预期。

Conclusion: 该架构对"委托行为"而非"模型行为"进行治理，作者明确界定了范围，并邀请以真实代理基准进行全系统评估作为下一步工作。

Abstract: Enterprise security was built to govern data boundaries: the protected surface was data at rest and in transit, and the controls -- access control, data-loss prevention, perimeter inspection -- governed crossings of that boundary. Production AI agents dissolve this assumption. An agent reads context, calls tools, invokes connectors, and modifies systems of record on an enterprise's behalf, so risk moves inside the workflow, into sequences of individually-permitted actions that may transform a business process no one authorized. Existing policy engines do not extend to this regime: they evaluate request-time decisions against atomic principals, where agentic systems require stateful evaluation against composite principals whose authority attenuates through delegation chains.
  We present a reference architecture for the runtime governance of production agents, built from four composable primitives: a five-plane decomposition (a reasoning plane that adjudicates intent, and four enforcement planes -- network, identity, endpoint, data -- that realize the decision), stop-anywhere mediation, composite principals with capability attenuation, and audit as a structured evidence substrate. We define a taxonomy of six interruption primitives that generalize allow and deny, state and argue for four correctness invariants, and demonstrate the foreclosure of seven production-agent threats across five concrete workflows. A reference implementation of the policy-engine core supplies measured evidence: attenuation correctness and evidence reconstructability hold on every trial, adjudication runs in single-digit microseconds, and the audit substrate's tamper-evidence behaves exactly as designed. We are explicit about scope: the architecture governs delegated action, not model behavior, and a full-system evaluation against a live agent benchmark is the invited next step.

</details>


### [345] [PROJECTMEM: A Local-First, Event-Sourced Memory and Judgment Layer for AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12329)
*Ripon Chandra Malo,Tong Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Projectmem 是一个开源、本地优先的记忆与判断层，通过追加式纯文本事件日志记录开发全过程，并生成 AI 可读摘要，同时提供确定性前置动作门控，防止 AI 编程助手重复已失败的修复或编辑已知脆弱文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 编程助手大多无状态，每次新会话都需重新读取项目文件、重新推导先前决策，甚至重复已失败的调试尝试。重建上下文每次消耗 5000-20000 tokens，瓶颈往往不在模型能力，而在于缺乏项目记忆。

Method: Projectmem 采用追加式纯文本事件日志，记录问题、尝试、修复、决策和笔记等类型化事件；通过确定性投影将日志转化为紧凑的 AI 可读摘要，经 MCP 协议提供服务；并内置确定性前置动作门控，在 Agent 即将重复失败修复或编辑已知脆弱文件时发出警告。系统完全离线运行，不可变日志同时作为可审计的溯源轨迹。

Result: 系统以三依赖 Python 包形式发布，包含 14 个 MCP 工具、19 条 CLI 命令、37 项自动化测试。通过为期两个月的自研究，在 10 个项目中记录了 207 条事件日志进行评估。

Conclusion: Projectmem 提出了"记忆即治理"（Memory-as-Governance）的框架：记忆不仅回答 Agent 的查询，更主动作用于其下一步行动，为解决 AI 编程助手的状态缺失问题提供了实用方案。

Abstract: AI coding assistants now support a growing share of software work, from quick scripts to production applications. Yet these agents remain largely stateless: each new session re-reads project files, re-derives prior decisions, and - most costly - may repeat debugging attempts that already failed. Reconstructing this context can consume an estimated 5,000-20,000 tokens per session; the bottleneck is often not model capability but missing project memory. We present projectmem, an open-source, local-first memory and judgment layer for AI coding agents. projectmem records development as an append-only, plain-text event log of typed events - issues, attempts, fixes, decisions, and notes - and deterministically projects that log into compact, AI-readable summaries served through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Beyond storage, projectmem adds a deterministic pre-action gate that warns an agent before it repeats a previously failed fix or edits a known-fragile file. We frame this as Memory-as-Governance: memory that does not merely answer the agent but acts on its next action. The system runs fully offline with no telemetry; its immutable log also serves as a provenance trail for reproducible, auditable AI-assisted development. projectmem ships as a three-dependency Python package (14 MCP tools, 19 CLI commands, 37 automated tests) and is evaluated through a two-month self-study across 10 projects comprising 207 logged events. Source code: https://github.com/riponcm/projectmem.

</details>


### [346] [Nonslop: A Gamified Experiment in Human-AI Collaborative Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12350)
*Maria Edwards,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过一个反乌托邦主题的游戏化写作实验，研究人类在创造性任务中何时接受或拒绝AI建议，揭示了效率与真实性之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的快速普及引发了对人类创造力和个人表达的担忧。现有研究多关注用户默认接受AI建议的行为，但缺乏对用户真实偏好的理解——即用户何时真正希望保持创作自主性，而非仅仅因为便利而接受AI建议。

Method: 设计了一个反乌托邦游戏化写作实验（N=74，共214条回复），刻意颠倒" helpful assistant"设计模式：AI主动提供词语建议，但游戏规则明确禁止参与者接受AI建议，从而创造能揭示用户真实偏好的条件。分析不同任务类型、用户行为和回复特征之间的关联。

Result: 揭示了用户在创造性任务中面临效率与真实性之间的张力；用户行为模式因任务类型、个人行为特征和回复特性而异，部分用户主动选择违反规则接受AI辅助，反映出对创作自主性的真实偏好。

Conclusion: 该游戏化方法为研究真实人机交互提供了新框架，同时为理解AI增强创造力中效率与真实性之间的深层矛盾提供了独特视角。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) raises critical questions about human creativity and individual expression in an era of AI-assisted creation. When do humans adopt AI suggestions, and what are the implications for individual voice?
  This study examines these questions through a gamified writing exercise where 74 participants (214 responses) replied to prompts while AI-generated word suggestions were available as they wrote. The game simulates a dystopian future in which an AI is attempting to learn from what remains of human individuality, and disincentivizes AI-like writing. In doing so, it attempts to create conditions that reveal authentic user preferences rather than default behaviors, such as accepting a readily available AI-generated suggestion. Note that this is a deliberate inversion of the "helpful assistant" design pattern; the system is explicitly forbidding you from accepting AI suggestions.
  We analyze user behavior patterns across different task types, user behaviors, and response characteristics to understand the factors influencing human-AI interaction in creative tasks. The study focuses on when users choose to maintain creative autonomy versus violating the rules of the game and accepting AI assistance. It also explores how these choices relate to response patterns, task characteristics, and user behavior. This gamified approach offers both a framework for studying authentic human-AI interaction and a provocative lens for understanding the tension between efficiency and authenticity in AI-augmented creativity.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [347] [Bayesian Causal Machine Learning for Cure Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11405)
*Antonio R. Linero,F. Javier Rubio,Piyali Basak*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出BartCure，一种贝叶斯因果机器学习方法，用于在存在治愈亚群的情况下估计因果效应。它将RMST的因果效应分解为随机治愈和随机潜伏成分，并在CALGB 40101乳腺癌试验中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存研究中，治疗可能通过两种机制惠及患者：提高治愈概率或延迟未治愈患者的失败时间。当前因果生存分析文献缺乏量化哪种机制占主导及其在亚群中异质性的方法。标准方法仅关注有限期生存或RMST，而治愈模型多不估计因果效应，存在明显空白。

Method: 提出BartCure——融合贝叶斯可加回归树（BART）与治愈率模型的因果机器学习方法。定义有治愈亚群存在下的新因果效应：将RMST的因果效应分解为随机治愈成分（cure fraction差异）和随机潜伏成分（latency effect差异），并关联至随机干预效应和主分层因果效应。采用贝叶斯非参数方法进行估计与推断。

Result: 模拟研究表明，BartCure在估计平均效应方面具有竞争力，且在保守检测治疗效应异质性方向上尤为有效。在CALGB 40101乳腺癌试验应用中，成功估计了平均和亚群因果效应，并识别出治疗效应的异质性模式。

Conclusion: BartCure为存在治愈亚群的生存数据提供了有意义的因果效应分解框架，弥补了因果生存分析与治愈模型之间的方法学鸿沟，能有效揭示治疗受益机制的异质性，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: In survival studies, treatments can benefit patients through different mechanisms: a treatment may increase the probability of being cured or delay failure among patients who are not cured. Quantifying which mechanism is dominant, and whether it varies across subpopulations, is clinically important, yet there is limited work in the causal machine learning literature addressing this problem. Standard causal survival learners target finite-horizon survival or restricted mean survival time, while many cure models capture cure structures without estimating causal effects. In this work, we define meaningful causal effects in the presence of a cured subpopulation and introduce BartCure, a Bayesian causal machine learning approach for estimating them. The causal effects we recommend decompose the causal effect on restricted mean survival time into a stochastic cure and stochastic latency component, and we relate these new effects to both stochastic intervention effects and causal effects in principal strata. In simulations, BartCure is competitive for estimating average effects and is especially effective at conservatively detecting the direction of treatment-effect heterogeneity. We apply BartCure to estimate average and subgroup causal effects and to identify treatment effect heterogeneity in the CALGB 40101 breast cancer trial.

</details>


### [348] [Group Sequential Sample Size for Comparing Two Survival Probabilities at a Specific Time Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11414)
*Susan Halabi,Lu Liu,Chenxi Yu,Yuan Wu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种同时确定两个生存概率检验样本量的新方法，可在固定设计和群序贯设计中控制I类错误，适用于新辅助治疗对比单纯手术、非比例风险场景（如免疫治疗）及随机II期试验，并提供了R Shiny交互工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含新辅助治疗的随机试验中，需在固定时间点评估生存结局，且免疫治疗等场景常违反比例风险假设（存在延迟效应、时变效应或生存曲线交叉）；此外，随机II期试验样本量小、依赖替代终点，亟需高效且能严格控制I类错误的样本量计算方法。

Method: 提出一种在预先指定时间点同时检验两个生存概率的样本量计算方法，支持固定设计和群序贯设计，通过模拟覆盖不同假设差异、失效分布、删失比例和检验效能，并整合Lan-DeMets误差消耗函数进行期中分析。

Result: 模拟显示方法性能稳健；期中分析在每次期中查看时均降低I类错误并提升检验效能，且结果不受潜在失效时间分布或误差消耗函数选择的影响。通过肾癌和前列腺癌案例进行了方法说明。

Conclusion: 该方法为固定时间点生存结局评估提供了灵活、稳健的样本量确定工具，特别适用于新辅助治疗试验、非比例风险场景及II期随机试验，配套的R Shiny应用便于研究者交互式计算样本量，具有广泛的实践价值。

Abstract: We propose a novel method that simultaneously determines the sample size for testing two survival probabilities at a pre-specified ltime while guaranteeing type I error control in both fixed and group-sequential trial designs. Simulations across varying hypothesized differences, failure distributions, censoring proportions, and nominal powers demonstrate consistent performance, while interim analyses highlight reduced type I error and increased power at each look, regardless of the underlying failure time distribution or spending function. Importantly, our method is especially useful for evaluating survival outcomes at a fixed time in randomized trials where one treatment arm includes neoadjuvant therapy prior to surgery while the other involves surgery alone. Furthermore, it is advantageous when the proportional hazards assumption is not satisfied, as often occurs in immunotherapy trials with delayed or time-varying treatment effects or crossing survival curves. The method is also applicable to randomized phase II trials, where smaller sample sizes and the use of intermediate or surrogate time-to-event endpoints demand efficient data use and robust error control. We illustrate the approach with motivating examples in renal and prostate cancer. An accompanying R Shiny application enables investigators to compute sample sizes interactively, facilitating practical trial planning in diverse settings.

</details>


### [349] [Second-Order Least Squares as a Special Case of the Polynomial Maximization Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11421)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文证明了在条件同方差非高斯误差的线性回归中，最优加权的二阶最小二乘法（SLS）与二次广义多项式矩方法（PMM）在总体估计方程层面上完全等价：二者选择相同的最优线性组合、求解同一正规方程组、共享影响函数，并达到共同的渐近方差。该等价性在异方差下瓦解，而PMM在高阶矩上保有SLS无法企及的效率储备。文中给出了二阶和三阶的闭合形式系数，并通过Lean 4形式化验证和蒙特卡洛模拟加以佐证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非高斯误差的线性回归中，OLS并非渐近有效。已有文献分别提出了SLS（利用残差前两阶条件矩）和PMM（利用残差前两阶无条件矩的多项式组合）来提升估计效率，但两者之间的关系尚未明确。本文旨在揭示这两种方法在总体层面的内在联系，并探讨在更高阶矩和异方差情形下两者的分化。

Method: 理论推导：从总体估计方程出发，比较SLS的最优加权方案与PMM的线性组合系数，证明两者在条件同方差下的一致性，并推导出共同的渐近方差表达式及方差缩减系数g₂的闭合形式。进一步将分析拓展至三阶PMM（g₃系数）、异方差边界条件和一般单调性证明（嵌套论证）。形式化验证：使用Lean 4对代数核心进行机器检验。数值模拟：通过蒙特卡洛实验展示有限样本下的等价性、效率储备和异方差边界行为。

Result: （1）条件同方差下，SLS与PMM在总体层面完全等价，渐近方差均为c₂g₂/N，其中g₂=1−γ₃²/(2+γ₄)。（2）异方差下两者分离：无条件PMM主体与条件SLS加权不再重合，对称误差下损失效率，非对称误差下损失一致性。（3）PMM在二阶以上保有效率储备：对于对称低峰态误差，SLS退化为OLS，而三阶PMM可提取峰态信息，典型非对称分布下效率增益达30%–50%。（4）单调性g_{S+1}≤g_S≤1通过嵌套论证成立。Lean 4验证了g₂、g₃闭合形式、g₂≤1及对称退化等代数性质。

Conclusion: SLS与PMM在条件同方差非高斯设定下本质上是同一估计方程的不同表达，这一等价性为两类文献搭建了桥梁。但在更一般的数据结构中，PMM通过纳入更高阶无条件矩信息具备内在的效率优势，揭示了多项式矩方法相对于条件矩方法的理论潜力。

Abstract: We prove that optimally weighted second-order least squares (SLS) and the degree-two generalized polynomial maximization method (PMM) are the same population estimating equation for linear regression with conditionally homoskedastic non-Gaussian errors: they choose the same optimal linear combination of the first two centered residual moments, solve one population normal system, share one influence function, and attain the common asymptotic variance $c_2g_2/N$ -- the ordinary-least-squares slope-variance factor $c_2$ scaled by the PMM variance-reduction coefficient $g_2=1-γ_3^2/(2+γ_4)$ (with $γ_3,γ_4$ the error skewness and excess kurtosis). Feasible plug-in implementations are therefore first-order equivalent, with only higher-order finite-sample differences. The identity is sharp: under heteroskedasticity the unconditional PMM body and the conditional SLS weighting separate, costing efficiency for symmetric errors and consistency for asymmetric errors. Beyond degree two, PMM holds an efficiency reserve that SLS cannot reach within its second-moment span. For symmetric platykurtic errors SLS collapses to ordinary least squares for the slope, while degree-three PMM exploits kurtosis information outside the SLS moment span through a closed-form coefficient $g_3$; for canonical asymmetric laws this reserve is $30$--$50\%$ within the degree-three polynomial moment class. The Lean 4 development machine-checks the degree-specific algebraic core -- the closed forms for $g_2$ and $g_3$, the $g_2\le1$ result, the design cancellations, and the symmetric collapse -- while the general monotonicity $g_{S+1}\le g_S\le1$ is proved analytically by nesting. A Monte Carlo study illustrates the equivalence, the reserve, and the heteroskedastic boundary at finite samples.

</details>


### [350] [A Likelihood Ratio Testing Approach for Interval-Censored Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11439)
*Yuan Wu,Susan Halabi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该文针对区间删失数据下的Cox比例风险模型，提出了一种基于样条筛的稳健似然比检验方法，建立了其渐近分布理论，并通过模拟和实际临床数据验证了该方法在小样本下具有更优的误差控制与检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区间删失数据在临床研究中普遍存在，传统基于Wald检验的方法在小样本条件下表现不稳定且功效不足，亟需一种数值稳定、理论完备且小样本性能优良的假设检验方法。

Method: 本文提出了基于样条筛（spline-sieve）的似然比检验框架：首先构建计算高效的样条筛非参数估计框架以确保数值稳定性，然后严格推导了所提似然比统计量的渐近分布，为区间删失数据下的Cox模型假设检验提供了可靠的理论基础。

Result: 大量模拟研究表明，与传统Wald型检验相比，所提方法在I类错误控制上更为优越，同时具有更高的检验功效。在一项真实临床数据集的分析中，该方法同样展现出良好的实用性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的样条筛似然比检验为区间删失数据下的Cox回归分析提供了一种稳健、高效且理论严谨的推断工具，尤其适用于小样本临床研究场景。

Abstract: Interval-censored data frequently arise in clinical research where event times are only known to fall within specific assessment windows. Although the Cox proportional hazards model is a standard approach for such data, existing Wald-type tests often suffer from instability or poor performance in small samples. In this paper, we propose a robust spline-sieve-based likelihood ratio test for interval-censored data. We develop a computationally efficient estimation framework that ensures numerical stability. Furthermore, we rigorously establish the asymptotic distribution of the proposed likelihood ratio statistic, providing a solid theoretical foundation for statistical inference. Extensive simulation studies demonstrate that our approach achieves superior error control and higher power compared with traditional approaches. The practical utility of the method is further illustrated through the analysis of a real-world clinical dataset.

</details>


### [351] [What is the Long-Term Value of Reliability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11526)
*Chenyu Qiu,Xu Kuang,Inessa Liskovich,Ali Rauh,Stefan Wager*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: Chronos LTV 是一个利用马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）来量化服务延迟等缺陷对关键业务指标长期影响的系统，通过边际策略效应（marginal policy effect）进行识别，并在序贯无混淆假设下用协变量平衡算法进行估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多在线平台的服务缺陷（如延迟交付）不仅影响单次交易，还可能通过改变客户的长期行为（如留存、复购、生命周期价值）产生深远影响。然而，现有方法难以在观测数据中识别和估计这种长期因果效应，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者将客户交互过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），将待估量形式化定义为"边际策略效应"——即移动平均延迟率的策略干预下的期望结果变化。在序贯无混淆假设（给定观测到的订单特征，延迟近似随机）下，证明该效应可识别，并提出一种简单的协变量平衡算法进行估计。

Result: 在该框架下，只要满足序贯无混淆条件，长期因果效应可以被一致地识别和估计，协变量平衡方法能够有效控制由于订单特征差异带来的混杂偏差。

Conclusion: Chronos LTV 提供了一种在观测数据中估计服务缺陷长期影响的有效方法，为平台型企业衡量延迟等服务问题的真实业务代价提供了因果推断工具。

Abstract: We describe Chronos LTV, a system to measure the long-term impact of delays and other service defects on key business metrics. We use Markov decision processes to model customer interactions over time, and formalize our target estimand as the marginal policy effect with respect to moving the average delay rate. Given this setup, we show that we can identify long-term effects under a sequential unconfoundedness assumption where delays are as good as random given observed order characteristics; and can estimate these effects using a simple covariate-balancing algorithm.

</details>


### [352] [Estimating the local false discovery rate under an unknown symmetric null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11548)
*Daniel Xiang,William Fithian,Nikolaos Ignatiadis,Jake A. Soloff,Asaf Weinstein*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究在双组模型中估计局部错误发现率（lfdr），其中零分布的唯一假设是关于零对称。作者提出通过估计替代密度比 f(-w)/f(w)（w>0）来估计 lfdr，并研究了多种估计方法，最终提出一种基于自然三次样条基的 logistic 回归方法。同时证明该替代量的任何一致估计量都能使阈值化多重检验程序实现渐近 lfdr 控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于现代多重假设检验框架（尤其是变量选择问题），其中用户指定的分数可转化为零分布关于零对称的统计量，而非零假设通常在右侧富集。现有方法如 knockoff filter 虽可利用该零性质控制 FDR，但更合适的目标是控制被拒绝假设的局部错误发现率（如 Soloff et al. 2024 在标准双组模型中所述）。本文旨在拓展这一方向，在仅假设零对称的更精简双组模型中估计 lfdr。

Method: 在仅假设零分布关于零对称（不要求已知 f₀）的双组模型中，作者将 lfdr 估计问题转化为估计替代密度比 f(-w)/f(w)（w>0，其中 f 为边缘密度）。研究并比较了多种估计方法，最终提出一种基于自然三次样条基的 logistic 回归方法。理论层面，证明了该替代比的任何一致估计量都能保证：在标称水平上对估计值进行阈值化处理的多重检验程序可达到渐近 lfdr 控制。

Result: 提出的基于自然三次样条 logistic 回归的估计方法能够有效估计替代密度比 f(-w)/f(w)，进而估计 lfdr。理论上证明，该替代量的任意一致估计量均可确保阈值化多重检验程序实现渐近 lfdr 控制，即当以标称水平 α 对估计的 lfdr 进行阈值化时，被拒绝假设的平均 lfdr 渐近不超过 α。

Conclusion: 在仅假设零分布对称的精简双组模型中，通过估计替代密度比 f(-w)/f(w) 来估计 lfdr 是一条可行且有效的途径。所提出的基于自然三次样条 logistic 回归的方法具有良好的理论与实用价值，为无需已知零分布具体形式下的局部错误发现率控制提供了新工具。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with estimating the local false discovery rate (lfdr) in a two-groups model where the only assumption regarding the null distribution is symmetry about zero. Our motivation comes from the contemporary framework for multiple hypothesis testing, particularly relevant in variable selection problems, which transforms any user-specified scores into statistics whose null distributions are symmetric about zero, whereas enrichment to the right of zero is generally expected for the non-nulls. While modern methods such as the knockoff filter (Barber and Candes; 2015) are able to exploit the null property for controlling the false discovery rate (FDR), an arguably more appropriate goal is to target control of the local false discovery rate for the rejected hypotheses, as proposed in Soloff et al. (2024) where the standard two-groups model (known $f_0$ and independence) is analyzed. Here, we take a step in this direction and propose to estimate the lfdr by targeting the surrogate density ratio $f(-w)/f(w)$, for $w>0$, where $f$ is the marginal density in the aforementioned ``stripped-down'' two-groups model. We study several estimators and propose a logistic regression based method with natural cubic spline basis. We also show that any consistent estimator of this surrogate yields asymptotic lfdr control of the multiple testing procedure that thresholds the estimate at the nominal level.

</details>


### [353] [The Triply-Randomized Negative Binomial Beta for Robust Regression and Conjugate Models of Bounded Support Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11624)
*Jimmy Lederman,Aaron Schein*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出三元随机化负二项贝塔分布（TNBbeta），通过引入边界参数ε解决了标准贝塔回归对异常值敏感、无法处理精确零值及缺乏共轭先验等问题，并利用Pólya-gamma增广实现高效的Gibbs采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝塔分布虽常用于[0,1]有界响应变量的回归建模，但存在三个关键缺陷：(1)对异常值敏感；(2)无法容纳精确的零观测值；(3)缺乏闭合形式的共轭先验，导致贝叶斯推断效率低下。亟需一种既能保留贝塔分布优势、又能克服上述局限的新分布族。

Method: 作者提出TNBbeta分布，通过三个相依负二项随机变量对标准贝塔分布的参数进行随机化构造。其核心参数包括中位数p、浓度参数q和边界参数ε（允许在0和1处有正密度）。将p和q通过logit链接函数与高斯潜变量连接后，借助Pólya-gamma增广可获得闭合形式更新。三个负二项变量的完全条件分布均为负二项分布，从而可构建简洁的辅助变量Gibbs采样器。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据实验中，TNBbeta回归模型在每秒有效样本量和留出预测性能上常优于标准贝塔回归，尤其在存在异常值时优势明显。在森林冠层覆盖度案例研究中，该框架可自然融合空间结构和精确零观测值。

Conclusion: 该工作显著扩展了可高效拟合的[0,1]有界支持数据的贝叶斯模型类别，为处理含零值、异常值的有界响应回归问题提供了统一且高效的框架。

Abstract: The beta distribution is the default choice of likelihood in many regression problems with a $[0,1]$-bounded support response despite its sensitivity to outliers, inability to accommodate exact zero observations, and a lack of closed-form conjugate priors. We address these shortcomings by introducing the triply-randomized negative binomial beta distribution, denoted $\mathrm{TNBbeta}(p,\,q,\,\varepsilon)$, parameterized by a median $p$, concentration parameter $q$, and boundary parameter $\varepsilon$ which permits positive density at $0$ and $1$. The TNBbeta arises by randomizing the parameters of a standard beta distribution with three dependent negative binomial random variables, each of whose complete conditional distribution we show is itself negative binomial. Moreover, connecting $p$ and $q$ to Gaussian latent variables with logit link functions yields closed-form updates via Pólya-gamma augmentation. Together, these properties yield simple auxiliary-variable Gibbs samplers for regression models of bounded-support data, which often outperform standard beta regression approaches in terms of effective sample size per second and held-out prediction, especially in the presence of outliers. In a case study of forest canopy cover, we demonstrate that this framework can easily incorporate spatial structure and exact zero observations. Overall, this work substantially expands the class of Bayesian models for $[0,1]$-bounded support data that can be fit efficiently.

</details>


### [354] [Bracketing Relationships of Weighted Average Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11715)
*Pengfei Tian,Fan Yang,Peng Ding*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 在观测性研究的因果推断中，重叠权重（overlap weight）下的平均处理效应（ATE）在处理效应与倾向得分满足单调关系时，被界定在处理组平均处理效应（ATT）和对照组平均处理效应（ATC）之间。该结果进一步推广至工具变量设定下的加权局部平均处理效应（LATE）及其他权重，并据此推荐使用"CP图"进行诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在观测性因果推断中，不同加权方法（如ATT、ATC、ATE权重）给出的估计结果往往难以调和。作者旨在揭示重叠权重下ATE与ATT、ATC之间的内在排序关系，从而为选择权重和理解估计差异提供理论基础，并提供一个可视化的诊断工具。

Method: 本文在观测性研究的标准框架下，采用理论推导的方法。首先在倾向得分与条件平均处理效应（CATE）满足单调性关系的假设下，证明了重叠权重（权重正比于给定协变量下处理的条件方差）下的ATE严格介于ATT与ATC之间。随后，将该结果扩展至二元工具变量和二元处理的LATE设定，并进一步推广至其他加权方案。最后，基于理论提出"CP图"（将估计的CATE对估计的倾向得分作图）作为诊断建议。

Result: 在CATE与倾向得分单调的条件下，重叠权重ATE满足 ATT ≤ ATE_overlap ≤ ATC（或反向排序）。该排序关系同样适用于工具变量框架下的加权LATE，并可推广至一类更广泛的权重族。CP图可用于直观检验单调性假设及评估不同权重估计之间差异的合理性。

Conclusion: 重叠权重ATE自然落在ATT与ATC之间（在单调性条件下），这为调和不同因果估计量之间的分歧提供了理论依据。研究者应使用CP图来诊断CATE与倾向得分的关系模式，以辅助选择合适的加权策略。

Abstract: Under the canonical setting of observational studies for causal inference, we show that the average treatment effect under the overlap weight, the weight that is proportional to the conditional variance of the treatment given the covariates, is bounded between the average treatment effects on the treated and control, under a monotonic relationship between the propensity score and the conditional average treatment effect. We further extend the result to weighted local average treatment effects, under the canonical setting with a binary instrumental variable and a binary treatment. We also extend the results to other weights. Based on the theory, we recommend the ``CP-plot'' of the estimated conditional average treatment effect against the estimated propensity score.

</details>


### [355] [Hierarchical excitatory processes for modelling event-time data in the presence of exogenous stimuli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11768)
*Francesco Sanna Passino,Nicholas A. Heard,Jeffrey W. Brown,William N. Frost,Vince P. Lyzinski*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: HEP是一种灵活的点过程模型，通过层次化结构对外部重复刺激下的兴奋效应进行建模，其核函数参数随时间动态演化，支持基于似然的推断和模型聚类，并在海兔神经数据中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重复外部刺激下观测事件时间数据时，现有点过程模型难以灵活捕捉兴奋效应强度随刺激重复而动态变化的模式，需要一个既能表征刺激驱动的兴奋性、又能解释其随时间演化的可解释框架。

Method: 提出层次兴奋过程(HEP)，将条件强度函数建模为外部刺激诱发兴奋效应的叠加，其中核函数参数按层次结构随时间动态演化；建立基于似然的推断方法，并将其嵌入基于模型的聚类框架以识别具有相似响应动态的潜在群组。

Result: 模拟研究表明该模型能有效恢复演化的潜在模式；在海兔(Aplysia)足神经节脉冲序列记录的应用中，HEP成功刻画了不同实验条件下神经元在重复刺激中的兴奋性特征。

Conclusion: HEP为重复刺激下的事件时间数据提供了一种兼具灵活性与可解释性的建模框架，在神经科学及其他涉及重复刺激-响应研究的领域中具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce the Hierarchical Excitatory Process (HEP), a flexible point process model for event-time data observed under repeated external stimuli. The proposed framework models the conditional intensity of a point process as a superposition of excitation effects induced by external stimuli, characterised by kernels with parameters dynamically evolving over time. This hierarchical construction enables modulation of excitation strength across repeated stimuli, providing an interpretable structure. We establish likelihood-based inference for the proposed model and embed HEP within a model-based clustering framework to identify latent groups sharing similar response dynamics. Simulation studies demonstrate the model's ability to recover evolving latent patterns, and an application to spike train recordings from the sea slug Aplysia pedal ganglion illustrates how HEPs are able to characterise stimulus-driven excitability of neurons across repeated stimulation under different experimental conditions.

</details>


### [356] [Model-based sparse mixed-type PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11887)
*Lauri Heinonen,Joni Virta*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了MTPCA，一种基于概率模型和矩估计的混合型数据主成分分析方法，能够处理连续、二值、整数及正连续变量，并支持稀疏载荷估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCA仅适用于连续型变量，而现实数据常包含多种类型（二值、计数、正连续等）。现有方法缺乏一个统一的、基于概率框架的混合型数据PCA方法，尤其缺乏对稀疏性的支持。

Method: 假设混合型数据由共享高斯潜变量通过指数族分布生成，利用矩方法估计潜变量协方差矩阵，进而进行主成分分析。同时提出基于经典稀疏PCA理论的载荷稀疏化方法，以及潜变量维数选择和主成分得分估计策略。

Result: 在模拟混合型数据上验证了方法性能，并在Zoo数据集（二值动物特征）上进行了实证展示。

Conclusion: MTPCA为混合型数据提供了一种统一的概率PCA框架，兼具稀疏性和可解释性，适用于多种实际应用场景。

Abstract: This work presents a new method for principal component analysis (PCA) of a mixed-type data consisting of continuous, binary, integer-valued and positive continuous variables. The data are assumed to come from a probability model, where the parameters of the exponential family distributions are determined by a set of shared Gaussian latent variables. The proposed method, MTPCA, is based on estimating the covariance matrix of these latent mixtures through the method of moments. A way to sparsify the component loadings is presented and aligns with the classical theory of sparse PCA. We propose a strategy for estimating the principal component scores and discuss the choice of the latent dimension. The method's performance is studied with a simulated mixed-type data and we illustrate the model on the Zoo data set consisting of binary animal characteristics.

</details>


### [357] [Composite likelihood inference of fractional Gaussian processes with sequentially optimal subset selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11962)
*Mathis Fourreau,Matthieu Garcin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究复合似然方法在线性高斯模型（尤其是分数布朗运动与分数高斯噪声）中的应用，推导了Fisher信息与Godambe信息的理论表达式，并设计了最大化Godambe信息的子集选择策略，以在估计精度与计算成本之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全观测最大似然估计在时间序列中计算成本高昂，复合似然方法通过将观测划分为多个子集来降低计算量，但其渐近性质与Godambe信息密切相关。作者旨在将这一方法系统性地应用于分数布朗运动与分数高斯噪声等线性高斯模型，并解决子集选择设计问题。

Method: (1) 对线性高斯模型（含fBm和fGn）推导Fisher信息矩阵与Godambe信息矩阵的显式理论表达式；(2) 提出一种序贯最大化Godambe信息的子集选择设计方案；(3) 通过数值模拟将所提方法与矩估计法、全最大似然估计进行对比；(4) 在真实数据（股指波动率序列与风速时间序列）上进行实证应用。

Result: 推导得到了fBm和fGn下Fisher信息与Godambe信息的理论闭式表达式；所提出的子集选择策略能够通过调节子集大小有效控制估计精度与计算成本的折中；模拟结果表明，该方法在保持较低计算成本的同时估计精度接近全最大似然估计，优于矩估计法；在股指波动率和风速数据的实证中也验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: 复合似然方法在线性高斯时间序列模型中是一种有效的参数估计策略，通过合理设计子集选择方案可在估计精度与计算效率之间实现灵活平衡，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The composite likelihood method reduces the computational cost of parameter estimation in time series by considering several subsets of observations instead of all observations at once. The asymptotic properties of this method are related to the Godambe information, an extension of the Fisher information that accounts for the dependence between subsets of observations. We aim to apply this method to linear Gaussian models, in particular fractional Brownian motion and fractional Gaussian noise. We derive theoretical expressions for their Fisher information and their Godambe information and deduce a subset selection design that sequentially maximizes the Godambe information. The size of the subsets then allows us to control the trade-off between estimation accuracy and computational cost. Through simulations, we compare this method with the method of moments and maximum likelihood estimation, and we apply it to real data, namely volatility series of a stock index and a wind speed time series.

</details>


### [358] [Introducing precision-weighted bias as a performance measure to inform the inclusion of adaptive designs in meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12015)
*Martin Law,David S. Robertson,Sofia S. Villar,Tim P. Morris,Babak Choodari-Oskooei,Thomas Jaki,Ian R. White*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的统计性能指标：精度加权偏倚（precision-weighted bias），定义为估计量的无条件偏倚乘以其精度。研究表明，共同效应Meta分析的总体偏倚约等于各研究精度加权偏倚的精度加权均值，而非无条件偏倚的均值。模拟显示，自适应设计虽可能有非零无条件偏倚，但精度加权偏倚常为零，纳入Meta分析几乎不改变总体偏倚。作者建议将精度加权偏倚作为无条件偏倚和条件偏倚的标准补充指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GRADE和CONSORT等指南常将自适应设计中偏倚增大的潜在风险视为系统评价排除此类设计的理由，但这种做法缺乏对偏倚如何传播至Meta分析总体估计的深入考量。作者旨在纠正这一认知偏差，提供一个更合理的指标来判定是否应将自适应设计纳入Meta分析。

Method: 作者首先从理论上推导了共同效应Meta分析中总体偏倚与各研究精度加权偏倚之间的关系，证明总体偏倚等于各研究精度加权偏倚的精度加权均值。随后通过模拟研究，对比自适应设计在无条件偏倚和精度加权偏倚两个指标下的表现，并评估纳入此类设计对Meta分析总体偏倚的实际影响。

Result: 理论推导表明，共同效应Meta分析的偏倚是各组分的精度加权偏倚的精度加权平均，而非无条件偏倚的平均。模拟结果显示，自适应设计虽然可能表现出非零的无条件偏倚，但其精度加权偏倚常为零，因此纳入后对Meta分析总体偏倚的影响微乎其微。

Conclusion: 精度加权偏倚是比无条件偏倚更优越的指标，用于判定是否应将自适应设计纳入Meta分析。作者建议在模拟研究中将精度加权偏倚作为无条件偏倚和条件偏倚的标准补充，以支持更包容、更准确的证据合成。

Abstract: We propose a novel, intuitive measure of statistical performance: precision-weighted bias. Precision-weighted bias is defined as the unconditional bias of an estimator weighted by the degree of information (precision) it contains. Current guidelines, such as GRADE and CONSORT, often view the potential for increased bias in adaptive designs as a deterrent for the inclusion of such designs in systematic reviews. However, we demonstrate that the bias in a common-effect meta-analysis is approximately equal to the precision-weighted average of the precision-weighted biases of its constituent studies, rather than of their unweighted unconditional biases. Through simulation studies, we show that while adaptive designs may exhibit unweighted bias, they frequently have zero precision-weighted bias. Consequently, including these designs often results in a negligible change to the overall meta-analysis bias. These results suggest that precision-weighted bias is a superior indicator for determining whether to include an adaptive design in a meta-analysis. We recommend that precision-weighted bias be used as a standard complement to unweighted unconditional and conditional bias in simulation studies to support more inclusive and accurate evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [359] [Adaptive spatial blocking for scalable clustering inference with applications to high-throughput spatial proteomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12021)
*Mingyu Go,Julia Wrobel,Hoseung Song*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自适应空间分块的Ripley's K函数检验框架，通过抽取不相交的局部块并聚合聚类证据，实现对大规模点图案的空间聚类推断，兼顾统计功效与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Ripley's K函数的空间聚类方法需利用图像中所有点的空间信息，在高通量空间蛋白质组学等大规模数据场景下计算代价过高，亟需可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 提出基于块的检验框架：设计自适应空间分块算法，在满足点计数和形状约束的条件下从图像中抽取不相交的局部块，随后在各块内评估聚类证据并聚合，利用渐近正态近似实现快速p值计算。

Result: 数值研究表明该方法在统计功效与计算效率之间取得良好平衡；在健康人肠道空间蛋白质组学数据中，成功检出浆细胞的强空间聚集及其与巨噬细胞的共定位，且能有效扩展至大图像。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应空间分块检验框架是一种适用于大规模点图案空间聚类分析的高效方法，兼具统计有效性和计算可扩展性。

Abstract: Ripley's K-function is a widely used spatial summary statistic for assessing clustering in point patterns. However, existing K-based methods can be computationally prohibitive for large-scale data, particularly in high-throughput spatial proteomics, because they rely on spatial information from all points in the image. To address this challenge, we propose a computationally efficient block-based testing framework that extracts disjoint local blocks from an image and aggregates clustering evidence across them. The proposed adaptive spatial blocking algorithm constructs blocks satisfying point-count and shape constraints, enabling scalable spatial clustering inference and fast p-value computation through an asymptotic normal approximation. Numerical studies demonstrate that the proposed method provides a favorable balance between statistical power and computational efficiency. In an application to healthy human intestine spatial proteomics data, our method detects strong spatial aggregation of plasma cells and colocalization between plasma cells and macrophages, while scaling favorably to large images.

</details>


### [360] [Bayesian Effect Selection for Additive Quantile Regression with an Application to Air Pollution Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12164)
*Nadja Klein,Aaron Wei Qi Lee,Jorge Mateu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯效应选择方法，用于加性分位数回归。通过Demmler-Reinsch基展开将每个加性效应正交分解为线性和非线性部分，并采用分离的spike-and-slab先验对两种成分进行变量选择，实现数据驱动的模型构建。在对马德里NO₂污染数据的分析中，揭示了极端浓度受气候变量和交通相关空间结构的不同驱动机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气污染监管限值通常基于浓度阈值的超标次数，与污染分布的 conditional quantiles 直接相关。现有基于惩罚样条的混合模型表示（MMR）虽能灵活刻画非线性效应，但无法有效分离线性和非线性成分。因此需要一种能够同时进行线性和非线性效应选择的分位数回归方法，以更好地理解协变量如何驱动极端污染事件。

Method: 1. 采用Demmler-Reinsch基展开替代传统MMR，将每个加性效应正交分解为线性和非线性两部分，并从理论上证明两者均可被一致估计。2. 基于非对称拉普拉斯工作似然构建贝叶斯分位数回归框架。3. 在线性和非线性成分的标量重要性参数上分别设置spike-and-slab先验，实现贝叶斯效应选择。4. 设计高效的Gibbs采样器进行后验推断。

Result: 1. 模拟研究表明，该方法对非对称拉普拉斯工作似然的模型误设具有稳健性，且性能优于传统MMR方法。2. 对马德里NO₂数据的分析揭示了极端污染浓度（阈值相关分位数）与气候变量（如温度、风速等）和交通相关空间结构之间的差异化驱动关系，这些关系在传统均值回归或无条件分位数分析中无法体现。

Conclusion: 该方法为极端空气污染事件的灵活建模提供了有力的统计工具，能够区分协变量的线性和非线性效应，支持短期决策制定，帮助地方当局减轻或预防NO₂浓度限值的超标事件。

Abstract: Air pollution regulatory limits are typically defined in terms of exceedances of concentration thresholds which are naturally related to conditional quantiles of the pollutant distribution and are therefore of direct relevance for assessing severe pollution events. At the same time, it is important to determine not only whether a covariate affects air pollution but also whether this effect is linear, nonlinear, or both. We address these issues by developing a Bayesian effect selection approach for additive quantile regression. While commonly used mixed model representations (MMRs) of penalized splines allow for flexible nonlinear effects, they do not provide a meaningful separation of linear and nonlinear effect components. We therefore employ a Demmler-Reinsch basis expansion, which yields an orthogonal decomposition of each additive effect into linear and nonlinear parts and show theoretically that both effect components can be estimated consistently. To facilitate data-driven model building, we propose Bayesian effect selection with separate spike and slab priors on the scalar importance parameters associated with the linear and nonlinear components and implement an efficient Gibbs sampler. Through simulation studies, we demonstrate robustness to the misspecification induced by the employed asymmetric Laplace working likelihood and show superior performance relative to the MMR. In a detailed analysis of air pollution data in Madrid, Spain we highlight the added value of flexibly modeling extreme nitrogen dioxide (NO$_2$) concentrations and reveal that threshold-relevant pollution levels are driven differently by climatological variables and traffic-related spatial structure. These findings underline the need for advanced statistical models that support short-term decision-making and help local authorities mitigate, or potentially prevent, exceedances of NO$_2$ concentration limits.

</details>


### [361] [Bayesian Triangulation Splines: Spatial Adaptation on Irregular Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12296)
*Sihyeon Pyeon,Sunwoo Lim,Seonghyun Jeong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯三角剖分样条（Bayesian triangulation splines），一种在多边形非矩形域上进行局部自适应非参数回归的方法。该方法利用约束Delaunay三角剖分尊重边界几何并适应异质平滑度，在全局Sobolev平滑假设下达到最优后验收缩率，且具有理想的空间自适应性质（达到oracle速率）。仿真表明其估计精度优于现有方法，同时保持较低模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二维非参数回归方法忽视域几何形状，允许跨边界平滑，在空间和地统计应用中常不成立；同时，适应空间变化的平滑度比一维情形更具挑战性，现有方法难以充分捕捉目标函数的局部结构。

Method: 提出贝叶斯三角剖分样条（Bayesian triangulation splines）：(1) 采用约束Delaunay三角剖分在任意多边形域上构造样条，尊重边界几何；(2) 通过局部自适应基函数适应异质平滑度；(3) 设计精心构造的先验分布提升经验性能；(4) 理论上证明在全局Sobolev平滑假设下达到最优后验收缩率，并具备理想空间自适应性（oracle速率），且该oracle保证不局限于约束Delaunay三角剖分，对任何满足弱形状正则条件的三角剖分均成立。

Result: 理论结果：(1) 在全局Sobolev平滑下达到最优后验收缩率，能自适应未知平滑度；(2) 展现出理想的空间自适应性，对非均匀或局部变化的结构特征达到oracle速率；(3) 该oracle保证适用于任意满足弱形状正则条件的三角剖分。仿真结果：所提方法估计精度优于现有方法，同时模型复杂度更低。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯三角剖分样条有效解决了非矩形二维域上边界约束与局部自适应平滑两大挑战，兼具理论最优性和实用价值。该方法不依赖特定剖分类型，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Conventional nonparametric regression methods for two-dimensional non-rectangular domains often overlook domain geometry and allow smoothing across boundaries. In spatial and geostatistical applications, this assumption is frequently invalid because domain boundaries typically constrain interactions among observations. Accommodating spatially varying smoothness is also substantially more challenging than in the univariate setting, and most existing methods do not adequately capture this local structure of the target function. To address these challenges, we propose Bayesian triangulation splines, which constructs locally adaptive splines over a polygonal domain. The method employs constrained Delaunay triangulations to respect boundary geometry and adapt to heterogeneous smoothness. A carefully designed prior further improves empirical performance. Under a global Sobolev smoothness assumption, we show that the proposed method achieves the optimal posterior contraction rate and adapts to unknown smoothness. We also show that the method exhibits ideal spatial adaptation in the sense that it achieves the oracle rate for inhomogeneous or locally varying structural features. Crucially, this oracle guarantee is not specific to constrained Delaunay triangulations, but holds over any triangulation satisfying weak shape-regularity conditions. Simulation studies confirm that the proposed method outperforms existing approaches by achieving higher estimation accuracy while maintaining low model complexity.

</details>


### [362] [Bayesian nonparametric Mallows model for clustering preference data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12305)
*Lorenzo Zuccato,Veronica Vinciotti,Valeria Vitelli*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于狄利克雷过程混合模型的贝叶斯非参数Mallows模型，用于偏好学习，能够同时对聚类数量和聚类分配进行联合推断，并已集成到R包BayesMallows中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于有限混合的Mallows模型需要先验指定或事后推断聚类数量，无法在推理过程中联合确定聚类数目，这限制了模型的灵活性和实用性。

Method: 作者提出了贝叶斯非参数Mallows模型，采用狄利克雷过程混合模型（DPMM）替代有限混合模型。该模型可同时对非空聚类数量、聚类分配以及聚类特定参数进行后验推断。采样算法已集成到现有R包BayesMallows中，支持不完全排序和成对比较数据。

Result: 模拟数据表明，非参数模型在恢复正确聚类数量方面优于有限混合模型；在电影评分实证数据中，该模型能有效利用被丢弃的评分数据，提供个性化电影推荐。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯非参数Mallows模型成功实现了聚类数量与分配的全联合推断，在聚类恢复和推荐系统任务上均表现良好，扩展了Mallows模型在偏好学习中的应用范围。

Abstract: Preference learning refers to the learning of latent patterns from ranking and preference data of different kinds. Typical aims of preference learning are to infer a shared consensus ranking, to learn individual-level preferences, and to perform unsupervised clustering. The Mallows model is among the few approaches that can achieve all these objectives jointly. Previous work has developed computationally tractable methods for Bayesian inference based on a MCMC Metropolis-Hastings scheme, where clustering is performed via a finite mixture of Mallows models. Inference on the number of clusters is then conducted a posteriori. Here we propose a Bayesian nonparametric Mallows model, based on a Dirichlet process mixture model. This allows joint inference on the number of non-empty clusters and on the clustering allocation, as well as posterior inference on cluster-specific parameters. The implementation of the proposed sampling algorithm is integrated into the existing R package BayesMallows, which also supports data in the form of incomplete rankings and pairwise comparisons. Simulated data show good performance of the nonparametric model compared to a finite mixture model in terms of recovery of the correct number of clusters, while empirical data on movie ratings show the model's effectiveness in providing personalized movie recommendations on discarded ratings.

</details>


### [363] [ShrinkageTrees: An R Package for Bayesian Tree Ensembles for Survival Analysis and Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12317)
*Tijn Jacobs*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: ShrinkageTrees 是一个用于生存分析和因果推断的贝叶斯树集成 R 包，在 AFT 框架下支持右删失和区间删失数据，通过树结构和步长双重正则化（包括 Horseshoe Forest）扩展至高维场景，并提供完整的建模工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯树集成方法在高维生存分析和因果推断场景中面临过拟合风险，缺乏将树结构正则化与步长收缩相结合的灵活框架，且尚无 Horseshoe 先验在森林模型中的实现。

Method: 基于贝叶斯加性回归树（BART），在加速失效时间（AFT）框架下建模右删失和区间删失生存结局；支持将模型分解为预后成分和治疗效应成分以进行因果推断。提供两类正则化：(1) 树结构正则化——深度惩罚先验和 Dirichlet 分裂先验；(2) 步长正则化——全局-局部收缩先验，首次实现 Horseshoe Forest（对步长施加 horseshoe 先验）。采用 Rcpp 高效后端、多链 MCMC 采样，并提供 S3 方法支持拟合、预测、因果效应估计和收敛诊断。

Result: 该包将贝叶斯树集成成功扩展至高维生存分析和因果推断设置，Horseshoe Forest 在步长收缩方面展现出良好的稀疏诱导特性，双重正则化策略有效提升模型在高维场景下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ShrinkageTrees 通过创新的双重正则化框架和 Horseshoe Forest 实现，填补了贝叶斯树集成在高维生存分析与因果推断中的方法空白，为应用研究者提供了完整的计算工具链。

Abstract: ShrinkageTrees is an R package for Bayesian tree ensembles in survival analysis and causal inference. The package implements Bayesian additive regression tree models for right- and interval-censored survival outcomes within an accelerated failure time (AFT) framework, with optional decomposition into prognostic and treatment-effect components for causal inference. Two complementary forms of regularisation are available: regularisation of the tree structure, via depth-penalising priors and Dirichlet splitting priors, and regularisation of the step heights, via global-local shrinkage priors. ShrinkageTrees provides the first implementation of the Horseshoe Forest, which places a horseshoe prior on the step heights. These regularisation strategies extend Bayesian tree ensembles to high-dimensional settings. An efficient Rcpp backend, multi-chain MCMC, and S3 methods support the full workflow: fitting, prediction, causal effect estimation, and convergence diagnostics.

</details>
